# Owner Material 022 — Dark Lord trilogy (Labyrinth of Evil, Revenge of the Sith, Dark Lord): novel notes from the other agent (reference copy)

**Imported 2026-10-21** (Oct 21, 2026) from the other agent's MEGA "Grok Notes" ([member]'s export), text files only. Source folder: `[local path]`. Only the current files were copied, not the `.bak-*` / `.pre-*` versions. Every file here is a byte-for-byte copy of its source, with nothing summarized, trimmed or edited.

**Trilogy reading order:** *Labyrinth of Evil* (Luceno, 2005) → *Revenge of the Sith* (Stover novelization, 2005) → *Dark Lord: The Rise of Darth Vader* (Luceno, 2005). All three are Legends and set in 19 BBY. LoE ends where ROTS begins (Battle of Coruscant), and Dark Lord picks up about four weeks after ROTS. The combined PDF and the table below follow this order.

**Status: reference copy only.**
- **Our own notes win on any conflict.** That covers `[local path]`, `verdicts.md`, the Suspect Insight debate notes and anything else we wrote. Where this folder disagrees with them, ours stands until we check the primary text ourselves.
- **Its verdicts count as opinion, not feats.** The "Verdict implications", "Proposed verdicts.md changes", "Scaling connections", "Debate takeaway" lines, diff leans, tier placements and confidence levels are the other agent's judgments. Treat them like a forum opinion. The feat and statement logs cite novel line numbers and quote the text, and the other agent says it script-checked its quotes, but we have not re-checked them against the books. They are secondhand reads and do not count toward our own MASTERY tier for any character.
- Paths inside the notes (`[local path]`, `[local path]`, `[local path]`, `research/work-labyrinth/…`, `_extract/…`) belong to the other agent's box. The novel text files do not exist here, so the `L####` line cites cannot be checked from this box.

Combined PDF (this README, then the three books in trilogy order): `../022-dark-lord-trilogy.pdf`, built from `../022-dark-lord-trilogy.md`. Each book note also has its own PDF next to it in this folder (pandoc → WeasyPrint via `tools/md2pdf.sh`, then linearized with qpdf, no images).

Peak pre-chest-crush Grievous kills six Jedi and loses a short exchange to Mace (terrain plus a push), Dooku's POV on Grievous ("hard-pressed", "kept some secrets"), Sidious shown as foresight and concealment with no combat feats, Anakin's rage-howl collapsing the Tythe archive ceiling, and the Invisible Hand duel set up as a staged test. Cast/version table, chapter plot, feats and statements by character, 40+ character profiles, verdict leans, a quote-verification report, a raw reading log (Appendix A), a script-built concordance of every sentence naming 12 major characters (Appendix B, about 56% of the file) and proposed verdicts.md changes. |

Exchange-by-exchange breakdowns of Dooku vs Anakin + Obi-Wan, Obi-Wan vs Grievous, Mace + three Masters vs Sidious, Yoda vs Sidious and Obi-Wan vs Vader on Mustafar, a curated statement table (S1–S51), 40+ character profiles, verdict leans and proposed verdicts.md changes. About 80% of the file (§9) is an auto-harvested quote bank of every line naming eight major characters. Reports 4,994 quotes script-verified. |

Sidious appears only through perception, foresight and statements. 66 feats (F1–F66), 75 statements (S1–S75), a 12-fight scaling table, story and prose notes, and 11 character analyses. |

# Labyrinth of Evil (novel, read in full): feats, scaling, quotes and story notes

<!-- read up to: chapter fifty-six (L5289, "[TO BE CONCLUDED]"), END OF BOOK; written 2026-10-19 in house style (lotf-7-fury-notes.md), deeper pass -->

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Labyrinth of Evil* by James Luceno (Del Rey, 2005). Episode III prequel novel. 56 numbered chapters, no prologue/epilogue in this edition. About 85.6k words (`wc -w` = 85,610).
- **Continuity:** Legends. Set in **19 BBY**, the last weeks of the Clone Wars. It opens at Cato Neimoidia and ends at the start of the Battle of Coruscant, at the moment the *Revenge of the Sith* film/novel begins (Anakin and Obi-Wan jump for Coruscant, L5287; Dooku is en route to the *Invisible Hand*, L5267; Palpatine is aboard the *Invisible Hand*, L5187).
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (5,290 lines; an OCR scan "get by Mara Jade", L9). `L####` is the line number in that file. One line = one paragraph, so a single `L` can hold several sentences. OCR noise is kept verbatim and marked *(sic)* where quoted: «Were is the Chancellor?» (L4549), «Palpatinc» (L5167), «l ife» (L4949), «crumbing» (L1261), «Obi-War's» (L117), «1;5C» (L819), «achive» (L4197), «Eckway» (L943), «Travalc» (L2029), «hated» for "hatred" (L4829).
- **Method:** I read every line in order, L1–L5290, in chunks of 370–630 lines, and kept a running log (Appendix A). Text inside «…» is copied from the file and was checked by script against the source (exact substring after normalizing whitespace, curly/straight quote marks, backticks and the OCR " - - " dash spacing). Fragments separated by «...» or by « / » (my marker for a paragraph or line break) are checked separately. Results are in §9. Appendix B is a script-built concordance of **every sentence in the book that names each major character**, which is the "don't undercount" layer; the hand-curated sections (§3–§4) add the pronoun-only lines and the context.
- **Chapter heading lines:** Ch1 L19, Ch2 L109, Ch3 L153, Ch4 L233, Ch5 L263, Ch6 L297, Ch7 L345, Ch8 L387, Ch9 L475, Ch10 L575, Ch11 L665, Ch12 L815, Ch13 L901, Ch14 L979, Ch15 L1055, Ch16 L1125, Ch17 L1309, Ch18 L1373, Ch19 L1443, Ch20 L1545, Ch21 L1569, Ch22 L1655, Ch23 L1719, Ch24 L1859, Ch25 L2249, Ch26 L2415, Ch27 L2559, Ch28 L2629, Ch29 L2955, Ch30 L3045, Ch31 L3173, Ch32 L3325, Ch33 L3495, Ch34 L3673, Ch35 L3779, Ch36 L3917, Ch37 L3945, Ch38 L4069, Ch39 L4187, Ch40 L4319, Ch41 L4363, Ch42 L4383, Ch43 L4447, Ch44 L4547, Ch45 L4659, Ch46 L4679, Ch47 L4767, Ch48 L4825, Ch49 L4855, Ch50 L4903, Ch51 L4985, Ch52 L5043, Ch53 L5115, Ch54 L5147, Ch55 L5247, Ch56 L5269.
- **Feat vs. statement:** §3 is what the narration shows happening (FEAT). §4 is what characters say or think (STATEMENT). Luceno writes in rotating tight third person, so a lot of "narration" is somebody's view; those lines are marked *(POV: name)*. Dooku's POV is the single most important source in the book for Grievous and Sidious scaling, and it is a biased one (he is proud, he is hiding things from Sidious, and he is wrong about some facts, e.g. he thinks «My time is at hand», L3365).
- **Headline (what this book is for a scaler):**
  1. **Grievous at his peak.** This is the pre-chest-crush Grievous. He kills **six named-rank Jedi on-page** (four Knights in the bunker, two Jedi pilots in the hangar) without a scratch that matters, and loses an exchange to **Mace Windu** on the mag-lev roof, where Mace wins by terrain (a slash through the roof) plus a Force push, not by beating his guard.
  2. **Dooku on Grievous:** «few, if any, Jedi would be capable of defeating him. There had been moments during the extensive combat sessions when even Dooku had been hard-pressed to outduel the cyborg. / But then, Dooku had kept some secrets to himself. Just in case.» (L1669–1671), and then, after watching him spar, «Hopeless» (L3361) and «I pity you should you have to face off against any of the Council Masters» (L3343).
  3. **There is no on-page duel with Dooku or Sidious.** Dooku only appears to Anakin and Obi-Wan by hologram on Tythe and throws droids at them (Ch39, Ch41); Sidious never draws a blade or uses an offensive power on-page. The Mace–Sidious, Yoda–Sidious and Dooku–Anakin/Obi-Wan fights are in *Revenge of the Sith*. What this book supplies for those matchups is **framing, foresight, concealment and the Sith Lords' own read of Anakin**, and the explicit setup of the Invisible Hand duel as a staged test (Ch55, L5261).
  4. **Sidious's power is shown as information power:** foresight as «access to streams of possibilities» (L4193), concealment «powerful enough to hide in plain sight» (L4245), clouding the Jedi search («How did we miss seeing this?» / «The answer is in the question.», L3773–3777), and a whole-galaxy plan that turns the Jedi's own hunt into his kidnapping.
  5. **Anakin's rage-amp:** his howl brings down the archive-hall ceiling on Tythe (L4381), and even Dooku can't tell whether the dome was weak or «Skywalker's voiced rage actually called the ceiling down» (L4661).
- **Plagueis:** named once, in Dooku's POV: «Of his youth, Sidious had offered little these past thirteen years; of his Master, Darth Plagueis, even less.» (L2451). Nothing else. No Plagueis feats or statements to scale here.

- **Layout:** §1 Cast · §2 Chapter-by-chapter plot (all 56) · §3 Feats by character (§3.1 ★ duels exchange by exchange) · §4 Statements by subject character (version-tagged, reliability-graded) · §5 Character analyses · §6 Scaling connections · §7 Verdict implications (diff leans) · §8 Biggest takeaways · §9 Quote verification report · Appendix A, raw reading log · Appendix B, script concordance (every naming sentence, 12 characters) · **Proposed verdicts.md changes** (last section, as requested).

## 2. Chapter-by-chapter plot notes (every chapter)

**Ch1 (L19–107) — Cato Neimoidia, the manax orchard.** Obi-Wan (now a Council member) and Anakin (a Knight; braid «ritually severed after his audacious actions at Praesitlyn», L39; face scarred by Ventress; mechanical right hand) fight through droids toward Nute Gunray's citadel. Anakin decapitates a droid a hair from Obi-Wan's head («your head was where my lightsaber needed to go», L37). Commander Cody and a hologram of Dodonna brief them; Cody proposes riding in under the harvester beetles. Obi-Wan needles Anakin about Palpatine and Padmé.

**Ch2 (L109–151) — Under the beetles.** The column breaks up in a minefield. Anakin bounds across the mines «the Force guiding him to safe landings» (L127), cuts the pincers off a beetle holding a commando, and keeps the clone out of shock with «a Jedi healing technique» (L143). Droidekas roll down from the ramparts.

**Ch3 (L153–231) — Into the nest.** Anakin calls an EMP airburst and SPHA-T barrage on the droidekas; Cody obeys because the order came from «"the Hero with no Fear,"» (L161). Inside, Obi-Wan banks a deflected bolt off four surfaces into a door control (L201): «The beauty of Form Three» (L207). They split: Obi-Wan is the bait, Anakin goes for Gunray.

**Ch4 (L233–261) — Anakin's POV.** It is personal: Naboo, the assassination attempts on Padmé, Geonosis. Anger is «a kind of fuel» (L241). He Force-pulls Obi-Wan's dropped rebreather and pockets it. TC-16 blows the team's cover in front of Gunray's super battle droids.

**Ch5 (L263–295) — Obi-Wan's POV.** Qui-Gon, Dooku («consummate planner, consummate duelist», L265), and Anakin: «Clearly Anakin was as strong in the Force as any Jedi who had ever sat on the Council.» (L267). A bolt punctures a container of fungus spores; Obi-Wan's rebreather is gone.

**Ch6 (L297–343) — Anakin's rage run.** He calls on anger, carves through the super battle droids, fails to cut or Force-open an iris blast door (L309), and gets through only with charges. Gunray's shuttle, the *Lapiz Cutter*, is gone. He turns back to rescue Obi-Wan.

**Ch7 (L345–385) — The spore fight.** Spore-drunk Obi-Wan has held a room against «better than fifty droids» (L351) in a giddy slow-motion zone. Anakin: «If only Mace, Kit, or Shaak Ti could have seen you.» (L359). Gunray escaped. The commandos find «something of interest».

**Ch8 (L387–473) — Gunray and Haako.** Backstory: Sidious at Dorvalla, Eriadu, Naboo; Sidious quietly kept Gunray's trials from producing verdicts (L417); Dooku revealed after Geonosis that he was Sith and that his Master was Sidious (L425). Gunray realizes he left behind the mechno-chair with Sidious's hyperwave transceiver, and his real fear is not Sidious or Dooku but «Grievous! What if Grievous should find out?» (L457).

**Ch9 (L475–573) — The mechno-chair.** The chair walks off with R2-D2 and arms itself. Obi-Wan «sensed a disturbance in the Force» and pulls Anakin down before a poison-gas jet (L565). Anakin rips the cable; the chair projects Sidious, and Gunray's voice: «my Lord Sidious.» (L573).

**Ch10 (L575–663) — Bail Organa on the new Coruscant.** Checkpoints, COMPOR, the Reflex Amendment, Senator Seti Ashgad's disappearance, the explosion of Valorum's freighter, red-robed guards. The Loyalist delegation (Bail, Padmé, Mon Mothma, Taneel, Breemu, Fang Zar, Chi Eekway) is stalled by Sate Pestage.

**Ch11 (L665–813) — Yoda and Obi-Wan.** The Senate was searched and nothing found (L683). Two Council members still think Dooku is the Dark Lord (L675). Yoda's history of Dooku and Sifo-Dyas, the Kamino payments from «Tyranus», and Yoda's theory that Dooku murdered Sifo-Dyas and erased Kamino (L759). «Uncover Sidious's tracks, you must.» (L813).

**Ch12 (L815–899) — Anakin and Palpatine.** Anakin's Tatooine "what if" (he'd have out-flown Saesee Tiin «And he still would have been stronger in the Force than any of them», L823). He senses a change in Padmé (L827). He holds the mechno-chair in place with the Force against three technicians and is empowered, then ashamed, by their fear (L831–837). Palpatine calls: «Anger is natural, Anakin.» (L865); «you are one of those people, Anakin» who should be above the rules (L883).

**Ch13 (L901–977) — Palpatine receives the Loyalists.** His chair «was said to house some sort of shield generator» (L917). He turns every demand aside («I view the Constitution as a living document», L957), and jokes that «Your Majesty» is «A title more fit for a king or emperor.» (L973). He makes Padmé blush about Anakin.

**Ch14 (L979–1053) — The Council's secret; Captain Dyne.** Anakin argues for «going farther with the Force than we allow ourselves. By riding its crest.» (L1005) and cites Kolar lying about Quinlan Vos (L1013). Dyne: the Sidious image is authentic; the code is an IGBC variant; the chair's gas is Zan Arbor's; a Xi Charrian signed the engravings. Padmé feels Anakin at a distance.

**Ch15 (L1055–1123) — Grievous introduced.** From the *Invisible Hand* Grievous sees through Gunray's staged "pursuit," has his gunners destroy the V-wings' hyperdrive rings **and a squadron of Gunray's own Vulture escorts** to punish him (L1063–1067), and studies the ARC-170s. Backstory: Geonosis catacombs, Hypori, the lightsaber trophies, the Huk War and the Jedi intervention on Kalee. «I am your keeper, Viceroy. Your protector.» (L1115). Lords Sidious and Tyranus «have engineered this» retreat (L1117).

**Ch16 (L1125–1307) — Charros IV.** The Xi Char polish the cruiser and TC-16. The engraver t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak flees into the rafters; Anakin and Obi-Wan chase him; Obi-Wan worries that if Anakin calls «too strongly on the Force» the workshop will come down (L1261). Both Force-topple tables and racks to scatter the Xi Char (L1269–1271). Lead: the transceiver came from Escarte.

**Ch17 (L1309–1371) — The code is broken.** Yoda: Obi-Wan is «Losing some of his better judgment»; Mace: «He's become Anakin's partisan.» (L1321–1323). An intercepted Grievous message names Belderone. Mace: «Grievous will kill millions just to make a point.» (L1355). The Council keeps it from Palpatine.

**Ch18 (L1373–1441) — Grievous's memories.** Kalee, his wives and offspring, the shuttle crash, the IGBC enforcer contract, San Hill's offer, the Geonosian rebuild. «In mere weeks he had surpassed any of Tyranus's previous students.» (L1421). He doesn't sleep (L1423). The Republic is waiting at Belderone.

**Ch19 (L1443–1543) — Battle of Belderone.** Anakin destroys «a dozen more fighters in a heartbeat» (L1469) and harasses the *Invisible Hand*'s bridge. Grievous senses Anakin's rage through the shields and viewport (L1513). Rather than fight, he fires on the evacuee convoy and jumps out (L1527–1529). «Losses at Belderone?» «Acceptable.» (L1539–1541).

**Ch20 (L1545–1567) — Yoda and Mace.** «Trained well by Dooku, General Grievous was» (L1547). Over ten thousand dead and twenty-seven Jedi (L1553). «Rooted out, Sidious must be.» (L1565).

**Ch21 (L1569–1653) — Grievous vs Gunray.** Four MagnaGuards sweep Gunray's sentries off their feet «as if they were children» (L1601). Gunray calls Grievous «Lord Sidious's alloy puppet» (L1635). Grievous deduces the chair is in enemy hands and lifts Gunray by the neck (L1651).

**Ch22 (L1655–1717) — Dooku on Kaon (the key Grievous chapter).** Dooku and San Hill engineered the shuttle crash; most of Grievous's injuries were inflicted after he was pulled from the wreck; the Geonosians altered his mind (L1663–1667). «few, if any, Jedi would be capable of defeating him» (L1669) and Dooku's «secrets» (L1671). Sith philosophy, the Rule of Two, Dooku's own history, Sidious recruiting him, the Sifo-Dyas murder, the title Darth Tyranus (L1705). Dooku hesitates over telling Sidious about the chair, «imagining the power that could be his» (L1717), then tells him.

**Ch23 (L1719–1857) — Mace and Yoda vs Palpatine.** Palpatine plays aggrieved about Belderone. Told about the Sidious recording he says «Sidious. I know the name...» (L1829) and then «Do whatever you must to hunt Sidious down.» (L1857).

**Ch24 (L1859–2247) — Escarte.** They get themselves arrested on purpose. Agent Travale; the Bith Thal K'sar is slated for execution. In a blackout they snap their cuffs, Obi-Wan opens the cell door with the Force, and the five Aqualish guards go down «almost before it began» (L2155). K'sar leads Anakin the wrong way into a dwarf spider droid.

**Ch25 (L2249–2413) — Spider droid; K'sar talks.** Anakin is driven into the ceiling and blacks out (L2287); K'sar kills the droid with a blaster (L2291). Anakin threatens to jettison K'sar (L2315) and gets the truth: a second transceiver went to Sienar Advanced Projects for an experimental ship, delivered by a pilot.

**Ch26 (L2415–2557) — Sidious and Dooku in The Works.** The building is «Strong in the dark side - - made so by Darth Sidious» and trained Dooku and Maul (L2437). Sidious's rare anger (L2483); his foresight («I begin to see the blazes along the trail», L2527); the Naos III trap; «See to it that Obi-Wan Kenobi ceases to be an irritant.» (L2543); «Orphan Skywalker once and for all, and he will shift.» (L2549).

**Ch27 (L2559–2627) — State of the Republic.** 200,000 more troopers to the Outer Rim; the «triad of evil» (L2609). Yoda: «A labyrinth of evil, this war has become.» (L2623).

**Ch28 (L2629–2953) — Naos III.** A cantina crawl finds Fa'ale Leh, who delivered the Sith courier: Sienar «told me it was for a Jedi» (L2873), delivered «to Coruscant, of course.» (L2879). Obi-Wan senses the roof charge and overturns the bed over her (L2881). Anakin and Obi-Wan kill two humans, two Trandoshans and a Falleen (L2883–2889). The ice-sled chase starts.

**Ch29 (L2955–3043) — The sled chase.** Obi-Wan turns a bolt into a pursuer's engine (L2963) and Force-pushes another pilot under the ice (L2965), then is snatched by a sharptooth collector's claw (L2971). Anakin Force-leaps from his sled to the claw, cuts open the skiff's cockpit floor and rides the claw down, «Force-fastened to the outside» (L2995–3007). Fa'ale, wounded: «An area called The Works.» (L3043).

**Ch30 (L3045–3171) — The Works raid.** Mace, Shaak Ti, ARC Commander Valiant and Captain Dyne enter the LiMerge building. «Supreme Chancellor Palpatine had overruled the committee» to allow it (L3055). Mace feels the dark side and «felt alert in all ways» (L3103). Forensics find Dooku (195 cm, boots) and a second being in slippers; the trail leads by tunnel to the western Senate District (L3167).

**Ch31 (L3173–3323) — Yoda vs Palpatine (round two).** The Outer Rim sieges; who the clones answer to; «A skilled duelist you are.» «When need be» (L3271–3273); Yoda on Dooku («Die fighting, he will.», L3285), on Sidious withdrawing if deprived of an apprentice (L3291), on Grievous («More machine than alive», L3305). «Coming closer to him, we are.» (L3323).

**Ch32 (L3325–3493) — Grievous spars; Dooku teaches; Sidious gives orders (the key duel-scaling chapter).** Grievous with three trophy sabers against his IG-100s; Dooku's critique and his Makashi disarm-and-point (L3343–3351); «Hopeless, he thought.» (L3361). Sidious, by hologram: «Such is the way of bounty hunters. I should have foreseen this.» (L3375), «The Force is strong in Skywalker» (L3379), the Tythe gambit, Utapau, and «Prepare, General, for what will be your finest hour.» (L3493).

**Ch33 (L3495–3671) — Belderone MedStar.** The Works building belonged to LiMerge Power; Tythe was LiMerge's headquarters and is now occupied. Anakin wants Coruscant instead: «Together, we can defeat any Sith.» (L3577). Obi-Wan presses about Padmé. «Infinite sadness» (L3659).

**Ch34 (L3673–3777) — The Grungeon tunnels.** Duelist Elite droids cut to pieces in the LiMerge building: «Only Sidious, Dooku, or Sidious's previous apprentice could have performed the amputations.» (L3685). Unet refugees. A hidden speeder bike that both Masters walked past: «How did we miss seeing this?» «The answer is in the question.» (L3773–3777).

**Ch35 (L3779–3915) — Palpatine and Anakin.** «I can't see into the future, my boy.» «What if I told you that I could?» (L3793–3797). «Obi-Wan is wise. But he has no heart, sir.» (L3831). «Soon you may have to make a choice.» (L3839). Anakin chooses Tythe.

**Ch36 (L3917–3943) — The trail turns toward 500 Republica.** The quarry is human; slipper size is known.

**Ch37 (L3945–4067) — Tythe space battle.** Red Squadron destroys a Techno Union ship at the moment its shields drop (L4007–4017) and is wiped out on the way down except for Anakin and Obi-Wan (L4047). They decide to fly in through the open top of the north containment dome.

**Ch38 (L4069–4185) — 500 Republica sub-basement; the attack begins.** The trail goes cold. The building shakes; district shields come up; comms are jammed. Mace senses «danger, frenzy, pain, and death.» (L4147) and sends Shaak Ti to find Palpatine.

**Ch39 (L4187–4317) — Dooku waits on Tythe; Yoda meditates.** Dooku's POV on Sidious's foresight (L4193) and on the Jedi (L4195); Anakin and Obi-Wan find him through his «Quey'tek technique» (L4215); fifty droids. Yoda, in the Force, hears Qui-Gon's voice and realizes «This was Sidious: powerful enough to hide in plain sight.» (L4245). The attack on Coruscant; «Not Grievous - - Sidious.» (L4299).

**Ch40 (L4319–4361) — Dyne finds the hidden lift.** A hydraulic platform beneath the trail's end; a 50 m shaft; one flesh-and-blood and droids behind a door.

**Ch41 (L4363–4381) — The archive hall.** Endless droids. «Can't have me becoming too powerful, now, can we, Master?» (L4375). Anakin's howl brings the ceiling down (L4381).

**Ch42 (L4383–4445) — The Senate evacuation.** Shaak Ti and Stass Allie can't find Palpatine (he's «in his residence», L4413). Bail flies Padmé, Mon Mothma and C-3PO; Vultures shoot them down.

**Ch43 (L4447–4545) — Grievous over Coruscant; Dyne dies.** Grievous's feigned desperation, mass-shadow mines, the Praesitlyn jamming suite; he orders crippled droid fighters to «convert to explosive devices» and hit plazas and shelters (L4467–4475). Yoda finally feels Sidious «on Coruscant!» (L4531). Two Geonosians' sonic weapons kill Dyne, Valiant and the commandos; Palpatine walks out unmasked with two assassin droids (L4537–4545).

**Ch44 (L4547–4657) — 500 Republica penthouse.** Shaak Ti can't read Palpatine through the Force (L4575). Grievous's gunboat blows in the window; Stass Allie and six Red Guards hold; Shaak Ti tackles Palpatine. They take him down to the mag-lev.

**Ch45 (L4659–4677) — Dooku digs out.** He isn't sure whether the dome failed or «Skywalker's voiced rage actually called the ceiling down» (L4661). Grievous: «Supreme Chancellor Palpatine will soon be ours.» (L4675).

**Ch46 (L4679–4765) — Padmé's crash.** Vulture droids in walking mode on a skybridge; Padmé and Bail shoot at one with near-empty rifles. Bail: the Separatists «have played right into his hands» (L4717).

**Ch47 (L4767–4823) — The mag-lev.** Mace and Kit clear the boarded car; **Mace + Kit vs Grievous + two MagnaGuards** on the roof (§3.1, Duel 1). The train drops when its controls are shot out.

**Ch48 (L4825–4853) — Buried on Tythe.** Anakin's vision of Padmé stalked by «a dark, towering creature with a mechanical head» (L4827). «I told you I was becoming more powerful.» (L4837). «Tythe was never the target. We were.» (L4853).

**Ch49 (L4855–4901) — The V-notch.** Mace and Kit hold the passengers in place with the Force; Mace thinks holding the train would take «Perhaps five Yodas.» (L4865). Palpatine goes to the Sah'c bunker with Shaak Ti, Stass Allie and four Knights. The hijacked escort gunship fires; Shaak Ti and Stass are blown ten meters, burning (L4895).

**Ch50 (L4903–4983) — The bunker.** Mace and Kit chase a decoy. Grievous's POV on the mag-lev («Tyranus had warned him about Mace Windu's prowess with a blade, and now he understood.», L4929). **Grievous vs four Jedi Knights and the Red Guards** (§3.1, Duel 3). Palpatine's taunts; the beacon.

**Ch51 (L4985–5041) — Aftermath.** Mace counts past forty dead in the tunnel (L4997). Shaak Ti: «I fought Grievous on Hypori... I knew what he was capable of. But this...» (L5005). The advisers leak the abduction.

**Ch52 (L5043–5113) — Tythe aftermath.** «You should keep me fighting. You shouldn't give me time to think.» (L5079). «My mind isn't as fast as my lightsaber.» (L5087). Palpatine's recorded plea; «Why does fate target the people who are most important to me?» (L5111).

**Ch53 (L5115–5145) — The shuttle chase.** Grievous out-flies Agen Kolar, Saesee Tiin and Pablo-Jill by using civilian traffic as cover (L5119–5123). TC-16's warning to C-3PO.

**Ch54 (L5147–5245) — The *Invisible Hand*.** Grievous has his own cruiser fire a disabling burst on his shuttle and tractor it in; **Grievous vs two Jedi pilots** (§3.1, Duel 4). The HoloNet broadcast. Gunray celebrates. Yoda: «Beginning, the final act was!» (L5245).

**Ch55 (L5247–5267) — Dooku's orders.** The Invisible Hand duel is set up as a test of Anakin: «Should he by some fluke best you, however, I will control the outcome to spare you any unnecessary embarrassment» (L5261).

**Ch56 (L5269–5289) — Launch.** «there's no one else I'd rather fly with» (L5271); «At least you said we.» (L5285). [TO BE CONCLUDED]

---

## 3. Feats by character (FEAT = what the narration shows)

Each entry gives the **version tag**, the chapter and line, a short exact quote, the **result**, and the **scaling implication**. Duels marked ★ are broken down exchange by exchange in §3.1.

### 3.1 ★ Duels, exchange by exchange

#### ★ Duel 1 — Mace Windu (+ Kit Fisto) vs Grievous (+ 2 MagnaGuards), mag-lev roof, Coruscant (Ch47, L4807–4821; Grievous's own view Ch50, L4929–4931)
*Versions: Mace, 19 BBY (LoE); Kit Fisto, 19 BBY; Grievous, 19 BBY (LoE, pre-crush) → computer-adapted mode; MagnaGuards (IG-100), 19 BBY.*
*Conditions:* the roof of a speeding mag-lev on a cantilevered line, in a strong headwind. Mace is «holding himself in place by the Force» (L4795); Grievous is held by magnetism. Grievous uses two sabers (no four-arm split). Mace and Kit have just cleared a car of battle droids and controlled the flying debris with the Force (L4795).
1. **Open.** Grievous draws two sabers. «By the time they were ignited, Mace was already on and all over the cyborg, batting away at the two blades, swinging low at Grievous's artificial legs, thrusting at his skeletal face.» (L4811) → Mace takes the initiative from the first instant. **Mace sets the tempo.**
2. **Footing.** «For the cyborg, though, the coherence hindered as much as it helped, whereas Mace never remained in one place for very long.» (L4811) → Mace's Force-anchoring lets him move; Grievous's magnetic anchoring pins him. The **conditions favor Mace**, a caveat against using this fight as a clean skill gap.
3. **Mace's read of Grievous.** «Grievous was well trained in the Jedi arts. He could recognize the hand of Dooku in the general's training and technique. His strikes were as forceful as any Mace had ever had to counter, and his speed was astonishing. But he didn't know Vaapad» (L4813) → the best single **Grievous hype line** in Legends from a Master's POV: top-of-scale strength and «astonishing» speed. The edge is **Vaapad**, i.e. technique and form knowledge.
4. **Side fight: Kit vs 2 MagnaGuards.** «the Nautolan's blade was a cyclone of blazing blue light» … «Kit simply wasn't allowing that.» (L4813); «he spun around the guards, claiming a limb from both with each rotation: left legs, right arms, right legs...» (L4815) → **Kit dismantles two IG-100s in seconds**, low diff. The wind finishes them.
5. **Grievous adapts.** «The loss … neither distracted nor slowed him. His sole setting was attack. Successful at analyzing Mace's lightsaber style, those same computers suggested that Grievous alter his stance and posture, along with the angle of his parries, ripostes, and thrusts.» (L4815) → **computer-adapted mode**: the processors learn an opponent's style mid-fight.
6. **The adaptation works.** «The result wasn't Vaapad, but it was close enough, and Mace wasn't interested in prolonging the contest any longer than necessary.» (L4817) → once adapted, Grievous is **close enough** to Vaapad that Mace chooses to end it with a trick instead of continuing the exchange. That is evidence the straight fence was **not free** for Mace.
7. **Terrain trick.** Mace «slashed, guiding it through the roof of the car, perpendicular to Grievous's stalwart advance» (L4817). «the living part of him wasn't always in perfect sync with his alloy servos.» (L4817) → a mind-body sync flaw: the organic brain and the servos disagree (sidestep vs lunge, L4819).
8. **Slip and counter.** «Grievous's left talon lost magnetic purchase on the roof, and the general faltered.» Mace goes for the gut, but «a swift half twist that would have sent Mace's head hurtling into the canyon had the maneuver prevailed» (L4819) → even off balance, **Grievous's counter nearly beheads Mace**. Real danger.
9. **Finish.** «Mace leapt backward, out of the range of the slicing blades, and Force-pushed outward, just at the instant of Grievous's single misstep.» (L4821) → **Grievous knocked off the train**; not wounded. He grabs the rail and is picked up by a borrowed gunship (L4931).
10. **Grievous's verdict (Ch50).** «Tyranus had warned him about Mace Windu's prowess with a blade, and now he understood. His literal "misstep" had shamed him» (L4929); «if not the brief duel on the roof of the train car» (L4929, he did not enjoy it).
- **Result:** Mace wins the exchange; Grievous is unharmed and goes on to complete the mission. Kit beats two MagnaGuards solo.
- **Scaling:** *Mace (19 BBY) > Grievous (19 BBY), low–mid diff.* Mace wins initiative and the exchange and is never touched, but he chose terrain plus a push over a straight finish once Grievous had adapted, and one half-twist nearly killed him. A brief duel, a ring-out, no injury. Grievous concedes Mace's superiority in his own POV. Kit treats MagnaGuards as fodder (cf. L1599: «more than a match for most»).

#### ★ Duel 2 — Dooku's spar demonstration vs Grievous + MagnaGuards, *Invisible Hand* forward hold (Ch32, L3327–3365)
*Versions: Dooku, 19 BBY (Tyranus); Grievous, 19 BBY (LoE, pre-crush); MagnaGuards.*
1. **Grievous spars with three trophy sabers** vs his IG-100s, «parrying thrusts of the guards' pulse-weaponed staffs, slicing the recycled air a hairbreadth from the expressionless faces of his opponents, incapacitating arm and leg servos when he could. Grievous was a force to be reckoned with, to be sure» (L3327).
2. **Speed.** «Grievous was fast, and so were his IG 100-series sparring partners. They had the advantage of size and brute strength. They executed moves almost faster than the human eye could follow.» (L3331) → a speed baseline for both Grievous and the IG-100s (Dooku's eye).
3. **Dooku's diagnosis.** «they were not invincible, not even Grievous, because they could be confused by unpredictability, and they had no understanding of finesse» (L3333); «Going through their programmed motions. An Ataro attack answered by Shii-Cho; Soresu answered by Lus-ma...» (L3339).
4. **The critique.** «Power moves served you well on Hypori against Jedi such as Daakman Barrek and Tarr Seir. But I pity you should you have to face off against any of the Council Masters.» (L3343)
5. **The demonstration.** A Makashi «rapid X in the air» (L3345), then «He flicked his blade quickly, ridding two of the guards of their staffs, then placing the glowing tip a millimeter from Grievous's death-helmeted visage.» (L3349) → **Dooku disarms two MagnaGuards and puts his blade at Grievous's face in one movement**, from a standing start, with Grievous mid-spar.
6. **The lesson.** «Finesse. Artfulness. Economy. Otherwise, my friend, I fear that you will end up beyond the repair of even the Geonosians.» (L3351); Grievous: «I take your meaning, my lord.» (L3355).
7. **Retry.** «Hopeless, he thought.» (L3361). «He watched Grievous disarm two of the MagnaGuards.» (L3365).
- **Result:** a demo, not a duel. Grievous was not trying to fight Dooku.
- **Scaling:** *Dooku (19 BBY) > Grievous (19 BBY)* in technique, which agrees with L1669–1671 (hard-pressed, but kept secrets). Do not use the demo as a full win: it is a tap on an unengaged student. It strongly supports **"Grievous loses to Council-level finesse duelists"** as Dooku's expert opinion.

#### ★ Duel 3 — Grievous (+6 MagnaGuards) vs four Jedi Knights, Red Guards and troopers, Sah'c bunker (Ch50, L4927–4959)
*Versions: Grievous, 19 BBY (LoE, pre-crush); four unnamed Jedi Knights (human male, Ithorian male, Talz male, Twi'lek female); Red Guards.*
0. **Approach.** «Grievous and six MagnaGuards cut a bloody swath through the broad corridors» (L4927). At the door he deflects fire from three troopers and decapitates them (L4933). He opens the hatch with Tyranus's code (L4935).
1. **Bolts by hand.** «Also for effect, he deflected the initial flurry of blaster bolts with his clawed hands, before drawing two of his lightsabers.» (L4939) → bare-hand bolt deflection.
2. **Grievous's assessment.** «he knew in the first moments of contest that he had nothing to worry about. Compared to Mace Windu, the four were mere novices, whose lightsaber techniques were some of the earliest Grievous had mastered.» (L4939) → POV-biased, but the results bear it out.
3. **Elite vs guards (sidebar).** The Red Guards' «force pikes sliced and jabbed through the near-impervious armor of the droids. But they were no real match» (L4941). «Grievous saw six of the soldiers and three of the Red Guards jolted to spasming deaths by the MagnaGuards' double-tipped scepters. One of his elite had gone down» (L4943). The surviving elites «altered their combat stances and offensive moves to adapt» (L4945).
4. **"Fencing."** «Fencing with his four adversaries - - for that's all the fight amounted to» (L4943); «If time wasn't of the essence, he might have protracted the fight.» (L4945)
5. **Kill 1 (human).** «Feinting with the blade in his right hand, he removed the head of one Jedi with the blade in his left.» (L4945)
6. **Kill 2 (Ithorian).** The Ithorian boots his comrade's rolling head, drops his guard, and «received as penalty a thrust to the heart» (L4947).
7. **Four blades + repulsors.** «For practice, Grievous called two more blades from his belt, grasping them in his feet even as the antigrav repulsors built into his legs were lifting him from the floor, making him every bit as agile as the Force did the Jedi.» (L4947)
8. **Kill 3 (Talz).** «he severed the blade hand of the Talz, then his opposing foot, then took his l ife, as well.» (L4949, *sic*)
9. **The Twi'lek.** «The fourth he intimidated into retreat by wheeling all four blades, transforming himself into a veritable chopping machine.» He lets her «land glancing blows on his forearms and shoulders. The burns did little more than add a new odor to the room.» (L4951) → **durability**: Jedi saber glancing hits on his limbs are cosmetic.
10. **Kill 4 (Twi'lek).** «Which he did with a single blade to the heart» (L4955).
11. **Red Guards.** He beheads a feinting guard with two blades (L4957), «speared from behind in both kidneys», «Opening the backs of another's thighs», «disemboweling the fourth» (L4959). The last is already dead.
- **Result:** four Jedi Knights dead, four Red Guards dead by Grievous; nine soldiers and guards dead by the elites; one MagnaGuard lost (blinded, still fighting).
- **Scaling:** *Grievous (19 BBY) vs 4 Jedi Knights at once: Grievous, low diff* (he chose to play with them). Bare-handed bolt deflection; saber glancing hits are trivial on his alloy limbs. This is the strongest single anti-Knight showing in the book.

#### ★ Duel 4 — Grievous vs two Jedi Knight pilots, *Invisible Hand* hangar (Ch54, L5173–5183)
*Versions: Grievous, 19 BBY (LoE, pre-crush); two unnamed Jedi Knights.*
1. Two pilots, sensing the incoming burst, «all but glued themselves to the shuttle» and survive the disabling fire (L5173). They blow their canopies and leap to the deck deflecting droid fire (L5175); one plunges his blade through the shuttle's hatch (L5177).
2. «Even while continuing to parry blaster bolts, they surged at him, engaging the two lightsabers he drew from his cloak.» (L5179)
3. «These Jedi were more proficient than the ones he had fought in the bunker, but not skilled enough to challenge him.» (L5181)
4. **One-move double kill.** «Grievous waited until the last instant to command his legs to raise him up several centimeters. Then he extended his lightsabers straight out from his sides, angled slightly downward. / Slipping past the flashing strikes of his opponents, Grievous's blades pierced the chests of both.» (L5181–5183)
- **Result:** two Jedi dead in one technique. Grievous is unhurt.
- **Scaling:** *Grievous vs 2 better-than-average Knights: low diff.* Combined with Duel 3: six Jedi Knights killed on-page, zero meaningful damage.

#### ★ Fight 5 — Obi-Wan + Anakin vs assassins (2 humans, 2 Trandoshans, 1 Falleen with a vibro-ax), Naos III (Ch28, L2881–2889)
*Versions: Obi-Wan, 19 BBY; Anakin, 19 BBY (Knight).*
1. Obi-Wan senses the roof charge «An instant before it blew inward» and overturns the bed over Fa'ale (L2881) → danger sense.
2. Anakin deflects bolts and parries the vibro-ax (L2883).
3. Obi-Wan calls his saber, leaps, and his «blade slicing both hands off one of the humans»; the second falls onto it (L2885).
4. Anakin «stood unmoving in the center of the room, holding his own against the two reptilian aliens and the wielder of the vibro-ax» (L2887); a Force push blows a Trandoshan «backward through the room's only window» (L2887).
5. Obi-Wan beheads the other Trandoshan (L2889: «Obi-Wan tracked the flight of the alien's head»).
6. Anakin's belly-slide «amputating the Falleen's legs at the knees», then «rid the Falleen of blaster and hand» and stabs him; the bandolier ignites (L2889).
- **Scaling:** fodder. Shows both have a normal skilled-Knight floor. The bounty hunters chose capture over killing (L3371).

#### ★ Fight 6 — Spore-intoxicated Obi-Wan vs 50+ droids, Cato Neimoidia (Ch7, L347–351)
*Version: Obi-Wan, 19 BBY (spore-intoxicated).*
- «More or less rooted in place, he swayed, wobbled, tottered, and reeled, evading or parrying an almost unremitting current of blaster bolts.» (L347); «He was in the Force, to be sure, but deep in some other zone as well, giddy with astonishment, as the world unfolded in slow motion.» (L349); «held his own against better than fifty droids» (L351).
- **Scaling:** Soresu at a high level, even impaired. The slow-motion zone is an involuntary altered state, so tag the version separately.

#### ★ Fight 7 — Anakin + Obi-Wan vs Dooku's droids, archive hall, Tythe (Ch39–41, L4199–4227, L4365–4381)
*Versions: Anakin, 19 BBY → rage-amped; Obi-Wan, 19 BBY; Dooku, 19 BBY (by hologram, rooms away).*
1. Dooku appears by **hologram**; he is «Rooms distant» in the control room (L4203). There is **no Dooku duel.**
2. Through Dooku's **Quey'tek** concealment they still sense him: «We know you're here, Dooku» … «We can sense you.» (L4211); «Exceptional, he thought. Despite his mastery of the Quey'tek technique for hiding oneself in the Force, they had located him!» (L4215).
3. Fifty droids, then more (L4217). «for every droid either of them destroyed, five more would appear» (L4369). Anakin is «Force-pushing four droids to the floor, hewing half a dozen more with his flashing blade» and runs across their heads (L4367). Obi-Wan tires (L4365).
4. **Rage-amp.** «"Dooku!" he howled. / With such force and wrath that the ceiling of the vast hall began to collapse.» (L4379–4381). The hall is «thirty meters high and three times that in circumference» (L4189).
5. **Aftermath.** Dooku, not sure whether the dome failed or «Skywalker's voiced rage actually called the ceiling down» (L4661), says that «Had Dooku not leapt forcefully at the last moment, he might have been buried» (L4661).
- **Scaling:** an AoE amp feat for **Anakin (rage-amped)**, with author-side ambiguity. Dooku himself treats it as possibly real, and the book's narration («With such force and wrath») leans real. Obi-Wan afterward: «On Vjun, you needed a grenade to do this much damage.» (L4835). (Collapse line: L4381.)

### 3.2 General Grievous

**Version: Grievous, 19 BBY (LoE, pre-crush)**, unless tagged.

| # | Ch | Line | Feat (exact quote) | Result | Scaling implication |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| G1 | 15 | L1063–1067 | «Target and destroy their hyperdrive rings. Then you are to target and destroy one squadron of the shuttle's escort Vulture fighters.» | Six rings and twelve of his own fighters destroyed to punish Gunray | Ruthless command; disciplining allies |
| G2 | 15 | L1081 | «And Jedi, of course - - though none had lived to speak of him.» *(Grievous, Geonosis 22 BBY, POV)* | Unknown number of Jedi killed in the Geonosis catacombs | Backstory statement-feat; no count |
| G3 | 15 | L1083 | «at Hypori he had been able to add several more, two of which he wore inside his command cloak even now» *(Grievous, Hypori)* | Saber trophies | Cross-ref: Hypori (Clone Wars 2003) |
| G4 | 15 | L1085 | «Grievous had become infamous during the long conflict: conquering worlds, defeating grand armies, exterminating entire colonies of Huk.» *(Grievous, Kaleesh organic)* | Warlord record | Strategic only; organic version |
| G5 | 18 | L1423 | «General Grievous did not sleep.» He uses «brief periods of stasis in a pod-like chamber» (L1425) | — | Stamina/maintenance note |
| G6 | 19 | L1513 | «He could sense it, even through the Invisible Hand's shimmering shields and the viewport's transparisteel.» | He reads Anakin's rage as a pilot | Perception; not Force-sensing as such |
| G7 | 19 | L1515 | «Grievous was confident that, if pressed, he could turn the tide of battle, but that was not his present mandate.» | Ordered to protect the Separatist Council | Statement of capability (POV) |
| G8 | 19 | L1527–1529 | the cruiser fired «at the convoy of evacuees and its escort starfighters» | Thousands dead; clean escape | Atrocity used as a disengagement tactic |
| G9 | 20 | L1553 | «More than ten thousand killed. Twenty-seven Jedi.» | Belderone toll (Mace) | Not all personal kills; fleet-level |
| G10 | 21 | L1601 | MagnaGuards sweep Gunray's sentries «as if they were children» | Intimidation | Elite baseline |

| G13 | 44 | L4609–4611 | Gunboat assault through Palpatine's window | Repelled by Stass Allie and the Red Guards | Grievous does not personally engage here |
| G14 | 47 | L4807–4821 | ★ Duel 1 vs Mace (+ Kit) | Knocked off the train | See §3.1 |
| G15 | 50 | L4929–4931 | grabs the mag-lev rail at the last instant; picked up by gunship | Survives the fall | Reflexes/durability |
| G16 | 50 | L4927–4959 | ★ Duel 3, four Knights + guards | All dead | Low-diff vs Knights |
| G17 | 50 | L4939 | deflects bolts with clawed hands | — | Reaction speed + hand durability |
| G18 | 50 | L4951 | «The burns did little more than add a new odor to the room.» | Saber glancing blows ineffective | Limb durability vs sabers (glancing only) |
| G19 | 53 | L5119–5123 | «Grievous was almost as adept at handling a ship as he was a lightsaber» … evades Kolar, Tiin and Pablo-Jill by using traffic and civilians | Escapes to the flagship | Pilot feat; note «Grievous was unable to match them maneuver for maneuver» (L5123) |

**Limits shown:** magnetic anchoring is a liability (L4811). A brain–servo sync lag (L4817). Dooku: «confused by unpredictability … no understanding of finesse» (L3333). He loses the Mace exchange. He is *not* a Force user: his rage-sense of Anakin is through observation (L1513). Palpatine unsettles him with a mind game («For no apparent reason, Grievous felt uncertain.», L5199).

### 3.8 Yoda

**Version: Yoda, 19 BBY.** No combat.
- **Y1, Ch39 L4229:** «he could see through the eyes of his fellow Jedi, almost as if they were the Temple's remote sensors» → remote viewing; on strong days he hears Qui-Gon (L4229–4231). **Yoda, 19 BBY (meditative)**.
- **Y2, Ch39 L4233–4235:** blocked by the dark side: «The dark side frustrated his every effort to see clearly.» → limit.
- **Y3, Ch43 L4529–4531:** watches the Jedi through the Force; «for the first time he could feel Sidious. Feel him on Coruscant!»
- **Y4, Ch54 L5241:** «No longer on Coruscant, Yoda thought» → correct read of Sidious leaving (as Palpatine, aboard the *Invisible Hand*), but still no identification.
- **Y5, Geonosis (recalled by Dooku) L2451:** «easy parrying and, indeed, handling of the Sith lightning» *(Yoda, Geonosis)*.

### 3.9 Kit Fisto, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, other Jedi
- **Kit Fisto, 19 BBY:** ★ two MagnaGuards dismembered (L4813–4815); debris control and bolt returns with Mace (L4793–4795); TK on the passengers (L4863); flies the cruiser in pursuit (L5125).
- **Shaak Ti, 19 BBY:** in the Works raid (Ch30). Tackles Palpatine and parries ricochets (L4603, L4613). Survives a gunship explosion that hurls her «ten meters through the air» and turns it into a tucked roll (L4895). Can't read Palpatine (L4575). Hypori memory (L5005).
- **Stass Allie, 19 BBY:** «Stass Allie stood immobile in the rush of wind, noise, and blaster bolts.» With six Red Guards: «Droids fell armless, legless, headless before they made it two meters into the room.» Her deflections hit the droids waiting on the gunboat (L4611). Survives the same pad blast (L4895–4897).
- **Agen Kolar, Saesee Tiin, Pablo-Jill:** come closest to stopping the shuttle (L5121).
- **27 Jedi die at Belderone** (L1553) **and six Knights die to Grievous on Coruscant** (L4945–4955, L5183).

### 3.10 MagnaGuards (IG-100)
- «trained in the Jedi arts, and more than a match for most» (L1599, Gunray POV).
- Sweep the Neimoidian sentries (L1601); spar with Grievous at near-invisible speed (L3331); adapt to the Red Guards' tactics (L4945); kill nine soldiers and guards (L4943). **Two are dismembered by Kit in seconds** (L4815). They lose staffs to Dooku's flick (L3349).

---

## 4. Statements: every scaling-relevant quote, grouped by the character it is about

Format: **Ch / L / speaker or POV → about [version]** — ‹quote› — *reliability* — note.
Reliability key: **A** = omniscient-leaning narration, or the result confirms it; **B** = an informed character's POV or dialogue (expert, but with an agenda or limited info); **C** = a biased, ignorant or self-serving source, or a rumor.
A comparative statement **links two versions only when the speaker knew both**; that is noted where it applies.

| 8 / L457 | Gunray → Grievous, 19 BBY | «Grievous! What if Grievous should find out?» | C | Fear factor > Sidious/Dooku *for Gunray* |
| 15 / L1073 | Grievous POV | Clone pilots «evinced none of the supernatural perception afforded to the Jedi by the Force» | B | He knows the difference between Force and skill |
| 15 / L1081 | Grievous POV → Grievous, Geonosis | «And Jedi, of course - - though none had lived to speak of him.» | B | Unknown count |
| 15 / L1085 | Narration → Grievous, Kaleesh | «As a former swordmaster» | A | Pre-cyborg skill base; organic version |
| 15 / L1121 | Grievous → Gunray | «If none offers, Chairman, then I will take one.» | — | Characterization |

Grievous's own POV; Dooku's chapter (L1669) partly confirms it |

The rebuild boosted his *prowess*: the cyborg is a different (stronger) version than the Kaleesh |

- Kit: «Nautolan hero of the Battle of Mon Calamari» (L4793, A); «the Nautolan's blade was a cyclone of blazing blue light» (L4813, A); Grievous ranks him with Mace (L4943, B).
- Shaak Ti: «As beautiful as a flower, as deadly as a viper, Shaak Ti was the Jedi Master one wanted by one's side in chaotic circumstances» (L3095, B: Mace POV); «I fought Grievous on Hypori» (L5005).

---

## 5. Character analyses: personality, arc, relationships, abilities, limits, and what LoE adds to their scaling

### 5.1 General Grievous — *Grievous, 19 BBY (LoE, pre-crush)*
- **Personality:** proud, theatrical («Posing for effect», L4939; «heightened the drama of his entry», L4935), contemptuous of Jedi as an Order but respectful of their craft («He could respect the Jedi for that», L1085). Shame-sensitive: glad no MagnaGuard survived to witness his "misstep" (L4929). Sadistic with Gunray (L1063–1067, L1651). He sincerely believes in the Kaleesh grievance (L1087). Dooku's chapter reveals he is **not who he thinks he is**: the crash was staged, the injuries inflicted afterward, and the mind altered (L1663–1667).
- **Arc in this book:** introduced as an off-screen terror (Ch8), shown in command (Ch15, Ch19, Ch21), humanized by memory (Ch18), deconstructed by Dooku (Ch22, Ch32), then at full power in Ch47–54: he fails against Mace, butchers six Knights and abducts the Chancellor. The book ends with him in his apparent "finest hour" (L3493), one book before his death.
- **Relationships:** Dooku (teacher; Dooku pities and uses him, «Poor Grievous… Pitiful creature», L1663); Sidious (master; Sidious's word games unsettle him, L5199); Gunray (contempt); his MagnaGuards (his own programming, L1599); Mace (the first Jedi who beats him on-page; Grievous is shamed); Shaak Ti (Hypori; she is traumatized by the bunker, L5005).
- **Abilities shown:** Jedi-form saber training under Dooku; two-, three- and four-blade styles, with feet for blades 3–4 (L4947); leg repulsors for aerial agility (L4947, L5181); bare-hand bolt deflection (L4939); limbs shrug off glancing saber hits (L4951); grip/magnetic feet (L4811); combat computers that analyze and adapt to an opponent's style mid-fight (L4815–4817); «astonishing» speed and top-tier striking force (L4813); fleet command, piloting (L5119), strategic terror (L4467–4475).
- **Limits shown:** no finesse; confused by unpredictability (L3333); a brain–servo sync lag (L4817); his magnetic stance is a liability (L4811); he loses to Mace in a short exchange; Dooku held «secrets» back in their spars (L1671); Dooku says he'd lose to «any of the Council Masters» (L3343). He's out-flown maneuver for maneuver by three Jedi aces (L5123). He isn't Force-sensitive, so he relies on his own perception.
- **What LoE adds to his scaling:**
  1. **The best on-page anti-Knight feats in his Legends record:** six Knights killed, low diff (Ch50, Ch54).
  2. **The best Legends hype line from a Master** (Mace: «as forceful as any…», «astonishing» speed).
  3. **A clean ceiling:** he loses to Vaapad Mace and, by Dooku's statement, to the Council tier.
  4. The **adaptive-computer mechanic**, which matters for any long fight (he gets better as the fight goes on).
- **Continuity caution:** the 2003 *Clone Wars* micro-series and LoE conflict (Wookieepedia flags this; see shaak-ti-quotes.txt / mace-legends-quotes.txt). In *Clone Wars* Ch.25 Grievous captures Palpatine after fighting Shaak Ti, Roron Corobb, Foul Moudama and others, and Mace crushes his chest. In LoE, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie survive the pad blast and the four Knights die in the bunker. Keep the two accounts separate; don't merge their kill lists.

### 5.2 Count Dooku / Darth Tyranus — *Dooku, 19 BBY*
- **Personality:** an aristocratic aesthete («What was to become of elegance and gallantry», L3329) who despises the saber-trophy habit (L3327). A self-justifying idealist: Sith as «balance» (L2443) and «order» (L2449). Proud and insecure about his standing with Sidious («Was Sidious thinking the same of him now?», L3363; «My time is at hand», L3365). He wavers on Tythe («What if the Jedi should triumph, after all?», L4665) and is quietly disloyal («imagining the power that could be his», L1717).
- **Arc:** in hiding; summoned to The Works (Ch26); the failed Naos III trap (Ch28–32); bait on Tythe (Ch39–45); he leaves for the Invisible Hand with orders to stage a duel he will lose in ROTS (Ch55). The reader knows, and Dooku doesn't, that the test is scripted around him.
- **Relationships:** Sidious (master; Dooku hides «secrets» from both sides, L1671); Grievous (student/tool); Yoda (former teacher; the Vjun conversation haunts him, L3335); Anakin and Obi-Wan («the scourge of Dooku's existence», L2521); Ventress (failed apprentice, L3361); Sifo-Dyas (his murder victim, L1705).
- **Abilities shown/stated:** Makashi precision («Finesse. Artfulness. Economy.», L3351); «the Temple's most agile swordmaster and instructor» (L1687); expert in all seven forms (he coached them, L3333); Sith lightning (Geonosis, L2451); Quey'tek concealment (L4215); a Force leap out of the dome collapse (L4661); strategic manipulation (Grievous's creation, the codes, the Nelvaan feint).
- **Limits:** his dark-side education was a «crash course» (L2445). Yoda's «easy parrying» and handling of his lightning (L2451). He was found through his concealment by the Kenobi/Skywalker pair (L4215). He was «hard-pressed» against Grievous at times (L1669), though partly by choice (L1671). Yoda doubts he is «a true Sith» (L3299). Sidious sees him as replaceable (Ch55, L5261: «we will have gained a powerful ally»).
- **What LoE adds to his scaling:**
  1. **Dooku > Grievous** in technique (the spar demo plus «secrets»), but not effortlessly (hard-pressed).
  2. **Dooku's own fear of the 19 BBY Anakin** («a greater threat to the Sith than anyone realized», L4663) and of the pair's synergy (L4663).
  3. **Dooku's admission that Yoda beat him on Geonosis** («easy parrying», L2451), wrapped in a face-saving excuse.
  4. **Sidious's expectation that Dooku would beat Anakin**, unless by «fluke» (L5261). Sidious hedges and «will control the outcome».

### 5.3 Darth Sidious / Palpatine — *Sidious, 19 BBY*
- **Personality:** patient, theatrical, contemptuous of his tools. Capable of rare anger (L2483) and visible stress (the lip twitch, L3385). He enjoys his own irony («A title more fit for a king or emperor.», L973; «Then I am assured of being greatly entertained.», L5201). He plays the frightened old man before the Jedi (L4575, L4605).
- **Arc:** the mechno-chair blunder exposes a trail to him (Ch9). The Jedi hunt him; he lets them close in (Ch30) while he converts the hunt into his own "kidnapping," which both ends the search and moves him onto the *Invisible Hand* for the Anakin test (L5249–5251).
- **Relationships:** Dooku (apprentice; deliberately disposable); Grievous (a «minion», L1703; Sidious toys with him, L4963–4983, L5191–5201); Anakin (the real prize, L5251; groomed in Ch12 and Ch35); Obi-Wan (to be killed, L5261); Yoda and Mace (deceived face-to-face, Ch23, Ch31); Plagueis (his unseen master, L2451).
- **Abilities shown/stated:** foresight as probability-current reading (L4193); concealment from Yoda, Mace and Shaak Ti at close range (L4245, L4575, Ch23, Ch31); clouding the search (L3773–3777, inferred); dark-side imprinting of a building (L2437); grand strategy (L4483); possible subtle mental pressure on Grievous (L5199, ambiguous).
- **Limits:** he can be surprised: «as at Geonosis, as in the case of Gunray's mechno-chair - - but not for long» (L4193); «I should have foreseen this.» (L3375). The Jedi truly «have my scent» (L3395). Yoda feels him once he acts (L4531).
- **What LoE adds to his scaling:** no combat data. The book strengthens **foresight/concealment** (relevant to "Sidious vs Mace" prep and "Yoda vs Sidious" information parity) and gives the **only direct Sidious > Yoda comparison in foresight** (Dooku, L4193). There is no Plagueis-comparative content.

### 5.4 Anakin Skywalker — *Anakin, 19 BBY (Knight / rage-amped)*
- **Personality:** impatient, loyal, angry, secretly married (Padmé feels him at a distance, Ch14; he feels her change, L827). He rationalizes anger as fuel (L241, L307) and resents Council secrecy. His trust in Palpatine grows (Ch12, Ch35), and he is close to Obi-Wan and resents him («he has no heart», L3831).
- **Arc:** Cato Neimoidia (rage run) → Charros → Belderone (ace) → Escarte (spider anti-feat; menaces K'sar) → Naos III (rescues Obi-Wan) → Tythe (brings down the ceiling; vision of Padmé) → «At least you said we.» (L5285). He ends wanting to «kill» Dooku (L4371) on the brink of ROTS.
- **Abilities:** raw Force power at Council level and above (L267); rage-amped telekinetic burst (L4381); multi-object TK (L1271, L2307); Force leaps and adhesion (L2995–3007); heat-shielding (L2285); precognitive piloting (L1469, L1509); visions (L4827).
- **Limits:** couldn't open a blast door, even angry (L309); knocked out by a dwarf spider (L2287); rage costs him focus (Obi-Wan's warnings, L4373).
- **What LoE adds:** **Dooku's fear** (L4663), **Sidious's "fluke" framing** (L5261) and **Obi-Wan's "more powerful than on Vjun" link** (L4835): three statements that scale the 19 BBY Anakin above his Geonosis self and close to Dooku going into ROTS.

### 5.5 Obi-Wan Kenobi — *Obi-Wan, 19 BBY (Council)*
- **Personality:** dry, protective, increasingly «Anakin's partisan» (L1323). He suspects Padmé (L3653ff). Self-description: «Infinite sadness» (L3659).
- **Abilities:** Soresu precision (L201–207, L347–351, L2963); danger sense (L565, L2881); TK at 20 m (L1269); door-opening TK (L2141).
- **Limits:** caught by a claw (L2971); tires vs endless droids (L4365).
- **What LoE adds:** Dooku's ladder puts him on the Drallig rung, below Mace and Yoda (L3347). Sidious: «good fortune» (L2517) and not a «forceful» threat (L2547–2549). His synergy with Anakin pierced Dooku's concealment (L4211–4215).

### 5.6 Mace Windu — *Mace, 19 BBY (Vaapad)*
- **Personality:** blunt, suspicious of Palpatine's circle but not of Palpatine; a leader under pressure (Ch38, Ch51).
- **Abilities:** Vaapad, which out-fences Grievous (L4813); fine TK control in a crowd (L4795); Force anchoring in a gale (L4795); a self-assessed TK limit (L4865); dark-side sensing (L3103, L4147).
- **Limits:** misses the clouded speeder bike (L3773); fooled by the decoy chase (Ch50); can't read Palpatine (nobody can).
- **What LoE adds:** the clearest **Mace > Grievous** on-page result (Ch47), and Dooku's ladder «Mace Windu or, stars help you, Yoda» (L3347). Both feed the Mace vs Sidious discussion only indirectly (Mace's sword tier, not his Sidious matchup).

### 5.7 Yoda — *Yoda, 19 BBY*
- **Personality:** sorrowful, self-critical about the Order (L4237–4239), wrong about the Senate (L683).
- **Abilities:** remote viewing (L4229); hears Qui-Gon on strong days (L4229–4231); senses Sidious once he acts (L4531); Dooku's lightning was «easy» for him on Geonosis (L2451); TK benchmark «five Yodas» (L4865).
- **Limits:** «The dark side frustrated his every effort to see clearly.» (L4235). Dooku: foresight with «one eye closed» (L4193).
- **What LoE adds:** an extra admission-against-interest that **Yoda > Dooku**, and a statement that **Sidious > Yoda at foresight**.

### 5.8 Jedi side characters (individual profiles)

Summary (original grouped entry): **Kit** dismantles two MagnaGuards in seconds, and Grievous ranks him with Mace (L4943), a strong mid-Council sword feat. **Shaak Ti** is a Hypori survivor who survives the blast, can't read Palpatine, and is emotionally shattered by the bunker massacre (L5005). **Stass Allie** holds a window breach with six Red Guards (L4611). Each profile below ends with a complete register of every sentence in the book that names the character (auto-harvested, verbatim, in book order).

#### 5.8.1 Kit Fisto (*Kit Fisto, 19 BBY*)
- **Personality and role:** a calm Council Master and Mace's partner for the whole Coruscant battle (Ch47–50). Hailed as the «Nautolan hero of the Battle of Mon Calamari» (L4793).
- **Arc:** on the Council (L2567) → rides the mag-lev with Mace, clearing droids out of a passenger car (L4791–4795) → holds the Red Guards and passengers in place with Mace when the train collapses (Ch49) → fights MagnaGuards on the roof (L4813) → chases the decoy gunboat → joins the starfighter pursuit of Grievous's shuttle (L5125).
- **Feats:** «the Nautolan's blade was a cyclone of blazing blue light» against two MagnaGuards: «like any weapon they needed to find their target, and Kit simply wasn't allowing that.» (L4813). With Mace he uses the Force to control flying debris inside the car (L4795).
- **Limits:** the shared Force-hold of the mag-lev car is a two-Master effort. Mace thinks a whole train would need «Perhaps five Yodas» (L4865).
- **Key line about him:** Grievous: «Jedi of the caliber of Windu and the tentacle-headed Kit Fisto» (L4943).
- **Scaling implications:** confirms Kit > two MagnaGuards (low difficulty), and Grievous himself ranks Kit with Mace.
- **Complete quote register (48 lines: every sentence that names Kit Fisto, verbatim, in book order):**

  - Ch44 L4641 «"Kit and I are nearby."»
  - Ch47 L4769 «Having boarded the mag-lev at its 500 Republica platform, he, Kit Fisto, Shaak Ti, and Stass Allie were in the car the Supreme Chancellor's Red Guards had commandeered - - second in a train of some twenty cars.»
  - Ch47 L4779 «Shaak Ti had told Mace and Kit about the Supreme Chancellor's earlier reluctance to leave his suite.»

#### 5.8.2 Shaak Ti (*Shaak Ti, 19 BBY*)
- **Personality:** quick, decisive, the first into close quarters: «As beautiful as a flower, as deadly as a viper, Shaak Ti was the Jedi Master one wanted by one's side in chaotic circumstances.» (L3095). She «had proved instrumental in the defense of Kamino and Brentaal IV» (L3095).
- **Arc:** with Mace and Dyne at The Works and in the Grungeon tunnels (Ch30, 34) → sent to find Palpatine (Ch38) → evacuates Padmé's group (L4433) → at 500 Republica with Stass Allie (Ch44) → caught in the gunship blast (L4895) → finds the bunker massacre (Ch51).
- **Feats:** Force-whispers to Mace across a tunnel (L3677); tackles Palpatine clear of Grievous's assault (L4603); survives a missile blast that «hurled them ten meters through the air» by turning it into a tucked roll (L4895).
- **Limits:** she cannot read Palpatine: «she found it difficult to get a sense of what he was truly feeling» (L4575). She is shaken by the bunker: «"I fought Grievous on Hypori," she said weakly. "I knew what he was capable of. But this... And taking Palpatine..."» (L5005).
- **Scaling implications:** a mid-tier Master and a Grievous survivor (Hypori). Sidious's concealment beats her close-range sensing.

#### 5.8.3 Stass Allie (*Stass Allie, 19 BBY*)
- **Personality:** blunt and duty-first: «"Chancellor, I'm sorry," Stass Allie chimed in, "but the Jedi are obliged to make this decision for you."» (L4577).
- **Arc:** on the Council (L2567) → searches the Senate Office Building and Republica (L4419) → holds the breach at 500 Republica as Grievous's craft attacks: «Six Red Guards raced to her side, their activated force pikes humming in concert with Allie's lightsaber.» (L4611) → is thrown by the gunship blast and lies «facedown nearby» (L4895) → arrives at the bunker after the massacre.
- **Scaling implications:** a defensive feat only (breach-holding with Red Guards). There is no duel with Grievous.
- **Complete quote register (47 lines: every sentence that names Stass Allie, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch27 L2567 «Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
  - Ch27 L2589 «Stass Allie asked while the Senate broke into extended applause.»
  - Ch39 L4263 «"I sent Stass Allie to assist Shaak Ti in guarding Chancellor Palpatine."»
  - Ch42 L4403 «With them were two female Jedi - - Masters Shaak Ti and Stass Allie.»
  - Ch42 L4419 «"Master Allie and I will check the Senate Office Building and Republica."»
  - Ch42 L4423 «"The turbolifts to the shelters are overwhelmed," Stass Allie said.»
  - Ch42 L4429 «Stass Allie took his arm.»
  - Ch42 L4435 «Stass Allie settled in behind her.»
  - Ch43 L4523 «Allie... to the Chancellor's quarters in Five Hundred Republica.»

  - Ch44 L4549 «Alongside her hurried Stass Allie, one hand on the hilt of her lightsaber.»
  - Ch44 L4551 «Stass Allie said, making it clear that she was going to get past them, one way or another.»
  - Ch44 L4557 «Stass Allie directed at Isard.»
  - Ch44 L4577 «"Chancellor, I'm sorry," Stass Allie chimed in, "but the Jedi are obliged to make this decision for you."»
  - Ch44 L4603 «Before a further word could fly from his mouth, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were in motion; Shaak Ti tackling Palpatine to the floor, while Allie ignited her blade and brought it vertically in front of her.»
  - Ch44 L4611 «Stass Allie shouted a moment before the entire window wall blew inward, filling the air with permaglass pebbles.»
  - Ch44 L4611 «Stass Allie stood immobile in the rush of wind, noise, and blaster bolts.»
  - Ch44 L4611 «Six Red Guards raced to her side, their activated force pikes humming in concert with Allie's lightsaber.»

  - Ch44 L4613 «As Allie and the Red Guards were felling the final few droids, the Separatist craft dropped back into the clouds, with bolts from the skimmers chasing it.»
  - Ch44 L4621 «Stass Allie started to say.»
  - Ch44 L4631 «Stass Allie nodded, then glanced at Palpatine.»
  - Ch44 L4635 «Allie waited until the Red Guards had gathered around him.»
  - Ch44 L4643 «Pressed into the turbolift with Stass Allie, Palpatine's guards and advisers, and Republica's security personnel, Shaak Ti watched the display tick off the floors.»
  - Ch44 L4649 «Stass Allie strode to the center of the platform and gazed up at the buildings that loomed on all sides.»
  - Ch44 L4651 «Allie blew out her breath.»
  - Ch44 L4655 «Allie indicated the stairway that led to the mag-lev platform.»
  - Ch46 L4685 «Padme took stock of herself; realized that she was reclined in the rear seat of Stass Allie's skimmer.»
  - Ch46 L4697 «"Master Allie doesn't strike me as someone who will sue for damages," the droid was saying.»
  - Ch46 L4751 «A second slagged what was left of Stass Allie's skimmer.»
  - Ch47 L4769 «Having boarded the mag-lev at its 500 Republica platform, he, Kit Fisto, Shaak Ti, and Stass Allie were in the car the Supreme Chancellor's Red Guards had commandeered - - second in a train of some twenty cars.»
  - Ch47 L4787 «In turn, Mace whirled to Shaak Ti and Allie.»
  - Ch47 L4799 «And at a fixed-pane window they had deliberately shattered, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were gazing toward the rear of the train.»
  - Ch49 L4863 «Farther forward in the car, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were keeping the Supreme Chancellor on his feet.»
  - Ch49 L4865 «He wondered if he, Kit, Shaak Ti, and Allie - - acting in concert - - could support the entire train, but dismissed the idea immediately.»
  - Ch49 L4871 «Behind them came four Jedi Knights, who rushed to join Shaak Ti and Stass Allie in guarding Palpatine.»
  - Ch49 L4883 «Mace turned to Shaak Ti, Allie, and the four newly arrived Jedi Knights.»

  - Ch49 L4891 «Shaak Ti instructed the four Jedi Knights to accompany the Red Guards, promising to join them after she and Stass Allie had apprised the Temple of their safe arrival.»
