  - Ch30 L3055 «Fortunately - - and admittedly unexpectedly - - Supreme Chancellor Palpatine had overruled the committee, and Mace had been allowed to compile a dream team that included not only ARC commander Valiant and Captain Dyne of Republic Intelligence, but also Jedi Master Shaak Ti and several capable Padawans.»
  - Ch30 L3073 «"Abandoned, perhaps, but not forgotten," Captain Dyne said from behind Valiant.»
  - Ch30 L3077 «Dyne nodded.»
  - Ch30 L3117 «"Everyone stay exactly where you are," Captain Dyne said when Mace and the rest of Aurek Team appeared at the mouth of a corridor that intersected the docking bay at its lengthwise centerpoint.»
  - Ch30 L3119 «Thirty meters to Mace's right, Dyne and two other Intelligence officers were interpreting the data being sent to them by several probe droids meandering with design throughout the room, some of them misting the floor with a highly volatile substance.»
  - Ch30 L3121 «"A Huppla Pasa Tisc sloop occupied this docking bay less than two standard weeks ago," Dyne said, loud enough for everyone to hear.»
  - Ch30 L3125 «"A reasonable supposition, Master Windu," Dyne said loudly.»
  - Ch30 L3129 «Dyne directed the droid to concentrate on certain areas, and studied the data again.»
  - Ch30 L3135 «The droid focused its lights on another area, and Dyne continued.»
  - Ch30 L3135 «"Here, being one met with being two, lighter in weight, perhaps shorter in stature, and wearing - - " Dyne consulted what Mace assumed to be some sort of database. "- - what can best be described as soft-soled footwear or slippers.»
  - Ch30 L3137 «Tasking the probe droids to track the prints of the second being, Dyne began to trail them, waving for Mace, Shaak Ti, and the commandos to follow.»
  - Ch30 L3139 «"Single file behind me," Dyne cautioned.»
  - Ch30 L3141 «By the time the two Jedi Masters caught up with Dyne and his droids, the Intelligence analyst was standing at the door to a dated turbolift.»
  - Ch30 L3143 «"Verified," Dyne said, grinning in self-satisfaction.»
  - Ch30 L3149 «The turbolift was just roomy enough for Dyne, his associates, Mace, Shaak Ti, the two team commanders, and two probe droids.»
  - Ch30 L3153 «"Footprints," Dyne said with enthusiasm.»
  - Ch30 L3155 «After the droids had disappeared inside and returned, Dyne swung to Mace, who was waiting with everyone else at the base of the turbolift.»
  - Ch30 L3159 «Mace and Shaak Ti joined Dyne and his teammates at the tunnel entrance.»
  - Ch30 L3161 «Dyne consulted a holomap.»
  - Ch30 L3165 «Dyne called up a series of displays and studied them in silence.»
  - Ch34 L3679 «Normally the probe droids would have the point, playing their lights and sensors across the dusty floor and tiled walls, sending data to Dyne and his team of analysts.»
  - Ch34 L3735 «"Talk about your corridor ghouls," Dyne said, eyeing the aliens as he approached Mace.»
  - Ch34 L3737 «"Next it'll be Cthons," Dyne said, referring to the flesh-eating humanoids believed by many Coruscanti to inhabit the world's underground.»
  - Ch34 L3743 «Dyne sighed in disappointment.»
  - Ch34 L3749 «"Within a couple of kilometers," Dyne said.»
  - Ch34 L3769 «They were a kilometer closer to the outlying areas of the Senate District when Dyne called to them from behind.»
  - Ch34 L3769 «Dyne's handheld sensor needed only a moment to discover a small control panel that operated the niche's sliding door.»
  - Ch36 L3925 «Some of the prints were so recent and well preserved that Dyne had been able to calculate the human's slipper size.»
  - Ch36 L3931 «His eyes fixed on the display screen of his data processor, Captain Dyne ambled toward Mace and Shaak Ti.»
  - Ch36 L3939 «Dyne glanced up, blinking at her.»
  - Ch38 L4077 «"Unless we can find prints that say otherwise, there's no guarantee our quarry gained entrance to the sub-basement from Five Hundred Republica itself, " Dyne pronounced, switching his handheld processor to standby mode.»
  - Ch38 L4081 «Dyne nodded.»
  - Ch38 L4095 «Dyne compressed his lips and shook his head.»
  - Ch38 L4101 «Dyne scanned the sub-basement, pursed his lips, and blew out his breath.»
  - Ch38 L4119 «"That could require several weeks," Dyne said cautiously.»
  - Ch38 L4123 «Dyne took a comlink from his belt and was about to activate it when the floor began to tremble.»
  - Ch38 L4133 «"Feels like something rammed the building," Dyne said.»
  - Ch38 L4139 «"My comlink isn't working," Dyne said, jabbing at the device's frequency search control with his forefinger.»
  - Ch38 L4143 «Dyne shook his head.»
  - Ch38 L4151 «Dyne pointed to his left.»
  - Ch38 L4157 «You and the rest of your team will assist Captain Dyne with the search.»
  - Ch38 L4161 «Mace looked at TC-16, then at Dyne.»
  - Ch40 L4321 «In the wake of the erratic and mostly unintelligible messages that had reached 500 Republica regarding the Separatists' surprise attack, Dyne had considered the sub-basement to be the safest place on Coruscant.»
  - Ch40 L4321 «With a battle raging in space, and notwithstanding Mace Windu's command to the contrary, Dyne had been tempted to suspend the search for Sidious's lair and report back to the Intelligence division, as he had ordered the other analysts to do.»
  - Ch40 L4323 «Electronically tethered to the probe droids, Dyne and the commandos had performed imagings of some of the partitions and walls, and investigated numerous unlit hollows and recesses.»
  - Ch40 L4325 «Dyne had learned that the initial jolts had owed not to bombardment, but to the fall of ships destroyed at the edge of space.»
  - Ch40 L4325 «Several hours into the cursory search, Dyne had been struck by the possibility that certain Coruscanti - - perhaps the Sith Lord himself - - could be helping to coordinate the attack.»
  - Ch40 L4329 «Dyne recalled wondering where the platform's shaft could lead.»
  - Ch40 L4331 «Dyne had decided to contact Mace Windu at the Jedi Temple for advice on how to proceed.»
  - Ch40 L4335 «Definitely the most dangerous place to be, Dyne thought as he wedged himself among the commandos.»
  - Ch40 L4341 «"If anyone's down here," Dyne said to Valiant, "they're probably aware we're on the way."»
  - Ch40 L4345 «But this one, Dyne told himself, was an archaeologist's dream.»
  - Ch40 L4347 «Dyne sent the droids to investigate, then studied the processor's data screen.»
  - Ch40 L4359 «Dyne's heart began to race.»
  - Ch43 L4533 «Captain Dyne stepped cautiously from the platform that had dropped the team into the unexplored depths of 500 Republica.»
  - Ch43 L4533 «Dyne took a breath to steady his nerves.»
  - Ch43 L4535 «Dyne stepped between the powered-down pair of probe droids, TC-16 following in his footsteps.»
  - Ch43 L4535 «They hadn't advanced three meters down the corridor when Dyne's ears pricked up at the sound of gurgled voices.»
  - Ch43 L4537 «Whirling, Dyne found himself staring down the wide muzzles of two organic-looking sonic weapons, grasped in the thick-fingered hands of two Geonosian soldier drones, barely visible in the shadows, their wings angled down toward the corridor's grimy floor.»
  - Ch43 L4537 «Dyne understood that it wasn't his life flashing before his eyes, but his death.»
  - Ch43 L4539 «It was possible, in that eternal moment of silence, that the troopers had reacted quickly enough to get off a few bolts, because when Dyne looked to his right, along the way he had come, there were no signs of the Geonosians or, for that matter, TC-16.»
  - Ch43 L4543 «His liquefying brain notwithstanding, Dyne found a moment to be astonished, because he recognized the man instantly.»
  - Ch43 L4545 «The fact that the man had made no attempt to mask himself assured and comforted Dyne that he was about to die, and shortly after the realization, he did.»
  - Ch44 L4647 «As they hurried for the tunnel, Shaak Ti took a moment to survey the huge space for some sign of Captain Dyne's team.»
  - Ch44 L4647 «Considering all that had happened since she left, it was likely that Dyne and Commander Valiant had curtailed the search for Sidious's hideaway.»
  - Ch47 L4775 «Briefly, he wondered what had become of Captain Dyne's team.»
  - Ch47 L4777 «Surmising that Dyne had called off the search for Sidious shortly after the attack had commenced, Intelligence hadn't dispatched a second search team - - aimed at locating Dyne and Valiant - - until neither of them had been heard from, even after communications had been restored to the Senate District.»
  - Ch47 L4777 «So had Dyne and the commandos fallen victim to Grievous's attack?»

#### 5.10.6 Commander Valiant (clone commando leader, *19 BBY*)
- **Role:** leads the Aurek/Bacta commando teams at The Works and below 500 Republica: «"Quiet as a tomb, General," Valiant said when the top ten levels had been secured.» (L3105); «When Mace nodded, Valiant said: "Light it up!"» (L3687). He dies with Dyne: «He watched Valiant and the explosives expert leave the floor and hurtle headlong into the door.» (L4539).
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (27 lines: every sentence that names Valiant, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch30 L3055 «Fortunately - - and admittedly unexpectedly - - Supreme Chancellor Palpatine had overruled the committee, and Mace had been allowed to compile a dream team that included not only ARC commander Valiant and Captain Dyne of Republic Intelligence, but also Jedi Master Shaak Ti and several capable Padawans.»
  - Ch30 L3067 «"Holoimages of the interior coming in," Valiant said.»
  - Ch30 L3073 «"Abandoned, perhaps, but not forgotten," Captain Dyne said from behind Valiant.»
  - Ch30 L3081 «Mace turned to Valiant.»
  - Ch30 L3087 «Valiant turned to the troopers who made up Aurek Team.»
  - Ch30 L3105 «"Quiet as a tomb, General," Valiant said when the top ten levels had been secured.»
  - Ch30 L3111 «Valiant was about to speak when his comlink toned.»
  - Ch30 L3149 «Comlinking troopers outside the building, Valiant ordered them to make their way to sub-basement two, but forewarned them to stay clear of the east turbolift and any nearby corridors or tunnels.»
  - Ch34 L3677 «Mace waved a signal to Commander Valiant, whose team moved forward stealthily, hugging the walls, their T-visor helmets allowing them to see in the dark.»
  - Ch34 L3687 «Standing nearby, Valiant looked to Mace for the go signal.»
  - Ch34 L3687 «When Mace nodded, Valiant said: "Light it up!"»
  - Ch34 L3689 «Tight on their heels, Mace heard Valiant yell: "Down on the floor!»
  - Ch34 L3693 «Edging through the commandos, he reached Valiant, whose BlasTech was aimed at a cowering crowd of thirty or so four-armed insectoid aliens, who were babbling in some language other than Basic, or speaking it with an accent so thick as to make their words unintelligible.»
  - Ch34 L3707 «Mace glanced at Valiant.»
  - Ch34 L3723 «"Undocumented refugees," Valiant said.»
  - Ch34 L3733 «He nodded to Valiant, who in turn told two of his troopers to carry out Mace's command.»
  - Ch38 L4155 «He turned to Valiant.»
  - Ch40 L4321 «But as ARC commander Valiant had pointed out, the search team's objective was as important to the war as the actions of the ships that were protecting Coruscant.»
  - Ch40 L4331 «But when his repeated attempts failed, he and Valiant had made a command decision to carry on without the Jedi.»
  - Ch40 L4341 «"If anyone's down here," Dyne said to Valiant, "they're probably aware we're on the way."»
  - Ch40 L4349 «"One flesh-and-blood behind the door," he whispered to Valiant.»
  - Ch40 L4355 «Valiant regarded the door.»
  - Ch43 L4533 «Valiant had the point, with the squad's explosives expert close behind, a thermal detonator in hand.»
  - Ch43 L4539 «He watched Valiant and the explosives expert leave the floor and hurtle headlong into the door.»
  - Ch43 L4543 «Behind them walked a human of medium height and build, who stepped nonchalantly over Valiant's grotesquely twisted body.»
  - Ch44 L4647 «Considering all that had happened since she left, it was likely that Dyne and Commander Valiant had curtailed the search for Sidious's hideaway.»
  - Ch47 L4777 «Surmising that Dyne had called off the search for Sidious shortly after the attack had commenced, Intelligence hadn't dispatched a second search team - - aimed at locating Dyne and Valiant - - until neither of them had been heard from, even after communications had been restored to the Senate District.»

#### 5.10.7 Commander Cody (*19 BBY*)
- **Role:** Obi-Wan's clone commander at Cato Neimoidia (Ch1–7). «Cody carried a short-stocked DC-15 blaster rifle and wore the white armor and imaging system helmet that had come to symbolize the Grand Army of the Republic» (L83). His wrist projector briefs the Jedi (L73, L81). He fights beside Obi-Wan in the redoubt (L289).
- **Scaling implications:** none. Supplies the "Anakin and Obi-Wan cut through droids" context for Ch1–7.
- **Complete quote register (36 lines: every sentence that names Cody, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch1 L67 «Standing in the bow, a clone commander named Cody waved hand signals to the troopers on shore and to others in the gunboat, who immediately fanned out to create a safe perimeter.»
  - Ch1 L67 «A few nimble leaps brought Cody face-to-face with Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
  - Ch1 L73 «Cody dropped to one knee, his right hand activating a device built into his left wrist gauntlet.»
  - Ch1 L77 «Obi-Wan looked at Cody when the hologram had faded.»
  - Ch1 L81 «Cody made an adjustment to the wrist projector, and a 3-D schematic of the redoubt formed in midair.»
  - Ch1 L83 «Cody carried a short-stocked DC-15 blaster rifle and wore the white armor and imaging system helmet that had come to symbolize the Grand Army of the Republic - - grown, nurtured, and trained on the remote world of Kamino, three years earlier.»
  - Ch1 L83 «Cody's position was designated by orange markings on his helmet crest and shoulder guards.»
  - Ch1 L85 «While Cody wasn't genetically an Advanced Recon Commando, he had ARC training and shared many ARC attributes.»
  - Ch1 L91 «Cody stood to his full height and pointed toward the orchards.»
  - Ch2 L117 «To Obi-War's right ran Cody, with his teammates trailing behind and flanking him.»
  - Ch2 L133 «Cody motioned everyone into a shallow irrigation trench just short of the mound.»
  - Ch2 L135 «He glanced at Cody and the rest.»
  - Ch2 L147 «Cody pressed a pair of macrobinoculars to his eyes and gazed upward.»
  - Ch3 L159 «"Comm fire support for an artillery strike," he ordered Cody, loud enough to be heard above STAP and DC-15 fire.»
  - Ch3 L161 «Cody was more than willing to comply.»
  - Ch3 L161 «There was, though, a chain of command to maintain, so Cody looked to Obi-Wan for confirmation.»
  - Ch3 L165 «The commando called for his comm specialist, who splashed through the shallow water and flattened himself alongside Cody.»
  - Ch3 L165 «When the spec had provided needed coordinates, Cody opened a frequency to the fire support base and spoke in a rush.»
  - Ch3 L171 «Cody shrugged.»
  - Ch3 L175 «Cody relayed the message.»
  - Ch3 L181 «Still without helmet, Cody stood up, signaling with both hands.»
  - Ch3 L191 «Cody ordered the squad to secure the area.»
  - Ch3 L231 «Obi-Wan, Cody, and the rest of Squad Seven set out for the turbolift shafts.»
  - Ch4 L235 «Oh, but it is personal, Anakin told himself while he watched Obi-Wan, Cody, and four troopers disappear into the turbolift.»
  - Ch5 L275 «"The next stop is ours, General," Cody said from behind him.»
  - Ch5 L275 «Obi-Wan turned, watched Cody slam a new blasterpack into his DC-15, heard the familiar whine of the weapon's repower mechanism.»
  - Ch5 L281 «Cody nodded, perhaps grinning beneath his helmet.»
  - Ch5 L287 «He, Cody, and the others threw themselves into the horizontal hail.»
  - Ch5 L289 «Cody said while he, Obi-Wan, and two commandos fought their way into a side room.»
  - Ch5 L289 «Cody pointed to the entrance to a second room, opposite their present position.»
  - Ch6 L333 «"Urgent from Commander Cody.»
  - Ch6 L339 «Anakin glanced into the glowing sky, then back at the commando who had delivered Cody's message.»
  - Ch6 L339 «"Where did you say Obi-Wan and Cody are?"»
  - Ch7 L353 «Then he carried him from the room to where Cody and several commandos were waiting, some with their helmets removed.»
  - Ch7 L361 «"We did this?" he said to Cody.»
  - Ch9 L487 «Commander Cody and the rest of Squad Seven were elsewhere in the palace, "mopping up," as the clones liked to say.»

#### 5.10.8 Nute Gunray, Viceroy (*19 BBY*)
- **Personality:** cowardly, vengeful, terrified of Grievous: «Gunray would have liked nothing more than to inform Dooku of the general's actions, but he feared that the Sith would stand with Grievous.» (L1103).
- **Arc:** flees Cato Neimoidia and leaves the mechno-chair behind (Ch7–8), which sets off the whole hunt → is punished by Grievous's gunners, who destroy a squadron of his own escort (L1063) → is cowed by MagnaGuards (L1633) → celebrates on Utapau after the abduction (L5219).
- **Backstory lines:** «But just when Gunray finally had Amidala in his grasp - - on trial, no less, for espionage - - Dooku had equivocated» (L423); on Dooku: «Whether a replacement for the fearsome Darth Maul, or a Sith even during his years in the Jedi Order, Gunray didn't care to know.» (L425).
- **Relationships:** Anakin hates him personally over Padmé (L235); Haako is his aide.
- **Scaling implications:** none. His mechno-chair is a lapse by Sidious («as in the case of Gunray's mechno-chair», L4193).
- **Complete quote register (148 lines: every sentence that names Gunray, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch1 L21 «Well under the fractured sky, in an orchard of manax trees that studded the lower ramparts of Viceroy Gunray's majestic redoubt, companies of clone troopers and battle droids were slaughtering one another with bloodless precision.»
  - Ch1 L63 «With his homeworld threatened and the purse worlds of Deko and Koru Neimoidia devastated, Viceroy Gunray would have been wiser to retreat to the Outer Rim, as other members of the Separatist Council were thought to be doing.»
  - Ch1 L75 «"Generals Kenobi and Skywalker, provincial recon unit reports that Viceroy Gunray and his entourage are making their way to the north side of the redoubt.»
  - Ch1 L75 «If your team is still committed to taking Gunray alive, you're going to have to skirt those defenses and find an alternative way into the palace.»
  - Ch1 L83 «"Assuming that Gunray's fortress is similar to what we found on Deko and Koru, the underground levels will contain fungus farms and processing and shipment areas.»
  - Ch3 L213 «"Provincial recon reports that Viceroy Gunray and his entourage are heading for the launching bays.»
  - Ch3 L219 «You lure the bodyguards away, I capture Gunray.»
  - Ch3 L231 «"Anakin, I know we've got a score to settle with Gunray, but don't make it personal.»
  - Ch4 L235 «It was personal because of what Nute Gunray had done to Naboo thirteen years ago.»
  - Ch4 L235 «It was personal because of Gunray's hiring of Jango Fett to assassinate Padme three years ago - - first with a bomb planted on her ship, then with the pair of kouhuns a changling had inserted into Padme's Senatorial quarters on Coruscant.»
  - Ch4 L237 «Even if he could put all that aside, as Obi-Wan plainly wanted him to do, it was personal because Gunray had aligned himself with Dooku and the Separatists, and the war they had planned from the start had brought ruin to a thousand worlds.»
  - Ch4 L251 «Gunray's party is nearby.»
  - Ch4 L257 «Noisy images of Nute Gunray and his entourage of elite officers formed in midair.»
  - Ch4 L259 «Nearly everyone has left - - because of the invasion, of course - - but I'm sure that we can make you comfortable, and that Viceroy Gunray will be most pleased - - "»
  - Ch4 L261 «Anakin leapt around the corner in time to see the Neimoidians set off at a run, red-eyed, flat-nosed Gunray casting a nervous glance over his shoulder.»
  - Ch6 L309 «Focused on the route Gunray and his lackeys had taken, Anakin raced through corridors, rounding corners without slowing down, sprinting for the launching bay at the far end of the final corridor.»
  - Ch6 L319 «"The shuttle - - Gunray's shuttle.»
  - Ch6 L323 «Gunray's named it the Lapiz Cutter."»
  - Ch6 L331 «Not until he had Gunray in his grip...»
  - Ch7 L369 «Obi-Wan ran his hand through his hair, then, as if just remembering, said: "Gunray!»
  - Ch8 L389 «First to set foot on deck, Viceroy Nute Gunray, attired in blood-red robes and sporting a tall, helmet-like miter, asked for a situation report from one of the goggle-wearing technicians who was waiting in the docking bay.»
  - Ch8 L391 «"Even now coordinates for the jump to lightspeed are being calculated, Viceroy," the nearest one said.»
  - Ch8 L393 «"Let us hope so," Gunray said, as the vessel was rocked by a massive explosion.»
  - Ch8 L393 «Behind Gunray walked settlement officer Rune Haako, wearing a crested skullcap; and behind Haako, various financial, legal, and diplomatic officers, each wearing a distinctive headpiece.»
  - Ch8 L393 «Droids were already beginning to unload the possessions - - the treasures - - for which Gunray had risked so much.»
  - Ch8 L399 «Sadness crept into Gunray's red orbs.»
  - Ch8 L403 «Gunray allowed a nod of agreement.»
  - Ch8 L411 «Gunray made a dismissive gesture with his fat fingers.»
  - Ch8 L413 «"But is even he enough, Viceroy? " he asked quietly.»
  - Ch8 L413 «Gunray said nothing, although he had been asking himself the same question for the past several weeks.»
  - Ch8 L413 «Ironically, the individual most responsible for that bright burning - - for the rise of Nute Gunray himself - - was the same individual who had repeatedly betrayed him, and to whom Gunray and the other Separatists were now forced to look for salvation.»
  - Ch8 L415 «Years of attempts by the Republic to try to convict Gunray and his chief officers, to break the hold the Trade Federation enjoyed on galactic shipping.»
  - Ch8 L415 «But not once during that time of public disgrace did Gunray mention the role Sidious had played.»
  - Ch8 L419 «It was during the final trial that Gunray had been approached by Count Dooku, who had promised that all would ultimately turn out well for the Trade Federation.»
  - Ch8 L419 «In a moment of weakness, Gunray had revealed the truth about his dealings with Darth Sidious.»
  - Ch8 L421 «Gunray had mixed feelings about Dooku's purpose in creating a Separatist movement, chiefly because corruption in the Republic Senate had so often worked to the Trade Federation's advantage.»
  - Ch8 L423 «Regardless, Gunray had resisted offers to throw his full support to the Separatists - - not when there were still profits to be made in countless Republic star systems.»
  - Ch8 L423 «Playing a game of his own, teasing Dooku along, he had informed Dooku that a precondition to their entering into any exclusive arrangement was the death of former Naboo Queen Padme Amidala, who had foiled Gunray on two occasions, and had been the loudest opposition voice at his trials.»
  - Ch8 L423 «But just when Gunray finally had Amidala in his grasp - - on trial, no less, for espionage - - Dooku had equivocated, refusing to have Amidala killed outright, and not lifting a hand against the Jedi until some two hundred of them had showed up with a clone army the Republic had grown in secret!»
  - Ch8 L425 «That day had provided Gunray with the first in what would be a series of narrow escapes.»
  - Ch8 L425 «Hurrying to the catacombs with Dooku at their side, Gunray and Haako had barely managed to flee the embattled surface and recall what core ships and droid carriers remained.»
  - Ch8 L425 «Whether a replacement for the fearsome Darth Maul, or a Sith even during his years in the Jedi Order, Gunray didn't care to know.»
  - Ch8 L425 «What mattered was simply that Nute Gunray was right back where he had been so many years earlier: in service to forces over which he had no control whatsoever.»
  - Ch8 L425 «But they found themselves facing a worthy opponent in the person of Supreme Chancellor Palpatine - - also from Naboo - - who had never much impressed Gunray, but who had managed through a combination of charm and artfulness not only to remain in power long past his term of office, but also, in conjunction with the Jedi, to conduct the war.»
  - Ch8 L427 «Slowly, the wheel began to turn, as one Separatist world after another was retaken by the Republic, and now Viceroy Nute Gunray himself had been driven from the Core.»
  - Ch8 L433 «Gunray said.»
  - Ch8 L435 «Gunray paced worriedly, trying to recall when and where he had last seen the device.»
  - Ch8 L439 «Gunray stared at him.»
  - Ch8 L443 «Gunray fell silent for a moment.»
  - Ch8 L447 «Gunray tried to convince himself.»
  - Ch8 L449 «Gunray rasped.»
  - Ch8 L457 «Gunray fairly screeched.»
  - Ch8 L465 «"Viceroy, we are prepared to make the jump to lightspeed."»
  - Ch8 L467 «Gunray cried.»
  - Ch8 L469 «"But, Viceroy, our vessel can only withstand so much bombardment."»
  - Ch8 L473 «Gunray rushed to join him at the console.»
  - Ch9 L481 «Shortly after Viceroy Gunray and his entourage were taken into custody in Theed."»
  - Ch9 L513 «"These items remain the property of Viceroy Gunray and other members of his party."»
  - Ch9 L567 «Good bet it's the same one Gunray tried to use on Qui-Gon and me at Naboo."»
  - Ch9 L573 «And to the meter-high figure in the hooded cloak, the unmistakable voice of Viceroy Nute Gunray was saying: "Yes, yes, of course.»
  - Ch11 L669 «Feeling unnecessary, Obi-Wan and Yoda decided to take a stroll down the corridor of Viceroy Gunray's now appropriated palace.»
  - Ch11 L685 «My first thought was that he was trying to ensure the safe escape of Gunray and the other Separatist leaders.»
  - Ch11 L779 «Dooku said that Gunray had gone to him for help, and that Dooku had tried to appeal to the Council.»
  - Ch11 L781 «"If Gunray hadn't been so keen on assassinating Padme Amidala,"»
  - Ch12 L853 «But I'm sorry to report that Viceroy Gunray escaped, and that fighting continues in the rock-arch cities."»
  - Ch12 L859 «If it involves Gunray, accept my word that he won't be able to hide from us forever.»
  - Ch12 L861 «"It's not about Gunray, sir.»
  - Ch14 L1031 «"For the time being we're proceeding on the assumption that Dooku - - or Sidious, for that matter - - might have developed the chips, and had them installed in transceivers awarded to Gunray and other key members of the Council of Separatists."»
  - Ch14 L1041 «"If Gunray has had the chair for fourteen years, then he could have been using it to contact Sidious during the Naboo crisis.»
  - Ch15 L1057 «Protected by two squadrons of Trade Federation Vulture fighters, Nute Gunray's organic-looking shuttle cut a blazing trail through the void of deep space, plasma bolts from a dozen Republic V-wings nipping at its upraised tail.»
  - Ch15 L1059 «To any other spectator it might appear that the Viceroy was risking his wattled neck, but Grievous knew better.»
  - Ch15 L1059 «Late to arrive at the rendezvous because of his decision to detour to Cato Neimoidia, Gunray was putting on a show for the general's benefit, attempting to make it seem that he had been chased to the Outer Rim when, in fact, he had undoubtedly allowed his hyperspace vectors to be plotted by Republic forces.»
  - Ch15 L1061 «More to the point, Gunray's vessel was in no real peril.»
  - Ch15 L1061 «At another time Grievous might have applauded their bravery by allowing them to escape with their lives, but Gunray's transparent attempts at pretense had exposed the fleet to surveillance, and now the Republic pilots would have to die.»
  - Ch15 L1063 «First, Gunray would have to be punished for his blunder; given a foretaste of what awaited him the next time he disobeyed a directive.»
  - Ch15 L1067 «An instant later, clouds of billowing fire began to erupt to both sides of Gunray's shuttle, and twelve droid fighters vanished from sight.»
  - Ch15 L1075 «Still, Gunray's shuttle was taking a beating.»
  - Ch15 L1077 «Grievous pictured Gunray, Haako, and the others strapped into luxurious acceleration couches, shivering with dread, perhaps sorry for the brief detour to Cato Neimoidia, wondering how a handful of Republic pilots had so easily decimated their squadrons, certainly comlinking the core ship to dispatch reinforcements.»
  - Ch15 L1077 «The general was almost of a mind to award the Republic pilots their kill, for he and Gunray had been at odds frequently over the past three years.»
  - Ch15 L1079 «From their first acquaintance, Gunray had made the mistake of treating Grievous as just another droid - - even though he had been told that this was not the case.»
  - Ch15 L1079 «Perhaps Gunray had thought of him as some mindless entity, like the reawakened Gen'Dai, Durge; or Dooku's misguided apprentice, Asajj Ventress; or the human bounty hunter called Aurra Sing - - all three of whom had been so driven by personal hatred of the Jedi that they had proved worthless, mere distractions while Grievous went about the real business of war.»
  - Ch15 L1079 «Had it not been for Grievous, Gunray and the rest might have suffered the same fate as Poggle the Lesser's lieutenant, Sun Fac.»
  - Ch15 L1079 «Grievous's actions in the catacombs that day - - with the Geonosians retreating by the thousands from the arena and companies of clone commandos following them in - - had allowed Gunray to escape the planet alive.»
  - Ch15 L1091 «Count Dooku and his Sith Master would never forgive him if he allowed anything untoward to happen to Gunray.»
  - Ch15 L1091 «Their knowledge of secret hyperlanes was unparalleled, and their immense army of infantry and super battle droids were rigged with devices that compelled them to respond principally to Gunray and his elite.»
  - Ch15 L1091 «It was time to spring Gunray from the trap he had fashioned for him.»
  - Ch15 L1097 «The tri-fighters had split into two groups, the larger forming up around Gunray's impaired shuttle and pouncing on the retreating V-wings, while the diverted squadron began to tease the pair of ARC-170s into duels and sallies.»
  - Ch15 L1101 «Still shaken from the ordeal of transiting from the core ship to the Invisible Hand, Nute Gunray sat restively in the cabin space to which he and Haako been shown immediately on disembarking.»
  - Ch15 L1103 «Gunray would have liked nothing more than to inform Dooku of the general's actions, but he feared that the Sith would stand with Grievous.»
  - Ch15 L1103 «Every bit as shaken, Rune Haako sat alongside Gunray at the cabin's gleaming table.»
  - Ch15 L1107 «Behind helmet holes that rendered his visage at once mournful and fearsome, sallow reptilian eyes fixed Gunray with a gimlet stare.»
  - Ch15 L1107 «In a synthesized voice, deep and grating, he said: "Welcome aboard, Viceroy.»
  - Ch15 L1109 «Gunray felt the gazes of everyone in the cabin fall on him.»
  - Ch15 L1115 «"I am your keeper, Viceroy.»
  - Ch15 L1115 «Striding into the cabin, he began to circle the table, stopping directly behind Gunray, towering over him.»
  - Ch15 L1115 «Peripherally, Gunray saw Haako slouch deeper into his chair, refusing to look either at him or at Grievous, circling his hands in a nervous gesture.»
  - Ch15 L1123 «Careful not to betray his sudden misgiving, Gunray traded covert glances with Haako.»
  - Ch16 L1143 «Dexter Jettster, his Besalisk friend on Coruscant, could probably have furnished the Intelligence analysts with everything they needed to know about Viceroy Gunray's mechno-chair.»
  - Ch16 L1159 «"My term of service to Viceroy Gunray demanded that I familiarize myself with the trader's tongues used by all the hive species, including the Xi Char, the Geonosians, the Colicoids, and many others.»
  - Ch16 L1221 «"Excellency, I speak your language as a result of my former employment in the court of Viceroy Nute Gunray."»
  - Ch16 L1239 «Here were all the priceless devices Obi-Wan and Anakin had found crammed into storage rooms in Gunray's Cato Neimoidia citadel.»
  - Ch16 L1247 «"A mechno-chair you adorned for Trade Federation viceroy, Nute Gunray, some fourteen standard years ago."»
  - Ch16 L1251 «But should the Prelate learn that the piece has for all these years resided with a personage such as Viceroy Gunray - - "»
  - Ch16 L1297 «"And not of Nute Gunray."»
  - Ch17 L1327 «Using the code, we were able to intercept a message sent to Viceroy Gunray, through the mechno-chair."»
  - Ch17 L1341 «"You'll be pleased to learn that I've chosen a world for us, Viceroy," Grievous was saying.»
  - Ch17 L1341 «"Viceroy?»
  - Ch17 L1341 «Viceroy!"»
  - Ch19 L1515 «He turned to watch the simulation playing above the tactical console, then swung back to the viewports, recalling the ARC-170 pilots who had hounded Gunray's shuttle only days earlier.»
  - Ch19 L1543 «"When all ships have arrived at the rally point, advise Viceroy Gunray that I will be paying him a visit."»
  - Ch21 L1571 «"General Grievous has left the docking bay," a Trade Federation lieutenant relayed to Gunray in his lavish quarters in the core ship's port-side command tower.»
  - Ch21 L1573 «Gunray said toward the comlink's audio pickup.»
  - Ch21 L1575 «"The general's shuttle availed itself of the tower docking ring, Viceroy. "»
  - Ch21 L1577 «Gunray swung around to face Rune Haako.»
  - Ch21 L1581 «"It has to be the mechno-chair," Gunray said, striding back and forth in front of the screen.»
  - Ch21 L1585 «Gunray came to a halt.»
  - Ch21 L1593 «Gunray nodded.»
  - Ch21 L1599 «Gunray saw that Grievous was accompanied by four of his elite MagnaGuards.»
  - Ch21 L1601 «Mirroring the Neimoidians, they raised their double-tipped electroshock batons, then swung them forward with such speed and precision that Gunray's sentinels were literally swept off their feet, as if they were children.»
  - Ch21 L1603 «"Admit us, Viceroy.»
  - Ch21 L1607 «Gunray said.»
  - Ch21 L1613 «"Viceroy!"»
  - Ch21 L1621 «Gunray paced for a moment, wringing his hands, then, straightening robes and miter and pulling his shoulders back, he pressed a fat finger to the hatch release.»
  - Ch21 L1625 «Gunray said from the center of the main room.»
  - Ch21 L1629 «Gunray touched himself in the chest.»
  - Ch21 L1633 «Stepping forward, a MagnaGuard thrust his staff to within a millimeter of Gunray's face.»
  - Ch21 L1635 «"Lord Sidious's alloy puppet," Gunray said, his voice quavering.»
  - Ch21 L1637 «Grievous raised his right claw and pointed to Gunray.»
  - Ch21 L1641 «Gunray gulped.»
  - Ch21 L1651 «Grabbing Gunray by the neck, Grievous lifted him a meter off the floor.»
  - Ch22 L1657 «Poor Gunray, Dooku thought.»
  - Ch22 L1659 «While the incident at Belderone wasn't conclusive proof that the Republic had managed to decrypt the Separatist code and intercept Grievous's transmission to Gunray, it was prudent to assume that this was the case.»
  - Ch22 L1667 «Dooku, especially, since he had no interest in commanding an army of droids, and already had his hands full nursemaiding the likes of Nute Gunray, Shu Mai, and the hive-minded others who eventually would form the Council of Separatists.»
  - Ch22 L1715 «And now, a new wrinkle: as a result of Nute Gunray's blunder at Cato Neimoidia, the Republic and the Jedi had chanced on a possible way to trace the whereabouts of Sidious and expose him.»
  - Ch23 L1793 «"A hyperwave transceiver that belonged to Viceroy Gunray was seized at Cato Neimoidia," Mace explained.»
  - Ch23 L1793 «A message transmitted by General Grievous to Viceroy Gunray regarding Belderone was monitored, and we acted on it."»
  - Ch23 L1825 «"It contained a stored message - - a message transmitted to Viceroy Gunray from Darth Sidious."»
  - Ch24 L1867 «Obi-Wan and Anakin hadn't been told when or even if they were going to make contact with the agent, but moments before leaving Belderone they had been informed that Thal K'sar - - the Bith artisan who allegedly had designed the hyperwave transceiver and holoprojector for Gunray's mechno-chair - - had been arrested, on charges yet to be learned.»
  - Ch26 L2493 «"From Viceroy Gunray to the Xi Char engraver to the Bith who implemented my designs for the hyperwave transceiver and holoprojector..."»
  - Ch26 L2523 «"My lord, the Jedi may search for others who contributed to fashioning the communications devices you distributed to Gunray, myself, and others.»
  - Ch28 L2669 «According to K'sar, Leh had been in the business of transporting shipments of spice from Ryloth to worlds in Hutt space when Sienar had hired her to deliver the experimental spacecraft for which K'sar had constructed a transceiver identical to the one he had affixed to Gunray's mechno-chair.»
  - Ch32 L3463 «Look what they risked on Cato Neimoidia in an effort to capture Viceroy Gunray.»
  - Ch37 L3975 «Obi-Wan and Anakin didn't need to be reminded of what had happened only weeks earlier on Cato Neimoidia when they had gone after Viceroy Gunray, but they were not about to forgo a chance to capture the Sith Lord.»
  - Ch39 L4193 «He could be surprised or taken off his guard - - as at Geonosis, as in the case of Gunray's mechno-chair - - but not for long.»
  - Ch45 L4667 «So much rested on whether Lord Sidious's plan could succeed on all fronts - - even though forced to unfold hastily, because of a foolish oversight by Nute Gunray.»
  - Ch46 L4721 «"Dooku, Grievous, Gunray, Palpatine."»
  - Ch54 L5219 «On Utapau, an Outer Rim world of vast sinkholes and lizard mounts, Viceroy Nute Gunray watched a grainy HoloNet image of General Grievous lower the boom on Coruscant.»
  - Ch54 L5219 «Gunray glanced at Shu Mai, Passel Argente, San Hill, and the rest, a backslapping fellowship all of a sudden.»

#### 5.10.9 Rune Haako (*19 BBY*)
- **Role:** Gunray's settlement officer and nervous voice of reason: «"Not a chance," puckered Haako said flatly.» (L397); «"Sidious," Haako said softly.» (L451); «"It's not too late," Haako said suddenly.» (L461). He flees the MagnaGuards (L1605).
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (29 lines: every sentence that names Haako, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch8 L393 «Behind Gunray walked settlement officer Rune Haako, wearing a crested skullcap; and behind Haako, various financial, legal, and diplomatic officers, each wearing a distinctive headpiece.»
  - Ch8 L393 «He called Haako aside while the others were exiting the sterile docking bay.»
  - Ch8 L397 «"Not a chance," puckered Haako said flatly.»
  - Ch8 L401 «"Your most cherished items accompany you," Haako said, gesturing to the containers already piled at the foot of the boarding ramp.»
  - Ch8 L413 «Haako hunched slightly at the reference.»
  - Ch8 L425 «Hurrying to the catacombs with Dooku at their side, Gunray and Haako had barely managed to flee the embattled surface and recall what core ships and droid carriers remained.»
  - Ch8 L429 «Eyes protruding from his mottled gray face, he swung to Rune Haako.»
  - Ch8 L431 «Haako stared at him.»
  - Ch8 L433 «Now Haako's eyes widened in apprehension.»
  - Ch8 L437 «"But you armed it to self-destruct," Haako said.»
  - Ch8 L441 «Haako gestured to himself.»
  - Ch8 L443 «"Haako, what if they should decide to tamper with it?"»
  - Ch8 L445 «Haako's broad slash of mouth twitched with worry.»
  - Ch8 L451 «"Sidious," Haako said softly.»
  - Ch8 L461 «"It's not too late," Haako said suddenly.»
  - Ch8 L463 «Haako shook his head negatively.»
  - Ch8 L473 «"Hurry," Haako insisted, "we haven't much time!"»
  - Ch15 L1077 «Grievous pictured Gunray, Haako, and the others strapped into luxurious acceleration couches, shivering with dread, perhaps sorry for the brief detour to Cato Neimoidia, wondering how a handful of Republic pilots had so easily decimated their squadrons, certainly comlinking the core ship to dispatch reinforcements.»
  - Ch15 L1101 «Still shaken from the ordeal of transiting from the core ship to the Invisible Hand, Nute Gunray sat restively in the cabin space to which he and Haako been shown immediately on disembarking.»
  - Ch15 L1103 «Every bit as shaken, Rune Haako sat alongside Gunray at the cabin's gleaming table.»
  - Ch15 L1115 «Peripherally, Gunray saw Haako slouch deeper into his chair, refusing to look either at him or at Grievous, circling his hands in a nervous gesture.»
  - Ch15 L1123 «Careful not to betray his sudden misgiving, Gunray traded covert glances with Haako.»
  - Ch21 L1577 «Gunray swung around to face Rune Haako.»
  - Ch21 L1583 «Haako asked.»
  - Ch21 L1589 «Haako was horrified.»
  - Ch21 L1597 «Haako said, aiming a quivering finger at the display screen.»
  - Ch21 L1605 «Haako spun on his heel and hurried for the suite's rear hatch.»
  - Ch21 L1609 «Haako threw over his shoulder.»
  - Ch21 L1615 «Haako stood in the open hatch.»

#### 5.10.10 TC-16 (protocol droid)
- **Personality:** fussy and self-aware: «"I'm fully aware that my circuits are corroded," TC-16 said.» (L517).
- **Arc:** a former Sidious-era protocol droid found on Cato Neimoidia (Ch9), who interprets on Charros IV (Ch16: «"The lightsabers are not weapons, Excellency," TC-16 said after a brief exchange.», L1223) → joins Dyne's team → survives the lair («there were no signs of the Geonosians or, for that matter, TC-16», L4539) → resurfaces to give C-3PO a cryptic warning (Ch53: «"A most curious encounter, Mistress," C-3PO said.», L5139).
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (45 lines: every sentence that names TC-16, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch4 L261 «One hand clamped over TC-16's small rectangle of vocabulator, a commando yanked the droid to one side, but it was too late.»
  - Ch6 L299 «TC-16 inserted into a brief pause in the firefight.»
  - Ch6 L307 «A muffled sound of dismay escaped TC-16's vocabulator.»
  - Ch6 L313 «Anakin's teammates joined him at the brink, one with a hand vised on TC-16's upper left arm.»
  - Ch6 L317 «TC-16 tipped his head to one side.»
  - Ch9 L487 «R2-D2 stood nearby, communing with TC-16.»
  - Ch9 L513 «"Really, sirs, I must protest," TC-16 interrupted, hurrying behind R2-D2.»
  - Ch9 L517 «"I'm fully aware that my circuits are corroded," TC-16 said.»
  - Ch9 L525 «"Sirs," TC-16 said from behind them.»
  - Ch9 L543 «"I've been trying to tell you, sirs," TC-16 said, gesturing toward the launching bay's ruined iris hatch.»
  - Ch9 L551 «Obi-Wan asked TC-16.»
  - Ch9 L559 «TC-16 translated the astromech's response.»
  - Ch9 L571 «R2-D2 yowled, and TC-16 moaned in distress.»
  - Ch12 L817 «In the launching bay Anakin kept his eyes on the mechno-chair, while R2- D2 and TC-16 kept their photoreceptors on Anakin.»
  - Ch16 L1175 «"I'm certain that it was constructed specifically for us," TC-16 said.»
  - Ch16 L1179 «"Guess they don't get many guests," Anakin said as he, Obi-Wan, and TC-16 started down the ramp.»
  - Ch16 L1191 «Occasionally a Xi Charrian would leap on TC-16 or make a grab for one of his limbs, but the droid was able to shake his assailants off.»
  - Ch16 L1199 «TC-16 exchanged chitterings and stridulations with them, and explained.»
  - Ch16 L1207 «The enormous building TC-16 had called a workshop topped two hundred meters in height and was crowned with latticework spires and towers that evoked strains of eerie music from the steady wind.»
  - Ch16 L1211 «Without ceremony, TC-16 approached the Prelate and tendered a greeting.»
  - Ch16 L1221 «TC-16 supplied the glottal stops and clicking sounds necessary to pronounce the name.»
  - Ch16 L1223 «"The lightsabers are not weapons, Excellency," TC-16 said after a brief exchange.»
  - Ch16 L1229 «They handed the lightsabers to TC-16, who presented them to the Prelate for inspection.»
  - Ch16 L1231 «"That went better than expected," Obi-Wan said as he, Anakin, and TC-16 were being escorted into the heart of Workshop Xcan.»
  - Ch16 L1241 «The few who did take notice of the three visitors in their midst showed more interest in TC-16 than in the Jedi.»
  - Ch16 L1243 «The escorts chittered to him briefly before TC-16 took over. "t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak, first allow me to say that your work is of such exceptional quality that the deities themselves must be covetous."»
  - Ch16 L1247 «TC-16 listened, then added: "But surely it was yours, for the inner portion of the rear leg bears your devotional symbol."»
  - Ch16 L1251 «"We understand your reluctance to discuss such matters," TC-16 was saying quietly.»
  - Ch16 L1253 «Without another chitter, the Xi Charrian let go his tools and launched himself from his work pallet - - not at TC-16 or either of the Jedi, but straight up into the web of overhead girders.»
  - Ch16 L1273 «But before a bolt could be fired TC-16 emerged from a side gallery, his body realigned and polished to a dazzling luster, shouting: "Look what they've done to me!"»
  - Ch16 L1279 «TC-16 translated.»
  - Ch16 L1281 «Obi-Wan looked at t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak, then at TC-16.»
  - Ch16 L1295 «TC-16 listened.»
  - Ch16 L1299 «Obi-Wan looked at TC-16.»
  - Ch16 L1303 «TC-16 said: "The transceiver unit arrived from a facility known as Escarte.»
  - Ch16 L1307 «"An asteroid mining facility," TC-16 explained, "belonging to the Commerce Guild."»
  - Ch34 L3711 «"Fine," Mace said, grabbing hold of TC-16 and pointing him in the direction of the terrified aliens.»
  - Ch34 L3721 «TC-16 listened, then said: "They say that they haven't the slightest idea where they are, General.»
  - Ch34 L3729 «"Apparently that's nothing new for them," TC-16 said.»
  - Ch38 L4161 «Mace looked at TC-16, then at Dyne.»
  - Ch40 L4323 «Only the interpreter droid, TC-16, was at a loss for something to do.»
  - Ch43 L4535 «Dyne stepped between the powered-down pair of probe droids, TC-16 following in his footsteps.»
  - Ch43 L4535 «He could sense TC-16 come to a sudden halt behind him.»
  - Ch43 L4539 «It was possible, in that eternal moment of silence, that the troopers had reacted quickly enough to get off a few bolts, because when Dyne looked to his right, along the way he had come, there were no signs of the Geonosians or, for that matter, TC-16.»
  - Ch44 L4647 «Just short of entering the tunnel, she caught a glance of a bright silver protocol droid that might have been TC-16 hastening toward the exit to the west skydock.»

#### 5.10.11 R2-D2
- **Role:** jacks into the mechno-chair, which walks off with him (Ch9: «they saw R2-D2 with his interface arm still jacked into the chair», L547), bickers with TC-16 (L515), and carries Palpatine's recorded plea in Ch52.
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (20 lines: every sentence that names R2-D2, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch9 L487 «R2-D2 stood nearby, communing with TC-16.»
  - Ch9 L513 «"Really, sirs, I must protest," TC-16 interrupted, hurrying behind R2-D2.»
  - Ch9 L515 «R2-D2 trilled and hooted at the battered protocol droid.»
  - Ch9 L515 «The two had been bickering since R2- D2's arrival a short time earlier.»
  - Ch9 L527 «R2-D2 began to loose a long series of whistles, chirps, and chitters.»
  - Ch9 L539 «R2-D2 honked and razzed, though as if from a distance.»
  - Ch9 L547 «The protocol droid following behind, they hurried into a compact data room, where they saw R2-D2 with his interface arm still jacked into the chair, and the gripper of his grasping arm clamped to the bar handle of a storage cabinet.»
  - Ch9 L551 «R2-D2 chattered and wheezed.»
  - Ch9 L557 «Obi-Wan glanced worriedly at R2- D2.»
  - Ch9 L571 «R2-D2 yowled, and TC-16 moaned in distress.»
  - Ch9 L571 «R2-D2 mewled in alarm.»
  - Ch12 L817 «In the launching bay Anakin kept his eyes on the mechno-chair, while R2- D2 and TC-16 kept their photoreceptors on Anakin.»
  - Ch19 L1449 «R2-D2's plaintive mewl needed no translation.»
  - Ch19 L1469 «Then he reopened a channel to R2-D2.»
  - Ch19 L1471 «R2-D2 loosed a series of urgent whistles and tweets as the starfighter was rocked to its shields.»
  - Ch24 L1865 «The protocol droid had been sent to Coruscant for further debriefing by Republic Intelligence; R2-D2 was on Belderone, where technicians were seeing to damages he had sustained during the battle there.»
  - Ch35 L3781 «With R2-D2 standing off to one side of the transmission grid, Anakin hung on the Supreme Chancellor's every word.»
  - Ch35 L3849 «He glanced at R2-D2, as if expecting commiseration, but the astromech only swiveled his dome, his processor status indicator flashing from blue to red.»
  - Ch35 L3875 «"Did you get through to him?" he asked as Anakin and R2-D2 approached.»
  - Ch37 L3959 «The two starfighters were flying abreast, almost wingtip-to-wingtip, astromech droids R2-D2 and R4-P17 in their respective sockets.»

#### 5.10.12 C-3PO
- **Role:** Padmé's droid. His «Your Majesty» slip draws Palpatine's line «A title more fit for a king or emperor.» (L973). «For a moment it appeared that C-3PO was speechless - - but only for a moment.» (L969). He is evacuated with Padmé (L4433) and has his «curious encounter» with TC-16 (L5139).
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (20 lines: every sentence that names C-3PO, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch1 L99 «"You can only be referring to See-Threepio.»
  - Ch12 L819 «With his mom; with C-3PO, without the bright shell he now wore - -»
  - Ch13 L917 «C-3PO was last to cross the threshold, twisting his head to regard both of the motionless guards.»
  - Ch13 L967 «Padme looked at C-3PO.»
  - Ch13 L969 «For a moment it appeared that C-3PO was speechless - - but only for a moment.»
  - Ch42 L4385 «"Hurry, Threepio," Padme said over her shoulder.»
  - Ch42 L4403 «She heard her name called, and turned to see Bail Organa and Mon Mothma angling toward where she and C-3PO were momentarily hampered.»
  - Ch42 L4433 «The Jedi led Padme, C-3PO, Bail, and Mon Mothma to the open-roofed skimmer.»
  - Ch42 L4439 «Padme clutched on to C-3PO as Bail veered away from the plasma bolts.»
  - Ch42 L4445 «C-3PO said.»
  - Ch46 L4697 «Mon Mothma hurried her away from the skimmer, to where Bail and C-3PO were hiding behind the blockish pedestal of a modernistic sculpture.»
  - Ch46 L4729 «"Perhaps if we throw ourselves on the mercy of the owners of the mall, they will raise the security grate," C-3PO started to say.»
  - Ch46 L4737 «C-3PO shouted as he watched them search the craft for weapons.»
  - Ch46 L4763 «C-3PO shook his head.»
  - Ch53 L5137 «Her gaze lowered, she caught sight of C-3PO, parting company with a silver protocol droid that disappeared into the crowd.»
  - Ch53 L5139 «"What was that about, Threepio?" she asked as he approached.»
  - Ch53 L5139 «"A most curious encounter, Mistress," C-3PO said.»
  - Ch53 L5141 «"In what way, Threepio?"»
  - Ch53 L5145 «C-3PO's photoreceptors locked on Padme.»
  - Ch54 L5205 «Gazing around, he spied Padme standing with C-3PO, arms cradled against her, elbows in the palms of her hands, her face raised to the light-splintered sky.»

#### 5.10.13 Fa'ale Leh ("Genne"), Twi'lek spicerunner turned courier pilot
- **Role:** she delivered the Sienar-built Scimitar «for a Jedi» to someone who was not one: «But the guy I handed the yoke over to - - he was no Jedi. Oh, he had a lightsaber and all, but... I don't know, there was something off about him.» (L2873). Bounty hunters attack her on Naos III and the Jedi rescue her across the ice (Ch28–29). She loses a lekku to blaster fire and recovers on the MedStar (L3497, L3519).
- **Key line:** «"Who told you where to find me? Had to be Thal K'sar, am I right? He's the only one still alive. That betraying little Bith - - "» (L2849).
- **Scaling implications:** none. She links the Naboo-era Sith ship to Coruscant and The Works.
- **Complete quote register (29 lines: every sentence that names Fa'ale Leh, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch28 L2667 «Despite the fact that the moon seemed a perfect place for a red-complected Twi'lek to hide, Obi-Wan doubted that Fa'ale Leh would be found here.»
  - Ch28 L2669 «More important, Naos III didn't offer much in the way of job opportunities for a former spicerunner unless Leh was one of the death-defying few who piloted loads of flash-frozen sharptooths to the Tion or Coreward on the Perlemian.»
  - Ch28 L2669 «According to K'sar, Leh had been in the business of transporting shipments of spice from Ryloth to worlds in Hutt space when Sienar had hired her to deliver the experimental spacecraft for which K'sar had constructed a transceiver identical to the one he had affixed to Gunray's mechno-chair.»
  - Ch28 L2763 «You mean Genne."»
  - Ch28 L2765 «"The name we know her by is Fa'ale Leh."»
  - Ch28 L2807 «Wearing trousers, boots, and an insulated jacket, Genne - - perhaps Fa'ale Leh - - was lounging on a narrow bed, her back and lekku against the headboard, long legs extended and crossed at the ankle.»
  - Ch28 L2847 «"Then you are Fa'ale Leh."»
  - Ch28 L2863 «"No need," Obi-Wan said as Fa'ale was bringing her left head-tail over her shoulder.»
  - Ch28 L2881 «An instant before it blew inward - - raining plastoid rafters, ice-covered roof panels, ceiling tiles, and two heavily armed Trandoshans - - he had rushed to the bed and overturned it, dumping Fa'ale Leh, foam mattresses, and bedcovers onto the cold floor.»
  - Ch28 L2887 «Parried bolts flew directly through the thin walls, rousing shrieks from Fa'ale's neighbors to both sides.»
  - Ch28 L2887 «A backflip carried Obi-Wan out of the blade's path, but Fa'ale's bedside table wasn't as fortunate.»
  - Ch28 L2895 «Deactivating his blade, Anakin pulled Fa'ale out from under the mattresses and bedding, and yanked her to her feet.»
  - Ch28 L2913 «Anakin hauled Fa'ale to her feet once more and planted himself face-to-face with her.»
  - Ch28 L2923 «Fa'ale took hold of one of the poles and vanished from sight.»
  - Ch28 L2927 «"I'm too drunk to drive," Fa'ale was saying.»
  - Ch28 L2933 «Obi-Wan mounted a second sled, while Fa'ale was positioning herself behind Anakin.»
  - Ch28 L2947 «"Downriver," Fa'ale said.»
  - Ch29 L2967 «Up ahead, he saw Anakin and Fa'ale lean their sled through a sweeping turn to the south, between two of Naos III's many hills.»
  - Ch29 L2967 «Lethal hyphens of light streaked down from the bridge that linked the hills, but not one found Anakin or Fa'ale.»
  - Ch29 L2975 «Red hands clamped around Anakin's waist, Fa'ale whooped and shouted, clearly enjoying herself.»
  - Ch29 L2981 «"Sharptooth collector," Fa'ale explained when she saw the snow skiff.»
  - Ch29 L2985 «"I don't see any way of reaching him in time," Fa'ale said.»
  - Ch29 L2989 «Fa'ale's hands clutched his robe.»
  - Ch29 L3017 «"Where's Fa'ale?"»
  - Ch29 L3019 «When they reached her, Fa'ale was laying facedown a few meters from the machine, which had been holed by blasterfire.»
  - Ch29 L3027 «Fa'ale sighed.»
  - Ch29 L3033 «Fa'ale shook her head.»
  - Ch33 L3497 «"Fa'ale is doing fine," Anakin said as he approached Obi-Wan jauntily.»
  - Ch33 L3519 «For four standard days they had been awaiting instructions from the Jedi Council and visiting the medical ward to check on Fa'ale's progress, and the strain of so much inactivity was beginning to show.»

#### 5.10.14 Thal K'sar, Bith transceiver engineer
- **Role:** a condemned prisoner on Escarte who built the mechno-chair transceiver and its twin (Ch24–25). He leads Anakin the wrong way, then saves him from the dwarf spider droid: «Ten meters down the corridor, K'sar was on his feet, the heavy weapon held in front of him in a two-handed grip, firing directly into the spider's bulging red photoreceptors and power cells.» (L2291). Anakin's dark interrogation draws out the Sienar lead: «"You're holding something back," Anakin said in a flat, menacing tone. "I can feel it."» (L2351); «Swallowing with difficulty, K'sar said: "It went to Sienar!"» (L2357).
- **Scaling implications:** none for him. Shows Anakin's dark-side interrogation drift and his limits without a saber (blacked out by the spider droid, L2287).
- **Complete quote register (48 lines: every sentence that names Thal K'sar, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch24 L1867 «Obi-Wan and Anakin hadn't been told when or even if they were going to make contact with the agent, but moments before leaving Belderone they had been informed that Thal K'sar - - the Bith artisan who allegedly had designed the hyperwave transceiver and holoprojector for Gunray's mechno-chair - - had been arrested, on charges yet to be learned.»
  - Ch24 L1885 «Our best hope for getting close to K'sar is to get ourselves arrested."»
  - Ch24 L2049 «"Already occupied by the Bith - - K'sar."»
  - Ch24 L2059 «Thal K'sar was slumped in a corner, his long-fingered hands cuffed in front of him.»
  - Ch24 L2059 «K'sar glanced up, but didn't return Obi-Wan's nod of greeting.»
  - Ch24 L2067 «Anakin ambled over to where K'sar was huddled.»
  - Ch24 L2069 «Though surprised to hear his own language spoken by a human, K'sar kept silent.»
  - Ch24 L2105 «Travale glanced at K'sar.»
  - Ch24 L2129 «"That means K'sar goes with you.»
  - Ch24 L2145 «Anakin swung to K'sar.»
  - Ch24 L2155 «In the red glow of emergency lights, K'sar cast a dumbfounded look around.»
  - Ch24 L2167 «Then into K'sar's hands he pressed the blaster he had summoned from the guard.»
  - Ch24 L2167 «K'sar stared at the weapon.»
  - Ch24 L2175 «K'sar asked.»
  - Ch24 L2185 «"K'sar will also know the way to Docking Bay Forty.»
  - Ch24 L2191 «"Security detail coming to check on the guards," K'sar said.»
  - Ch24 L2197 «K'sar's long legs propelled him at a fast clip.»
  - Ch24 L2207 «Several minutes of running brought them to a rickety stairway, which K'sar didn't hesitate to climb.»
  - Ch24 L2209 «"I'd still like to know what you did to end up in detention," Anakin asked from behind K'sar.»
  - Ch24 L2219 «K'sar stopped to catch his breath.»
  - Ch24 L2235 «K'sar said suddenly.»
  - Ch24 L2237 «"What's wrong?' K'sar eyes filled with dread.»
  - Ch24 L2247 «K'sar words were swallowed by the sound of servomotors and hydraulics.»
  - Ch25 L2283 «Perched at the juncture of four splayed legs, the hemispherical body was dominated by two huge circular photoreceptors, which appeared to be fixed on Anakin and K'sar as the droid rushed in to make the kill.»
  - Ch25 L2285 «Anakin threw K'sar to one side and rolled as the dwarf spider fired.»
  - Ch25 L2291 «Ten meters down the corridor, K'sar was on his feet, the heavy weapon held in front of him in a two-handed grip, firing directly into the spider's bulging red photoreceptors and power cells.»
  - Ch25 L2291 «Finally the droid collapsed, the tip of its cannon slamming into the floor at K'sar's feet.»
  - Ch25 L2297 «"We're close," K'sar said in a daze.»
  - Ch25 L2301 «K'sar nodded, and they hurried through the final stretch, emerging from a tunnel opening at the rear of the docking bay.»
  - Ch25 L2303 «Anakin took a moment to study the disposition of the droids, then turned to K'sar, who seemed to have recovered from the ordeal in the tunnel.»
  - Ch25 L2307 «K'sar nodded.»
  - Ch25 L2307 «Anakin leapt out of the corridor, deliberately calling attention to himself to distract the droids from firing at K'sar.»
  - Ch25 L2307 «Following K'sar up the boarding ramp, he rushed into the cockpit and began to power up the cruiser's defensive systems.»
  - Ch25 L2309 «When the flight systems were online he left the cockpit to search for K'sar, who was sitting on the floor of the main hold, panting.»
  - Ch25 L2331 «K'sar started.»
  - Ch25 L2353 «K'sar gulped, and clutched at his neck.»
  - Ch25 L2357 «Swallowing with difficulty, K'sar said: "It went to Sienar!"»
  - Ch25 L2367 «K'sar paused, then added: "But I knew the pilot Sienar hired to deliver the ship."»
  - Ch25 L2379 «Obi-Wan glanced at Thal K'sar, thinking the Bith might feel the same.»
  - Ch25 L2379 «Instead, K'sar was curled up on the hold's worn acceleration couch.»
  - Ch25 L2401 «"K'sar seemed rather... subdued.»
  - Ch26 L2479 «"I suspect that the planned disappearance of Thal K'sar did not go according to plan," Sidious said finally.»
  - Ch26 L2481 «Hours from execution, K'sar was rescued and spirited from the facility by a Republic Intelligence agent, who had the help of two Jedi."»
  - Ch28 L2651 «"Blame Thal K'sar.»
  - Ch28 L2669 «According to K'sar, Leh had been in the business of transporting shipments of spice from Ryloth to worlds in Hutt space when Sienar had hired her to deliver the experimental spacecraft for which K'sar had constructed a transceiver identical to the one he had affixed to Gunray's mechno-chair.»
  - Ch28 L2669 «It had long been assumed that the tattooed Zabrak Sith had performed the modifications, but information supplied by K'sar suggested that Raith Sienar's Advanced Projects Laboratory had been responsible not only for building the ship, but also for implementing Darth Sidious's designs.»
  - Ch28 L2699 «"K'sar didn't supply you with anything else to go on - - scars, tattooed lekku, anything?"»
  - Ch28 L2849 «Had to be Thal K'sar, am I right?»

#### 5.10.15 Agent Travale (Republic Intelligence, undercover on Escarte)
- **Role:** a Republic mole posing as a Commerce Guild security captain. He "arrests" the Jedi and frees them during the blackout (Ch24): «"Welcome to the detention level," Travale said, back in character, and shoving Obi-Wan out into the anteroom beyond.» (L2043). He foreshadows the Interdictor: «"These things are the future," Travale said. "Fill a ship with enough tractor beam arrays and you could prevent an enemy from jumping to hyperspace. "» (L2277). He gets out with them (L2375).
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (34 lines: every sentence that names Travale, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch24 L2017 «"You're Travale," Obi-Wan said, using the code name he had been furnished.»
  - Ch24 L2031 «Travale hadn't been given the reason, but Obi-Wan nodded in acknowledgment.»
  - Ch24 L2035 «"My thought, too," Travale said.»
  - Ch24 L2043 «"Welcome to the detention level," Travale said, back in character, and shoving Obi-Wan out into the anteroom beyond.»
  - Ch24 L2047 «"Show our two guests to cell four-eight-one-six," Travale told the sergeant among them.»
  - Ch24 L2051 «"Misery loves company," Travale said.»
  - Ch24 L2075 «The door grated open, revealing Travale and two Aqualish security officers.»
  - Ch24 L2075 «Without a word, the aliens standing to either side of Travale grabbed him by the arms and hurled him headlong into the cell.»
  - Ch24 L2077 «Travale was cuffed, and rattled.»
  - Ch24 L2091 «Obi-Wan asked Travale.»
  - Ch24 L2105 «Travale glanced at K'sar.»
  - Ch24 L2117 «"Take the tunnel to the left of the guard station," Travale was telling Obi-Wan when Anakin returned.»
  - Ch24 L2131 «Travale nodded toward the cell block corridor.»
  - Ch24 L2137 «Obi-Wan did the same, then snapped Travale's open.»
  - Ch24 L2137 «Travale smiled broadly.»
  - Ch24 L2143 «Travale shook his head in wonderment.»
  - Ch24 L2149 «"Emergency power should come on shortly," Travale said.»
  - Ch24 L2159 «"Two out of three," Travale said.»
  - Ch24 L2173 «"Here's where we part company," Travale said to Anakin.»
  - Ch24 L2181 «Travale chuckled.»
  - Ch24 L2189 «As Travale and Obi-Wan were running off, Anakin noticed that one of the turbolift cars was descending.»
  - Ch24 L2197 «But instead of going left as Travale had advised, he turned right at the first intersection.»
  - Ch25 L2251 «"Someone's coming," Obi-Wan warned Travale.»
  - Ch25 L2253 «Initially, Travale had made a few mistakes, but he had sorted through his confusion and was almost done.»
  - Ch25 L2257 «"Never a lightsaber when you need one," Travale whispered.»
  - Ch25 L2261 «Travale shook his head back and forth in appreciation of Obi-Wan's skill.»
  - Ch25 L2267 «Travale studied Obi-Wan for a moment.»
  - Ch25 L2271 «Obi-Wan touched Travale on the arm, and motioned with his bearded chin to the tower.»
  - Ch25 L2275 «The Jedi watched over Travale's shoulder as he dialed the coupling power feed to zero.»
  - Ch25 L2277 «"These things are the future," Travale said.»
  - Ch25 L2281 «"There will be," Travale said.»
  - Ch25 L2373 «Obi-Wan and Travale negotiated the cofferdam that linked Escarte's air lock to a docking ring just forward of the cruiser's tri-barreled thruster fantail.»
  - Ch25 L2375 «Stepping into the main hold, Travale gave a shout of joy.»
  - Ch25 L2387 «"Not a skill I expect to draw on again, but, yes, thanks to Travale."»

#### 5.10.16 Raith Sienar (offstage)
- **Role:** a starship magnate and corporate spy (L1237: «Revealed to be a "nonbeliever," Sienar had been exiled from Charros IV»). He commissioned the experimental courier «for a Jedi» (L2873), and «Raith Sienar's Advanced Projects Laboratory had been responsible not only for building the» courier (L2669). Information from K'sar suggested that Sienar's firm made the Sith ship's modifications.
- **Scaling implications:** none.
- **Complete quote register (19 lines: every sentence that names Raith Sienar, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch16 L1235 «"We can thank Raith Sienar for some of that."»
  - Ch16 L1237 «Almost two decades earlier, the wealthy and influential owner-president of Sienar Design Systems - - a chief supplier of starfighters to the Republic - - had spent time among the Xi Char, mastering ultraprecision engineering techniques he would later incorporate into his own designs.»
  - Ch16 L1237 «Revealed to be a "nonbeliever," Sienar had been exiled from Charros IV, and been made the target of bounty hunters, four of whom Sienar had managed to strand at a black hole known only to him and a handful of other hotshot hyperspace explorers.»
  - Ch16 L1237 «Sienar had engaged in similar acts of corporate espionage among the Trade Federation, Baktoid Armor, Corellian Engineering, and Incom Corporations, but the Xi Char had a long memory for what they considered sacrilege.»
  - Ch16 L1239 «Ten years back, Obi-Wan and Anakin had had their own brush with Sienar at the living world known as Zonama Sekot.»
  - Ch16 L1239 «Because Sienar had been partly responsible for Zonama Sekot's disappearance, he was also the reason that the Xi Char no longer accepted human apprentices.»
  - Ch17 L1315 «It has to be driving them mad, knowing that Sienar is getting even richer on techniques he basically stole from them."»
  - Ch25 L2357 «Swallowing with difficulty, K'sar said: "It went to Sienar!"»
  - Ch25 L2359 «"Raith Sienar?"»
  - Ch25 L2361 «"To Sienar Advanced Projects.»
  - Ch25 L2367 «K'sar paused, then added: "But I knew the pilot Sienar hired to deliver the ship."»
  - Ch28 L2669 «According to K'sar, Leh had been in the business of transporting shipments of spice from Ryloth to worlds in Hutt space when Sienar had hired her to deliver the experimental spacecraft for which K'sar had constructed a transceiver identical to the one he had affixed to Gunray's mechno-chair.»
  - Ch28 L2669 «It had long been assumed that the tattooed Zabrak Sith had performed the modifications, but information supplied by K'sar suggested that Raith Sienar's Advanced Projects Laboratory had been responsible not only for building the ship, but also for implementing Darth Sidious's designs.»
  - Ch28 L2671 «Obi-Wan and Anakin might have gone directly to the source - - to Raith Sienar - - had Supreme Chancellor Palpatine not vetoed the idea.»
  - Ch28 L2841 «"We want to talk to you about a star courier you piloted for Sienar Advanced Projects."»
  - Ch28 L2867 «"So who sent you - - Sienar?»
  - Ch28 L2873 «Sienar - - Raith Sienar himself - - told me it was for a Jedi.»
  - Ch29 L3035 «"Someone more important than Raith Sienar doesn't want us to learn too much about the star courier."»
  - Ch30 L3061 «A product of Sienar Advanced Projects Laboratory, the craft had been modified - - perhaps unwittingly by Sienar itself - - for the Sith who had killed Qui-Gon Jinn.»

#### 5.10.17 Palpatine's advisers: Sate Pestage, Mas Amedda, Armand Isard, Sly Moore, Sim Aloo, Janus Greejatus, Jannie Ha'Nook
- **Pestage:** the gatekeeper who stonewalls the delegation (Ch10: «Pestage spread his hands in a patronizing way.», L653) and pushes the bunker plan («"Chancellor, communications will be clearer from the bunker," Pestage said.», L4569).
- **Isard:** Director of Intelligence, who presses for evacuation (L4571, L4591). «She couldn't help noticing that Pestage and Isard - - two of Palpatine's closest advisers - - looked nervous.» (L4557). The leak of the bunker codes points at this circle (Ch51).
- **Mas Amedda:** Speaker, and the man in line to inherit war powers (L4555). Palpatine on the alternative: «And as for Mas Amedda, he won't last a week.» (L5209).
- **The rest:** Aloo, Greejatus, Ha'Nook and Sly Moore are named in the Ch13 lineup (L903).
- **Scaling implications:** none. Shaak Ti: «Grievous had help. Help from someone close to the top.» (L5009).
- **Complete quote register (27 lines: every sentence that names Palpatine's advisers, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch10 L613 «Bail could recall clearly the day he had stood with Palpatine, Mas Amedda, Malastarian Senators, and others on a balcony of the Senate Office Building, watching tens of thousands of clone troopers march into the enormous ships that would take the war to the Separatists.»
  - Ch10 L641 «But then the door to Palpatine's office slid open, and Sate Pestage, one of Palpatine's chief advisers, appeared.»
  - Ch10 L647 «Pestage glanced at the appointments secretary.»
  - Ch10 L653 «Pestage spread his hands in a patronizing way.»
  - Ch10 L659 «Pestage looked at Bail.»
  - Ch10 L663 «Pestage forced a smile.»
  - Ch13 L903 «Advisers Sim Aloo and Janus Greejatus; director of Intelligence Armand Isard; senior member of the Security and Intelligence Council, Jannie Ha'Nook of Glithnos; Chagrian Speaker of the Senate Mas Amedda; and staff aide Sly Moore, tall and ethereal looking in her Umbaran shadowcloak.»
  - Ch13 L903 «Last to exit was Pestage.»
  - Ch13 L909 «Just then Palpatine himself appeared, glancing at Bail and the others, then at Pestage.»
  - Ch13 L913 «Why wasn't I informed of this?" he asked Pestage.»
  - Ch42 L4411 «"Mas Amedda," he explained for Padme's benefit.»
  - Ch42 L4417 «Amedda gazed down at her in sudden concern.»
  - Ch44 L4555 «Should anything befall the Chancellor, authority to initiate battle campaigns and issue war codes would pass temporarily to Speaker of the Senate, Mas Amedda, who, Shaak Ti had learned, was already safely ensconced in a hardened bunker deep beneath the Great Rotunda.»
  - Ch44 L4557 «She couldn't help noticing that Pestage and Isard - - two of Palpatine's closest advisers - - looked nervous.»
  - Ch44 L4557 «Stass Allie directed at Isard.»
  - Ch44 L4559 «Isard made his lips a thin line.»
  - Ch44 L4569 «"Chancellor, communications will be clearer from the bunker," Pestage said.»
  - Ch44 L4571 «Isard added: "All those familiarization drills you so despised were conducted for just this scenario, sir."»
  - Ch44 L4587 «"Supreme Chancellor," Pestage was saying.»
  - Ch44 L4591 «"All the more reason to move you to safety as quickly as possible," Isard said.»
  - Ch44 L4595 «"We do, sir," Isard said.»
  - Ch44 L4615 «She turned to face Isard.»
  - Ch44 L4617 «Elsewhere in the room, Pestage was helping Palpatine to his feet.»
  - Ch44 L4621 «Isard gestured to one of the side rooms.»
  - Ch44 L4625 «"We can't just take him to a public shelter," Isard said.»
  - Ch54 L5207 «What if they do kill him, and power falls into the hands of Mas Amedda and the rest of that gang?»
  - Ch54 L5209 «And as for Mas Amedda, he won't last a week.»

#### 5.10.18 The Separatist Council: San Hill, Shu Mai, Poggle the Lesser, Passel Argente, Wat Tambor
- **Role:** Grievous was «San Hill and Poggle the Lesser's gift to Dooku» (L459). San Hill oversaw the rebuild that made him: «IBC chairman San Hill himself had overseen the details of the arrangement.» (L1385). In the Ch15 meeting: «"What world will accept us now?" equine-faced San Hill asked in a disconsolate voice.» (L1119). Shu Mai leaked the Belderone rendezvous (L1587). They celebrate on Utapau (L5219).
- **Scaling implications:** none. Supports the Grievous-origin entries (engineered crash).
- **Complete quote register (10 lines: every sentence that names Separatist Council, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch8 L459 «Supreme Commander of the droid armies, General Grievous had been San Hill and Poggle the Lesser's gift to Dooku.»
  - Ch15 L1079 «Had it not been for Grievous, Gunray and the rest might have suffered the same fate as Poggle the Lesser's lieutenant, Sun Fac.»
  - Ch15 L1103 «Other members of the Separatist Council occupied the choice seats: the almost two-dimensionally thin San Hill, Muun chairman of the InterGalactic Banking Clan; the Skakoan foreman of the Techno Union, Wat Tambor, encased in the cumbersome pressure suit that supplied him with methane; the vestigial-winged Geonosian Poggle the Lesser, Archduke of the Stalgasin Hive; the stalk-necked Gossam president of the Commerce Guild, Shu Mai; the cranial-horned Corporate Alliance Magistrate, Passel Argente; and former Republic Senators Po Nudo and Tikkes - - Aqualish and Quarren, respectively.»
  - Ch15 L1119 «"What world will accept us now?" equine-faced San Hill asked in a disconsolate voice.»
  - Ch18 L1385 «IBC chairman San Hill himself had overseen the details of the arrangement.»
  - Ch18 L1389 «That was when San Hill had paid him a second visit.»
  - Ch21 L1587 «"Immediately on being apprised by Shu Mai of the Belderone rendezvous, I contacted Grievous, expressing anger that he hadn't informed me personally.»
  - Ch22 L1661 «After the Sith Lord had expressed interest in Grievous to Dooku, Dooku, in turn, had expressed interest in Grievous to Chairman San Hill, of the InterGalactic Banking Clan.»
  - Ch22 L1667 «Dooku, especially, since he had no interest in commanding an army of droids, and already had his hands full nursemaiding the likes of Nute Gunray, Shu Mai, and the hive-minded others who eventually would form the Council of Separatists.»
  - Ch54 L5219 «Gunray glanced at Shu Mai, Passel Argente, San Hill, and the rest, a backslapping fellowship all of a sudden.»

#### 5.10.19 IG-100 MagnaGuards
- **Description:** «Benefiting from Grievous's own programming, as well as from the instruction Grievous had received from Dooku, the elite were trained in the Jedi arts, and more than a match for most.» (L1599).
- **Feats:** spar with Grievous at near-invisible speed (Ch32, Dooku POV: «They executed moves almost faster than the human eye could follow», L3331); kill «six of the soldiers and three of the Red Guards» in the bunker; one, «blinded and savagely slashed», keeps fighting (L4943).
- **Limits:** easily disarmed by Dooku (L3349) and shut down by Kit (L4813). One is «holed by more blaster bolts than would» be expected (L4993).
- **Scaling implications:** the MagnaGuard tier sits above the Red Guards and clone troopers and below Council Masters.
- **Complete quote register (14 lines: every sentence that names MagnaGuards, verbatim, in book order):**
  - Ch21 L1599 «Gunray saw that Grievous was accompanied by four of his elite MagnaGuards.»
  - Ch21 L1623 «The general swept into the suite, the four MagnaGuards in his angry wake spreading out to both sides, ready for violence.»
  - Ch21 L1633 «Stepping forward, a MagnaGuard thrust his staff to within a millimeter of Gunray's face.»
  - Ch32 L3327 «In a forward hold of Grievous's flagship, Dooku watched the cyborg general duel with his elite MagnaGuards, three of his trophy lightsabers in constant motion, parrying thrusts of the guards' pulse-weaponed staffs, slicing the recycled air a hairbreadth from the expressionless faces of his opponents, incapacitating arm and leg servos when he could.»
  - Ch32 L3365 «He watched Grievous disarm two of the MagnaGuards.»
  - Ch47 L4813 «To the rear of the car, where Grievous's pair of MagnaGuards had made the mistake of pitting themselves against Kit Fisto, the Nautolan's blade was a cyclone of blazing blue light.»
  - Ch50 L4927 «Grievous and six MagnaGuards cut a bloody swath through the broad corridors that led ultimately to Palpatine's sanctuary.»
  - Ch50 L4929 «His literal "misstep" had shamed him, and he was grateful that the two MagnaGuards that had fought at his side had not survived to bear witness to it.»
  - Ch50 L4943 «Fencing with his four adversaries - - for that's all the fight amounted to - - Grievous saw six of the soldiers and three of the Red Guards jolted to spasming deaths by the MagnaGuards' double-tipped scepters.»
  - Ch50 L4979 «The MagnaGuards stepped forward to make certain that Palpatine obeyed.»
  - Ch51 L4993 «Steps into the tunnel, their worst fears were realized when they saw three troopers hauling away a MagnaGuard, holed by more blaster bolts than would have been needed to demolish a police skimmer.»
  - Ch51 L4999 «Here, too, were the shells of two more MagnaGuards.»
  - Ch54 L5163 «Grievous swiveled his seat toward Palpatine, who was strapped into an acceleration couch between two MagnaGuards.»
  - Ch54 L5185 «Diminutive between two MagnaGuards, Palpatine was waiting at the foot of the shuttle boarding ramp.»

#### 5.10.20 The Red Guards (Senate Guard)
- **Role:** Palpatine's bodyguards: «Despite having been notified of their arrival, the imposing Red Guards kept their force pikes raised in defensive postures.» (L4549). They fight beside Stass Allie (L4611) and die in the bunker to Grievous and the MagnaGuards: «The same was true for those Red Guards whose corpses had been burned open by lightsaber.» (L4999).
- **Scaling implications:** a fodder baseline in Grievous's bunker feat.
- **Complete quote register (22 lines: every sentence that names Red Guards, verbatim, in book order):**

  - Ch44 L4555 «Additional Red Guards and several of Palpatine's advisers stood watching him, some with comlinks plugged into their ears, others with devices Shaak Ti understood to be vital to the continued operation of the Republic military.»
  - Ch44 L4611 «Six Red Guards raced to her side, their activated force pikes humming in concert with Allie's lightsaber.»
  - Ch44 L4613 «As Allie and the Red Guards were felling the final few droids, the Separatist craft dropped back into the clouds, with bolts from the skimmers chasing it.»

  - Ch44 L4649 «"Wait here," Shaak Ti instructed the Red Guards and Palpatine when they had reached the mouth of the tunnel.»

  - Ch47 L4783 «Mace told the Jedi and Red Guards.»
  - Ch47 L4799 «Deactivating their blades, Mace and Kit rushed into the forward car, which was now filled with Palpatine's advisers and those passengers the Jedi women and Red Guards had relocated from the train's lead car.»
