- Ch11 L803: «If the Jedi hadn't learned of the clone army, Dooku's Separatists would have suddenly appeared on the scene with tens of millions of battle droids, fleets of warships, and seceded from the Republic without contest.»
- Ch11 L809: «"Years matter not. Busy fighting a war, you have been. Mentoring your headstrong Padawan. In pursuit of Dooku and his minions... Darker, events became. Attempting to turn this war to their own uses, Dooku and Sidious are."»
- Ch11 L811: «"We'll have Dooku soon enough."»
- Ch11 L813: «Grown beyond Dooku this war has.»
- Ch13 L939: «"Is there not some other way than continued warfare?" Bail asked. "Surely Dooku can be persuaded to listen to reason now."»
- Ch13 L941: «Such decisions were what prompted many worlds to ally with Count Dooku in the first place."»
- Ch14 L1009: «Dooku understood that.»
- Ch14 L1011: «"Dooku is a Sith, Anakin. He may have had his good reasons for leaving the Order, but he is nothing now but a master of deceit. He and Sidious prey on the weak-willed. They deceive themselves into believing that they are infallible."»
- Ch14 L1013: «"But I've seen instances where the Jedi lie to one another. Master Kolar lied about Quinlan Vos going to the dark side. We're lying now, by not sharing our information about Sidious with Chancellor Palpatine. What would Sidious or Dooku have to say about our lies?"»
- Ch14 L1027: «"The chair bears the stamps of several of the manufacturers affiliated with Dooku," Dyne continued. "The hyperwave receiver is equipped with a type-summoning chip and transponding antenna that are similar to ones we discovered in a mine-laying chameleon droid Master Yoda brought back from Ilum."»
- Ch14 L1029: «"An image of Dooku, the droid contained."»
- Ch14 L1031: «"For the time being we're proceeding on the assumption that Dooku - - or Sidious, for that matter - - might have developed the chips, and had them installed in transceivers awarded to Gunray and other key members of the Council of Separatists."»
- Ch15 L1079: «Perhaps Gunray had thought of him as some mindless entity, like the reawakened Gen'Dai, Durge; or Dooku's misguided apprentice, Asajj Ventress; or the human bounty hunter called Aurra Sing - - all three of whom had been so driven by personal hatred of the Jedi that they had proved worthless, mere distractions while Grievous went about the real business of war.»
- Ch15 L1091: «Count Dooku and his Sith Master would never forgive him if he allowed anything untoward to happen to Gunray.»
- Ch15 L1103: «Gunray would have liked nothing more than to inform Dooku of the general's actions, but he feared that the Sith would stand with Grievous.»
- Ch15 L1117: «"The Republic fools itself believing that they have you on the run, but, in fact, Lord Sidious and Darth Tyranus have engineered this, for reasons that will be made clear soon enough.»
- Ch16 L1145: «Aayla Secura and the Caamasi Jedi Ylenic It'kla had taken a Techno Union defector into custody on Corellia; Quinlan Vos had gone undercover to infiltrate Dooku's circle of dark side apprentices... And Supreme Chancellor Palpatine hadn't been told - - or learned since - - about any of the covert operations.»
- Ch17 L1327: «Dyne grinned. "We've succeeded in deciphering the code Dooku - - and, we have to assume, Sidious - - has been using to communicate with the Council of Separatists. Using the code, we were able to intercept a message sent to Viceroy Gunray, through the mechno-chair."»
- Ch18 L1419: «Only then had he been presented to Count Dooku, and only then had his real training begun.»
- Ch18 L1419: «But from Dooku - - Lord Tyranus - - he came to understand the inner workings of the Sith.»
- Ch18 L1421: «Tyranus himself had trained him in lightsaber technique. In mere weeks he had surpassed any of Tyranus's previous students. It helped, of course, to have an indestructible body reminiscent of a Krath wardroid. The ability to tower over most sentient beings. Crystal circuitry. Four grasping appendages..»
- Ch19 L1515: «His Sith Masters had ordered him to safeguard the lives of the Council members - - though, in fact, the Confederacy needed none other than Lords Sidious and Tyranus.»
- Ch20 L1547: «"Trained well by Dooku, General Grievous was," Yoda said.»
- Ch20 L1549: «Yoda recalled his duel with Dooku in the solar sailer's docking bay on Geonosis.»
- Ch20 L1549: «Dooku bested, left with no alternative but to distract and flee...»
- Ch21 L1599: «Benefiting from Grievous's own programming, as well as from the instruction Grievous had received from Dooku, the elite were trained in the Jedi arts, and more than a match for most.»
- Ch22 L1657: «Poor Gunray, Dooku thought. Pitiful creature... But for having left the mechno-chair behind on Cato Neimoidia, he deserved all the fear Grievous had put into him.»
- Ch22 L1659: «Secluded in his castle on Kaon, Dooku had just spoken with the general and was pondering how best to handle the situation.»
- Ch22 L1659: «Dooku had already ordered the general to refrain from using the code for the time being.»
- Ch22 L1661: «After the Sith Lord had expressed interest in Grievous to Dooku, Dooku, in turn, had expressed interest in Grievous to Chairman San Hill, of the InterGalactic Banking Clan.»
- Ch22 L1663: «Poor Grievous, Dooku thought.»
- Ch22 L1663: «Then the Separatists would simply have to look elsewhere for a commander, Dooku had told Hill.»
- Ch22 L1667: «Sidious and Dooku couldn't have been more pleased with the result.»
- Ch22 L1667: «Dooku, especially, since he had no interest in commanding an army of droids, and already had his hands full nursemaiding the likes of Nute Gunray, Shu Mai, and the hive-minded others who eventually would form the Council of Separatists.»
- Ch22 L1669: «No need to coax him to release his anger and rage, as Dooku had been forced to do during the training of his so-called Dark Jedi disciples.»
- Ch22 L1669: «There had been moments during the extensive combat sessions when even Dooku had been hard-pressed to outduel the cyborg.»
- Ch22 L1671: «But then, Dooku had kept some secrets to himself. Just in case.»
- Ch22 L1685: «It was into such times that Dooku had been born, placed because of a strong connection to the Force among an Order that had grown complacent, self-involved, arrogant about the power they wielded in the name of the Republic.»
- Ch22 L1687: «Having hailed from nobility and great wealth, Dooku yearned for prestige.»
- Ch22 L1689: «Without the Jedi ever realizing it, Dooku had been as disruptive to the Order as would be a young boy raised in slavery on Tatooine.»
- Ch22 L1693: «Dooku had said as much to anyone who would listen.»
- Ch22 L1693: «Dooku's true confidant had been Master Sifo-Dyas, who, while also disturbed by what was occurring, was too weak to take action.»
- Ch22 L1697: «Dooku had given thought to seeking him out, perhaps killing him.»
- Ch22 L1699: «Sidious's boldness surprised him at first, but it hadn't taken long for Dooku to become fascinated by the Sith.»
- Ch22 L1703: «Dooku was an eager student, and yet Sidious had continued to hold him at arm's length.»
- Ch22 L1703: «Sidious had recognized in Dooku the makings of a true accomplice - - an equal from the other camp, already trained in the Jedi arts, a master duelist, a political visionary.»
- Ch22 L1703: «But he needed to gauge the depth of Dooku's commitment.»
- Ch22 L1705: «And so with the murder of Sifo-Dyas, Dooku had embraced the dark side fully, and Sidious had conferred on him the title Darth Tyranus. His final act before leaving the Jedi Order was to erase all mentions of Kamino from the Jedi archives.»
- Ch22 L1707: «Then, as Tyranus, he had found Fett on Bogg 4; had instructed the Mandalorian to deliver himself to Kamino; and had arranged for payments to be made to the cloners through circuitous routes...»
- Ch22 L1709: «Ten years passed. Under its new Supreme Chancellor, the Republic recovered somewhat, then grew more corrupt and beset with problems than before. As best they could, Sidious and Tyranus helped things along.»
- Ch22 L1713: «All at once, here was Qui-Gon Jinn's former Padawan, right under Dooku's nose.»
- Ch22 L1713: «But when he had informed Sidious of Obi-Wan's presence, Sidious had only said, Allow events to play out, Darth Tyranus.»
- Ch22 L1717: «He had to be informed, Dooku thought. Or did he? For a heartbeat he hesitated, imagining the power that could be his. Then he went directly to the hyperwave transmitter Sidious had given him, and began his transmission.»
- Ch23 L1831: «"Dooku's Sith Master, Sidious is. Learned of him on Geonosis, Master Kenobi did. But eluded us, proof of him has."»
- Ch23 L1835: «"Dismissed that, we have. But lying about Sidious, Dooku wasn't."»
- Ch26 L2433: «And yet Dooku loved the place.»
- Ch26 L2435: «The antithesis of his native Serenno, The Works was very much a home away from home for the human who had earned the title Darth Tyranus.»
- Ch26 L2437: «Strong in the dark side - - made so by Darth Sidious - - the building had been the place of Dooku's apprenticeship, just as it had served as a training ground for Darth Maul before Dooku, and who knew who or how many other Sith disciples before Maul.»
- Ch26 L2437: «During the ten years preceding the outbreak of the war - - when Count Dooku of Serenno was believed to have been peddling his Separatist agenda to disenfranchised worlds in the Mid and Outer Rims - - he had, in fact, spent long periods of time in The Works, coming and going at will, or as required of him by Darth Sidious.»
- Ch26 L2441: «Having ordered the sloop's FA-4 pilot droid to remain in the ball cockpit, Dooku was walking some of the stiffness of the long voyage out of his legs.»
- Ch26 L2441: «Normally measured, Dooku's pace was rushed and somewhat haphazard - - evidence to anyone who knew him that the Count was troubled.»
- Ch26 L2445: «With Dooku, Sidious hadn't had to waste precious hours on lightsaber technique, nor on ridding Dooku of ill habits born of a lifetime spent in the Jedi Temple, for Dooku had long before rid himself of those.»
- Ch26 L2445: «Instead, Sidious had focused on giving Dooku what had amounted to a crash course in tapping into the power of the dark side - - a mere taste of which had proved intoxicating.»
- Ch26 L2445: «Enough to convince Dooku that no course was left open to him but to abandon the Order; more, that his entire life had been preparation for his apprenticeship to Sidious.»
- Ch26 L2451: «The sound of soft footfalls made Dooku turn.»
- Ch26 L2451: «More than once it had occurred to Dooku that Sidious and Yoda had certain qualities in common.»
- Ch26 L2451: «On Geonosis, Yoda's easy parrying and, indeed, handling of the Sith lightning Dooku hurled at him had come as a surprise.»
- Ch26 L2451: «Yoda probably believed that he had defeated Dooku on Geonosis.»
- Ch26 L2451: «But in fact, Dooku had only fled the fight to safeguard the plans he had been carrying - - the technical readouts to what would one day become the Ultimate Weapon...»
- Ch26 L2453: «"Welcome, Darth Tyranus," Sidious said as he drew nearer.»
- Ch26 L2455: «"Lord Sidious," Dooku said, bowing slightly at the waist. "I spared no haste in leaving Kaon."»
- Ch26 L2477: «One of Dooku's previous visits had followed the assassination of a faithless Senator by Jedi Knight Quinlan Vos.»
- Ch26 L2477: «Duped by Dooku on several occasions, Vos had managed to track Dooku to The Works, though he apparently hadn't perceived just how deeply the dark side had taken root there.»
- Ch26 L2483: «Dooku had been able to count on one hand the number of times he had seen Sidious angry. Suddenly he needed two hands.»
- Ch26 L2485: «"I would hear more of this, Lord Tyranus," Sidious said with purposeful slowness. "I have since learned that these same two Jedi recently visited the Xi Char world of Charros Four."»
- Ch26 L2487: «Well ahead of Dooku, Sidious said: "The engraver of the mechno-chair..."»
- Ch26 L2501: «Sidious eyed Dooku from beneath the hood of his cloak.»
- Ch26 L2513: «Dooku exhaled with purpose. "Skywalker and Kenobi."»
- Ch26 L2519: «Without turning from the view, he added: "I am displeased by this turn of events, Lord Tyranus. Greatly displeased."»
- Ch26 L2521: «Once Master and Padawan, Kenobi and Skywalker had become the scourge of Dooku's existence.»
- Ch26 L2521: «Also as instructed, Dooku had made Kenobi aware of the existence of Darth Sidious, as a means of confusing the Jedi Order by telling them the truth.»
- Ch26 L2521: «Enraged, the young Jedi had proved a powerful opponent, and Dooku suspected that he had grown only more powerful since Geonosis.»
- Ch26 L2529: «Sidious turned to Dooku, grinning malevolently. "Their single-mindedness will deliver them into our hands, Lord Tyranus. We will set our trap for them on Naos Three."»
- Ch26 L2531: «Dooku allowed his skepticism to show. "As remote a world as can be found in known space, my lord."»
- Ch26 L2535: «Dooku decided to take it on faith.»
- Ch26 L2541: «Dooku nodded. "It is done."»
- Ch26 L2557: «Sidious gazed at him. "In good time, Lord Tyranus. All in good time."»
- Ch30 L3063: «As part of Vos's covert mission to penetrate Count Dooku's inner circle of dark side apprentices, Vos had been tasked with assassinating a duplicitous Senator, named Viento.»
- Ch30 L3063: «Immediately following the assassination - - and a brutal duel with Master K'Kruhk - - Vos had met briefly with Dooku in The Works.»
- Ch30 L3063: «There, Dooku had informed his would-be protege that Vos had been incorrect in assuming that Viento was a Sith, and had again denied that he himself answered to any Master.»
- Ch30 L3065: «Of greater interest to the Jedi and Republic Intelligence was the fact that Dooku had managed to arrive at and depart Coruscant without detection.»
- Ch30 L3123: «"Dooku's ship," Mace said.»
- Ch30 L3133: «It was Dooku! Mace thought.»
- Ch30 L3135: «Dooku - - for all intents and purposes - - to a balconied niche above the docking gate.»
- Ch30 L3135: «Following the same route, the pair returned to the docking bay and separated: Dooku to his ship; our unknown quarry, presumably to the turbolifts."»
- Ch30 L3169: «Mace walked two meters into the darkness, and ran his hand down the tunnel's tiled wall. Hundreds of Senators are now under the influence of a Sith Lord called Darth Sidious, Dooku had told Obi-Wan on Geonosis.»
- Ch31 L3275: «"You never fully described what went on between you and Count Dooku on Vjun. Was he at all inclined to return to the Order - - to the side of the Republic?"»
- Ch31 L3281: «"That may make Dooku difficult to rehabilitate."»
- Ch31 L3287: «"This Darth Sidious, as well - - should Dooku be found and killed?"»
- Ch31 L3297: «"Then what if you should find Sidious first, and kill him? Would Dooku's power increase?"»
- Ch31 L3299: «"Only Dooku's determination. Different it will be, because a Sith late he has become." Yoda shook his head. "Hard to know if Dooku a true Sith is, or simply with the power of the dark side infatuated."»
- Ch31 L3305: «"More machine than alive, Grievous is - - though more dangerous for it. But without Dooku's or Sidious's leadership, collapse the Separatists will. Bound together by the Sith they are. Mortared by the dark side of the Force."»
- Ch32 L3327: «In a forward hold of Grievous's flagship, Dooku watched the cyborg general duel with his elite MagnaGuards, three of his trophy lightsabers in constant motion, parrying thrusts of the guards' pulse-weaponed staffs, slicing the recycled air a hairbreadth from the expressionless faces of his opponents, incapacitating arm and leg servos when he could.»
- Ch32 L3327: «Grievous was a force to be reckoned with, to be sure, but Dooku deplored his habit of collecting lightsabers.»
- Ch32 L3327: «Grievous's habit struck Dooku as the worst kind of profanation.»
- Ch32 L3333: «Dooku had taught Grievous well, and Grievous had taught his elite well.»
- Ch32 L3333: «Coupled with Dooku's coaching, their programming in the seven classic forms of lightsaber dueling - - in the Jedi arts - - made them lethal opponents.»
- Ch32 L3335: «Still, it was sad that it had fallen to Dooku to help usher them out.»
- Ch32 L3339: «Grievous and his guards were dancing. Going through their programmed motions. An Ataro attack answered by Shii-Cho; Soresu answered by Lus-ma... Dooku couldn't suffer another moment of it.»
- Ch32 L3357: «Dooku withdrew his blade.»
- Ch32 L3361: «Dooku seated himself and watched them go at it.»
- Ch32 L3363: «Dooku grew troubled.»
- Ch32 L3363: «Thinking: This is where I failed poor Dooku.»
- Ch32 L3363: «Standard days earlier, Dooku had sent Sidious a coded transmission that was as much apology as explanation, and had yet to hear from him.»
- Ch32 L3365: «And Dooku.»
- Ch32 L3365: «What was Count Dooku of Serenno?»
- Ch32 L3369: «"There have been matters that warranted my close attention, Lord Tyranus. "»
- Ch32 L3377: «Dooku blinked. Was this an admission of failure on Sidious's part? Was Sidious's upper lip twitching, or was it nothing more than noise in the transmission?»
- Ch32 L3383: «"Yes, that time is drawing near, Lord Tyranus. But first we need to provide the Jedi with something that distracts them from hunting me."»
- Ch32 L3391: «Dooku was stunned.»
- Ch32 L3395: «"They think they have my scent, Lord Tyranus, and perhaps they do."»
- Ch32 L3405: «Sidious fell silent for a long moment, then said: "Perhaps, Lord Tyranus. Perhaps."»
- Ch32 L3413: «"What is thy bidding, Master?" Grievous asked from behind Dooku.»
- Ch32 L3415: «Sidious turned slightly toward Grievous, but continued to speak to Dooku.»
- Ch32 L3431: «Again Dooku was stunned. So, too, was Grievous.»
- Ch32 L3439: «"But Tythe, my lord," Dooku said with equal care. "Less a world than a corpse."»
- Ch32 L3445: «"It may prove costly to us, my lord. The Republic will almost certainly flatten it," Dooku said.»
- Ch32 L3449: «Confusion wrinkled Dooku's forehead.»
- Ch32 L3453: «"We won't have to, Lord Tyranus. Their own investigations will lead them to that conclusion. Moreover, Kenobi and Skywalker will oversee the counterattack."»
- Ch32 L3457: «"They will not pass up an opportunity to capture Count Dooku."»
- Ch32 L3459: «Dooku saw Grievous's armorplast head elevate in surprise.»
- Ch32 L3463: «"The Jedi are predictable, Lord Tyranus. I needn't tell you this. Look what they risked on Cato Neimoidia in an effort to capture Viceroy Gunray. They are obsessed with bringing their enemies to justice, instead of merely administering justice themselves."»
- Ch32 L3469: «Dooku inclined his head.»
- Ch32 L3475: «"Hold Kenobi and Skywalker, Lord Tyranus. Entertain them. Play to their weakness. Demonstrate your mastery, as you have on previous occasions."»
- Ch33 L3523: «Not surprisingly, it turns out to have been the same one where Quinlan Vos met with Dooku last year.»
- Ch33 L3523: «Once inside, Mace's team discovered more than we could have even hoped for - - evidence of a more recent visit by Dooku, and of the person he apparently went to Coruscant to see."»
- Ch33 L3527: «Even if it wasn't, it's likely that Dooku has other confederates on Coruscant, and tracking them down could eventually lead us to Sidious.»
- Ch33 L3555: «"Better: Dooku is there."»
- Ch33 L3575: «"The way I remember it, we didn't fare all that well against Dooku."»
- Ch34 L3685: «Only Sidious, Dooku, or Sidious's previous apprentice could have performed the amputations.»
- Ch34 L3685: «Shortly before Dooku had left the Jedi Order to return to his native Serenno - - during the period when he had taken the title Count and had first gone public with his discontents about the Republic - - he had been known to frequent a tavern called the Golden Cuff, which had been a watering hole for Senators, lobbyists, and aides.»
- Ch34 L3685: «Analysts at the Temple were going through files of security cam holoimages thirteen years old, hoping to find images of Dooku and anyone he may have met with repeatedly.»
- Ch34 L3685: «Thus far, no images of Dooku had surfaced in the recordings that had survived.»
- Ch34 L3685: «Even if images of Dooku's tavern mates did surface, the Jedi had no means of identifying any of them as Darth Sidious, but the images could provide an additional starting point for further investigation.»
- Ch34 L3761: «"Only because our quarry is aware that we're closing in on him. He failed to silence the ones Obi-Wan and Anakin searched out, and by now he knows that we've discovered his and Dooku's den. It's unlikely he will wait around for us to surprise him."»
- Ch34 L3765: «"Assuming there's anything left after Dooku sterilizes the place. From everything we've seen, Sidious and Dooku don't make many mistakes."»
- Ch35 L3859: «"No," Anakin said in a rush. "No. Thank you, sir, but... I'm needed on Tythe. Dooku is there."»
- Ch35 L3863: «"Yes," Palpatine was saying. "Dooku is the key to everything just now. Despite all our victories in the inner systems... Do you suspect he and General Grievous may have some secret strategy?"»
- Ch35 L3911: «"Anakin, let's get Dooku and end this."»
- Ch36 L3929: «That much the droids had determined from smudged fingerprints found on the speeder bike's steering grip and cushioned seat. The repulsorlift machine had also provided the droids with fibers, hairs, and other detritus. Slowly, a portrait of Dooku's unknown confederate was being compiled.»
- Ch37 L3951: «"Just once I wish we could repay Dooku and Grievous in kind," Anakin said over the tactical net, as Red Squadron dropped from the belly of the Integrity and rocketed toward Tythe.»
- Ch37 L3973: «A burst-transmission from survivors of the assault on the Republic's small base had confirmed Dooku's presence on the surface.»
- Ch37 L3973: «In the end it was decided that saturation bombardment, augmented by limited starfighter engagement, would send Dooku fleeing, in keeping with the Republic's strategy to force the Separatists deeper into the galaxy's spiral arms.»
- Ch37 L3975: «The Jedi had insisted nevertheless that an attempt be made to take Dooku alive. Obi-Wan and Anakin didn't need to be reminded of what had happened only weeks earlier on Cato Neimoidia when they had gone after Viceroy Gunray, but they were not about to forgo a chance to capture the Sith Lord.»
- Ch37 L3983: «"All the more reason to believe that Dooku was ordered here by Sidious."»
- Ch37 L3989: «"Dooku will know," he started to say, when the starfighter's proximity scanners stammered a warning. "Techno Union starship is veering to intercept us."»
- Ch37 L4053: «"Dooku's sloop."»
- Ch39 L4189: «In the ruined archive hall of LiMerge Power's plasma facility, Count Dooku waited for Kenobi and Skywalker to arrive.»
- Ch39 L4189: «Dooku could imagine it when it had hummed with life and activity, before the catastrophe.»
- Ch39 L4191: «Such superhuman feats were almost enough to convince Dooku that they still had the Force with them.»
- Ch39 L4197: «Dooku took a moment to set in place his compact welcoming device, then hurried through a series of decontamination chambers into the facility's control room, which overlooked the rear of the archive hall and the vast space enclosed by the containment dome itself.»
- Ch39 L4201: «"Dooku!" young Skywalker said, as if his tone of voice should suffice to send shivers down the backbones of his opponents. "Show yourself!"»
- Ch39 L4203: «Rooms distant, Dooku merely spread his hands in a gesture of greeting, and aimed his words at the holoprojector's microphone.»
- Ch39 L4211: «"We know you're here, Dooku," Kenobi said suddenly - - and with irksome audio distortion. "We can sense you."»
- Ch39 L4213: «Dooku sighed in disappointment. They weren't hearing him. Worse, the video feed was also becoming hopelessly corrupted. More through the Force than the holocam feed he saw them moving toward the very doorway he had taken to reach the control room.»
- Ch39 L4217: «Plucking his comlink from his belt, Dooku's right thumb leapt across the small touch pad. Heralded by the sound of metallic footfalls, fifty infantry droids crowded into the archive hall through two opposing doorways, perpendicular to the one through which the Jedi had entered.»
- Ch39 L4225: «Touched by their camaraderie, Dooku smiled to himself. Darth Sidious had his work cut out for him if he ever expected to turn Skywalker to the dark side.»
- Ch39 L4243: «He hadn't had a sense of that danger until he had fought Dooku on Geonosis.»
- Ch39 L4245: «Powerful enough to instruct his apprentice, Dooku, to expose him, and still remain hidden from the Jedi.»
- Ch39 L4279: «"Secret, those routes were. Known to us and few others." Yoda looked at Mace. "Unrestricted access to the archives, Dooku had. Access enough to erase all mentions of Kamino. Access enough to learn of explorations in the Deep Core."»
- Ch39 L4283: «"Dooku isn't leading this attack. Obi-Wan confirmed that he is on Tythe."»
- Ch41 L4365: «In what had served as the archive room for LiMerge Power's plasma facility, droid parts were piling up so fast and so high that Obi-Wan and Anakin could scarcely see Dooku's wavering holoimage any longer.»
- Ch41 L4365: «The decapitations and amputations were no longer as surgical as they had been when Dooku had first unleashed the droids.»
- Ch41 L4369: «But for every droid either of them destroyed, five more would appear, creating an impenetrable barrier between them and the doorway through which Dooku had certainly disappeared moments before they had arrived.»
- Ch41 L4371: «"Dooku!" Anakin snarled through clenched teeth. "I will kill you!"»
- Ch41 L4377: «In the hope that Dooku was listening from afar, he shouted: "Whatever happens here, Dooku, your Confederacy is finished!»
- Ch41 L4379: «More droids appeared. To Dooku, this was nothing more than a game, Obi-Wan told himself. But if it was a demonstration of Force ability Dooku wanted, then Anakin was still more than willing to provide it. "Dooku!" he howled.»
- Ch43 L4483: «Ordinarily he would have given thought to contingency plans, but he trusted that Lords Sidious and Tyranus would apprise him of any changes.»
- Ch43 L4511: «"Dooku," Yoda cut her off. "Shares our secrets with his confederates, he does."»
- Ch45 L4661: «Dragging himself out from under plasteel girders and chunks of ferrocrete, Count Dooku came shakily to his feet and gazed in astonished disbelief at the shambles of the control room.»
- Ch45 L4661: «Had Dooku not leapt forcefully at the last moment, he might have been buried, as the two Jedi were, somewhere below, in the expanse of rubble that covered the archive room.»
- Ch45 L4665: «On Vjun, Yoda had implied that the Jedi Temple would always be open to Dooku's return...»
- Ch45 L4667: «Was there, then, a life of retirement somewhere in the galaxy for the former Count Dooku of Serenno?»
- Ch45 L4673: «"Play it!" Dooku said as he hurried up the sloop's aft boarding ramp and into the instrument-filled main hold. A paused holoimage of the cyborg floated in blue light. Throwing off his dusty cape, Dooku paced while the FA-4 triggered the recording to replay.»
- Ch45 L4675: «"Lord Tyranus," Grievous said, in motion suddenly and genuflecting. "Supreme Chancellor Palpatine will soon be ours."»
- Ch45 L4677: «Dooku exhaled in satisfaction. "And just in time," he muttered. As if recalled to life, he positioned himself on the transmission grid and sent a simple return message: "General, I will join you shortly."»
- Ch46 L4721: «Bail continued. "Dooku, Grievous, Gunray, Palpatine."»
- Ch47 L4771: «Not everything could be traced to Dooku.»
- Ch47 L4771: «It was conceivable that Dooku had furnished Grievous with data on hyperlanes that skimmed the outer limits of the Deep Core.»
- Ch47 L4771: «That much, Dooku could have pilfered from the Jedi archives before he left the Order, presumably when he was erasing mentions of Kamino from the data banks.»
- Ch47 L4771: «Similarly, Dooku could have supplied Grievous with the orbital coordinates of specific communications satellites and mirrors, or with tactical information regarding the location of dedicated shield generators on the surface.»
- Ch47 L4773: «But Palpatine had only just been elected Supreme Chancellor when Dooku left Coruscant to return to Serenno, and back then, some thirteen years ago, Palpatine had been living in a high-rise tower close to the Senate Building. So how had Grievous known to go to 500 Republica?»
- Ch47 L4813: «He could recognize the hand of Dooku in the general's training and technique.»
- Ch48 L4849: «"Some other time. Dooku, first." Scampering over the remains of the dome, droid parts, buried pieces of furniture, overturned shelves of holodocuments, they raced for the landing platform, arriving in time to see Dooku's sloop, one among dozens of Separatist vessels, streaking for space.»
- Ch50 L4929: «At Palpatine's apartment, Grievous had managed to fool everyone by placing the gunboat on display, then clandestinely transferring himself and his combat droids into the Republic gunship Lord Tyranus had promised would be waiting for them.»
- Ch50 L4929: «Tyranus had warned him about Mace Windu's prowess with a blade, and now he understood.»
- Ch50 L4935: «While doing so would have heightened the drama of his entry, he did the next best thing. He used the code Tyranus had provided.»
- Ch50 L4979: «The stud switch and control pad for the emergency beacon were precisely where Tyranus said they would be.»
- Ch50 L4979: «After entering the code Tyranus had provided, Grievous pressed his alloy hand to the switch.»
- Ch52 L5045: «Dooku hadn't fled alone. The only indications of Tythe's invasion were the hulking remains of Separatist and Republic warships, tumbling indolently in starlight.»
- Ch52 L5073: «Obi-Wan folded his arms across his chest. "You wouldn't be saying that if we'd captured Dooku."»
- Ch54 L5167: «Was he under the delusion that Lords Sidious and Tyranus would simply hold him for ransom?»
- Ch54 L5199: «"Very good." For no apparent reason, Grievous felt uncertain. "Lord Tyranus will soon be here to take charge of you."»
- Ch55 L5249: «Dooku had ordered the droid pilot of the sloop to revert from hyperspace for a brief time at the planet Nelvaan.»
- Ch55 L5251: «And Palpatine was hardly the prize he appeared to be. The greater prize, Sidious had told Dooku during their most recent communication, was Anakin Skywalker.»
- Ch55 L5253: «"Long have you watched him," Dooku had said, repeating words Sidious himself had spoken. "Longer than you know, Lord Tyranus. Longer than you know. And the time has come to test him again."»
- Ch55 L5261: «But above all you must make the contest appear real, Lord Tyranus."»
- Ch55 L5263: «"I will treat it as if it were my crowning achievement," Dooku had promised.»

### B.3 Sidious / Palpatine: 447 entries. Aliases: `Sidious|Palpatine|Palpatinc|Supreme Chancellor|the Chancellor|Dark Lord`. Version: Sidious, 19 BBY (Palpatine lines from non-knowing POVs are 'as Palpatine')

- Ch1 L101: «"Think what you will." Obi-Wan narrowed his eyes with purpose. "Oh, I see. But I would have thought Senator Amidala of greater interest to you than Supreme Chancellor Palpatine." Before Anakin could respond, he added: "Despite that she's a politician also."»
- Ch1 L105: «Obi-Wan regarded Anakin for a moment. "What's more, if Chancellor Palpatine had genuine concern for your welfare, he would have kept you closer to Coruscant."»
- Ch4 L239: «Despite the Senate's attempts to bind the hands of Supreme Chancellor Palpatine, so that corrupt politicians could continue to turn a profit.»
- Ch5 L269: «His faith in Anakin had grown so strong that he had become Anakin's staunchest defender to those on the Council who had grown apprehensive about the young man's prowess, and uncomfortable with his confidential, almost familial relationship with Supreme Chancellor Palpatine.»
- Ch5 L271: «If Obi-Wan was, as Anakin sometimes said, the father he never had, then Palpatine was his wise uncle, adviser, mentor in the ways of life outside the Temple.»
- Ch5 L271: «But how could he not, having been all but anointed "the Chosen One," continually bolstered by Palpatine's praise, driven to prove to his former Master that he could be the perfect Jedi Knight.»
- Ch8 L415: «The Sith Lord, Darth Sidious.»
- Ch8 L415: «But not once during that time of public disgrace did Gunray mention the role Sidious had played.»
- Ch8 L417: «But also because he had sensed that Sidious had not abandoned him completely.»
- Ch8 L417: «Rather, the Dark Lord was somehow seeing to it that the trials never came to fruition, that no lasting verdicts were rendered or punishments handed down.»
- Ch8 L419: «In a moment of weakness, Gunray had revealed the truth about his dealings with Darth Sidious.»
- Ch8 L421: «In much the same way that the Trade Federation had amassed an army right under the nose of Supreme Chancellor Finis Valorum, Dooku - - in plain sight - - was seeing to it that Baktoid Armor Workshops was supplying weapons to any corporations that agreed to ally with him.»
- Ch8 L425: «The war was begun, and it was Dooku's turn for revelations: he, too, was Sith, and his Master was none other than Sidious!»
- Ch8 L425: «For a time it appeared that Sidious and Dooku's dreams of toppling the Republic might succeed after all.»
- Ch8 L425: «But they found themselves facing a worthy opponent in the person of Supreme Chancellor Palpatine - - also from Naboo - - who had never much impressed Gunray, but who had managed through a combination of charm and artfulness not only to remain in power long past his term of office, but also, in conjunction with the Jedi, to conduct the war.»
- Ch8 L451: «"Sidious," Haako said softly.»
- Ch8 L453: «"Not Sidious!"»
- Ch9 L573: «And to the meter-high figure in the hooded cloak, the unmistakable voice of Viceroy Nute Gunray was saying: "Yes, yes, of course. Trust that I will see to it personally, my Lord Sidious."»
- Ch10 L577: «These days, an appointment with Supreme Chancellor Palpatine was not something to be taken lightly - - even for a member of the so-called Loyalist Committee.»
- Ch10 L599: «Rarely a mention of the Jedi, save for when one of them was commended by Palpatine in the Senate's Great Rotunda.»
- Ch10 L599: «To the extent that friendships could be forged with them, Bail had come to know a few: Jedi Masters Obi-Wan Kenobi, Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin - - the privileged few who also were allowed to meet personally with Palpatine.»
- Ch10 L601: «Even Palpatine's harshest critics in the Senate or in the various media couldn't hold him fully accountable for what Coruscant had become.»
- Ch10 L601: «Though hardly the innocent he sometimes pretended to be, Palpatine was not to blame.»
- Ch10 L603: «Even in those days Palpatine had had his share of influential friends.»
- Ch10 L603: «Still, Bail couldn't help but wonder who might have succeeded Palpatine as Supreme Chancellor if the Separatist crises on Raxus Prime and Antar 4 had not occurred when they did, just as Palpatine's term of office was ending.»
- Ch10 L611: «As a consequence - - and by an overwhelming majority - - the Senate had voted to amend the Constitution, and to extend Palpatine's term indefinitely, with the understanding that he would voluntarily step down from office when the crisis was resolved.»
- Ch10 L611: «Formerly gracious and unassuming Palpatine was suddenly democracy's champion, vowing that he could not condone a Republic divided against itself.»
- Ch10 L613: «But Palpatine himself had refused to come out in favor of building an army for the Republic.»
- Ch10 L613: «Bail could recall clearly the day he had stood with Palpatine, Mas Amedda, Malastarian Senators, and others on a balcony of the Senate Office Building, watching tens of thousands of clone troopers march into the enormous ships that would take the war to the Separatists.»
- Ch10 L615: «Regardless, Bail had put his feelings aside and had played his part, endorsing bills he might have previously denounced, supporting Palpatine's "efficient streamlining of cumbersome bureaucracy.»
- Ch10 L615: «The sudden disappearance of Senator Seti Ashgad after he had argued against installation of surveillance cams in the Senate Building; the suspicious explosion of a star freighter aboard which Finis Valorum was a passenger; the passage of a security bill that granted Palpatine wide-ranging powers over Coruscant...»
- Ch10 L617: «The behavior of the Supreme Chancellor himself - - frequently isolated by his covey of advisers and illegal cadre of red-robed personal bodyguards; his unbending resolve to continue fighting until the war was won.»
- Ch10 L617: «Gone was humble, self-deprecating Palpatine.»
- Ch10 L621: «"A momentous occasion, Bail," she said into his left ear. "An audience with Palpatine."»
- Ch10 L625: «"An extraordinary world, Naboo," Bail said. "I'll never understand how it spawned someone as stubborn as our Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch10 L631: «"You underestimate Palpatine's acuity," Padme said. "Besides, he appreciates frank speech."»
- Ch10 L641: «They were an animated group when they finally arrived at the holding office, directly beneath the Great Rotunda, where Palpatine's human appointments secretary asked them to wait in the receiving area.»
- Ch10 L641: «But then the door to Palpatine's office slid open, and Sate Pestage, one of Palpatine's chief advisers, appeared.»
- Ch10 L653: «Pestage spread his hands in a patronizing way. "Such times require sacrifices, Senator. Or perhaps you feel you've risked more than the Supreme Chancellor has."»
- Ch10 L655: «Bail spoke up. "No one is implying that the Supreme Chancellor has been anything but tireless in his... devotion. But the fact remains that he agreed to see us, and we're not about to leave here until he honors his pledge."»
- Ch11 L667: «The technicians had succeeded in inducing the mechno-chair's holoprojector to replay the image of Lord Sidious, and Republic cryptographers working with the Jedi were confident that the unique device would yield even greater secrets once it was relocated to Coruscant and examined thoroughly.»
- Ch11 L671: «Obi-Wan wasn't sure if Yoda was pondering the image of Sidious, or the fact that two Jedi had been killed during the fighting on Cato Neimoidia.»
- Ch11 L675: «The name Sidious had come up only once since the war began - - on Geonosis, when Dooku had told an imprisoned Obi-Wan that a Sith Lord by that name had hundreds of Republic Senators under his influence.»
- Ch11 L675: «And yet, even after Dooku had revealed himself to be Sith-trained, Yoda and others on the Council continued to believe that he had been lying about Sidious.»
- Ch11 L675: «Two Council members were convinced that Dooku was the Dark Lord, having somehow tutored himself - - by Sith Holocron, perhaps - - in the use of dark side powers.»
- Ch11 L675: «Now that Sidious appeared to be real, Obi-Wan didn't know what to think.»
- Ch11 L681: «"Master Yoda," Obi-Wan said, "is it possible that Dooku wasn't lying about the Senate being under the control of Sidious?"»
- Ch11 L683: «"If in control of the Senate Sidious was, would not defeated the Republic already be?»
- Ch11 L683: «Had he not, searched we would have for Sidious, leaving Dooku to escalate his war.»
- Ch11 L689: «"Could he have been trained by this... Sidious?"»
- Ch11 L691: «"Stands to reason, it does. Accepted by Sidious he was, following the death of the one you killed."»
- Ch11 L719: «"But how could Dooku have searched out Sidious? Or was it the other way around?"»
- Ch11 L721: «"Impossible to know. But accept Sidious as a mentor Dooku did."»
- Ch11 L725: «"Also impossible to know. Believed he might have, that Sidious Dooku would hunt down. To destroy."»
- Ch11 L779: «"When I was being held captive on Geonosis, Dooku told me that the Trade Federation had been allied with Sidious during the blockade of Naboo, but that they had later been betrayed by him.»
- Ch11 L809: «"Years matter not. Busy fighting a war, you have been. Mentoring your headstrong Padawan. In pursuit of Dooku and his minions... Darker, events became. Attempting to turn this war to their own uses, Dooku and Sidious are."»
- Ch11 L813: «And to justice all those Sidious to the dark side has turned.»
- Ch11 L813: «"Uncover Sidious's tracks, you must.»
- Ch12 L817: «Just as Obi-Wan had said, they resented the fact that Anakin had tampered with the chair, despite the fact that, had he not, the chair would have blown itself to pieces, taking with it the holoimage of Sidious and whatever other communications memories it might contain.»
- Ch12 L847: «"General Skywalker!" a trooper called from behind him. Anakin turned, saw the trooper motioning to the shuttle. "Hyperwave commo for you - - from the office of the Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch12 L849: «Above a holoprojector plate in the ship's comm center, a flickering image of Supreme Chancellor Palpatine was resolving.»
- Ch12 L849: «When Anakin had positioned himself on the transmission grate, Palpatine smiled.»
- Ch12 L855: «Palpatine's smile faltered. "Yes, I was informed as much."»
- Ch12 L857: «It wasn't the first time Anakin had heard from Palpatine in the field. At Jabiim, Palpatine had ordered Anakin to retreat before the planet fell to the Separatists; at Praesitlyn he had praised Anakin for having saved the day. Still, the communications were often as awkward as they were flattering.»
- Ch12 L859: «"What's wrong, my boy?" Palpatine asked. "I sense that you're troubled about something. If it involves Gunray, accept my word that he won't be able to hide from us forever. None of them will. One day you'll have your chance for complete victory."»
- Ch12 L865: «"That's a mistake," Palpatine said gently. "Anger is natural, Anakin. I thought we'd been through all this - - regarding what took place on Tatooine?"»
- Ch12 L869: «Not that I'm suggesting that the Jedi are," Palpatine was quick to add.»
- Ch12 L873: «"Do you think I'm immune to anger?" Palpatine said into the short silence. "I've never seen you angry."»
- Ch12 L891: «Palpatine smiled.»
- Ch12 L895: «"Anakin, I will somehow see to it that you return from the Outer Rim soon," Palpatine said.»
- Ch13 L903: «He was preparing to vent his exasperation on Palpatine's appointments secretary when the door to the Supreme Chancellor's office opened once again, and his advisers began to file out between the imposing, red-cowled guards that flanked the opening.»
- Ch13 L909: «"Good to know, since the Supreme Chancellor still has much to attend to." Just then Palpatine himself appeared, glancing at Bail and the others, then at Pestage. "Senator Organa, Senator Amidala - - all of you. What a delight to find you here."»
- Ch13 L911: «"Supreme Chancellor," Bail said, "we were under the impression we had an appointment with you."»
- Ch13 L913: «Palpatine lifted an eyebrow. "Indeed? Why wasn't I informed of this?" he asked Pestage.»
- Ch13 L917: «Palpatine frowned.»
- Ch13 L917: «Bail took a seat directly across from Palpatine's high-backed chair, which was said to house some sort of shield generator - - necessary for his protection, as were the guards, though something that would have been unheard of three years earlier.»
- Ch13 L919: «Saturated in red, the windowless, carpeted office contained several singular pieces of statuary, as did Palpatine's chambers in the Senate Office Building, and his suite in the crown of 500 Republica. Rumored to work for days on end without sleeping, Palpatine seemed alert, curious, somewhat imperious.»
- Ch13 L923: «"We'll come directly to the point, Supreme Chancellor," Bail said. "Now that the Confederacy has been chased from the Core and Inner Rim, we wish to discuss the abrogation of some of the measures that were enacted in the name of public safety."»
- Ch13 L925: «Palpatine gazed at Bail over steepled fingers. "Our recent victories have made you feel so secure?"»
- Ch13 L927: «"They have, Supreme Chancellor," Padme said.»
- Ch13 L931: «"I see," Palpatine said slowly.»
- Ch13 L937: «Palpatine shook his head.»
- Ch13 L945: «Palpatine stood up and paced away from his huge chair, turning his back to everyone.»
- Ch13 L949: «"I serve the Republic, Senator Organa," Palpatine said, cutting him off. "Introduce a measure to repeal in the Senate. I will accept whatever outcome ensues from a vote."»
- Ch13 L957: «"I view the Constitution as a living document," Palpatine interrupted. "As such, it must be allowed to expand and contract according to circumstances. Otherwise, what do we have but stasis."»
- Ch13 L961: «Palpatine grinned faintly. "Of course."»
- Ch13 L965: «"Just as I knew we would." Palpatine beamed at her. "Senator Amidala, is that not the droid Jedi Skywalker constructed?"»
- Ch13 L973: «Palpatine returned an abrupt laugh. "A title more fit for a king or emperor." He glanced at Padme. "In fact, I have just spoken with him, Your Highness."»
- Ch13 L977: «Palpatine held her gaze. "Why Senator Amidala, I do believe you're blushing."»
- Ch14 L983: «"Jedi Council business?" Anakin asked when Obi-Wan joined him. "Nothing of the sort. Yoda believes that the mechno-chair may yield clues to the whereabouts of Darth Sidious. He wants us to take up the search."»
- Ch14 L985: «Anakin didn't respond immediately. "Master, aren't we obligated to notify the Supreme Chancellor of our find?"»
- Ch14 L1011: «"Dooku is a Sith, Anakin. He may have had his good reasons for leaving the Order, but he is nothing now but a master of deceit. He and Sidious prey on the weak-willed. They deceive themselves into believing that they are infallible."»
- Ch14 L1013: «"But I've seen instances where the Jedi lie to one another. Master Kolar lied about Quinlan Vos going to the dark side. We're lying now, by not sharing our information about Sidious with Chancellor Palpatine. What would Sidious or Dooku have to say about our lies?"»
- Ch14 L1023: «"While we still don't know whether the mechno-chair was left behind deliberately, as some kind of trap, the image of Sidious is authentic.»
- Ch14 L1031: «"For the time being we're proceeding on the assumption that Dooku - - or Sidious, for that matter - - might have developed the chips, and had them installed in transceivers awarded to Gunray and other key members of the Council of Separatists."»
- Ch14 L1041: «Obi-Wan stroked his chin in thought. "If Gunray has had the chair for fourteen years, then he could have been using it to contact Sidious during the Naboo crisis. If we could learn who manufactured the chair..."»
- Ch14 L1051: «The brief meeting with Palpatine had left her flustered - - but for all the wrong reasons.»
- Ch14 L1051: «And yet, despite her best intentions, Palpatine had brought Anakin to the fore.»
- Ch14 L1051: «Had the Supreme Chancellor learned of their secret ceremony on Naboo, from Anakin or others?»
- Ch15 L1117: «"The Republic fools itself believing that they have you on the run, but, in fact, Lord Sidious and Darth Tyranus have engineered this, for reasons that will be made clear soon enough.»
- Ch16 L1145: «Aayla Secura and the Caamasi Jedi Ylenic It'kla had taken a Techno Union defector into custody on Corellia; Quinlan Vos had gone undercover to infiltrate Dooku's circle of dark side apprentices... And Supreme Chancellor Palpatine hadn't been told - - or learned since - - about any of the covert operations.»
- Ch17 L1321: «"Reach the ears of the Supreme Chancellor, it won't," Yoda said. "But surprised I was by Obi-Wan's report. Losing some of his better judgment, Obi-Wan is."»
- Ch17 L1327: «Dyne grinned. "We've succeeded in deciphering the code Dooku - - and, we have to assume, Sidious - - has been using to communicate with the Council of Separatists. Using the code, we were able to intercept a message sent to Viceroy Gunray, through the mechno-chair."»
- Ch17 L1345: «"About his image, Sidious cares, ummm?" Mace's clean-shaven upper lip curled. "What was the source of the transmission?"»
- Ch17 L1351: «Mace nodded. "And Belderone next." Again his gaze fell on Dyne. "Anything further on the source of the original Sidious transmission?"»
- Ch17 L1361: «Dyne nodded. "You're right, of course. If no actions are taken, and word of this intelligence were to leak..." He regarded Yoda. "Do we inform the Supreme Chancellor?"»
- Ch17 L1369: «"Obi-Wan and Anakin aren't far from Belderone," Mace said. "But they're pursuing another lead to Sidious's whereabouts."»
- Ch19 L1515: «His Sith Masters had ordered him to safeguard the lives of the Council members - - though, in fact, the Confederacy needed none other than Lords Sidious and Tyranus.»
- Ch20 L1561: «Yoda fell silent for a moment, then said: "Speak with the Supreme Chancellor, we must."»
- Ch20 L1565: «"With the war's end, it will." Yoda turned slightly to regard Mace. "A terrible warning, Belderone is. Increasing, the power of the dark side is. Rooted out, Sidious must be."»
- Ch21 L1631: «"What are you talking about, you... abomination. When Lord Sidious hears that you promised us a world you could not deliver - - "»
- Ch21 L1635: «"Lord Sidious's alloy puppet," Gunray said, his voice quavering. "If not for the Trade Federation, you would have no army to command."»
- Ch22 L1661: «That was originally what had brought him to the attention of Sidious.»
- Ch22 L1667: «Sidious and Dooku couldn't have been more pleased with the result.»
- Ch22 L1673: «The essence of the dark side lay in a willingness to use any means possible to arrive at a desired end - - which, in the case of Lord Sidious, meant a galaxy brought under the dominion of a single, brilliant mind.»
- Ch22 L1677: «For two hundred years before the coming of Darth Sidious the power of the dark side had been gaining strength, and yet the Jedi had made only minimal efforts to thwart it.»
- Ch22 L1689: «His discontent had continued to grow and fester; his frustration with the Republic Senate, with ineffectual Supreme Chancellor Valorum, with the shortsightedness of the Jedi Council members themselves.»
- Ch22 L1697: «As it happened, there had been no need to hunt for Sidious, for it was Sidious who had approached him.»
- Ch22 L1699: «Sidious's boldness surprised him at first, but it hadn't taken long for Dooku to become fascinated by the Sith.»
- Ch22 L1703: «Dooku was an eager student, and yet Sidious had continued to hold him at arm's length.»
- Ch22 L1703: «Sidious had recognized in Dooku the makings of a true accomplice - - an equal from the other camp, already trained in the Jedi arts, a master duelist, a political visionary.»
- Ch22 L1703: «One of your former confidants at the Jedi Temple has perceived the coming change, Sidious had told him.»
- Ch22 L1705: «And so with the murder of Sifo-Dyas, Dooku had embraced the dark side fully, and Sidious had conferred on him the title Darth Tyranus. His final act before leaving the Jedi Order was to erase all mentions of Kamino from the Jedi archives.»
- Ch22 L1709: «Ten years passed. Under its new Supreme Chancellor, the Republic recovered somewhat, then grew more corrupt and beset with problems than before. As best they could, Sidious and Tyranus helped things along.»
- Ch22 L1711: «Sidious had the ability to see deep into the future, but there was always the unexpected. With the power of the dark side, however, came flexibility.»
- Ch22 L1713: «But when he had informed Sidious of Obi-Wan's presence, Sidious had only said, Allow events to play out, Darth Tyranus.»
- Ch22 L1715: «And now, a new wrinkle: as a result of Nute Gunray's blunder at Cato Neimoidia, the Republic and the Jedi had chanced on a possible way to trace the whereabouts of Sidious and expose him.»
- Ch22 L1715: «The mechno-chair's exceptional transceiver - - and others like it - - had been created for Sidious by a host of beings, a few of whom were still alive.»
- Ch22 L1715: «And if agents of the Republic - - or the Jedi, for that matter - - were clever and persistent enough, they could succeed in learning more about Sidious than he would want anyone to learn...»
- Ch22 L1717: «He had to be informed, Dooku thought. Or did he? For a heartbeat he hesitated, imagining the power that could be his. Then he went directly to the hyperwave transmitter Sidious had given him, and began his transmission.»
- Ch23 L1721: «Mace Windu couldn't recall a visit to the Supreme Chancellor's chambers in the Senate Office Building when his attention hadn't been drawn to Palpatine's curious and somehow unsettling collection of quasi-religious statuary.»
- Ch23 L1721: «On one occasion, picking up on Mace's interest, Palpatine had offered lengthy and enthusiastic accounts of when and how he had come by some of the pieces.»
- Ch23 L1723: «Just now Mace's eyes were on a small bronzium statue Palpatine had once identified as Wapoe, the mythical artisan demigod of disguise.»
- Ch23 L1725: «"I'm relieved that you contacted me, Master Jedi," the Supreme Chancellor was saying from the far side of his expansive desk. "As I was about to contact you on a matter of some gravity."»
- Ch23 L1731: «Palpatine touched his steepled fingers to his lower lip, then inhaled and sat back in his throne of a chair.»
- Ch23 L1737: «Palpatine smiled without showing his teeth.»
- Ch23 L1743: «"A Jedi operation," Palpatine said slowly. "And by all accounts you, that is, the Jedi, were successful in routing General Grievous's forces."»
- Ch23 L1751: «Palpatine regarded him.»
- Ch23 L1759: «He looked askance at Palpatine.»
- Ch23 L1765: «Palpatine spread his hands in a gesture of appeasement.»
- Ch23 L1775: «Palpatine smiled tolerantly.»
- Ch23 L1785: «Palpatine took the rebuke in stride.»
- Ch23 L1795: «Palpatine was staring at him in disbelief.»
- Ch23 L1801: «Palpatine considered it, then frowned.»
- Ch23 L1811: «Palpatine's face took on sudden color.»
- Ch23 L1815: «"A decade old, such reproaches are, Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch23 L1823: «Now Palpatine waited.»
- Ch23 L1825: «"It contained a stored message - - a message transmitted to Viceroy Gunray from Darth Sidious."»
- Ch23 L1827: «Palpatine's broad forehead wrinkled in uncertainty.»
- Ch23 L1829: «"Sidious. I know the name..."»
- Ch23 L1831: «"Dooku's Sith Master, Sidious is. Learned of him on Geonosis, Master Kenobi did. But eluded us, proof of him has."»
- Ch23 L1833: «"Now I recall," Palpatine said. "Obi-Wan was told that this Sidious had somehow infiltrated the Senate."»
- Ch23 L1835: «"Dismissed that, we have. But lying about Sidious, Dooku wasn't."»
- Ch23 L1837: «Palpatine swiveled his chair toward the room's immense curved window, the panorama of Coruscant.»
- Ch23 L1845: «Again, Palpatine paused to consider Yoda's words.»
- Ch23 L1847: «"I think I begin to understand the reasons for your secrecy. The Jedi are attempting to expose Sidious."»
- Ch23 L1851: «"Might the capture of Sidious end the war?"»
- Ch23 L1855: «Palpatine nodded in finality.»
- Ch23 L1857: «"Then I trust that you will accept my apologies. Do whatever you must to hunt Sidious down."»
- Ch26 L2437: «Strong in the dark side - - made so by Darth Sidious - - the building had been the place of Dooku's apprenticeship, just as it had served as a training ground for Darth Maul before Dooku, and who knew who or how many other Sith disciples before Maul.»
- Ch26 L2437: «During the ten years preceding the outbreak of the war - - when Count Dooku of Serenno was believed to have been peddling his Separatist agenda to disenfranchised worlds in the Mid and Outer Rims - - he had, in fact, spent long periods of time in The Works, coming and going at will, or as required of him by Darth Sidious.»
- Ch26 L2439: «Its signature sail, however, had been obtained with Sidious's help from a dealer in pre-Republic antiquities in the Gree Enclave.»
- Ch26 L2441: «Even so, in moments when he could put aside the reasons for his visit, he surveyed the docking bay with a certain fondness, recalling the years he had spent under Sidious's tutelage, learning the ways of the Sith, practicing the dark arts, perfecting himself.»
- Ch26 L2445: «With Dooku, Sidious hadn't had to waste precious hours on lightsaber technique, nor on ridding Dooku of ill habits born of a lifetime spent in the Jedi Temple, for Dooku had long before rid himself of those.»
- Ch26 L2445: «Instead, Sidious had focused on giving Dooku what had amounted to a crash course in tapping into the power of the dark side - - a mere taste of which had proved intoxicating.»
- Ch26 L2445: «Enough to convince Dooku that no course was left open to him but to abandon the Order; more, that his entire life had been preparation for his apprenticeship to Sidious.»
- Ch26 L2451: «Of his youth, Sidious had offered little these past thirteen years; of his Master, Darth Plagueis, even less.»
- Ch26 L2451: «More than once it had occurred to Dooku that Sidious and Yoda had certain qualities in common.»
- Ch26 L2453: «"Welcome, Darth Tyranus," Sidious said as he drew nearer.»
- Ch26 L2455: «"Lord Sidious," Dooku said, bowing slightly at the waist. "I spared no haste in leaving Kaon."»
- Ch26 L2459: «Whether by nature or design, Sidious's words came slowly, sibilantly.»
- Ch26 L2471: «"And still you have come here," Sidious said.»
- Ch26 L2475: «Sidious nodded. "Then let us speak of these things in real time."»
- Ch26 L2479: «"I suspect that the planned disappearance of Thal K'sar did not go according to plan," Sidious said finally.»
- Ch26 L2483: «Dooku had been able to count on one hand the number of times he had seen Sidious angry. Suddenly he needed two hands.»
- Ch26 L2485: «"I would hear more of this, Lord Tyranus," Sidious said with purposeful slowness. "I have since learned that these same two Jedi recently visited the Xi Char world of Charros Four."»
- Ch26 L2487: «Well ahead of Dooku, Sidious said: "The engraver of the mechno-chair..."»
- Ch26 L2491: «Sidious pondered it for a moment.»
- Ch26 L2497: «"And what if they should?" Sidious snapped. "Do you think that would bring an end to what I have set in motion?"»
- Ch26 L2501: «Sidious eyed Dooku from beneath the hood of his cloak.»
- Ch26 L2515: «Sidious took a long moment to respond.»
- Ch26 L2521: «On Geonosis he had deliberately allowed them to pursue him - - just as Sidious had instructed him to do.»
- Ch26 L2521: «Also as instructed, Dooku had made Kenobi aware of the existence of Darth Sidious, as a means of confusing the Jedi Order by telling them the truth.»
- Ch26 L2521: «Long have I watched young Skywalker, Sidious had once admitted.»
- Ch26 L2525: «Sidious made a gesture accepting that defeat.»
- Ch26 L2529: «Sidious turned to Dooku, grinning malevolently. "Their single-mindedness will deliver them into our hands, Lord Tyranus. We will set our trap for them on Naos Three."»
- Ch26 L2545: «Sidious sneered the name.»
- Ch26 L2549: «Sidious shook his head. "But Skywalker does. And Kenobi... Kenobi has been as a father to him. Orphan Skywalker once and for all, and he will shift. "»
- Ch26 L2557: «Sidious gazed at him. "In good time, Lord Tyranus. All in good time."»
- Ch27 L2561: «Having suffered through all four hours of Palpatine's State of the Republic address to the Senate, interrupted dozens of times by standing ovations - - an archaic tradition not practiced since the era of Supreme Chancellor Valorum Eixes - - Bail Organa watched from the backseat of the air taxi as a trio of assault cruisers lifted off into Coruscant's flame-orange sky, casting their wedge-shaped shadows on the spired roof of the Jedi Temple.»
- Ch27 L2567: «A holorecording of Palpatine's speech was running in the center of the room when Bail was admitted. Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
- Ch27 L2569: «"And so it is with a heavy heart that I commit two hundred thousand additional troopers to the Outer Rim sieges," the holoimage of the Supreme Chancellor was saying, "though in full confidence that the end of this brutal conflict is now in sight.»
- Ch27 L2569: «Droid cams buzzed around the Great Rotunda to highlight the more well-known of the Palpatine-friendly factions then, coming full circle, closed on Palpatine's thirty-meter-tall podium to linger on the two dozen human naval officers who were standing just below the summit, clapping enthusiastically.»
- Ch27 L2573: «Dressed in robes of magenta and forest green, Palpatine continued.»
- Ch27 L2603: «Mace leaned forward in his chair. "What can Palpatine be thinking - - committing half of Coruscant's home force to the Outer Rim sieges?"»
- Ch27 L2605: «"Emboldened Palpatine is, by what we achieved at Belderone."»
- Ch27 L2607: «"The Supreme Chancellor singles out Mygeeto, Saleucami, and Felucia," Plo Koon said from beneath the mask that supplied him with life gases.»
- Ch27 L2619: «"Some of us are concerned that the Supreme Chancellor has been persuaded to press these sieges as a means of acquiring worlds by force. There are bills before the Senate now that could grant him the authority to overrule local system governments."»
- Ch28 L2669: «It had long been assumed that the tattooed Zabrak Sith had performed the modifications, but information supplied by K'sar suggested that Raith Sienar's Advanced Projects Laboratory had been responsible not only for building the ship, but also for implementing Darth Sidious's designs.»
- Ch28 L2671: «Obi-Wan and Anakin might have gone directly to the source - - to Raith Sienar - - had Supreme Chancellor Palpatine not vetoed the idea.»
- Ch30 L3055: «Fortunately - - and admittedly unexpectedly - - Supreme Chancellor Palpatine had overruled the committee, and Mace had been allowed to compile a dream team that included not only ARC commander Valiant and Captain Dyne of Republic Intelligence, but also Jedi Master Shaak Ti and several capable Padawans.»
- Ch30 L3169: «Mace walked two meters into the darkness, and ran his hand down the tunnel's tiled wall. Hundreds of Senators are now under the influence of a Sith Lord called Darth Sidious, Dooku had told Obi-Wan on Geonosis.»
- Ch31 L3175: «In the Supreme Chancellor's Senate Office Building chambers, Yoda sat staring across the desk at Palpatine, silhouetted against the long window that overlooked western Coruscant.»
- Ch31 L3177: «How many Supreme Chancellors had he sat with in this office and others like it? he asked himself.»
- Ch31 L3177: «With Palpatine, the Force was not placed last.»
- Ch31 L3181: «"Exhort me, you need not, Supreme Chancellor," Yoda said.»
- Ch31 L3183: «Palpatine smiled dryly.»
- Ch31 L3193: «Palpatine's frown contained a hint of warning, of malice.»
- Ch31 L3203: «Palpatine paused to consider the remark, then leveled a hard gaze.»
- Ch31 L3211: «Palpatine sat upright, as if struck.»
- Ch31 L3217: «Appeased, Palpatine said: "Then perhaps the clones can be trained to respond to others, as well as they respond to the Jedi."»
- Ch31 L3231: «Palpatine interlocked his fingers.»
- Ch31 L3243: «Palpatine spread his hands.»
- Ch31 L3247: «"Reconsidered we have, Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch31 L3253: «Palpatine forced a fatigued exhale.»
- Ch31 L3261: «Yoda wagged his forefinger at Palpatine.»
- Ch31 L3265: «"These Sith to whom you keep referring." Palpatine fell silent in thought, then said: "When you were believed killed at Ithor, Master Windu said as much to me."»
- Ch31 L3269: «Palpatine regarded him.»
- Ch31 L3273: «"When need be, Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch31 L3287: «"This Darth Sidious, as well - - should Dooku be found and killed?"»
- Ch31 L3291: «"Difficult to say. Deprived of an apprentice, Sidious may withdraw - - to preserve the Sith."»
- Ch31 L3297: «"Then what if you should find Sidious first, and kill him? Would Dooku's power increase?"»
- Ch31 L3305: «"More machine than alive, Grievous is - - though more dangerous for it. But without Dooku's or Sidious's leadership, collapse the Separatists will. Bound together by the Sith they are. Mortared by the dark side of the Force."»
- Ch31 L3307: «Palpatine leaned forward with interest.»
- Ch31 L3315: «"Relieved, I am, Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch31 L3317: «Palpatine reclined in his chair.»
- Ch31 L3319: «"Tell me, how goes the hunt for Darth Sidious?"»
- Ch32 L3361: «Sidious had once confessed that he had erred similarly in his training of Darth Maul.»
- Ch32 L3363: «Was Sidious thinking the same of him now?»
- Ch32 L3363: «Standard days earlier, Dooku had sent Sidious a coded transmission that was as much apology as explanation, and had yet to hear from him.»
- Ch32 L3365: «He glanced at the hold's holoprojector table a moment before a blue holoimage of Sidious appeared above it.»
- Ch32 L3373: «Sidious was dismissive.»
- Ch32 L3377: «Dooku blinked. Was this an admission of failure on Sidious's part? Was Sidious's upper lip twitching, or was it nothing more than noise in the transmission?»
- Ch32 L3379: «"The Force is strong in Skywalker," Sidious went on.»
- Ch32 L3385: «Sidious's upper lip was definitely twitching. Was this worry? Worry from someone fond of saying that things were going precisely as planned?»
- Ch32 L3389: «"The Twi'lek's information led them to our rendezvous on Coruscant," Sidious said in a scurrilous voice.»
- Ch32 L3399: «From parsecs distant, Sidious stared at him.»
- Ch32 L3405: «Sidious fell silent for a long moment, then said: "Perhaps, Lord Tyranus. Perhaps."»
- Ch32 L3409: «Sidious shook his head.»
- Ch32 L3415: «Sidious turned slightly toward Grievous, but continued to speak to Dooku.»
- Ch32 L3421: «"The Republic is monitoring you?" Sidious asked the general.»
- Ch32 L3435: «Sidious adopted a faint grin.»
- Ch32 L3473: «Sidious grinned once more.»
- Ch32 L3481: «"No, General," Sidious cut in. "I have something else in mind for you and the rest of the fleet. But tell me, can you tuck your charges somewhere safe for the time being?"»
- Ch32 L3483: «"The planet Utapau comes to mind, Lord Sidious."»
- Ch32 L3493: «"Regarding Coruscant, yes." Sidious paused, then said: "We must accelerate those plans. Prepare, General, for what will be your finest hour."»
- Ch33 L3525: «"Sidious?"»
- Ch33 L3527: «Even if it wasn't, it's likely that Dooku has other confederates on Coruscant, and tracking them down could eventually lead us to Sidious.»
- Ch33 L3527: «Intelligence discovered that the building belonged to a corporation called LiMerge Power, which was believed to have been involved in the manufacture and distribution of prohibited weapons during Finis Valorum's term as Supreme Chancellor.»
- Ch33 L3531: «"Why not? At Naboo, the Trade Federation was in league with Sidious. The entire Confederacy is in league with him now."»
- Ch33 L3545: «"Another attempt by Sidious to erase the trail we've been following?"»
- Ch33 L3549: «"The Council was able to convince Palpatine of the need to retake Tythe, and he has authorized a full battle group to divert there. It seems he is finally willing to follow Master Yoda's advice about concentrating on dismantling the Confederacy leadership."»
- Ch33 L3565: «"The search for Sidious began with us. We discovered the first clues. If he's thought to be on Coruscant, then we're the ones who should be there to capture him."»
- Ch33 L3567: «"Anakin, Mace and Shaak Ti are more than capable of seeing to that - - if Sidious is even there."»
- Ch33 L3571: «"Not as easily as... we could. Sidious is a Sith Lord!"»
- Ch33 L3579: «"Anakin, is this really about capturing Sidious?"»
- Ch33 L3583: «"Honor? Since when did this war become a contest for first place? If you're thinking that the capture of Sidious will earn you a place on the Council - - "»
- Ch33 L3619: «"Then your protests have nothing to do with capturing Sidious."»
- Ch33 L3623: «"Well, which is it - - home or Sidious?"»
- Ch33 L3671: «"Speak with Palpatine. Maybe he'll see something in this that I've missed."»
- Ch34 L3685: «Only Sidious, Dooku, or Sidious's previous apprentice could have performed the amputations.»
- Ch34 L3685: «Even if images of Dooku's tavern mates did surface, the Jedi had no means of identifying any of them as Darth Sidious, but the images could provide an additional starting point for further investigation.»
- Ch34 L3745: «"Our chances for picking up Sidious's trail decrease with each person who passes."»
- Ch34 L3765: «"Assuming there's anything left after Dooku sterilizes the place. From everything we've seen, Sidious and Dooku don't make many mistakes."»
- Ch35 L3781: «As big as life, Palpatine's holoimage spoke from atop a projector table in a private comlink lounge aboard the medical frigate. With R2-D2 standing off to one side of the transmission grid, Anakin hung on the Supreme Chancellor's every word.»
- Ch35 L3783: «"Of course, the Council doesn't understand," Palpatine said. "Surely you don't find that surprising."»
- Ch35 L3791: «Palpatine smiled lightly.»
- Ch35 L3799: «"I would believe you," Palpatine said without pause. "Tell me what you see."»
- Ch35 L3807: «Palpatine gazed away from the holocam.»
- Ch35 L3827: «Palpatine spread his hands.»
- Ch35 L3837: «Palpatine waited until he was certain Anakin was finished.»
- Ch35 L3845: «Palpatine smiled again.»
- Ch35 L3849: «Anakin fought the temptation to reveal everything about the search for Darth Sidious. He glanced at R2-D2, as if expecting commiseration, but the astromech only swiveled his dome, his processor status indicator flashing from blue to red.»
- Ch35 L3853: «Palpatine adopted a sympathetic expression.»
- Ch35 L3857: «In time you will learn to trust your feelings; then you will be invincible. Palpatine's advice to him, three years earlier.»
- Ch35 L3863: «"Yes," Palpatine was saying. "Dooku is the key to everything just now. Despite all our victories in the inner systems... Do you suspect he and General Grievous may have some secret strategy?"»
- Ch37 L3973: «But on the possibility that Tythe was a calculated diversion, Palpatine's naval command staff had agreed to committing only a single battle group from the Outer Rim fleet.»
- Ch37 L3983: «"All the more reason to believe that Dooku was ordered here by Sidious."»
- Ch38 L4073: «For a time, Mace had considered appealing to Palpatine for permission, since the Supreme Chancellor had an upper-level suite in the building.»
- Ch38 L4075: «For company, the probe droids had scores of custodial and maintenance droids, but the trail to Sidious had gone cold. Lost among countless footprints that covered the floor.»
- Ch38 L4185: «"Crowd control!" he told the commandos. To Shaak Ti, he said: "Find Palpatine. See to it he's conveyed to safety. I'll send backup."»
- Ch39 L4193: «Once again, Darth Sidious had divined the actions they would take well in advance of their own deciding.»
- Ch39 L4193: «Sidious wasn't unerring.»
- Ch39 L4193: «Such mastery was one of the skills that distinguished Sidious from Yoda, who believed the future was so much in motion it could not be read with any clarity - - especially during times when the dark side was on the ascendant.»
- Ch39 L4205: «"Stand not amazed, young Jedi. Is this not the way you had your first glimpse of Lord Sidious?"»
- Ch39 L4215: «Exceptional, he thought. Despite his mastery of the Quey'tek technique for hiding oneself in the Force, they had located him! Ah, well then, time to entertain them, in observance of Sidious's wishes.»
- Ch39 L4225: «Touched by their camaraderie, Dooku smiled to himself. Darth Sidious had his work cut out for him if he ever expected to turn Skywalker to the dark side.»
- Ch39 L4245: «This was Sidious: powerful enough to hide in plain sight.»
- Ch39 L4263: «"Tiin, Koon, Ki-Adi-Mundi, and some of the others are on their way up the well," Mace said. "I sent Stass Allie to assist Shaak Ti in guarding Chancellor Palpatine."»
- Ch39 L4267: «"Well trained the Supreme Chancellor's Red Guards are. But display due concern for his safety, the Jedi must."»
- Ch39 L4287: «"Maybe Palpatine will heed the Council's warnings next time."»
- Ch39 L4299: «"Not Grievous - - Sidious."»
- Ch39 L4309: «"This will only encourage Palpatine to triple the size of the clone army, construct more and more starships and fighters, strike at more worlds. With the Senate crippled, no one will oppose him."»
- Ch40 L4321: «With a battle raging in space, and notwithstanding Mace Windu's command to the contrary, Dyne had been tempted to suspend the search for Sidious's lair and report back to the Intelligence division, as he had ordered the other analysts to do.»
- Ch41 L4377: «The Republic has all of you on the run - - even your master, Sidious."»
- Ch42 L4393: «Many delegates who might not have been on Coruscant had come to hear Palpatine's State of the Republic address, and had remained onworld to attend the endless parties that followed.»
- Ch42 L4393: «In light of the surprise attack, Palpatine's reassurances seemed even more woefully premature now than when he had uttered them.»
- Ch42 L4393: «And despite the fact that the Supreme Chancellor's optimistic remarks had been echoed throughout the Great Rotunda, Padme couldn't help notice that many of her peers were surrounded by cadres of bodyguards, or sporting body armor, jet packs, or other emergency escape devices.»
- Ch42 L4393: «Clearly Palpatine had failed to lull everyone into complacency.»
- Ch42 L4397: «Even if the rumors proved true, Padme was convinced that Palpatine would find some way to drive Grievous from the Core - - again.»
- Ch42 L4405: «"Have you seen the Chancellor?" Bail asked when he could. She shook her head. "He's probably in the holding office."»
- Ch42 L4411: «"Mas Amedda," he explained for Padme's benefit. "He'll know where to find the Chancellor."»
- Ch42 L4413: «The tall, horned, gray-complected Chagrian fairly shouldered his way through the crowd. "The Supreme Chancellor had no meetings scheduled until later today," he said in answer to Bail's question. "I assume he is in his residence."»
- Ch42 L4417: «Amedda gazed down at her in sudden concern. "And the Chancellor wasn't?"»
- Ch43 L4483: «Ordinarily he would have given thought to contingency plans, but he trusted that Lords Sidious and Tyranus would apprise him of any changes.»
- Ch43 L4483: «Those little-known routes had been furnished by Sidious, who was less concerned with battlefield tactics than with long-range strategies.»
- Ch43 L4523: «"... Fisto and I... Senate building. Shaak... Allie... to the Chancellor's quarters in Five Hundred Republica. We... with them - - "»
- Ch43 L4529: «Shutting his eyes to distance himself farther, he stretched out with his feelings, placing in his mind's eye Mace and Kit Fisto skimming through the deranged sky; Shaak Ti and Stass Allie hurrying toward Palpatine's quarters in 500 Republica; Saesee Tiin, Agen Kolar, Bultar Swan, and other Jedi Masters and Jedi Knights streaking from Coruscant's envelope in their starfighters, local space flashing with energy bolts and globular explosions, ships too numerous to count embroiled in a monumental battle...»
- Ch43 L4531: «Yoda stretched out farther, immersing himself fully in the Force - - only to feel his breath catch in his throat. Frigid, the current became. Arctic. And for the first time he could feel Sidious. Feel him on Coruscant!»
- Ch44 L4549: «"Were is the Chancellor?»
- Ch44 L4549: «Shaak Ti demanded of the three Red Guards stationed outside the entrance to Palpatine's suite in 500 Republica.»
- Ch44 L4553: «A broad hallway lined with sculptures and holoart images led into the suite itself, which, like Palpatine's chambers in the Senate Office Building, was predominantly red.»
- Ch44 L4555: «Scarcely acknowledging her presence, Palpatine paced into the room like a caged animal, hands clasped behind his back, Senatorial robes trailing along the richly carpeted floor.»
- Ch44 L4555: «Additional Red Guards and several of Palpatine's advisers stood watching him, some with comlinks plugged into their ears, others with devices Shaak Ti understood to be vital to the continued operation of the Republic military.»
- Ch44 L4555: «Should anything befall the Chancellor, authority to initiate battle campaigns and issue war codes would pass temporarily to Speaker of the Senate, Mas Amedda, who, Shaak Ti had learned, was already safely ensconced in a hardened bunker deep beneath the Great Rotunda.»
- Ch44 L4557: «She couldn't help noticing that Pestage and Isard - - two of Palpatine's closest advisers - - looked nervous. "Why is he still here?" Stass Allie directed at Isard.»
- Ch44 L4561: «Shaak Ti practically had to plant herself in Palpatine's path to get his attention.»
- Ch44 L4563: «"Supreme Chancellor, we need to escort you to shelter."»
- Ch44 L4565: «They were not strangers. Palpatine had personally commended her for her actions at Geonosis, Kamino, Dagu, Brentaal IV, and Centares. He stopped briefly to regard her, then swung around and paced away from her.»
- Ch44 L4573: «Palpatine sent him a skewered grin. "Practice and reality are different matters. The Supreme Chancellor of the Galactic Senate does not hide from enemies of the Republic. Can I be any clearer?"»
- Ch44 L4575: «The fact that Palpatine was flustered, confused, possibly frightened was obvious. But when Shaak Ti attempted to read him through the Force, she found it difficult to get a sense of what he was truly feeling.»
- Ch44 L4583: «Palpatine deployed his signature penetrating gaze. "And what will you do should I refuse? Use the Force to drag me from my quarters? Pit your lightsabers against the weapons of my guards, who are also sworn to safeguard me?"»
- Ch44 L4587: «"Supreme Chancellor," Pestage was saying. "You must listen to reason - - "»
- Ch44 L4589: «"Reason?" Palpatine snapped. He aimed a finger toward the window. "Have you gazed into our once tranquil skies? Is there anything reasonable about what's occurring there?"»
- Ch44 L4593: «Palpatine stared at him. "In other words, you agree with the Jedi."»
- Ch44 L4597: «"And you?" Palpatine asked the captain of his guards.»
- Ch44 L4601: «Palpatine stormed to the window. "Perhaps you need to take a closer look - - "»
- Ch44 L4603: «Before a further word could fly from his mouth, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were in motion; Shaak Ti tackling Palpatine to the floor, while Allie ignited her blade and brought it vertically in front of her.»
- Ch44 L4605: «"Unhand me!" Palpatine said. "How dare you?"»
- Ch44 L4613: «Keeping Palpatine in a crouch, she grabbed a handful of his robes and began to guide him deeper into the room, her upraised lightsaber parrying bolts that ricocheted from the walls and ceiling.»
- Ch44 L4613: «Releasing Palpatine to the custody of two guards, Shaak Ti raced to the window and gazed down into the clouds.»
- Ch44 L4617: «Elsewhere in the room, Pestage was helping Palpatine to his feet. "Ready now, sir?"»
- Ch44 L4619: «Palpatine returned a wide-eyed nod. "These familiarization drills you've been conducting,"»
- Ch44 L4621: «Stass Allie started to say. Isard gestured to one of the side rooms. "The suite is equipped with a secret turbolift that serves a secure, midlevel skydock. An armored gunship is standing by to transport the Chancellor to a bunker complex in the Sah'c District."»
- Ch44 L4631: «Stass Allie nodded, then glanced at Palpatine. "Supreme Chancellor, your guards are going to encircle you. You are not to attempt to leave that circle under any circumstances. Do you understand?"»
- Ch44 L4633: «Palpatine nodded. "I'll do whatever you say."»
- Ch44 L4643: «Pressed into the turbolift with Stass Allie, Palpatine's guards and advisers, and Republica's security personnel, Shaak Ti watched the display tick off the floors. No one spoke until the car had reached the first sublevel.»
- Ch44 L4647: «Considering all that had happened since she left, it was likely that Dyne and Commander Valiant had curtailed the search for Sidious's hideaway.»
