- Ch44 L4649: «"Wait here," Shaak Ti instructed the Red Guards and Palpatine when they had reached the mouth of the tunnel.»
- Ch45 L4665: «Sidious, apprehended and defeated?»
- Ch45 L4667: «So much rested on whether Lord Sidious's plan could succeed on all fronts - - even though forced to unfold hastily, because of a foolish oversight by Nute Gunray.»
- Ch45 L4675: «"Lord Tyranus," Grievous said, in motion suddenly and genuflecting. "Supreme Chancellor Palpatine will soon be ours."»
- Ch46 L4715: «"Palpatine will never live this down," Mon Mothma was saying. "Committing so many of our ships and troopers to the Outer Rim sieges. As if this war he is so intent on winning could never come to Coruscant."»
- Ch46 L4717: «The Senate will be blamed for voting to escalate the sieges, and while we're mired in accusations and counteraccusations of accountability, Palpatine will quietly accrue more and more power.»
- Ch46 L4721: «Bail continued. "Dooku, Grievous, Gunray, Palpatine."»
- Ch46 L4723: «Mon Mothma nodded sadly. "The Jedi could have stopped this war. Now they're Palpatine's pawns."»
- Ch47 L4769: «Having boarded the mag-lev at its 500 Republica platform, he, Kit Fisto, Shaak Ti, and Stass Allie were in the car the Supreme Chancellor's Red Guards had commandeered - - second in a train of some twenty cars.»
- Ch47 L4769: «Through a gap in the protective circle the guards had forged, Mace caught a glimpse of Palpatine, his head of wavy gray hair lowered in what might have been anguish or deep concentration.»
- Ch47 L4771: «Many Coruscanti knew that Palpatine resided in 500 Republica, but the location of his suite was a well-kept secret.»
- Ch47 L4771: «More important, how had Grievous known that Palpatine wasn't to be found in either of his offices?»
- Ch47 L4773: «But Palpatine had only just been elected Supreme Chancellor when Dooku left Coruscant to return to Serenno, and back then, some thirteen years ago, Palpatine had been living in a high-rise tower close to the Senate Building. So how had Grievous known to go to 500 Republica?»
- Ch47 L4775: «Sidious?»
- Ch47 L4775: «As many on the Jedi Council feared, Sidious's network of agents and assets might have infiltrated the Republic military command itself.»
- Ch47 L4775: «Mace caught another glimpse of Palpatine, insulated by the flowing red robes of his handpicked bodyguards.»
- Ch47 L4777: «Surmising that Dyne had called off the search for Sidious shortly after the attack had commenced, Intelligence hadn't dispatched a second search team - - aimed at locating Dyne and Valiant - - until neither of them had been heard from, even after communications had been restored to the Senate District.»
- Ch47 L4777: «Shaak Ti hadn't seen them when she and Palpatine's protectors had whisked the Supreme Chancellor through 500 Republica's sub-basement.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Palpatine's guards would have commandeered the entire train if Palpatine hadn't intervened, refusing to allow it.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Shaak Ti had told Mace and Kit about the Supreme Chancellor's earlier reluctance to leave his suite.»
- Ch47 L4781: «Screams issued from Coruscanti wedged into the cars to the front and rear of Palpatine's.»
- Ch47 L4783: «Racing alongside Palpatine's car, the same droid responsible for the collisions surged into a steep climb and disappeared from view.»
- Ch47 L4783: «A tumult of terrified cries rose from the car directly behind Palpatine's, and hands and feet began to pound against the passageway door.»
- Ch47 L4787: «In turn, Mace whirled to Shaak Ti and Allie. "Move the Chancellor into the forward car!"»
- Ch47 L4799: «Deactivating their blades, Mace and Kit rushed into the forward car, which was now filled with Palpatine's advisers and those passengers the Jedi women and Red Guards had relocated from the train's lead car.»
- Ch47 L4799: «Mace and Kit continued to squirm forward, arriving in the Supreme Chancellor's car just as the mag-lev was emerging from the skytunnel.»
- Ch47 L4799: «In the middle of the car, Palpatine stood at the center of the cordon the Red Guards had formed around him.»
- Ch47 L4803: «"And battle droids don't just drop from the sky. There's a third craft." Kit's bulging black eyes indicated Palpatine. "They want to take him alive."»
- Ch49 L4863: «Steps into Palpatine's car, Mace and Kit called on the Force to prevent everyone from flailing toward the vestibule door.»
- Ch49 L4863: «Farther forward in the car, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were keeping the Supreme Chancellor on his feet.»
- Ch49 L4867: «He knew that it was only going to get worse, since none of them would be allowed to leave until Palpatine had been moved to safety.»
- Ch49 L4869: «Pressed in among his Red Guards, Palpatine couldn't even be seen.»
- Ch49 L4869: «Disengaging from the mag-lev, the platform was moving away from the train before a single passenger - - even any of Palpatine's advisers - - could scramble out onto its twin.»
- Ch49 L4871: «Behind them came four Jedi Knights, who rushed to join Shaak Ti and Stass Allie in guarding Palpatine.»
- Ch49 L4885: «"We'll see the Chancellor the rest of the way to the bunker.»
- Ch49 L4885: «Shaak Ti was the last to board the gunship that would deliver Palpatine to shelter, somewhere deep in the narrow service chasms that fractured the exclusive Sah'c neighborhood.»
- Ch49 L4885: «Encircled by the contingent of Red Guards, Palpatine stood silently in the rear of the troop bay.»
- Ch49 L4887: «Palpatine's gunship had taken off to the south, and had immediately begun to descend.»
- Ch49 L4887: «Halfway to the floor of the canyon, a gunship that had seen recent action fell in alongside the Supreme Chancellor's, and remained just off to starboard and slightly astern through the numerous twists and turns that led ultimately to the mountainous structure that served as the bunker complex.»
- Ch49 L4889: «The complex could be reached by alternative means - - under normal circumstances, Palpatine would have been conveyed by repulsorlift speeder through deep tunnels that arrived from 500 Republica, the Great Rotunda, and the Senate Office Building - - but the ravine was the best way of entering from anywhere west of the Senate or Financial Districts.»
- Ch49 L4891: «The craft bearing the Supreme Chancellor had scarcely touched down when the side doors flew out and back, and the Red Guards hurried Palpatine off to a waiting speeder.»
- Ch50 L4925: «"Pilot!" Mace called toward the gunship. "Comlink the Supreme Chancellor's bunker, and arrange for us to be cleared through the shield."»
- Ch50 L4927: «Grievous and six MagnaGuards cut a bloody swath through the broad corridors that led ultimately to Palpatine's sanctuary.»
- Ch50 L4929: «At Palpatine's apartment, Grievous had managed to fool everyone by placing the gunboat on display, then clandestinely transferring himself and his combat droids into the Republic gunship Lord Tyranus had promised would be waiting for them.»
- Ch50 L4929: «He had been forced to improvise when Palpatine's protectors had opted to follow an alternate route to the bunker, and he had enjoyed chasing the mag-lev - - if not the brief duel on the roof of the train car.»
- Ch50 L4937: «"Under no circumstances are you to harm the Chancellor," he exhorted his elite, while layers of the thick hatch were retracting.»
- Ch50 L4937: «The astonishment registered by Palpatine and his quartet of Jedi Knights assured Grievous that he could not have made a more dramatic entry.»
- Ch50 L4941: «Behind him rushed his elite droids, with a single purpose in mind: to tear into the guards and soldiers arrayed in a defensive semicircle in front of Palpatine.»
- Ch50 L4941: «Tall, elegant looking, dramatic in their red robes and face-masked cowls, the Supreme Chancellor's protectors were well trained and fought with passion.»
- Ch50 L4943: «Perhaps if Palpatine had been intelligent enough to have surrounded himself with real Jedi - - Jedi of the caliber of Windu and the tentacle-headed Kit Fisto - - the engagement might have gone differently.»
- Ch50 L4959: «Then, deactivating his lightsabers, he turned to Palpatine.»
- Ch50 L4963: «Palpatine neither cowered nor protested. He merely said: "You will be a true loss to the forces you represent." The remark took Grievous by surprise. Was this praise?»
- Ch50 L4965: «"Four Jedi Knights, all these soldiers and guards," Palpatine went on, gesturing broadly. "Why not wait until Shaak Ti and Stass Allie arrive." He cocked his head to one side. "I think I hear them coming. They are Masters, after all."»
- Ch50 L4967: «Grievous didn't respond immediately. Was Palpatine trying to trick him?»
- Ch50 L4971: «Palpatine mocked him with a sneer. "Do you actually believe that this plan will succeed?"»
- Ch50 L4975: «"So you take orders," Palpatine said, moving with deliberate lethargy. "Which of us, then, is the lesser?" Before Grievous could reply, he added: "My death won't end this war, General."»
- Ch50 L4977: «Understandably, Lord Sidious had his plan, but did he actually believe that Palpatine's death would prompt the Jedi to lay down their lightsabers?»
- Ch50 L4977: «Thrown into turmoil by the Chancellor's death, could the Senate order the Jedi to stand down?»
- Ch50 L4979: «"Move," he told Palpatine.»
- Ch50 L4979: «The MagnaGuards stepped forward to make certain that Palpatine obeyed.»
- Ch50 L4979: «Palpatine watched him from the doorway.»
- Ch51 L4987: «It hadn't taken long to piece together what had happened: the Separatists had managed to hijack a Republic gunship and infiltrate the bunker complex shield by timing their arrival to coincide with that of the ship carrying Palpatine, Shaak Ti, and the others.»
- Ch51 L4991: «Even less remained of the gunship that had delivered Palpatine to the complex.»
- Ch51 L4995: «But had the fall been deliberate - - part of an increasingly elaborate ruse - - or had Grievous originally planned to abduct Palpatine from the train?»
- Ch51 L4995: «Unless, of course, he had received prior intelligence on the number of Red Guards in Palpatine's detail, and the number of troopers and other combatants stationed in the bunker complex.»
- Ch51 L4999: «But Palpatine was missing.»
- Ch51 L5001: «"Sir, the Supreme Chancellor was gone by the time we arrived," a commando explained. "His captors exited the complex by way of the south tunnels."»
- Ch51 L5005: «"I fought Grievous on Hypori," she said weakly. "I knew what he was capable of. But this... And taking Palpatine..."»
- Ch51 L5007: «Mace supported her. "There will be no negotiations. The Supreme Chancellor won't allow it."»
- Ch51 L5011: «Kit nodded. "We'll find out who. But our first priority is to rescue the Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch51 L5015: «"I can show you," Shaak Ti said. Turning, she activated a security recording that had captured Grievous and several of his humanoid guards dragging Palpatine to the south landing pad, butchering the handful of troopers posted there, scrambling into a waiting tri-winged shuttle, lifting off into sunset clouds...»
- Ch51 L5029: «Mace's eyes widened in alert. "Do your gunners know that the Supreme Chancellor is aboard? Do they realize they can't fire on the shuttle?"»
- Ch51 L5037: «"On whose orders?" Mace fumed. "The Supreme Chancellor's top advisers."»
- Ch52 L5103: «The droid whistled and zithered. Throwing himself into open cockpit, Anakin toggled switches. Obi-Wan had just reached the base of the ladder when he heard Palpatine's voice issuing through the cockpit annunciators.»
- Ch53 L5129: «When word of Supreme Chancellor Palpatine's capture had first been rumored, then verified, all anyone in the crowd could ask was, How, in three short years, had it come to this?»
- Ch53 L5131: «Would the starfighters be able to overtake the shuttle in which Palpatine was being held captive by a cyborg monster?»
- Ch53 L5133: «What would become of the Republic should the Supreme Chancellor be killed, or Coruscant occupied by tens of thousands of battle droids?»
- Ch53 L5137: «Her sadness was personal now, not for Palpatine, though his abduction hollowed her.»
- Ch54 L5149: «Uncertain as to where Grievous had stashed Palpatine, the pilots of the trio of starfighters were being careful with their bolts, but every hit was inflicting further damage to the stabilizers and shield generator.»
- Ch54 L5151: «Plasma fire from the Invisible Hand's point-defense weapons had only prompted them to close ranks with the shuttle, using it in the same way Grievous was using Palpatine - - as a kind of screen. The mechanical voice of a control droid aboard the cruiser issued from the shuttle's cockpit speakers.»
- Ch54 L5163: «Grievous swiveled his seat toward Palpatine, who was strapped into an acceleration couch between two MagnaGuards.»
- Ch54 L5165: «The Supreme Chancellor had been unexpectedly compliant since leaving the bunker, at times brazen enough to take Grievous to task for his less-than-perfect piloting skills.»
- Ch54 L5167: «Palpatine had barked at him repeatedly.»
- Ch54 L5167: «What did Palpatine think was going to become of him after they reached the Invisible Hand?»
- Ch54 L5167: «Was he under the delusion that Lords Sidious and Tyranus would simply hold him for ransom?»
- Ch54 L5167: «Why not kill Palpatinc sooner rather than later?»
- Ch54 L5167: «Palpatine had mocked him.»
- Ch54 L5171: «Palpatine sneered. "With you at the controls, I'm certain it will."»
- Ch54 L5175: «Somewhere aboard the shuttle there would be extravehicular gear Palpatine could don.»
- Ch54 L5175: «But with life support failing, Palpatine was already in enough trouble.»
- Ch54 L5177: «Before the shuttle had even settled to the deck, one of the Jedi had plunged his glowing blue blade straight through the starboard hatch. Hurrying aft through thickening smoke, Grievous caught sight of Palpatine's expression of derision.»
- Ch54 L5185: «"Jettison the bodies," Grievous instructed. "Choose a place where the Republic can have a good look at them." Diminutive between two MagnaGuards, Palpatine was waiting at the foot of the shuttle boarding ramp. "Take him," Grievous said.»
- Ch54 L5187: «Lifting Palpatine by his armpits, the combat droids followed Grievous through the cruiser, and at last through an oval of opalescent portal into a large cabin space containing a situation table surrounded by chairs.»
- Ch54 L5187: «Grievous ordered the guards to set the Supreme Chancellor down in a swivel chair at the head of the table and to shackle his hands.»
- Ch54 L5191: «Palpatine's voice was sinister when he spoke. "You can display me, but I won't speak."»
- Ch54 L5201: «Palpatine smiled without showing his teeth. "Then I am assured of being greatly entertained."»
- Ch54 L5203: «His frightening visage dominating every frequency of the HoloNet, he delivered a message of gloom and doom, forecasting the end of Palpatine's reign, the long-delinquent downfall of the corrupt Republic, a bright new future for all the worlds and all the species that had been enslaved to it...»
- Ch54 L5205: «"The Separatists have nothing to gain by killing Palpatine.»
- Ch54 L5225: «"Kill the Supreme Chancellor, Grievous will."»
- Ch54 L5231: «"The Senate will give away Coruscant to effect Palpatine's release."»
- Ch54 L5233: «"Worse the situation will be if the Supreme Chancellor dies. Fall, the Republic will."»
- Ch54 L5239: «"Master Yoda, we were close to capturing Sidious. I could feel it."»
- Ch54 L5241: «"Knew this, Sidious did. Hiding, he is." No longer on Coruscant, Yoda thought.»
- Ch55 L5249: «The sloop's Geonosian technology would mask the fact that he had jumped almost immediately to Coruscant to join Grievous, and to play out the final act of the drama Sidious had composed.»
- Ch55 L5249: «The abduction of Palpatine had not only abbreviated the search for him, but also allowed Sidious to escape Coruscant undetected.»
- Ch55 L5249: «Sidious would never have allowed the Jedi to expose him.»
- Ch55 L5251: «And Palpatine was hardly the prize he appeared to be. The greater prize, Sidious had told Dooku during their most recent communication, was Anakin Skywalker.»
- Ch55 L5253: «"Long have you watched him," Dooku had said, repeating words Sidious himself had spoken. "Longer than you know, Lord Tyranus. Longer than you know. And the time has come to test him again."»
- Ch55 L5261: «"You will duel them," Sidious had said.»

### B.4 Anakin: 563 entries. Aliases: `Anakin|Skywalker|Chosen One`. Version: Anakin, 19 BBY (rage-amped where he calls on anger)

- Ch1 L29: «He dropped into a crouch even before Anakin's lips formed the final word, lightsaber aimed to the ground to keep from impaling his onrushing former Padawan.»
- Ch1 L31: «In a tuck, Obi-Wan pivoted on his right foot in time to see the droid's spindly body collapse. The fact that Anakin had saved his life was nothing new, but Anakin's blade had passed a little too close for comfort. Eyes somewhat wide with surprise, he came to his feet.»
- Ch1 L35: «Anakin held his blade to one side. In the strobing light of battle his blue eyes shone with wry amusement.»
- Ch1 L39: «Anakin used the honorific not as learner to teacher, but as Jedi Knight to Jedi Council member.»
- Ch1 L45: «Anakin's grin blossomed into a full-fledged smile.»
- Ch1 L51: «Anakin booted the battle droid's blaster aside.»
- Ch1 L59: «Two scorched droids little more than arms and torsos lifted their weapons as the Jedi approached, but Anakin only raised his left hand in a Force push that shoved the droids flat onto their backs.»
- Ch1 L65: «Cato Neimoidia was as close to Coruscant as Obi-Wan and Anakin had been in almost four standard months, and with the last remaining Separatist strongholds now cleared from the Core and Colonies, they expected to be back in the Outer Rim by week's end.»
- Ch1 L67: «A few nimble leaps brought Cody face-to-face with Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch1 L71: «"Show us," Anakin said.»
- Ch1 L75: «"Generals Kenobi and Skywalker, provincial recon unit reports that Viceroy Gunray and his entourage are making their way to the north side of the redoubt.»
- Ch1 L93: «Obi-Wan glanced uncertainly at Anakin and motioned him off to one side. "It's just the two of us. What do you think?"»
- Ch1 L97: «Anakin canted his head and grinned. "There are others."»
- Ch1 L101: «"Think what you will." Obi-Wan narrowed his eyes with purpose. "Oh, I see. But I would have thought Senator Amidala of greater interest to you than Supreme Chancellor Palpatine." Before Anakin could respond, he added: "Despite that she's a politician also."»
- Ch1 L105: «Obi-Wan regarded Anakin for a moment. "What's more, if Chancellor Palpatine had genuine concern for your welfare, he would have kept you closer to Coruscant."»
- Ch1 L107: «Anakin placed his artificial hand on Obi-Wan's left shoulder. "Perhaps, Master. But then, who would look after you?"»
- Ch2 L115: «Obi-Wan, Anakin, and the commandos who made up Squad Seven had to run to keep up with the beetles as they hurried their fresh-picked loads from the orchard to the cave-like entrance to a natural mound at the base of the redoubt.»
- Ch2 L117: «Anakin's greater height required him to remain farther back, almost directly under the beetle's pug tail.»
- Ch2 L123: «"Stay close to the ones who are still headed for the nest!" Anakin shouted.»
- Ch2 L127: «All at once Anakin was out from under his protective harvester.»
- Ch2 L127: «Anakin's final leap dropped him directly in front of the harvester that had seized the commando.»
- Ch2 L133: «Supporting the commando trooper with his left arm, Anakin warded off blaster bolts on the run.»
- Ch2 L133: «Anakin slid into the trench a moment later, lowering the commando gently to the muddy slope.»
- Ch2 L141: «"Not much I can do for him here," the medspec told Anakin. "Maybe if we can get an FX-Seven air-dropped - - "»
- Ch2 L143: «"We don't need a droid," Anakin interrupted. Kneeling, he placed his hands on the injured commando's abdomen and used a Jedi healing technique to keep the clone from going into deep shock.»
- Ch3 L157: «But Anakin had another idea.»
- Ch3 L161: «Cody was more than willing to comply. After all, the order had come directly from "the Hero with no Fear," as Anakin was sometimes known. "The Warrior of the Infinite." There was, though, a chain of command to maintain, so Cody looked to Obi-Wan for confirmation.»
- Ch3 L173: «"Tell them we've got a wounded trooper for the Rimsoo," Anakin said. The term stood for "Republic Mobile Surgical Unit."»
- Ch3 L187: «Obi-Wan had never visited a fungus farm, but no sooner had he and Anakin dashed through the cave-like opening to the nest than the briefings he had received more than ten standard years earlier came back to him in a flash.»
- Ch3 L191: «Cody ordered the squad to secure the area. Those in the rear were still taking sporadic fire from STAPs, but the droid pilots couldn't get close to the entrance because of the bodies of dead beetles piled outside. Squad Seven's medspec hurried over to Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch3 L199: «"Adverse how?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch3 L201: «Obi-Wan glanced at Anakin.»
- Ch3 L201: «Anakin was already racing for the tunnel, lightsaber gripped in both hands, deflecting most of the bolts back through the entrance.»
- Ch3 L201: «Obi-Wan leapt to one side, raising his blade to deal with two bolts that got past Anakin.»
- Ch3 L203: «Showering sparks, the device shorted out, and a slab of thick alloy dropped from its pocket in the wall, sealing the tunnel with a loud thud! Switching off his lightsaber, Anakin cast a complimentary glance over his shoulder.»
- Ch3 L209: «"You've always been better at evasion than I have," Anakin said. "I prefer more straightforward tactics."»
- Ch3 L215: «Anakin swung to Obi-Wan. "One of us has to divert the droids."»
- Ch3 L221: «Obi-Wan compressed his lips. "From a certain point of view, Anakin."»
- Ch3 L223: «Anakin scowled. "Fine. Then I'll be the bait this time."»
- Ch3 L227: «Anakin couldn't restrain a smile. "I knew you'd listen to reason, Master. " He singled out four commandos. "You'll come with me."»
- Ch3 L231: «Obi-Wan, Cody, and the rest of Squad Seven set out for the turbolift shafts. Obi-Wan hadn't gone five meters when he stopped and swung around. "Anakin, I know we've got a score to settle with Gunray, but don't make it personal. We want to take him alive!"»
- Ch4 L235: «Oh, but it is personal, Anakin told himself while he watched Obi-Wan, Cody, and four troopers disappear into the turbolift.»
- Ch4 L235: «The woman Anakin loved above all else.»
- Ch4 L239: «It made Anakin's blood boil.»
- Ch4 L241: «In peaceful times Anakin might have been able to bridle his rage, but now he relied on it to drive him forward, to transform him into the person he needed to be.»
- Ch4 L243: «For all his skills, Anakin still looked to Obi-Wan for guidance.»
- Ch4 L243: «Opening one of the pouches on his belt, Anakin wedged the rebreather inside.»
- Ch4 L249: «Anakin could only shake his head in wonder. In Mos Espa, on Tatooine, he and his mother had lived simply, and never wanted for anything. His grin was short-lived. Anger and despair made him grit his teeth.»
- Ch4 L251: «Anakin brought his team to a halt.»
- Ch4 L251: «The commando listened, then spoke to Anakin with hand signals.»
- Ch4 L255: «The muscles in Anakin's jaw bunched. "Then we have to act quickly."»
- Ch4 L257: «No one contested when Anakin held point position.»
- Ch4 L257: «Approaching an intersection, Anakin made a halting gesture with his left hand.»
- Ch4 L257: «The commando to Anakin's left nodded in confirmation, then extended a finger-thin holocam around the corner and activated his gauntlet holoprojector.»
- Ch4 L259: «Anakin motioned for silence, and was just about to step into the intersecting corridor when a banged-up silver protocol droid appeared from across the hall, raising its hands in delighted surprise.»
- Ch4 L261: «Anakin leapt around the corner in time to see the Neimoidians set off at a run, red-eyed, flat-nosed Gunray casting a nervous glance over his shoulder.»
- Ch4 L261: «As for the super battle droids, they had about-faced and were marching stiff-legged in Anakin's direction.»
- Ch5 L267: «For a time the same issues had stood at the center of Obi-Wan's conflicts with Anakin.»
- Ch5 L267: «Clearly Anakin was as strong in the Force as any Jedi who had ever sat on the Council.»
- Ch5 L267: «Someday Anakin would come to accept that, and then he would be truly unstoppable.»
- Ch5 L267: «Qui-Gon had had the insight to recognize it more than a decade earlier, and Obi-Wan felt duty-bound to his former Master to help Anakin fulfill his destiny.»
- Ch5 L269: «His faith in Anakin had grown so strong that he had become Anakin's staunchest defender to those on the Council who had grown apprehensive about the young man's prowess, and uncomfortable with his confidential, almost familial relationship with Supreme Chancellor Palpatine.»
- Ch5 L271: «If Obi-Wan was, as Anakin sometimes said, the father he never had, then Palpatine was his wise uncle, adviser, mentor in the ways of life outside the Temple.»
- Ch5 L271: «Obi-Wan understood that Anakin envied him for having been appointed to the Council.»
- Ch5 L271: «But how could he not, having been all but anointed "the Chosen One," continually bolstered by Palpatine's praise, driven to prove to his former Master that he could be the perfect Jedi Knight.»
- Ch5 L271: «On countless occasions Anakin's bold actions had allowed them to prevail against seemingly impossible odds.»
- Ch5 L271: «What he could say was that he had learned to trust Anakin's instincts.»
- Ch6 L301: «"Keep him quiet," Anakin snapped at the commando closest to the droid.»
- Ch6 L303: «"But, sirs - - " A second commando glanced at Anakin and motioned down the corridor behind them. "Six infantry droids advancing. We're going to be caught in a crossfire."»
- Ch6 L305: «Anakin gave his head a quick shake. "Wrong. Follow me - - and bring the droid."»
- Ch6 L307: «Fury clouded Anakin's eyes.»
- Ch6 L307: «Scarcely letting a shot get past him, so that the commandos following in his wake could concentrate their fire on the ones Anakin only wounded.»
- Ch6 L309: «Focused on the route Gunray and his lackeys had taken, Anakin raced through corridors, rounding corners without slowing down, sprinting for the launching bay at the far end of the final corridor.»
- Ch6 L311: «Anakin paced behind him, waiting.»
- Ch6 L311: «Anakin charged through the irising seal even before it had opened fully.»
- Ch6 L311: «Anakin ran to the platform's forward-curving edge, eyes scanning Cato Neimoidia's light-riddled night sky for some sign of the fleeing ship.»
- Ch6 L313: «Anakin's teammates joined him at the brink, one with a hand vised on TC-16's upper left arm.»
- Ch6 L315: «"What type of ship is it?" Anakin demanded of the droid.»
- Ch6 L327: «Anakin turned through a circle, gloved hand gripped on the lightsaber pommel, the other balled into a fist.»
- Ch6 L327: «Anakin began to pace again, then stopped, yanking a commando around by his shoulder.»
- Ch6 L331: «Anakin returned to the lip of the platform, blowing his breath into the night. The battle appeared to be winding down, except within him. Not until he had Gunray in his grip...»
- Ch6 L333: «"General Skywalker," a commando said from behind him. "Urgent from Commander Cody. He and General Kenobi are pinned down on level one."»
- Ch6 L335: «Anakin shot him a questioning look. "By droids?"»
- Ch6 L339: «Anakin glanced into the glowing sky, then back at the commando who had delivered Cody's message. "General, forward command reports that your starfighter is on the way," another commando updated. Again, Anakin glanced at the sky, only to turn back to the commando. "Where did you say Obi-Wan and Cody are?"»
- Ch6 L343: «Anakin compressed his lips. "All right. Let's go rescue them."»
- Ch7 L351: «Anakin had his rebreather in his mouth as he approached the room in which Obi-Wan had held his own against better than fifty droids, all of which lay scattered about the room.»
- Ch7 L351: «A weaving, shuffling, staggering Obi-Wan was dealing with the last of them when Anakin entered.»
- Ch7 L353: «"Anakin," he said happily.»
- Ch7 L353: «When Anakin went to him, Obi-Wan promptly collapsed in his arms.»
- Ch7 L353: «Anakin deactivated Obi-Wan's blade and inserted a rebreather into his mouth - - the same one that had ended up on the floor of the grotto.»
- Ch7 L355: «"Exactly what lightsaber form were you using back there, Master?" Anakin asked when Obi-Wan had come around and the rebreathers were no longer necessary.»
- Ch7 L359: «"More the absence of it." Anakin laughed shortly. "If only Mace, Kit, or Shaak Ti could have seen you."»
- Ch7 L365: «Obi-Wan regarded Anakin in confusion.»
- Ch7 L367: «"I'll explain later," Anakin said.»
- Ch7 L371: «Anakin's shoulders dropped. "The entire entourage escaped the palace."»
- Ch7 L375: «Anakin shrugged. "And leave you?" He paused, then added: "Of course, if I'd known you'd become master of a new lightsaber form..."»
- Ch7 L381: «"If not, there'll be other times, Anakin. We'll see to it."»
- Ch7 L383: «Anakin nodded. "I know that, Master."»
- Ch7 L385: «Obi-Wan was about to add something when a helmeted commando stepped from a nearby turbolift and hurried over to them. "General Kenobi, General Skywalker, we've found something of interest among the equipment the Neimoidians left behind."»
- Ch9 L479: «Squatting alongside it, Anakin looked up at him. "Where?"»
- Ch9 L483: «Anakin shook his head. "I don't remember seeing it."»
- Ch9 L487: «On the far side of the spacious bay, up on its hardstand, sat Anakin's sleek yellow starfighter.»
- Ch9 L487: «Anakin examined the chair's holoprojector without touching it.»
- Ch9 L493: «Anakin frowned. "That could take forever."»
- Ch9 L495: «Obi-Wan folded his arms and regarded him. "Are you in a rush, Anakin?"»
- Ch9 L505: «Anakin grimaced. "What do you know about running diagnostics? Master."»
- Ch9 L507: «"I'm not exactly a stranger to the Temple's cyberlabs, Anakin."»
- Ch9 L511: «"Anakin," Obi-Wan started to say.»
- Ch9 L515: «"You don't have a say in the matter," Anakin said. R2-D2 trilled and hooted at the battered protocol droid. The two had been bickering since R2- D2's arrival a short time earlier.»
- Ch9 L519: «"Don't pay Artoo any mind, TeeCee," Anakin said. "He's been spoiled by another protocol droid. Haven't you, Artoo?"»
- Ch9 L523: «"Anakin!»
- Ch9 L523: «Anakin stood up and joined Obi-Wan on the launch platform.»
- Ch9 L539: «"Keep quiet, TeeCee," Anakin said. R2-D2 honked and razzed, though as if from a distance. "And you, too, Artoo." Obi-Wan glanced over his shoulder, and his jaw dropped.»
- Ch9 L541: «"Where's the mechno-chair?" Anakin swung around and scanned the bay. "Where's Artoo?"»
- Ch9 L543: «"I've been trying to tell you, sirs," TC-16 said, gesturing toward the launching bay's ruined iris hatch. "The chair walked away - - taking your high-thinking little droid with it!" Obi-Wan stared at Anakin in bewilderment. "Well, it couldn't have gotten far on foot, Master."»
- Ch9 L547: «"That's Artoo," Anakin said.»
- Ch9 L549: «"What's it doing?" Obi-Wan asked. Anakin made his face long and shook his head. "Recharging itself?"»
- Ch9 L553: «"He's saying, sir, that the mechno-chair has just armed itself to self-destruct!" Anakin made a mad dash for the console.»
- Ch9 L555: «"Artoo, unplug yourself!" Obi-Wan shouted. "Anakin, get away from that thing!"»
- Ch9 L557: «Anakin's fingers were already busy undoing leads that linked the holoprojector unit to the chair. "Can't, Master. Now we know there's something stored in this chair no one wants us to see." Obi-Wan glanced worriedly at R2- D2. "How much time, Artoo?"»
- Ch9 L561: «Obi-Wan rushed to Anakin's side. "There isn't time, Anakin. Besides, it could be rigged to detonate if tampered with."»
- Ch9 L565: «"You'll deactivate us in the process!" Obi-Wan sensed a disturbance in the Force. Without thinking, he pulled Anakin to the floor an instant before the chair shot a stream of white vapor into the space Anakin had occupied. Coughing, Obi-Wan covered his mouth and nose with the wide sleeve of his robe.»
- Ch9 L569: «"Thank you, Master," Anakin said. "What's that make it, twenty-five to thirty-seven?"»
- Ch9 L571: «Anakin studied the chair for a moment.»
- Ch9 L571: «Before Obi-Wan could even think about stopping him, Anakin had leaned forward and wrenched the interface cable from the control console.»
- Ch9 L571: «A web of blue energy gamboled around the chair and the console, knocking Anakin onto his backside.»
- Ch10 L599: «Young Anakin Skywalker or some other.»
- Ch11 L667: «Yoda was aboard, eager to see for himself what Obi-Wan and Anakin had discovered.»
- Ch11 L669: «Refusing to let the mechno-chair out of his sight, Anakin had demanded to oversee its transfer to the waiting shuttle.»
- Ch11 L685: «But instead of killing Anakin or me, he deliberately left us alive, to send a message to the Jedi."»
- Ch11 L705: «"The prophecy of the Chosen One?"»
- Ch11 L707: «"The larger prophecy: that unfold the dark times would. Born into their midst the Chosen One is, to return balance to the Force."»
- Ch11 L709: «"Anakin," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch11 L813: «A chance this war to conclude, you and Anakin have been given."»
- Ch12 L817: «In the launching bay Anakin kept his eyes on the mechno-chair, while R2- D2 and TC-16 kept their photoreceptors on Anakin.»
- Ch12 L817: «Just as Obi-Wan had said, they resented the fact that Anakin had tampered with the chair, despite the fact that, had he not, the chair would have blown itself to pieces, taking with it the holoimage of Sidious and whatever other communications memories it might contain.»
- Ch12 L819: «Probably because of Anakin's own musings about what might have become of him had the Jedi not been forced to land on Tatooine to find a replacement part for Padme's starship.»
- Ch12 L829: «He cut his eyes to the three human technicians who were fitting the mechno-chair into a crash-foam safety harness. One of them was working too fast, and almost knocked the chair over. Anakin shot to his feet and stormed across the bay.»
- Ch12 L831: «The three techs strained to move it, baffled until they realized what Anakin had done.»
- Ch12 L837: «Anakin beetled his brows in anger and advanced a step. The three techs began to back away from the chair. They're afraid of me. They've heard about me. For an instant, their fear empowered him; then he felt shame, and averted his glance.»
- Ch12 L843: «Anakin swallowed hard. "It's important, that's all. I don't want anything to happen to it." He let the mechno-chair settle to the floor.»
- Ch12 L845: «"Carefully, this time," the eldest said, refusing to so much as glance at Anakin.»
- Ch12 L847: «"General Skywalker!" a trooper called from behind him. Anakin turned, saw the trooper motioning to the shuttle. "Hyperwave commo for you - - from the office of the Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch12 L849: «Without a word, Anakin spun on his heel and ascended the shuttle's boarding ramp.»
- Ch12 L849: «When Anakin had positioned himself on the transmission grate, Palpatine smiled.»
- Ch12 L851: «"Congratulations, Anakin, on your victory at Cato Neimoidia."»
- Ch12 L857: «It wasn't the first time Anakin had heard from Palpatine in the field. At Jabiim, Palpatine had ordered Anakin to retreat before the planet fell to the Separatists; at Praesitlyn he had praised Anakin for having saved the day. Still, the communications were often as awkward as they were flattering.»
- Ch12 L861: «Anakin wet his lips. "It's not about Gunray, sir. Just a small incident here that made me angry."»
- Ch12 L863: «"What incident?" Anakin was tempted to disclose the details of his and Obi-Wan's discovery, but Yoda had told him to remain silent about the mechno-chair. "Nothing important," he said. "But I always feel guilty when I become angry."»
- Ch12 L865: «"That's a mistake," Palpatine said gently. "Anger is natural, Anakin. I thought we'd been through all this - - regarding what took place on Tatooine?"»
- Ch12 L869: «"Anakin, you're a passionate young man.»
- Ch12 L871: «Anakin suppressed an impulse to reveal his marriage to Padme, as well.»
- Ch12 L877: «Anakin forced a derisive breath. "The Senate should simply follow your lead. Instead, they block you. They tie your hands. It's as if they envy the power they gave you."»
- Ch12 L881: «"Some individuals should be above the rules," Anakin grumbled.»
- Ch12 L883: «"A case can be made for it. And, indeed, you are one of those people, Anakin. But you must know when to act, and when not to."»
- Ch12 L885: «Anakin nodded. "I understand." He paused, then said, "How is Coruscant, sir? I miss it."»
- Ch12 L889: «Anakin searched for some way to frame the question he needed to ask. "I guess you've been meeting frequently with the Loyalist Committee."»
- Ch12 L891: «He looked directly at Anakin.»
- Ch12 L893: «Anakin swallowed nervously. "Will you tell her... will you tell her hello from me?"»
- Ch12 L895: «"Anakin, I will somehow see to it that you return from the Outer Rim soon," Palpatine said.»
- Ch13 L965: «"Just as I knew we would." Palpatine beamed at her. "Senator Amidala, is that not the droid Jedi Skywalker constructed?"»
- Ch13 L975: «"Anakin?" Padme said in surprise.»
- Ch14 L981: «Returning to the launching bay with Yoda, Obi-Wan observed Anakin and Yoda trade the briefest of looks, the meaning of which escaped him.»
- Ch14 L983: «"Jedi Council business?" Anakin asked when Obi-Wan joined him. "Nothing of the sort. Yoda believes that the mechno-chair may yield clues to the whereabouts of Darth Sidious. He wants us to take up the search."»
- Ch14 L985: «Anakin didn't respond immediately. "Master, aren't we obligated to notify the Supreme Chancellor of our find?"»
- Ch14 L987: «"We are, Anakin, and we will."»
- Ch14 L999: «Obi-Wan tried to discern Anakin's intent. "Even Yoda is not infallible, if that's what you're getting at."»
- Ch14 L1001: «"The Jedi should be." Anakin glanced furtively at Obi-Wan. "We could be."»
- Ch14 L1007: «"Master Sora Bulq and many others would agree, Anakin. But few Jedi have the stomach for such a ride. We're not all as self-composed as Yoda or Master Windu."»
- Ch14 L1011: «"Dooku is a Sith, Anakin. He may have had his good reasons for leaving the Order, but he is nothing now but a master of deceit. He and Sidious prey on the weak-willed. They deceive themselves into believing that they are infallible."»
- Ch14 L1015: «"The Jedi are not a cult, Anakin.»
- Ch14 L1017: «Anakin grew quiet. "I'm only asking, Master."»
- Ch14 L1019: «How might Anakin have fared under Qui-Gon's guidance? he wondered.»
- Ch14 L1019: «He was merely Anakin's adoptive mentor, and a flawed mentor in many ways.»
- Ch14 L1019: «So eager to live up to the memory of Qui-Gon that he was continually overlooking Anakin's attempts to live up to him.»
- Ch14 L1037: «"Zan Arbor," Anakin said angrily. "The gas used on the Gungans at Ohma-D'un." He looked at Obi-Wan. "No wonder you were able to sense it!"»
- Ch14 L1039: «Dyne glanced from Anakin to Obi-Wan. "The gas-emitter mechanism is identical to what you find in some of the Techno Union's E-Five-Twenty-Two assassin droids."»
- Ch14 L1043: «Yoda laughed. "Ahead of Obi-Wan, the experts are," he said to Anakin. "We know who's responsible for the chair's Neimoidian engravings,"»
- Ch14 L1047: «"How do you know?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch14 L1051: «Though her every other thought was of Anakin, she had resolved to put him from her mind for the meeting; to focus on what was expected of her both as a public servant and concerned citizen of the Republic.»
- Ch14 L1051: «And yet, despite her best intentions, Palpatine had brought Anakin to the fore.»
- Ch14 L1051: «Had Anakin confessed to him? she wondered.»
- Ch14 L1051: «Had the Supreme Chancellor learned of their secret ceremony on Naboo, from Anakin or others?»
- Ch14 L1053: «She could feel Anakin at a great remove.»
- Ch14 L1053: «Anakin sitting beside her.»
- Ch14 L1053: «Wasn't Shmi's gaze fixed on her when Shmi said, referring to Anakin: He was meant to help you.»
- Ch16 L1127: «The twin-piloted ship had been an antique twenty years earlier, but its sublight and hyperdrive engines were reliable, and with vessels deployed on so many fronts Obi-Wan and Anakin couldn't be choosy.»
- Ch16 L1127: «Obi-Wan had plotted the three jumps it had taken them to reach the Xi Char world from the Inner Rim, but Anakin had done all the piloting.»
- Ch16 L1129: «"Landing coordinates coming in," Anakin said, eyes fixed on a display screen set into the instrument panel.»
- Ch16 L1133: «Anakin looked at him and laughed.»
- Ch16 L1143: «But Yoda had insisted that Obi-Wan and Anakin attempt to speak personally with the Xi Charrian whose sigil had been discovered on the walking chair.»
- Ch16 L1143: «Anakin was correct about Obi-Wan's having had more than his share of such assignments.»
- Ch16 L1149: «"Do you think the Xi Char will talk to us?" Anakin said.»
- Ch16 L1155: «Anakin adopted a thin-lipped expression of wariness. "I hope they won't hold it against us that I destroyed so many of their fighters."»
- Ch16 L1161: «"Why's that?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch16 L1165: «"Wear your flaws proudly, TeeCee," Anakin said, raising and clenching his right hand. "I do, mine."»
- Ch16 L1171: «Anakin made adjustments to the controls to compensate for strong winds that were buffeting the ship. "I'll take it over Tatooine any day."»
- Ch16 L1177: «Anakin glanced at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch16 L1179: «"Guess they don't get many guests," Anakin said as he, Obi-Wan, and TC-16 started down the ramp.»
- Ch16 L1179: «The wild chitterings of the hundreds-strong mob of welcomers was so loud that Anakin had to raise his voice to be heard.»
- Ch16 L1183: «"Just be sure to follow my lead, Anakin."»
- Ch16 L1189: «Obi-Wan and Anakin watched in awe as patches of carbon scoring disappeared, dents were straightened, pieces of superstructure were realigned, and transparisteel viewports were polished.»
- Ch16 L1191: «"Let's remember to tip them when we leave," Anakin said. Occasionally a Xi Charrian would leap on TC-16 or make a grab for one of his limbs, but the droid was able to shake his assailants off.»
- Ch16 L1195: «"Would that be such a bad thing," Anakin said, "after what you claim to have been through?"»
- Ch16 L1203: «"No weapons," Anakin said quietly. "That's a good sign."»
- Ch16 L1209: «Anakin wondered aloud.»
- Ch16 L1211: «Without ceremony, TC-16 approached the Prelate and tendered a greeting. The droid had tasked his vocoder to provide Obi-Wan and Anakin with simultaneous translations of his utterances. "May I present Jedi Obi-Wan Kenobi, and Jedi Anakin Skywalker," he began.»
- Ch16 L1225: «Obi-Wan was already reaching for his lightsaber, but Anakin looked dubious. "You did say you would follow my lead."»
- Ch16 L1227: «Anakin opened his cloak. "I said I'd try, Master."»
- Ch16 L1231: «"That went better than expected," Obi-Wan said as he, Anakin, and TC-16 were being escorted into the heart of Workshop Xcan.»
- Ch16 L1233: «Anakin wasn't convinced. "You're too trusting, Master. I sense much suspicion."»
- Ch16 L1239: «Ten years back, Obi-Wan and Anakin had had their own brush with Sienar at the living world known as Zonama Sekot.»
- Ch16 L1239: «Here were all the priceless devices Obi-Wan and Anakin had found crammed into storage rooms in Gunray's Cato Neimoidia citadel.»
- Ch16 L1241: «The same pair of outsized aliens who had escorted Obi-Wan and Anakin to the Prelate's chancery guided them to t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak's altar, which was located in the workshop's western colonnade, the piers of which were decorated with mosaics of engraved tiles.»
- Ch16 L1249: «Anakin nudged Obi-Wan in the upper arm: Xi Charrians working nearby were beginning to take a keen interest in the conversation.»
- Ch16 L1255: «Obi-Wan watched him for a moment, then turned to Anakin. "I don't think he wants to speak with us."»
- Ch16 L1257: «Anakin kept his eyes on t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak. "Well, he has to." And with that, he leapt in pursuit.»
- Ch16 L1259: «"Anakin, wait!»
- Ch16 L1259: «Hurling himself from truss to truss like some circus performer, Anakin arrived quickly at the intricate tracery surrounding the partially opened roof window through which t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak was desperately trying to squirm.»
- Ch16 L1261: «The Xi Charrian's insectile forelegs were already outside the window when Anakin leapt again, clutching on to him in an effort to return him to the floor.»
- Ch16 L1261: «Chittering madly, he leapt for a higher window, this time taking Anakin with him.»
- Ch16 L1261: «Anakin was still trying to drag his quarry down, but his weight was insufficient to the task.»
- Ch16 L1261: «Fearing what might result should Anakin call too strongly on the Force - - Obi-Wan had visions of the entire workshop crumbing to pieces! - - he fairly flew after them, barely managing at the apex of his ascent to grab hold of t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak's rear legs.»
- Ch16 L1263: «Cascading onto the floor, Obi-Wan and Anakin lost their hold on t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak, and suddenly couldn't tell one Xi Charrian from the next.»
- Ch16 L1265: «"No mayhem!" Obi-Wan shouted. Anakin showed him a wide-eyed glance from beneath a three-meter-tall heap of irate Xi Charrians.»
- Ch16 L1271: «"Quickly, Anakin!" Even with his hands pinned under him, Anakin managed to upend a pallet of kitchen appliances, then knock over a carefully arranged collection of toys, then tear from the wall more than half a dozen sconces. Chittering in dismay, more Xi Charrians raced off.»
- Ch16 L1273: «Twenty Xi Charrians flattened themselves to the floor as the Prelate brought the blasters to bear on Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch16 L1275: «A babble of chitterings was exchanged, before the Prelate swung back to Obi-Wan and Anakin, raising the blasters once more.»
- Ch16 L1277: «"But they meant no harm, Excellency!" the droid intervened. "t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak fled in response to their questions! Master Obi-Wan and Jedi Skywalker sought merely to ascertain the reason!"»
- Ch16 L1293: «Obi-Wan and Anakin traded eager looks. "Was the hyperwave transceiver already affixed to it?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch16 L1297: «"He's afraid," Anakin said. "And not of Nute Gunray."»
- Ch16 L1305: «"Escarte?" Anakin said.»
- Ch17 L1323: «"We both know why," Mace said. "He's become Anakin's partisan."»
- Ch17 L1325: «"If the Chosen One Skywalker is, then a hundred such diplomatic incidents we should suffer without concern." Yoda shut his eyes for a moment, then looked at the Intelligence analyst. "But come to tell us of these things, Captain Dyne hasn't."»
- Ch17 L1369: «"Obi-Wan and Anakin aren't far from Belderone," Mace said. "But they're pursuing another lead to Sidious's whereabouts."»
- Ch17 L1371: «"Wait, the lead will. Needed Obi-Wan and Anakin will be." Yoda turned to the still image of General Grievous. "Prepare carefully for this battle, we must."»
- Ch19 L1445: «With its stubby wings and bulbous aft cockpit, Anakin's starfighter was closer in design to the Delta-7 Aethersprite he had flown at the start of the war than it was to the newer-generation V-wings and ARC-170s flown by clone pilots.»
- Ch19 L1447: «Plus, Anakin had made a few significant modifications.»
- Ch19 L1447: «Launched from the Integrity, Anakin poured on speed in an effort to catch up with the ARCs and V-wings that had been first to deploy from the assault cruiser's massive ventral bay.»
- Ch19 L1459: «Switching his comlink over to the battle net, Anakin hailed his wingmate. "I say we leave the small stuff to Odd Ball and the other pilots, and go straight for the ones that matter."»
- Ch19 L1461: «Accustomed to Anakin's disregard for call signs, Obi-Wan answered in kind. "Anakin, there are approximately five hundred droids positioned between Grievous and us. What's more, the capital ships are too heavily shielded."»
- Ch19 L1469: «Anakin scanned the threat-assessment display, committing to memory vector lines of the closest enemy fighters.»
- Ch19 L1473: «Blue lightning coruscated across the console, and droid fighters appeared to port and starboard. More bolts found their mark, throwing Anakin hard against the safety harness. "Just what I needed," he said, in appreciation. Swerving hard to starboard, he caught the first ship with a sideslip shot.»
- Ch19 L1475: «As it did, Anakin raced into its aft wash and triggered the lasers.»
- Ch19 L1475: «Anakin checked the display to make certain that Obi-Wan was still with him.»
- Ch19 L1489: «Anakin squared off with the enemy leader of the cloverleaf clash, and began to harass it with laser bolts. Adapting to his tactics, Obi-Wan fell back; then leapt his starfighter into kill position and opened up.»
- Ch19 L1491: «Anakin said when the wing leader vanished.»
- Ch19 L1491: «Signaling Obi-Wan to follow, Anakin climbed out of the main battle, veering tangent to it, and rocketed toward the nearest of the Separatists' needle-nosed picket ships.»
- Ch19 L1497: «The picket Anakin had piqued with missiles was under heavy bombardment.»
- Ch19 L1499: «Struck broadside, the picket burst like an overripe fruit, venting long plumes of incandescence and spilling light and guts into space. Anakin jinked away, whooping into the comlink.»
- Ch19 L1503: «"This hardly seems the time to bait him, Anakin. Have you had a look at those point-defense arrays?"»
- Ch19 L1509: «Anakin pushed the starfighter to its limits, paying out plasma and missiles that exploded harmlessly against the great ship's deflector shield.»
- Ch19 L1509: «Snap-rolling, Anakin slid the starfighter hard to port, belly-up, and continued to fire.»
- Ch19 L1511: «Anakin pictured Grievous standing stalwart behind the transparisteel viewports. "A taste of what's coming when we meet in the flesh," he growled.»
- Ch19 L1513: «Anakin Skywalker.»
- Ch19 L1513: «And in the starfighter that was guarding Anakin's stern: Obi-Wan Kenobi.»
- Ch19 L1523: «Obi-Wan wondered if he had ever witnessed a more lucid demonstration of the Jedi axiom than Anakin's Force-centered, death-defying harassment of Grievous's ship.»
- Ch19 L1523: «His speck of a starfighter all but nose-to-nose with the mammoth cruiser, leaving Obi-Wan to deal with the vengeful droid fighters Anakin was either ignorant of or deliberately disregarding.»
- Ch19 L1525: «But he was indifferent to his own fate, wondering instead: What if Anakin should be killed?»
- Ch19 L1525: «As the Chosen One, was he destined to fulfill both the title and the prophecy?»
- Ch19 L1527: «Though the cruiser's shield was removing the sting of Anakin's laser bolts, he could not be deterred from persevering.»
- Ch19 L1527: «Obi-Wan thought for a moment that Grievous was actually going to bring all forward guns to bear on Anakin.»
- Ch19 L1533: «"Anakin, the evacuees need our help!" Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch19 L1535: «"I'm coming, Master." Obi-Wan watched Anakin's starfighter break off its futile pursuit of the cruiser. Farther out, Separatist ships were disappearing from sight as they made the jump to lightspeed.»
- Ch22 L1685: «They waited for the Chosen One to be born, mistakenly believing that only he or she would be capable of restoring balance.»
- Ch22 L1691: «A Trade Federation blockade of Naboo, rumors of a Chosen One found on a desert world, the death of Qui-Gon Jinn at the hands of a Sith... How could the Council members not see what was happening? How could they continue to claim that the dark side obscured all?»
- Ch24 L1863: «"It was TeeCee-Sixteen who told us that," Anakin said. "Maybe something was lost in translation."»
- Ch24 L1865: «Obi-Wan and Anakin had the old white ship to themselves, and had exchanged their Jedi robes for outfits more suitable to itinerant spacers.»
- Ch24 L1867: «Obi-Wan and Anakin hadn't been told when or even if they were going to make contact with the agent, but moments before leaving Belderone they had been informed that Thal K'sar - - the Bith artisan who allegedly had designed the hyperwave transceiver and holoprojector for Gunray's mechno-chair - - had been arrested, on charges yet to be learned.»
- Ch24 L1871: «"Escarte," Anakin said.»
- Ch24 L1877: «Anakin activated the comm and said as much into the microphone.»
- Ch24 L1883: «"Intercept vector," Anakin said. "Any last-minute instructions, Master?"»
- Ch24 L1887: «Anakin grinned.»
- Ch24 L1891: «Obi-Wan already was, and so was able to remain more or less upright in the chair as Anakin firewalled the thrusters and threw the cruiser into a hard turn - - not away from the corvette, but aimed directly toward it.»
- Ch24 L1895: «"They're warning us away, Anakin."»
- Ch24 L1897: «Anakin kept the cruiser on course.»
- Ch24 L1913: «Dropping the cruiser's nose, Anakin increased speed. He seemed bent on maneuvering directly under the corvette, but at the last moment he pulled back on the control yoke, taking the cruiser through a spiraling, high-boost climb. A fusillade from the corvette's forward batteries narrowly missed clipping the ship's tail.»
- Ch24 L1919: «Obi-Wan saw that two patrol craft were rushing in to join the pursuit. With flashes of scarlet light racing alongside, Anakin whipped the cruiser through a teeth-rattling bank and shot for the thick of the asteroid belt.»
- Ch24 L1923: «Anakin had the ship tipped to one side, intent on weaving it through a cluster of rocks, when a laser bolt struck the closest asteroid.»
- Ch24 L1923: «Anakin took a firm grip on the control yoke and yanked the cruiser into a turn.»
- Ch24 L1923: «The patrol craft clung doggedly, angling to outflank the larger ship, but Anakin kept cheating the turn tighter and tighter, forcing the fighters to break off.»
- Ch24 L1923: «The cruiser had no sooner realigned itself than it gave a sudden lurch, snapping Obi-Wan and Anakin back into their seats, then forward into the console.»
- Ch24 L1923: «Anakin reached over his head to make adjustments, and the cruiser raced forward once more, only to freeze, then tremble.»
- Ch24 L1937: «Anakin showed him a long-suffering look.»
- Ch24 L1945: «Anakin's hands flew across the instruments, shutting down some systems and activating others.»
- Ch24 L1947: «"Don't try to fight it, Anakin. This ship won't hold together."»
- Ch24 L1949: «A deep shudder from the bowels of the cruiser reinforced his words. Anakin clenched his jaw, then let his hands fall to his sides.»
- Ch24 L1953: «Ordered out of the ship, Obi-Wan and Anakin stood at the foot of the boarding ramp with their hands clamped on top of their heads.»
- Ch24 L1957: «Anakin nodded slightly.»
- Ch24 L1963: «"Such drama," Anakin said.»
- Ch24 L1969: «The light-complected, blond security officer was as tall as Anakin and wider in the shoulders.»
- Ch24 L1969: «The captain looked Obi-Wan and Anakin up and down, then circled them once, hands clasped behind his back.»
- Ch24 L1979: «"We were hoping to find freelance work," Anakin said.»
- Ch24 L2011: «Drawing a blaster from his hip holster, he motioned Obi-Wan and Anakin toward the turbolifts.»
- Ch24 L2013: «The captain indicated an unoccupied lift and followed Obi-Wan and Anakin inside. When two Gossams hurried for the same lift, he waved them away. As soon as the door closed, he lowered his weapon and spoke with a sudden urgency.»
- Ch24 L2021: «Anakin's eyebrows met in a V.»
- Ch24 L2037: «"You could arrange that?" Anakin said.»
- Ch24 L2053: «Emerged from the enclosure of display screens, a four-eyed Aqualish led Obi-Wan and Anakin into a narrow corridor lined with detention cells.»
- Ch24 L2061: «"Some fix," Anakin said loudly when the cell sealed.»
- Ch24 L2067: «"Ah, she had it coming." Anakin ambled over to where K'sar was huddled. "What landed you in here?" he asked.»
- Ch24 L2069: «Though surprised to hear his own language spoken by a human, K'sar kept silent. When Anakin made a second attempt, the Bith said in Basic, "It's none of your concern. Please leave me alone."»
- Ch24 L2071: «Anakin shrugged and joined Obi-Wan on the far side of the room.»
- Ch24 L2081: «Anakin glanced at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch24 L2097: «"Us," Anakin amended. "You're coming along."»
- Ch24 L2103: «"How long before the power fails?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch24 L2107: «Anakin stood up and crossed the room.»
- Ch24 L2117: «"Take the tunnel to the left of the guard station," Travale was telling Obi-Wan when Anakin returned. "Keep taking lefts until you reach a stairway, then follow that to the docking level."»
- Ch24 L2119: «"You're going a different way?" Anakin said.»
- Ch24 L2125: «Anakin grinned at him. "I believe it's your turn..."»
- Ch24 L2129: «"That means K'sar goes with you. Don't allow him out of your sight, Anakin."»
- Ch24 L2135: «"Don't worry about them," Anakin said.»
- Ch24 L2141: «Anakin and Obi-Wan were standing by the door when the cell's grime-encrusted illuminator faltered and died. Obi-Wan shoved his hands sideways through the air, and the door retracted.»
- Ch24 L2145: «Anakin swung to K'sar. "Now! Hurry!"»
- Ch24 L2151: «Anakin wasn't halfway to the anteroom when one of the guards appeared at the end of the narrow corridor.»
- Ch24 L2151: «Before the guard could realize what was happening, his raised blaster was soaring down the corridor into Anakin's hand.»
- Ch24 L2153: «By then Obi-Wan and Anakin were on them, dropping them with punches, side kicks, Force pushes.»
- Ch24 L2163: «Anakin pressed his forefinger to his lips with elaborate seriousness.»
- Ch24 L2173: «"Here's where we part company," Travale said to Anakin. "Remember: stay left until you reach the stairway."»
- Ch24 L2183: «He swung back to Anakin.»
- Ch24 L2189: «As Travale and Obi-Wan were running off, Anakin noticed that one of the turbolift cars was descending.»
- Ch24 L2193: «Anakin nodded toward the dark corridor they were supposed to take.»
- Ch24 L2197: «K'sar's long legs propelled him at a fast clip. But instead of going left as Travale had advised, he turned right at the first intersection. Anakin grabbed him by the shoulder and spun him around.»
- Ch24 L2203: «Anakin regarded him in silence.»
- Ch24 L2207: «Anakin tapped him into motion. Several minutes of running brought them to a rickety stairway, which K'sar didn't hesitate to climb.»
- Ch24 L2209: «"I'd still like to know what you did to end up in detention," Anakin asked from behind K'sar.»
- Ch24 L2227: «Anakin heard a note of indecision in his voice.»
- Ch24 L2233: «The rock walls of the corridor into which they raced bore the scars of the giant drills that had hollowed Escarte. Light and oxygen were scant, and the uneven floor was slippery. Anakin clamped his right arm around the Bith's narrow waist to help him along.»
- Ch24 L2241: «Anakin prevented him from moving.»
- Ch25 L2283: «Perched at the juncture of four splayed legs, the hemispherical body was dominated by two huge circular photoreceptors, which appeared to be fixed on Anakin and K'sar as the droid rushed in to make the kill.»
- Ch25 L2285: «Anakin threw K'sar to one side and rolled as the dwarf spider fired.»
- Ch25 L2285: «The head pivoted, photoreceptors finding Anakin, and the weapon discharged again.»
- Ch25 L2285: «Anakin flipped himself away.»
- Ch25 L2287: «Anakin leapt.»
- Ch25 L2287: «Falling short of the mark, Anakin curled his body, counting on momentum to carry him forward.»
- Ch25 L2287: «Feigning a sidestep, Anakin hurled himself completely under the droid, but still couldn't find cover.»
- Ch25 L2289: «Without a clear plan in mind, Anakin chased it, heard the dome begin to pivot once more, then the sound of a hand blaster set on full automatic.»
- Ch25 L2293: «Anakin eased around the smoking machine and gently removed the blaster from the Bith's shaking grip. The droid's dome pinged as it cooled; a steady susurration escaped the blaster's gas chamber.»
- Ch25 L2295: «"How much farther?" Anakin asked after a moment.»
- Ch25 L2303: «Anakin took a moment to study the disposition of the droids, then turned to K'sar, who seemed to have recovered from the ordeal in the tunnel.»
- Ch25 L2307: «Anakin leapt out of the corridor, deliberately calling attention to himself to distract the droids from firing at K'sar.»
- Ch25 L2309: «Traversing the cruiser's fore and aft cannons, Anakin fired, burying the droids under huge chunks of ferrocrete blown from the walls and ceiling.»
- Ch25 L2313: «"Guild corvettes are probably already on the way." Anakin stepped closer to him, his expression darkening visibly.»
- Ch25 L2323: «Anakin glared at him from beneath an angrily furrowed brow.»
- Ch25 L2331: «K'sar started. His wrinkled mouth puckered and he stared at Anakin in terror.»
- Ch25 L2351: «"You're holding something back," Anakin said in a flat, menacing tone. "I can feel it."»
- Ch25 L2359: «Anakin blinked in surprise. "Raith Sienar?"»
- Ch25 L2383: «"The usual close calls," Anakin said evasively.»
- Ch25 L2389: «Anakin glanced at the console, waiting for the cofferdam telltale to go off, then called on the thrusters to move the cruiser away from Escarte.»
- Ch25 L2395: «Anakin shrugged.»
- Ch25 L2403: «Anakin busied himself with the controls.»
- Ch25 L2411: «Before Obi-Wan could reply, Anakin said: "Hyperspace coordinates coming in."»
- Ch26 L2513: «Dooku exhaled with purpose. "Skywalker and Kenobi."»
- Ch26 L2517: «"The so-called Chosen One, and a Jedi with enough good fortune to almost make one believe in luck."»
- Ch26 L2521: «Once Master and Padawan, Kenobi and Skywalker had become the scourge of Dooku's existence.»
- Ch26 L2521: «In the sloop's docking bay he had demonstrated his mastery to Kenobi and Skywalker - - although Skywalker hadn't been as easily defeated the second time they had dueled.»
- Ch26 L2521: «Long have I watched young Skywalker, Sidious had once admitted.»
- Ch26 L2527: «He paused to consider something, then said: "Yes, I begin to see the blazes along the trail Skywalker and Kenobi will follow."»
- Ch26 L2533: «"Nevertheless, Kenobi and young Skywalker will find their way to it."»
- Ch26 L2549: «Sidious shook his head. "But Skywalker does. And Kenobi... Kenobi has been as a father to him. Orphan Skywalker once and for all, and he will shift. "»
- Ch27 L2627: «"We can only hope that Obi-Wan and Anakin find their way to the source of this war before it's too late."»
- Ch28 L2631: «With a slurping sound, Anakin's right leg sank almost to the knee in the muck that passed for Naos III's main street.»
- Ch28 L2631: «An equally onomatopoeic sound accompanied Anakin's reclaiming of the leg, and expletives flew from his lips as he hopped off on his left foot toward solid ground.»
- Ch28 L2645: «Pieces of a collapsed skyway had been moved to one side of the street, left to marinate in a puddle much like the one Anakin had inadvertently waded into, and fashioned by areas of radiant heating that still functioned beneath the mostly ruined ceramacrete paving.»
- Ch28 L2647: «Anakin began stomping his boot on the solid ice. Ultimately the clingy, unidentifiable pink thing decided that it had had enough and flew off into a snowdrift.»
- Ch28 L2653: «Anakin gazed around.»
- Ch28 L2659: «Anakin winced. "You know it's time to end the partnership when that happens. In fact, I could see you and Yoda teaming up. You share the same fondness for caution and lectures."»
- Ch28 L2671: «Obi-Wan and Anakin might have gone directly to the source - - to Raith Sienar - - had Supreme Chancellor Palpatine not vetoed the idea.»
- Ch28 L2671: «Under its subsidiary, Rothana Heavy Engineering - - the builders of the Acclamator-class assault ships, as well as the AT-TE walkers - - KDY had also supplied the Confederacy with the Storm Fleet, which had been "the Terror of the Perlemian" until retired from service with the help of Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch28 L2677: «"On this street," Anakin said. "If we stop for a drink in each one, we'll be drunk before we reach the bridge."»
- Ch28 L2685: «Anakin grinned.»
- Ch28 L2693: «"Not a lot to do in Naos Three when you're not fishing," Anakin said. "I've the distinct impression that some drinking goes on even during work hours."»
- Ch28 L2695: «Replacing two Rodians who stumbled away from the curved bar, they ordered drinks. Anakin sipped from his glass.»
- Ch28 L2701: «Anakin shook his head.»
- Ch28 L2707: «Anakin took another sip.»
- Ch28 L2719: «"The way you were strutting around, what female could help herself?" Mimicking Anakin's voice, Obi-Wan said: "`Jedi business.'"»
- Ch28 L2725: «Anakin raised his glass.»
- Ch28 L2741: «"We're looking for a Lethan female," Anakin said.»
- Ch28 L2753: «"Look," Anakin said, "this one probably isn't a... masseuse."»
- Ch28 L2779: «Anakin and Obi-Wan traded confused glances.»
- Ch28 L2789: «"Jedi mind trick?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch28 L2799: «"Yes, Anakin, we should be on guard."»
- Ch28 L2805: «They made certain that their lightsabers were in easy reach, but left them affixed to their belts and concealed. Obi-Wan hit the door-release stud, then followed Anakin into the chill room.»
- Ch28 L2811: «"We're already at the legal limit," Anakin said, vigilant.»
- Ch28 L2819: «"Was that surprise or disappointment?" Anakin asked Obi-Wan.»
- Ch28 L2825: «"Only when necessary," Anakin said.»
- Ch28 L2835: «Anakin took a forward step.»
- Ch28 L2851: «"Tell us about the courier," Anakin said, cutting her off.»
- Ch28 L2869: «"Who was it built for?" Anakin said.»
- Ch28 L2877: «"Where did you deliver it?" Anakin pressed.»
- Ch28 L2883: «In hand and activated, Anakin's lightsaber was already a streak of blue light, deflecting blaster bolts and parrying swings of a vibro-ax in the meaty hands of a red-skinned Falleen who had burst through the door.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Off to Obi-Wan's left Anakin stood unmoving in the center of the room, holding his own against the two reptilian aliens and the wielder of the vibro-ax.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Screaming in anger, the alien raised a clawed hand to his bleeding brow ridge, even while his other hand continued to trigger bolts at Anakin.»
- Ch28 L2887: «As the bolts began to go wide, Anakin raised his left hand, pushing it through the air in the Trandoshan's direction and blowing him backward through the room's only window.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Determined to make the most of Anakin's split attention, the reptiloid's partner risked a lunge forward.»
- Ch28 L2889: «Anakin backed away from the circling blade, then dived forward, sliding across the wet floor on his belly with his lightsaber held out in front of him and amputating the Falleen's legs at the knees.»
- Ch28 L2889: «In midflight from the vibrating blade, Obi-Wan watched Anakin rid the Falleen of blaster and hand, and thrust his lightsaber directly into the Falleen's chest.»
- Ch28 L2895: «Deactivating his blade, Anakin pulled Fa'ale out from under the mattresses and bedding, and yanked her to her feet. Wobbling drunkenly, she took in the ruined room.»
- Ch28 L2899: «Catching sight of a bottle that had somehow survived the violence, she started for it. When Anakin tightened his hold on her, she balled her hands and hammered at his chest and upper arms.»
- Ch28 L2903: «"Not till we say it is," Anakin said.»
- Ch28 L2909: «Anakin began to drag her toward the door.»
- Ch28 L2913: «A blaster bolt destroyed what was left of the window frame. Anakin hauled Fa'ale to her feet once more and planted himself face-to-face with her.»
- Ch28 L2923: «Obi-Wan and Anakin followed her out the door to a utility closet at the end of the hall.»
- Ch28 L2923: «Anakin went next.»
- Ch28 L2929: «Anakin had already straddled the machine's narrow seat, and was studying the controls.»
- Ch28 L2933: «With the flip of a switch, the speeder's engine coughed to life, then began to purr loudly in the hollow of the cave. Obi-Wan mounted a second sled, while Fa'ale was positioning herself behind Anakin.»
- Ch28 L2935: «"That one, then that one," Anakin said, pointing out the ignition switch and the warmer. Demonstrating, he added: "Thrusters, pitch control, steer like this."»
- Ch28 L2939: «"Like this, like this!" Anakin emphasized, demonstrating again, then indicating another set of switches on the control panel of Obi-Wan's machine.»
- Ch29 L2959: «The repulsorlift would have allowed him to fly over the obstacles - - as Anakin was doing, farther downriver - - but Obi-Wan just couldn't get the hang of it.»
- Ch29 L2967: «Up ahead, he saw Anakin and Fa'ale lean their sled through a sweeping turn to the south, between two of Naos III's many hills.»
- Ch29 L2967: «Lethal hyphens of light streaked down from the bridge that linked the hills, but not one found Anakin or Fa'ale.»
- Ch29 L2967: «Unable to replicate Anakin's deft turns, Obi-Wan was falling farther behind with each quarter kilometer, and was now making himself an easy target for the assassins on the bridge.»
- Ch29 L2975: «Suspended on a swaying cable, the claw began to ascend toward the open belly of a graceless snow skiff. Red hands clamped around Anakin's waist, Fa'ale whooped and shouted, clearly enjoying herself. Even through the daze of too many drinks - - or more likely because of them.»
- Ch29 L2979: «"Been there, done that," Anakin said over his shoulder.»
- Ch29 L2979: «Bringing brakes and thrusters to bear, Anakin powered the sled through a fast 180 and shot back upriver, under the bridge they had just left behind, dodging the unrelenting fire of hand blasters.»
- Ch29 L2987: «"Get ready to take the control bars!" Anakin said.»
- Ch29 L2995: «Pouring on all speed, Anakin steered the sled up the side of the hill that supported one half of the bridge.»
- Ch29 L2995: «The pilots of the skiff saw him coming, and banked hard to starboard, but not soon enough to prevent Anakin from latching onto the claw.»
- Ch29 L3003: «Obi-Wan heard the snap-hiss of Anakin's lightsaber. Peering through the metal fingers of the claw, he saw what was coming.»
- Ch29 L3005: «"Anakin, wait - - "»
- Ch29 L3007: «As the claw came within reach of the cargo hold, Anakin swung his lightsaber and sliced open the floor of the skiff's cockpit.»
- Ch29 L3007: «An instant before the crash, Anakin severed the claw cable, and the cage plummeted, striking the slippery ground and racing down to the frozen river, out onto the ice, spinning crazily, with Obi-Wan bouncing around inside and Anakin Force-fastened to the outside through all the unpredictable pitches and tumbles.»
- Ch29 L3009: «The skiff crashed into the hillside. By the time the claw came to a rest on the far side of the river, the two Jedi were so covered in snow they looked like wampas. Anakin's lightsaber made short work of the fingers of the claw. Obi-Wan scrambled out, spitting snow, and shaking like a hound.»
- Ch29 L3019: «Anakin scanned the hillsides.»
- Ch29 L3019: «Gently turning her over, Anakin saw that one bolt had amputated the Twi'lek's right lekku.»
- Ch29 L3033: «"They're gone," Anakin said. Fa'ale shook her head. "After all these years, they finally - - "»
- Ch29 L3039: «Anakin raised her up.»
- Ch30 L3061: «The Lethan pilot Obi-Wan and Anakin had searched out on Naos III hadn't been able to furnish anything more than a portrait of the building to which she had delivered the star courier.»
- Ch32 L3329: «Two was bad enough - - in the form they had been used by Darth Maul, or in Anakin Skywalker's sad attempt to employ the technique on Geonosis.»
- Ch32 L3371: «"Born, no doubt, of my failure at Naos Three. The ones I sent had every opportunity to kill Kenobi, Skywalker, and the Twi'lek pilot. Instead, they decided to attempt their capture, to extract additional funds from me, as well as to bolster their reputations."»
- Ch32 L3379: «"The Force is strong in Skywalker," Sidious went on.»
- Ch32 L3453: «"We won't have to, Lord Tyranus. Their own investigations will lead them to that conclusion. Moreover, Kenobi and Skywalker will oversee the counterattack."»
- Ch32 L3475: «"Hold Kenobi and Skywalker, Lord Tyranus. Entertain them. Play to their weakness. Demonstrate your mastery, as you have on previous occasions."»
- Ch33 L3497: «"Fa'ale is doing fine," Anakin said as he approached Obi-Wan jauntily. "Two more days of bacta and she'll be on her feet. She says she's through with Naos Three, though. She might even remain here on Belderone."»
- Ch33 L3503: «Anakin frowned.»
- Ch33 L3511: «Anakin stepped deliberately into Obi-Wan's gaze.»
- Ch33 L3521: «Anakin was staring dumbfounded at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch33 L3533: «Anakin shrugged impatiently.»
- Ch33 L3543: «Anakin mulled it over for a moment.»
- Ch33 L3557: «Anakin turned his back to Obi-Wan. His face was flushed when he finally swung around.»
- Ch33 L3567: «"Anakin, Mace and Shaak Ti are more than capable of seeing to that - - if Sidious is even there."»
- Ch33 L3569: «Anakin was shaking his head.»
- Ch33 L3577: «"All that's changed!" Anakin said, becoming angrier as he spoke. "I'm stronger than I was. You're stronger. Together, we can defeat any Sith."»
- Ch33 L3579: «"Anakin, is this really about capturing Sidious?"»
- Ch33 L3601: «Anakin started to reply, bit back whatever it was he had in mind to say, and began again.»
- Ch33 L3629: «"Anakin, is it Padme?"»
- Ch33 L3631: «Anakin rolled his eyes.»
- Ch33 L3637: «Anakin compressed his lips, then said, "I won't lie to you and say that I don't miss her."»
- Ch33 L3651: «Anakin's brows beetled in anger.»
- Ch33 L3653: «"I never claimed to be the Chosen One. That was Qui-Gon. Even the Council doesn't believe it anymore, so why should you?"»
- Ch33 L3661: «Anakin regarded him.»
- Ch33 L3669: «Obi-Wan rested his hands on Anakin's shoulders.»
- Ch34 L3761: «"Only because our quarry is aware that we're closing in on him. He failed to silence the ones Obi-Wan and Anakin searched out, and by now he knows that we've discovered his and Dooku's den. It's unlikely he will wait around for us to surprise him."»
- Ch34 L3763: «"That's true. But there's much to gain from simply identifying him. If not here, then by means of something Obi-Wan and Anakin discover on Tythe."»
- Ch35 L3781: «As big as life, Palpatine's holoimage spoke from atop a projector table in a private comlink lounge aboard the medical frigate. With R2-D2 standing off to one side of the transmission grid, Anakin hung on the Supreme Chancellor's every word.»
- Ch35 L3787: «"It's obvious that you're upset, Anakin, but you must be patient. Your time will come."»
- Ch35 L3795: «Anakin's face contorted.»
- Ch35 L3809: «"I suppose I could invent some pretext..." His gaze returned to Anakin. "But is that wise?"»
- Ch35 L3815: «"He's the one who suggested I contact you," Anakin said sharply.»
- Ch35 L3819: «Anakin blew out his breath.»
- Ch35 L3823: «"Your former Master is wiser than you think, Anakin."»
- Ch35 L3837: «Palpatine waited until he was certain Anakin was finished.»
- Ch35 L3841: «Anakin nodded.»
- Ch35 L3849: «Anakin fought the temptation to reveal everything about the search for Darth Sidious. He glanced at R2-D2, as if expecting commiseration, but the astromech only swiveled his dome, his processor status indicator flashing from blue to red.»
- Ch35 L3851: «Finally Anakin said: "I don't know what to do, sir."»
- Ch35 L3859: «"No," Anakin said in a rush. "No. Thank you, sir, but... I'm needed on Tythe. Dooku is there."»
- Ch35 L3875: «"Did you get through to him?" he asked as Anakin and R2-D2 approached.»
- Ch35 L3881: «Anakin averted his gaze.»
- Ch35 L3889: «Anakin looked at him.»
- Ch35 L3899: «"Yes, there is," Anakin cut him off. "And you're living proof of it."»
- Ch35 L3905: «Anakin's eyes tracked the approaching light.»
- Ch35 L3909: «He still wasn't smiling. Obi-Wan closed his hand around Anakin's upper right arm.»
- Ch35 L3911: «"Anakin, let's get Dooku and end this."»
- Ch35 L3913: «Anakin swallowed and nodded.»
- Ch37 L3951: «"Just once I wish we could repay Dooku and Grievous in kind," Anakin said over the tactical net, as Red Squadron dropped from the belly of the Integrity and rocketed toward Tythe.»
- Ch37 L3955: «Anakin grunted.»
- Ch37 L3963: «"Prepare to break hard to starboard," Anakin said over the net to the entire squadron. "Watch your countdown displays. On my mark, ten seconds to break..."»
- Ch37 L3971: «"They know their job," Anakin responded.»
- Ch37 L3975: «The Jedi had insisted nevertheless that an attempt be made to take Dooku alive. Obi-Wan and Anakin didn't need to be reminded of what had happened only weeks earlier on Cato Neimoidia when they had gone after Viceroy Gunray, but they were not about to forgo a chance to capture the Sith Lord.»
- Ch37 L3977: «With droid fighters still pouring from the curving arms of Trade Federation Lucrehulks, and the recoiling barrels of Commerce Guild cannons filling local space with storms of unleashed energy, Anakin led the starfighters on a weaving course through the heart of the enemy fleet.»
- Ch37 L3997: «"We'll end up too far off course," Anakin said.»
- Ch37 L4003: «There was no time to argue the point. Rolling to port, Obi-Wan fell in behind Anakin and fired his thrusters. Trailing behind, Red Squadron accelerated and banked for the narrow-waisted vessel.»
- Ch37 L4005: «"Ready proton torpedoes," Anakin said. "Sow them just above the fuel cells."»
- Ch37 L4007: «Tight on Anakin, the ten remaining starfighters yawed for the waist of the ship, just forward of its cluster of cylindrical fuel cells.»
- Ch37 L4007: «Dropping his craft to within one hundred meters of the pinched hull, Anakin began to hug the surface, surging onto a course that would whip Red Squadron through a tight circle around the forward ends of the fuel cells.»
- Ch37 L4013: «The disabling run completed, Anakin boosted for Tythe.»
- Ch37 L4017: «Obi-Wan regained control of his craft and once more attached himself to Anakin's six.»
- Ch37 L4019: «"Insertion point in fifteen seconds," Anakin updated. "Dial inertial compensators to maximum. All power to the ablative shields. Deceleration burn on my mark..."»
- Ch37 L4023: «Punching through the blankets of clouds at suicidal velocity, Obi-Wan's starfighter went into a roll. Pinwheeling before his eyes, Tythe was a kaleidoscopic furor of white and brown, smeared occasionally with striations of blue-green. Anakin's voice grew loud in his ears.»
- Ch37 L4035: «"Locking in target coordinates," Anakin said.»
- Ch37 L4041: «"Target facility is ten kilometers west," Anakin said. "We should be hearing from artillery soon enough."»
- Ch37 L4045: «Anakin and Obi-Wan paid out the last of their proton torpedoes in futile attempts to save Reds Three, Four, and Five.»
- Ch37 L4047: «When they had juked their way through the worst of it, Anakin came alongside Obi-Wan. It was just the two of them now.»
- Ch37 L4049: «"Point three-oh," Anakin said. "On the landing platform."»
- Ch37 L4059: «"There's another way," Anakin said, as they were emerging from their flyby. "We go in through the north dome."»
- Ch37 L4067: «"Radiation?" Anakin laughed. "The maneuver alone will probably kill us!"»
- Ch39 L4189: «In the ruined archive hall of LiMerge Power's plasma facility, Count Dooku waited for Kenobi and Skywalker to arrive.»
- Ch39 L4191: «Jedi like Kenobi and Skywalker, who wanted to believe as much.»
- Ch39 L4195: «That sense of entitlement was evident in the way Kenobi and Skywalker called on the Force in their fervor to confront him: opening doors with waves of their hands, clearing obstacles from their path with similar gestures, moving with what appeared to be numinous speed and agility, flourishing their blue blades as if they were powered by the will of the Force itself...»
- Ch39 L4197: «It was more important, though, that Kenobi and Skywalker be able to see him, than he them.»
- Ch39 L4201: «"Dooku!" young Skywalker said, as if his tone of voice should suffice to send shivers down the backbones of his opponents. "Show yourself!"»
