- Ch39 L4207: «Instead of replying, Kenobi touched Skywalker on the arm, and the two of them began to scan the hall, no doubt in an attempt to locate him through the Force.»
- Ch39 L4219: «"- - beginning to... things almost as much... I hate sand," Skywalker was saying to his former mentor as he raised his lightsaber over one shoulder.»
- Ch39 L4225: «Touched by their camaraderie, Dooku smiled to himself. Darth Sidious had his work cut out for him if he ever expected to turn Skywalker to the dark side.»
- Ch39 L4245: «Did he know about Skywalker?»
- Ch39 L4245: «What better way to ensure total victory than by killing or corrupting the Chosen One?»
- Ch39 L4245: «Someone birthed by the Force itself, Qui-Gon would have said - - never a doubt that Anakin's mother might have been lying.»
- Ch39 L4249: «The Sith were aware of Skywalker. How would he react when they tried finally to ensnare him?»
- Ch41 L4365: «In what had served as the archive room for LiMerge Power's plasma facility, droid parts were piling up so fast and so high that Obi-Wan and Anakin could scarcely see Dooku's wavering holoimage any longer.»
- Ch41 L4367: «Neither he nor Anakin was relying on lightsabers only.»
- Ch41 L4367: «Force-pushing four droids to the floor, hewing half a dozen more with his flashing blade, Anakin leapt from Obi-Wan's side, landed on the head of a perplexed droid, and began to race toward the far side of the hall, using other heads as stepping-stones.»
- Ch41 L4371: «"Dooku!" Anakin snarled through clenched teeth. "I will kill you!"»
- Ch41 L4373: «"Control your rage, Anakin," Obi-Wan managed to say between breaths. "Don't give him the satisfaction."»
- Ch41 L4375: «Anakin shot him a worrisome scowl. "Can't have me becoming too powerful, now, can we, Master?"»
- Ch41 L4377: «Whirling, he deflected their first barrage, then fought his way to cover behind a heap of dismembered droids, where Anakin joined him.»
- Ch41 L4379: «More droids appeared. To Dooku, this was nothing more than a game, Obi-Wan told himself. But if it was a demonstration of Force ability Dooku wanted, then Anakin was still more than willing to provide it. "Dooku!" he howled.»
- Ch42 L4393: «Where is Anakin?»
- Ch42 L4397: «That meant that Anakin would be recalled.»
- Ch42 L4445: «"We're doomed!" C-3PO said. Padme understood that Bail was swerving for a landing platform that abutted a wide skybridge. Tears streaming from her eyes, stricken with a sudden nausea, she placed her right hand on her abdomen. Anakin! she said to herself. Anakin!»
- Ch45 L4661: «Had the containment dome been so weak that it had succumbed to flurries of ricocheting blaster bolts, or had Skywalker's voiced rage actually called the ceiling down?»
- Ch45 L4661: «But Skywalker...»
- Ch45 L4663: «Because admitting to any alternative explanation meant accepting that Skywalker was potentially a greater threat to the Sith than anyone realized.»
- Ch45 L4663: «Initially, it had cheered him to observe that Skywalker and Kenobi had finally learned to fight together; to see how powerful they had become in partnership.»
- Ch45 L4663: «Kenobi making full use of his inherent discretion to balance young Skywalker's inattentive rowdiness.»
- Ch46 L4725: «What had the Jedi gotten Anakin into - - taking him from Tatooine, from his youth, his mother?»
- Ch46 L4725: «What had she gotten Anakin into?»
- Ch48 L4827: «In the darkness, buried alive, Anakin stretched out with his feelings.»
- Ch48 L4837: «Anakin shook the vision from his mind. "I told you I was becoming more powerful."»
- Ch48 L4843: «"Go ahead," Anakin said. "If you don't say it, I will."»
- Ch48 L4853: «Anakin watched the sloop for a moment longer, then looked at Obi-Wan. "That's not the reason, Master. We've been tricked. Tythe was never the target. We were."»
- Ch52 L5047: «"We were all beginning to wonder if you were going to return," a human crew chief said by way of welcoming Obi-Wan and Anakin back to the assault cruiser's ventral landing bay. Obi-Wan descended the ladder affixed to the starfighter's cockpit. "When did the Separatists jump?"»
- Ch52 L5051: «Leaping to the deck, Anakin laughed nastily. "Believe whatever you want."»
- Ch52 L5063: «Anakin took a sudden interest in the crew chief's remarks. "Could be local interference," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch52 L5067: «Anakin shot Obi-Wan an embittered look and began to storm away.»
- Ch52 L5069: «"Anakin," Obi-Wan said, following in his footsteps.»
- Ch52 L5071: «Anakin whirled on him. "We were wrong to come here, Master. I was wrong to come here. It was all a feint, and we fell for it. We're being kept away from Coruscant. I can feel it."»
- Ch52 L5077: «Obi-Wan regarded him carefully. "See what, Anakin?"»
- Ch52 L5079: «Anakin started to speak, then cut himself off and began again. "You should keep me fighting. You shouldn't give me time to think."»
- Ch52 L5081: «Obi-Wan rested his hands on Anakin's shoulders. "Calm yourself."»
- Ch52 L5083: «Anakin shrugged him off, a new fire in his eyes. "You're my best friend. Tell me what I should do. Forget for a moment that you're wearing the robes of a Jedi and tell me what I should do!"»
- Ch52 L5085: «Stung by the gravity in Anakin's voice, Obi-Wan fell silent for a moment, then said: "The Force is our ally, Anakin. When we're mindful of the Force, our actions are in accord with the will of the Force. Tythe wasn't a wrong choice. It's simply that we're ignorant of its import in the greater scheme."»
- Ch52 L5087: «Anakin lowered his head in sadness. "You're right, Master. My mind isn't as fast as my lightsaber." He stared at his artificial limb. "My heart isn't as impervious to pain as my right hand."»
- Ch52 L5089: «Obi-Wan felt as if someone had knotted his insides. He had failed his apprentice and closest friend. Anakin was suffering, and the only balm he offered were Jedi platitudes. His body heaved a stuttering breath. He had his mouth open to speak when the crew chief interrupted.»
- Ch52 L5091: «"General Skywalker, something has your astromech very flustered."»
- Ch52 L5093: «Obi-Wan and Anakin swung to Anakin's starfighter.»
- Ch52 L5095: «"Artoo?" Anakin said in a concerned tone. The astromech tooted, shrilled, chittered.»
- Ch52 L5097: «"Does he understand droid?" the crew chief asked Obi-Wan as Anakin hurried past him.»
- Ch52 L5099: «"That droid," Obi-Wan said. Anakin began to scale the cockpit ladder.»
- Ch52 L5103: «The droid whistled and zithered. Throwing himself into open cockpit, Anakin toggled switches. Obi-Wan had just reached the base of the ladder when he heard Palpatine's voice issuing through the cockpit annunciators.»
- Ch52 L5105: «"Anakin, if you are receiving this message, then I have urgent need of your help..."»
- Ch52 L5107: «The crew chief's comlink toned. Obi-Wan glanced from the crew chief to Anakin and back again. "What is it?" he asked in a rush.»
- Ch52 L5111: «Obi-Wan gaped at him. Above him, Anakin lifted his face to the high ceiling and let out a sustained snarl. Glaring down at Obi-Wan, he said: "Why does fate target the people who are most important to me?"»
- Ch52 L5113: «"Crew chief!" Anakin cut him off. "Refuel and rearm our starfighters at once!"»
- Ch53 L5133: «Anakin, she said to herself, as if she could reach him with a thought.»
- Ch53 L5135: «Anakin.»
- Ch53 L5137: «She wept for a future she and Anakin might have had.»
- Ch55 L5251: «And Palpatine was hardly the prize he appeared to be. The greater prize, Sidious had told Dooku during their most recent communication, was Anakin Skywalker.»
- Ch55 L5257: «"The depth of his anger. His willingness to go beyond the Force, as the Jedi know it, and to call on the power of the dark side. General Grievous will activate a special beacon that will call Skywalker and Kenobi back to Coruscant, and onto the stage we will set for them."»
- Ch55 L5261: «His only purpose is to die and, in so doing, ignite young Skywalker to tap the depths of his fear and rage.»
- Ch55 L5261: «Should you defeat Skywalker easily, then we will know that he is not prepared to serve us.»
- Ch56 L5271: «Neither pilot wore a helmet, so Anakin could hear Obi-Wan plainly when he shouted: "For all the finks and jukes you've taken me through, there's no one else I'd rather fly with."»
- Ch56 L5273: «Anakin canted his head and smiled. "It's about time you admitted it. Can I take that to mean you'll follow my lead without question?"»
- Ch56 L5279: «"The day you call for help, I'll know that we're both in over our heads." Anakin adopted a serious look. "Obi-Wan, you don't know how many times you've already rescued me."»
- Ch56 L5283: «Anakin laughed lightly. "Who'll restore peace to the galaxy if we don't?"»

### B.5 Obi-Wan: 481 entries. Aliases: `Obi-Wan|Obi-War|Kenobi`. Version: Obi-Wan, 19 BBY (Ch7 spore-intoxicated)

- Ch1 L21: «A flashing fan of blue energy lit the undersides of a cluster of trees: the lightsaber of Obi-Wan Kenobi.»
- Ch1 L23: «Attacked by two sentry droids, Obi-Wan stood his ground, twisting his upraised blade right and left to swat blaster bolts back at his enemies.»
- Ch1 L23: «Obi-Wan moved again.»
- Ch1 L25: «The crushing sounds of their ceaseless gnawing provided an eerie cadence to the rumbling detonations and the hiss and whine of blaster bolts. From off to Obi-Wan's left came a sudden click of servos; to his right, a hushed cry of warning.»
- Ch1 L29: «A blaster discharged into soft soil, then the stalked, elongated head of a battle droid struck the ground not a meter from Obi-Wan's feet, sparking as it bounced and rolled out of sight, repeating: "Roger, roger...»
- Ch1 L31: «In a tuck, Obi-Wan pivoted on his right foot in time to see the droid's spindly body collapse. The fact that Anakin had saved his life was nothing new, but Anakin's blade had passed a little too close for comfort. Eyes somewhat wide with surprise, he came to his feet.»
- Ch1 L41: «His mechanical right hand sheathed in an elbow-length glove. He had let his hair grow long the past few months, falling almost to his shoulders now. His face he kept clean-shaven, unlike Obi-Wan, whose strong jaw was defined by a short beard.»
- Ch1 L55: «Obi-Wan scowled.»
- Ch1 L59: «They resumed their charge, moving with the supernatural speed and grace afforded by the Force, Obi-Wan's brown cloak swirling behind him.»
- Ch1 L65: «Cato Neimoidia was as close to Coruscant as Obi-Wan and Anakin had been in almost four standard months, and with the last remaining Separatist strongholds now cleared from the Core and Colonies, they expected to be back in the Outer Rim by week's end.»
- Ch1 L65: «Obi-Wan heard movement on the far side of the irrigation canal.»
- Ch1 L67: «A few nimble leaps brought Cody face-to-face with Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch1 L75: «"Generals Kenobi and Skywalker, provincial recon unit reports that Viceroy Gunray and his entourage are making their way to the north side of the redoubt.»
- Ch1 L77: «Obi-Wan looked at Cody when the hologram had faded.»
- Ch1 L85: «Over the years Obi-Wan had formed battlefield partnerships with several Advanced Recon Commandos - - Alpha, with whom he had been imprisoned on Rattatak, and Jangotat, on Ord Cestus.»
- Ch1 L89: «"I agree that we can probably reach the upper levels, Commander," Obi-Wan said at last. "But how do you propose we reach the fungus farms to begin with? "»
- Ch1 L93: «Obi-Wan glanced uncertainly at Anakin and motioned him off to one side. "It's just the two of us. What do you think?"»
- Ch1 L95: «"I think you worry too much, Master." Obi-Wan folded his arms across his chest. "And who'll worry about you if I don't?"»
- Ch1 L101: «"Think what you will." Obi-Wan narrowed his eyes with purpose. "Oh, I see. But I would have thought Senator Amidala of greater interest to you than Supreme Chancellor Palpatine." Before Anakin could respond, he added: "Despite that she's a politician also."»
- Ch1 L105: «Obi-Wan regarded Anakin for a moment. "What's more, if Chancellor Palpatine had genuine concern for your welfare, he would have kept you closer to Coruscant."»
- Ch1 L107: «Anakin placed his artificial hand on Obi-Wan's left shoulder. "Perhaps, Master. But then, who would look after you?"»
- Ch2 L115: «Obi-Wan, Anakin, and the commandos who made up Squad Seven had to run to keep up with the beetles as they hurried their fresh-picked loads from the orchard to the cave-like entrance to a natural mound at the base of the redoubt.»
- Ch2 L117: «Hunched over, Obi-Wan ran with his lightsaber in hand but deactivated.»
- Ch2 L117: «Obi-Wan suspected that outbound beetles were relaying accounts of potential perils to the nest posed by the Republic's unrelenting barrage.»
- Ch2 L117: «To Obi-War's right ran Cody, with his teammates trailing behind and flanking him.»
- Ch2 L119: «Obi-Wan felt a ripple in the Force an instant before the harvester's right foreleg tripped a land mine.»
- Ch2 L125: «Obi-Wan was doing just that when he noticed that the trampled commando was back on his feet and staggering toward him, tapping the side of his helmet with the palm of his gloved hand, and obviously indifferent to where he placed his booted feet.»
- Ch2 L129: «Obi-Wan could almost smell the pheromone release, and decipher the information being exchanged: The area is rife with predators!»
- Ch2 L133: «By the time Obi-Wan arrived, the troopers were deployed in a circle, and continuing to pour fire into the sky.»
- Ch2 L133: «Obi-Wan gazed at the face of the injured clone.»
- Ch2 L147: «Cody pressed a pair of macrobinoculars to his eyes and gazed upward. "That's no ordinary avalanche," he said, passing the glasses to Obi-Wan.»
- Ch2 L149: «Obi-Wan raised the glasses and waited for the lens to autofocus. Rolling toward the trench at better than eighty kilometers per hour were some of the most feared of the Separatists' infantry arsenal.»
- Ch3 L161: «Cody was more than willing to comply. After all, the order had come directly from "the Hero with no Fear," as Anakin was sometimes known. "The Warrior of the Infinite." There was, though, a chain of command to maintain, so Cody looked to Obi-Wan for confirmation.»
- Ch3 L163: «Obi-Wan nodded. "Do as he says."»
- Ch3 L169: «"Pulse weapons don't discriminate, Commander," Obi-Wan thought to point out.»
- Ch3 L177: «Then: a nova-bright flare of white light, followed two seconds later by a roar that turned Obi-Wan's eardrums to mush.»
- Ch3 L181: «Still without helmet, Cody stood up, signaling with both hands. Obi-Wan interpreted the gestures: Sixty count, then suit up and break for the entrance to the nest. He prepared by calming himself.»
- Ch3 L187: «Obi-Wan had never visited a fungus farm, but no sooner had he and Anakin dashed through the cave-like opening to the nest than the briefings he had received more than ten standard years earlier came back to him in a flash.»
- Ch3 L191: «Cody ordered the squad to secure the area. Those in the rear were still taking sporadic fire from STAPs, but the droid pilots couldn't get close to the entrance because of the bodies of dead beetles piled outside. Squad Seven's medspec hurried over to Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch3 L195: «Obi-Wan quirked his brows together. "Toxic, Sergeant?"»
- Ch3 L201: «Obi-Wan glanced at Anakin.»
- Ch3 L201: «Obi-Wan leapt to one side, raising his blade to deal with two bolts that got past Anakin.»
- Ch3 L207: «"The beauty of Form Three," Obi-Wan said with theatrical nonchalance. "You should try it sometime."»
- Ch3 L211: «Obi-Wan rolled his eyes. "Master of understatement."»
- Ch3 L213: «"General Kenobi," the comm spec said from across the grotto. "Provincial recon reports that Viceroy Gunray and his entourage are heading for the launching bays. They're protected by super battle droids, a group of which are now closing on our position."»
- Ch3 L215: «Anakin swung to Obi-Wan. "One of us has to divert the droids."»
- Ch3 L217: «"One of us," Obi-Wan repeated. "Haven't we been through this before?"»
- Ch3 L221: «Obi-Wan compressed his lips. "From a certain point of view, Anakin."»
- Ch3 L225: «"That makes no sense," Obi-Wan said quickly, shaking his head. "We play to our separate strengths."»
- Ch3 L231: «Obi-Wan, Cody, and the rest of Squad Seven set out for the turbolift shafts. Obi-Wan hadn't gone five meters when he stopped and swung around. "Anakin, I know we've got a score to settle with Gunray, but don't make it personal. We want to take him alive!"»
- Ch4 L235: «Oh, but it is personal, Anakin told himself while he watched Obi-Wan, Cody, and four troopers disappear into the turbolift.»
- Ch4 L235: «Even Obi-Wan didn't know, for that would have created problems.»
- Ch4 L237: «Even if he could put all that aside, as Obi-Wan plainly wanted him to do, it was personal because Gunray had aligned himself with Dooku and the Separatists, and the war they had planned from the start had brought ruin to a thousand worlds.»
- Ch4 L241: «Yes, just as Yoda had sensed after Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan had freed him from slavery on Tatooine and brought him to the Jedi Temple, he had a lot of anger in him.»
- Ch4 L241: «Twice he might have been able to kill Dooku himself had Obi-Wan not held him back.»
- Ch4 L243: «For all his skills, Anakin still looked to Obi-Wan for guidance.»
- Ch4 L243: «On the fly he used the Force to call the thing to his left hand and realized that it was Obi-Wan's rebreather, which must have fallen from its utility pouch during the brief exchange with the unseen battle droids.»
- Ch4 L243: «But no matter; Obi-Wan was probably already in the lower levels of the redoubt, where there would be little need for the device.»
- Ch5 L265: «Qui-Gon Jinn hadn't believed in baiting, Obi-Wan thought as he and the commandos rode the turbolift to the fortress's lowest level.»
- Ch5 L267: «For a time the same issues had stood at the center of Obi-Wan's conflicts with Anakin.»
- Ch5 L267: «But as Obi-Wan had told him time and again, the essence of being a Jedi didn't hinge on attaining mastery of the Force, but on attaining mastery over oneself.»
- Ch5 L267: «Qui-Gon had had the insight to recognize it more than a decade earlier, and Obi-Wan felt duty-bound to his former Master to help Anakin fulfill his destiny.»
- Ch5 L271: «If Obi-Wan was, as Anakin sometimes said, the father he never had, then Palpatine was his wise uncle, adviser, mentor in the ways of life outside the Temple.»
- Ch5 L271: «Obi-Wan understood that Anakin envied him for having been appointed to the Council.»
- Ch5 L271: «But just as often it had been Obi-Wan's circumspection that had pulled them back from the brink.»
- Ch5 L271: «Whether foresight was something innate in Obi-Wan or the result of his continuing fascination with the unifying Force - - the long view - - Obi-Wan couldn't say.»
- Ch5 L275: «"The next stop is ours, General," Cody said from behind him. Obi-Wan turned, watched Cody slam a new blasterpack into his DC-15, heard the familiar whine of the weapon's repower mechanism. Reflexively, he placed his thumb on the lightsaber's activator button.»
- Ch5 L283: «Obi-Wan recalled a conversation he had had on Ord Cestus with a clone trooper named Nate, regarding analogies between the Jedi and the clones: the former ushered by midi-chlorians to serve the Force; the latter, grown and programmed to serve the Republic.»
- Ch5 L285: «But Obi-Wan wondered what he would have had to say about the war.»
- Ch5 L287: «Obi-Wan knew, because the corridor quickly became a torrent of blaster bolts.»
- Ch5 L287: «Two commandos fell to fire while Obi-Wan's team was making its way down the corridor in the direction of the citadel's packing and shipping rooms.»
- Ch5 L289: «Cody said while he, Obi-Wan, and two commandos fought their way into a side room.»
- Ch5 L291: «He tapped Obi-Wan on the shoulder.»
- Ch5 L291: «Obi-Wan shot for the room, deflecting bolts and mangling two super battle droids that stood in his way.»
- Ch5 L291: «Obi-Wan glanced at the wall-mounted mechanism that operated the sliding doors.»
- Ch5 L293: «Each time they opened, a battle droid would squeeze into the room, firing away, forcing Obi-Wan back toward the entryway through which he had originally come, where a brutal firefight was still raging between commandos and super battle droids.»
- Ch5 L293: «Obi-Wan realized instantly what the substance was.»
- Ch6 L333: «"General Skywalker," a commando said from behind him. "Urgent from Commander Cody. He and General Kenobi are pinned down on level one."»
- Ch6 L339: «Anakin glanced into the glowing sky, then back at the commando who had delivered Cody's message. "General, forward command reports that your starfighter is on the way," another commando updated. Again, Anakin glanced at the sky, only to turn back to the commando. "Where did you say Obi-Wan and Cody are?"»
- Ch7 L347: «Not much had changed, except within Obi-Wan, who felt as if he had downed three bottles of Whyren's Reserve.»
- Ch7 L347: «Bleary-eyed but lucid, tipsy but sure-footed, weary but attentive, Obi-Wan seemed to be the sum of all contrasts.»
- Ch7 L351: «Anakin had his rebreather in his mouth as he approached the room in which Obi-Wan had held his own against better than fifty droids, all of which lay scattered about the room.»
- Ch7 L351: «A weaving, shuffling, staggering Obi-Wan was dealing with the last of them when Anakin entered.»
- Ch7 L351: «When the final droid collapsed, Obi-Wan aimed the blade of his lightsaber casually toward the floor and stood swaying in place, breathing hard but almost grinning.»
- Ch7 L353: «When Anakin went to him, Obi-Wan promptly collapsed in his arms.»
- Ch7 L353: «Anakin deactivated Obi-Wan's blade and inserted a rebreather into his mouth - - the same one that had ended up on the floor of the grotto.»
- Ch7 L355: «"Exactly what lightsaber form were you using back there, Master?" Anakin asked when Obi-Wan had come around and the rebreathers were no longer necessary.»
- Ch7 L361: «Obi-Wan blinked in confusion and glanced around at the carnage of droids in the shipping area. "We did this?" he said to Cody.»
- Ch7 L365: «Obi-Wan regarded Anakin in confusion.»
- Ch7 L369: «Obi-Wan ran his hand through his hair, then, as if just remembering, said: "Gunray! Did you get him?"»
- Ch7 L373: «Obi-Wan mulled it over for a moment. "You could have gone after them."»
- Ch7 L377: «Obi-Wan's eyes brightened. "They'll be taken in orbit."»
- Ch7 L385: «Obi-Wan was about to add something when a helmeted commando stepped from a nearby turbolift and hurried over to them. "General Kenobi, General Skywalker, we've found something of interest among the equipment the Neimoidians left behind."»
- Ch9 L477: «Sickle-footed, humpbacked, incised with intricate designs, the mechno-chair sat in the launching bay of the now seized fortress, amid a heap of equally exquisite belongings left by the fleeing Neimoidians. Obi-Wan was circling it, right hand caressing his bearded chin. "I think I've seen this chair before."»
- Ch9 L481: «Obi-Wan stopped. "On Naboo. Shortly after Viceroy Gunray and his entourage were taken into custody in Theed."»
- Ch9 L485: «Obi-Wan snorted. "I suspect you were too excited about having blown up the Droid Control Ship to take much notice of anything. What's more, I saw it only for a moment. But I do remember being struck by the design of the holoprojector plate. I'd never seen one quite like it - - or since, for that matter."»
- Ch9 L495: «Obi-Wan folded his arms and regarded him. "Are you in a rush, Anakin?"»
- Ch9 L511: «"Anakin," Obi-Wan started to say.»
- Ch9 L523: «Obi-Wan said sharply.»
- Ch9 L523: «Anakin stood up and joined Obi-Wan on the launch platform.»
- Ch9 L523: «Obi-Wan was pointing to a blinking light that was growing larger by the second in the night sky.»
- Ch9 L527: «"Not now," Obi-Wan said. R2-D2 began to loose a long series of whistles, chirps, and chitters. "If and when they give the okay," Obi-Wan went on, "then feel free to dissect the entire chair, if that's your objective."»
- Ch9 L539: «"Keep quiet, TeeCee," Anakin said. R2-D2 honked and razzed, though as if from a distance. "And you, too, Artoo." Obi-Wan glanced over his shoulder, and his jaw dropped.»
- Ch9 L543: «"I've been trying to tell you, sirs," TC-16 said, gesturing toward the launching bay's ruined iris hatch. "The chair walked away - - taking your high-thinking little droid with it!" Obi-Wan stared at Anakin in bewilderment. "Well, it couldn't have gotten far on foot, Master."»
- Ch9 L549: «"What's it doing?" Obi-Wan asked. Anakin made his face long and shook his head. "Recharging itself?"»
- Ch9 L551: «"Never seen such tenacity in a mechno-chair." R2-D2 chattered and wheezed. "What's Artoo saying?" Obi-Wan asked TC-16.»
- Ch9 L555: «"Artoo, unplug yourself!" Obi-Wan shouted. "Anakin, get away from that thing!"»
- Ch9 L557: «Anakin's fingers were already busy undoing leads that linked the holoprojector unit to the chair. "Can't, Master. Now we know there's something stored in this chair no one wants us to see." Obi-Wan glanced worriedly at R2- D2. "How much time, Artoo?"»
- Ch9 L561: «Obi-Wan rushed to Anakin's side. "There isn't time, Anakin. Besides, it could be rigged to detonate if tampered with."»
- Ch9 L565: «"You'll deactivate us in the process!" Obi-Wan sensed a disturbance in the Force. Without thinking, he pulled Anakin to the floor an instant before the chair shot a stream of white vapor into the space Anakin had occupied. Coughing, Obi-Wan covered his mouth and nose with the wide sleeve of his robe.»
- Ch9 L571: «Before Obi-Wan could even think about stopping him, Anakin had leaned forward and wrenched the interface cable from the control console.»
- Ch10 L599: «To the extent that friendships could be forged with them, Bail had come to know a few: Jedi Masters Obi-Wan Kenobi, Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin - - the privileged few who also were allowed to meet personally with Palpatine.»
- Ch11 L667: «The shuttle whose landing lights had caught Obi-Wan's attention on Cato Neimoidia carried more than Intelligence analysts and technicians.»
- Ch11 L667: «Yoda was aboard, eager to see for himself what Obi-Wan and Anakin had discovered.»
- Ch11 L669: «Feeling unnecessary, Obi-Wan and Yoda decided to take a stroll down the corridor of Viceroy Gunray's now appropriated palace.»
- Ch11 L671: «Obi-Wan wasn't sure if Yoda was pondering the image of Sidious, or the fact that two Jedi had been killed during the fighting on Cato Neimoidia.»
- Ch11 L673: «Reaching the end of the long corridor, Obi-Wan and Yoda turned and started back. Without taking his eyes from the floor, Yoda said: "Found something important, you have, Obi-Wan. That Count Dooku is in league with someone, proof this is. That in this war a greater part the Sith play than we realize."»
- Ch11 L675: «The name Sidious had come up only once since the war began - - on Geonosis, when Dooku had told an imprisoned Obi-Wan that a Sith Lord by that name had hundreds of Republic Senators under his influence.»
- Ch11 L675: «At the time, Obi-Wan assumed that Dooku was lying, in order to persuade Obi-Wan that he was still aligned with the Jedi, although attempting to thwart the powers of the dark side by his own methods.»
- Ch11 L675: «Now that Sidious appeared to be real, Obi-Wan didn't know what to think.»
- Ch11 L677: «Thirteen years earlier, when Obi-Wan had fought and killed a Sith on Naboo, had he killed a Master or an apprentice?»
- Ch11 L679: «But the horned and tattooed Sith whom Obi-Wan killed could not have trained Dooku, because Dooku had still been a member of the Jedi Order then. As clouded as the dark side made some things, there was simply no way Dooku could have been living a double life within the walls of the Temple itself.»
- Ch11 L681: «"Master Yoda," Obi-Wan said, "is it possible that Dooku wasn't lying about the Senate being under the control of Sidious?"»
- Ch11 L683: «He glanced up at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch11 L683: «What think you, Obi-Wan?»
- Ch11 L685: «Obi-Wan folded his arms.»
- Ch11 L687: «"Right you are, Obi-Wan. Pride undid him. Forced him, it did, to show us his true face."»
- Ch11 L693: «Obi-Wan considered it. "I've heard rumors about Dooku's early fascination with the dark side. Was there not an incident in the Temple involving a stolen Sith Holocron?"»
- Ch11 L695: «But understand, Obi-Wan, a Jedi Dooku was.»
- Ch11 L695: «He glanced at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch11 L697: «Obi-Wan sat, his heart wanting to race. "A stern Master Dooku was, to Qui-Gon and others,"»
- Ch11 L703: «"Never absent they were, Obi-Wan. But stronger suddenly. Closer to the surface. Spoke much of the prophecy, Dooku did."»
- Ch11 L709: «"Anakin," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch11 L713: «Obi-Wan waited.»
- Ch11 L715: «"And yet the Council dismissed Dooku as an idealist," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch11 L731: «Obi-Wan turned to face Yoda. "Master, did Sifo-Dyas order the clone army? "»
- Ch11 L739: «Obi-Wan gave in to some of his frustration. "I should have questioned Lama Su more extensively."»
- Ch11 L743: «"Did they?" Obi-Wan said in surprise. "When?"»
- Ch11 L757: «"Someone knew," Obi-Wan said, more to himself. "Dooku?" he asked Yoda.»
- Ch11 L761: «Obi-Wan recalled his visit to the archives to search out the location of Kamino, only to be told by Jocasta Nu that the planetary system didn't exist.»
- Ch11 L765: «Obi-Wan pictured Fett in the execution arena on Geonosis, standing behind Dooku in a box reserved for dignitaries. "He had knowledge of both armies. Could he have killed Sifo-Dyas?"»
- Ch11 L779: «Obi-Wan fell silent for a moment.»
- Ch11 L781: «"More lies," Yoda said. "Building a case to enlist you in his cause, Dooku was." You must join me, Obi-Wan, Dooku had said, and together we will destroy the Sith! "If Gunray hadn't been so keen on assassinating Padme Amidala,"»
- Ch11 L783: «Obi-Wan mused. "If I'd failed to trace the saberdart that killed the changeling..."»
- Ch11 L791: «Obi-Wan's eyes narrowed. "Mine wasn't a case of blind luck."»
- Ch11 L803: «Yoda was correct, Obi-Wan told himself.»
- Ch11 L803: «The difference between knowledge and wisdom, Obi-Wan's friend Dex might have said, as he did on identifying the source of the saberdart that had killed Zam Wessel, when the Temple analysis droids couldn't.»
- Ch11 L805: «Yoda was regarding him when he lifted his head. "Reveal you, your thoughts do, Obi-Wan. Believe I should have told you sooner, you do."»
- Ch11 L813: «Yoda looked hard at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch12 L817: «Just as Obi-Wan had said, they resented the fact that Anakin had tampered with the chair, despite the fact that, had he not, the chair would have blown itself to pieces, taking with it the holoimage of Sidious and whatever other communications memories it might contain.»
- Ch12 L819: «1;5CObi-Wan's little joke.»
- Ch12 L863: «"What incident?" Anakin was tempted to disclose the details of his and Obi-Wan's discovery, but Yoda had told him to remain silent about the mechno-chair. "Nothing important," he said. "But I always feel guilty when I become angry."»
- Ch12 L867: «"Obi-Wan doesn't show anger - - except, of course, at me. Even then, it's more like... aggravation."»
- Ch12 L869: «Unlike Obi-Wan and the others, you weren't raised in the Temple, where younglings are taught to conquer their anger by transcending it.»
- Ch14 L981: «Returning to the launching bay with Yoda, Obi-Wan observed Anakin and Yoda trade the briefest of looks, the meaning of which escaped him.»
- Ch14 L983: «"Jedi Council business?" Anakin asked when Obi-Wan joined him. "Nothing of the sort. Yoda believes that the mechno-chair may yield clues to the whereabouts of Darth Sidious. He wants us to take up the search."»
- Ch14 L999: «Obi-Wan tried to discern Anakin's intent. "Even Yoda is not infallible, if that's what you're getting at."»
- Ch14 L1001: «"The Jedi should be." Anakin glanced furtively at Obi-Wan. "We could be."»
- Ch14 L1015: «"Don't compare us to them," Obi-Wan said, more harshly than he meant.»
- Ch14 L1019: «Obi-Wan took a calming breath.»
- Ch14 L1021: «"Carries on his shoulders the weight of the galaxy, Obi-Wan does," Yoda said, approaching with one of the Intelligence analysts. "Ease your concerns, this news might," he added before Obi-Wan could respond.»
- Ch14 L1025: «"Better you feel already, ummm?" Yoda said to Obi-Wan, motioning with his gimer stick.»
- Ch14 L1033: «"Is the mechno-chair the same one I saw on Naboo?" Obi-Wan asked.»
- Ch14 L1035: «He looked at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch14 L1037: «"Zan Arbor," Anakin said angrily. "The gas used on the Gungans at Ohma-D'un." He looked at Obi-Wan. "No wonder you were able to sense it!"»
- Ch14 L1039: «Dyne glanced from Anakin to Obi-Wan. "The gas-emitter mechanism is identical to what you find in some of the Techno Union's E-Five-Twenty-Two assassin droids."»
- Ch14 L1041: «Obi-Wan stroked his chin in thought. "If Gunray has had the chair for fourteen years, then he could have been using it to contact Sidious during the Naboo crisis. If we could learn who manufactured the chair..."»
- Ch14 L1043: «Yoda laughed. "Ahead of Obi-Wan, the experts are," he said to Anakin. "We know who's responsible for the chair's Neimoidian engravings,"»
- Ch16 L1127: «The twin-piloted ship had been an antique twenty years earlier, but its sublight and hyperdrive engines were reliable, and with vessels deployed on so many fronts Obi-Wan and Anakin couldn't be choosy.»
- Ch16 L1127: «Obi-Wan had plotted the three jumps it had taken them to reach the Xi Char world from the Inner Rim, but Anakin had done all the piloting.»
- Ch16 L1131: «Obi-Wan was pleasantly surprised. "That will teach me not to be skeptical. In the past when we've been informed that Intelligence has done the advance work, I've found that to be anything but the case."»
- Ch16 L1139: «Obi-Wan sat back in his chair, waiting for the rest of it.»
- Ch16 L1139: «Obi-Wan plucked at his beard.»
- Ch16 L1143: «Obi-Wan had argued against going to Charros IV.»
- Ch16 L1143: «But Yoda had insisted that Obi-Wan and Anakin attempt to speak personally with the Xi Charrian whose sigil had been discovered on the walking chair.»
- Ch16 L1143: «Now Obi-Wan wondered why he had been so averse to making the trip.»
- Ch16 L1143: «Anakin was correct about Obi-Wan's having had more than his share of such assignments.»
- Ch16 L1151: «Obi-Wan swiveled to face him.»
- Ch16 L1157: «Obi-Wan laughed shortly. "Yes, let's hope your fame hasn't spread this far into the Outer Rim. But in fact, our success hinges almost entirely on whether TeeCee-Sixteen can speak Xi Char as fluently as he claims."»
- Ch16 L1159: «"Master Kenobi, I assure you that I can speak the tongue almost as well as an indigenous Xi Charrian," the protocol droid chimed in from one of the cockpit's rear seats.»
- Ch16 L1167: «The cruiser was descending into Charros IV's ice-clouded atmosphere. Leaning toward the viewport, Obi-Wan gazed down on an arid, almost treeless world. The Xi Char lived on high plateaus, hemmed in by ranges of snowcapped mountains. Expansive black-water lakes dotted the landscape.»
- Ch16 L1169: «"A bleak planet," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch16 L1173: «Obi-Wan shrugged. "I can think of far worse places to live than Tatooine. "»
- Ch16 L1177: «Anakin glanced at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch16 L1177: «At the top of the ramp, Obi-Wan raised the hood of his cloak against a frigid wind that howled down the slopes.»
- Ch16 L1179: «"Guess they don't get many guests," Anakin said as he, Obi-Wan, and TC-16 started down the ramp.»
- Ch16 L1185: «Obi-Wan didn't know what to make of the sheer eagerness he felt from the aliens.»
- Ch16 L1187: «"TeeCee, are they normally so zealous?" Obi-Wan asked.»
- Ch16 L1189: «"Yes, Master Kenobi.»
- Ch16 L1189: «Obi-Wan and Anakin watched in awe as patches of carbon scoring disappeared, dents were straightened, pieces of superstructure were realigned, and transparisteel viewports were polished.»
- Ch16 L1211: «Without ceremony, TC-16 approached the Prelate and tendered a greeting. The droid had tasked his vocoder to provide Obi-Wan and Anakin with simultaneous translations of his utterances. "May I present Jedi Obi-Wan Kenobi, and Jedi Anakin Skywalker," he began.»
- Ch16 L1213: «Waving away his retinue, the Prelate pivoted his long head to regard Obi-Wan.»
- Ch16 L1215: «"TeeCee," Obi-Wan said, "tell him we're sorry to have disturbed him during his ablutions."»
- Ch16 L1225: «Obi-Wan was already reaching for his lightsaber, but Anakin looked dubious. "You did say you would follow my lead."»
- Ch16 L1231: «"That went better than expected," Obi-Wan said as he, Anakin, and TC-16 were being escorted into the heart of Workshop Xcan.»
- Ch16 L1239: «Ten years back, Obi-Wan and Anakin had had their own brush with Sienar at the living world known as Zonama Sekot.»
- Ch16 L1239: «Here were all the priceless devices Obi-Wan and Anakin had found crammed into storage rooms in Gunray's Cato Neimoidia citadel.»
- Ch16 L1241: «The same pair of outsized aliens who had escorted Obi-Wan and Anakin to the Prelate's chancery guided them to t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak's altar, which was located in the workshop's western colonnade, the piers of which were decorated with mosaics of engraved tiles.»
- Ch16 L1243: «Obi-Wan could see how the sound of their ceaseless humming could be slightly unnerving. t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak was engrossed in engraving a corporate logo into a piece of starship console.»
- Ch16 L1249: «Anakin nudged Obi-Wan in the upper arm: Xi Charrians working nearby were beginning to take a keen interest in the conversation.»
- Ch16 L1255: «Obi-Wan watched him for a moment, then turned to Anakin. "I don't think he wants to speak with us."»
- Ch16 L1259: «Obi-Wan said, then added, more to himself, "Oh, what's the use," and sprang up toward the ceiling.»
- Ch16 L1261: «Ten meters away, Obi-Wan paralleled the Xi Charrian's flight into the upper reaches of the vaulted ceiling, where the chase had now roused scores of roosting Xi Charrians, inciting more than a few to join in.»
- Ch16 L1261: «Fearing what might result should Anakin call too strongly on the Force - - Obi-Wan had visions of the entire workshop crumbing to pieces! - - he fairly flew after them, barely managing at the apex of his ascent to grab hold of t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak's rear legs.»
- Ch16 L1263: «Cascading onto the floor, Obi-Wan and Anakin lost their hold on t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak, and suddenly couldn't tell one Xi Charrian from the next.»
- Ch16 L1265: «"No mayhem!" Obi-Wan shouted. Anakin showed him a wide-eyed glance from beneath a three-meter-tall heap of irate Xi Charrians.»
- Ch16 L1269: «Obi-Wan glanced around the workshop.»
- Ch16 L1269: «With a shoving motion of his free hand, Obi-Wan overturned a small table twenty meters away, spilling several stacks of freshly engraved comlinks and droid summoners.»
- Ch16 L1273: «Obi-Wan cautioned.»
- Ch16 L1273: «Twenty Xi Charrians flattened themselves to the floor as the Prelate brought the blasters to bear on Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch16 L1275: «A babble of chitterings was exchanged, before the Prelate swung back to Obi-Wan and Anakin, raising the blasters once more.»
- Ch16 L1277: «"But they meant no harm, Excellency!" the droid intervened. "t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak fled in response to their questions! Master Obi-Wan and Jedi Skywalker sought merely to ascertain the reason!"»
- Ch16 L1279: «The Prelate's gaze singled out t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak. TC-16 translated. "Master Kenobi, the Prelate advises you to pose your questions, and to leave Charros Four before he has a change of heart."»
- Ch16 L1281: «Obi-Wan looked at t'laalak-s'lalak-t'th'ak, then at TC-16. "Ask him if he remembers the chair."»
- Ch16 L1293: «Obi-Wan and Anakin traded eager looks. "Was the hyperwave transceiver already affixed to it?" Anakin asked.»
- Ch16 L1299: «Obi-Wan looked at TC-16. "Ask him who made the transceiver. Ask him where it shipped from."»
- Ch17 L1321: «"Reach the ears of the Supreme Chancellor, it won't," Yoda said. "But surprised I was by Obi-Wan's report. Losing some of his better judgment, Obi-Wan is."»
- Ch17 L1369: «"Obi-Wan and Anakin aren't far from Belderone," Mace said. "But they're pursuing another lead to Sidious's whereabouts."»
- Ch17 L1371: «"Wait, the lead will. Needed Obi-Wan and Anakin will be." Yoda turned to the still image of General Grievous. "Prepare carefully for this battle, we must."»
- Ch19 L1461: «Accustomed to Anakin's disregard for call signs, Obi-Wan answered in kind. "Anakin, there are approximately five hundred droids positioned between Grievous and us. What's more, the capital ships are too heavily shielded."»
- Ch19 L1465: «Obi-Wan sighed into the comlink microphone.»
- Ch19 L1475: «Anakin checked the display to make certain that Obi-Wan was still with him.»
- Ch19 L1489: «Anakin squared off with the enemy leader of the cloverleaf clash, and began to harass it with laser bolts. Adapting to his tactics, Obi-Wan fell back; then leapt his starfighter into kill position and opened up.»
- Ch19 L1491: «Signaling Obi-Wan to follow, Anakin climbed out of the main battle, veering tangent to it, and rocketed toward the nearest of the Separatists' needle-nosed picket ships.»
- Ch19 L1497: «Obi-Wan followed, unleashing with all cannons.»
- Ch19 L1501: «"We've got a clear shot at Grievous!" he told Obi-Wan. With its tapered bow and large outrigger fins, the general's cruiser resembled a classic-era Coruscant skyscraper laid on its side.»
- Ch19 L1513: «And in the starfighter that was guarding Anakin's stern: Obi-Wan Kenobi.»
- Ch19 L1523: «Obi-Wan wondered if he had ever witnessed a more lucid demonstration of the Jedi axiom than Anakin's Force-centered, death-defying harassment of Grievous's ship.»
- Ch19 L1523: «His speck of a starfighter all but nose-to-nose with the mammoth cruiser, leaving Obi-Wan to deal with the vengeful droid fighters Anakin was either ignorant of or deliberately disregarding.»
- Ch19 L1525: «"He really is going to be the death of me," Obi-Wan mumbled.»
- Ch19 L1525: «Was it Obi-Wan's duty - - more, the duty of all the Jedi - - to see to it that he survived at all costs?»
- Ch19 L1527: «Even Obi-Wan's repeated attempts to hail him through the battle net had had no effect.»
- Ch19 L1527: «Obi-Wan thought for a moment that Grievous was actually going to bring all forward guns to bear on Anakin.»
- Ch19 L1529: «Obi-Wan felt a great disturbance in the Force, as ship after ship disintegrated or erupted in flames.»
- Ch19 L1529: «The follow-up volley Obi-Wan waited for never arrived.»
- Ch19 L1533: «"Anakin, the evacuees need our help!" Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch19 L1535: «"I'm coming, Master." Obi-Wan watched Anakin's starfighter break off its futile pursuit of the cruiser. Farther out, Separatist ships were disappearing from sight as they made the jump to lightspeed.»
- Ch22 L1713: «Having traced Fett to Kamino, Obi-Wan Kenobi had turned up on Geonosis.»
- Ch22 L1713: «But when he had informed Sidious of Obi-Wan's presence, Sidious had only said, Allow events to play out, Darth Tyranus.»
- Ch23 L1831: «"Dooku's Sith Master, Sidious is. Learned of him on Geonosis, Master Kenobi did. But eluded us, proof of him has."»
- Ch23 L1833: «"Now I recall," Palpatine said. "Obi-Wan was told that this Sidious had somehow infiltrated the Senate."»
- Ch24 L1861: «"When the Xi Charrian said it was an asteroid mining operation, I wasn't picturing an actual asteroid," Obi-Wan said from the copilot's seat of the Republic cruiser.»
- Ch24 L1865: «Obi-Wan and Anakin had the old white ship to themselves, and had exchanged their Jedi robes for outfits more suitable to itinerant spacers.»
- Ch24 L1867: «Obi-Wan and Anakin hadn't been told when or even if they were going to make contact with the agent, but moments before leaving Belderone they had been informed that Thal K'sar - - the Bith artisan who allegedly had designed the hyperwave transceiver and holoprojector for Gunray's mechno-chair - - had been arrested, on charges yet to be learned.»
- Ch24 L1875: «"We're freelance merchants in search of work," Obi-Wan reminded him.»
- Ch24 L1881: «Obi-Wan's gaze drifted to the viewport. Off to starboard, a corvette was coming about.»
- Ch24 L1891: «Obi-Wan already was, and so was able to remain more or less upright in the chair as Anakin firewalled the thrusters and threw the cruiser into a hard turn - - not away from the corvette, but aimed directly toward it.»
- Ch24 L1905: «Obi-Wan watched the corvette grow larger in the viewport. The console continued to chime, in escalating alerts. An instant later, two turbolaser beams streaked across the cruiser's bow.»
- Ch24 L1907: «Obi-Wan clenched his hands on the chair armrests.»
- Ch24 L1915: «"Enough plausibility," Obi-Wan said. "Level out and signal that we're complying."»
- Ch24 L1919: «Obi-Wan saw that two patrol craft were rushing in to join the pursuit. With flashes of scarlet light racing alongside, Anakin whipped the cruiser through a teeth-rattling bank and shot for the thick of the asteroid belt.»
- Ch24 L1923: «Rubble from the explosion peppered the cruiser's shields, but the console displays confirmed Obi-Wan's hunch that no damage had been done.»
- Ch24 L1923: «The cruiser had no sooner realigned itself than it gave a sudden lurch, snapping Obi-Wan and Anakin back into their seats, then forward into the console.»
- Ch24 L1925: «Obi-Wan scanned the displays.»
- Ch24 L1951: «"Look at it this way," Obi-Wan said, as the cruiser was being drawn toward the distant facility. "At least you made them work for it."»
- Ch24 L1953: «Ordered out of the ship, Obi-Wan and Anakin stood at the foot of the boarding ramp with their hands clamped on top of their heads.»
- Ch24 L1955: «"Not exactly the warm welcome we received on Charros Four," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch24 L1965: «"No mind tricks," Obi-Wan cautioned.»
- Ch24 L1969: «The captain looked Obi-Wan and Anakin up and down, then circled them once, hands clasped behind his back.»
- Ch24 L1973: «"Let's just say we've been forced to adapt to the times," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch24 L1989: «"Exactly," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch24 L1999: «"Can't we simply pay a fine and be on our way?" Obi-Wan asked as the magnetic cuffs snapped into place around his wrists.»
- Ch24 L2011: «Drawing a blaster from his hip holster, he motioned Obi-Wan and Anakin toward the turbolifts.»
- Ch24 L2013: «The captain indicated an unoccupied lift and followed Obi-Wan and Anakin inside. When two Gossams hurried for the same lift, he waved them away. As soon as the door closed, he lowered his weapon and spoke with a sudden urgency.»
- Ch24 L2017: «"You're Travale," Obi-Wan said, using the code name he had been furnished.»
- Ch24 L2027: «"Execution seems a rather harsh penalty," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch24 L2031: «Travale hadn't been given the reason, but Obi-Wan nodded in acknowledgment.»
- Ch24 L2043: «"Welcome to the detention level," Travale said, back in character, and shoving Obi-Wan out into the anteroom beyond.»
- Ch24 L2053: «Emerged from the enclosure of display screens, a four-eyed Aqualish led Obi-Wan and Anakin into a narrow corridor lined with detention cells.»
- Ch24 L2057: «"Then we'll hope to be released before lunch," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch24 L2059: «Thal K'sar was slumped in a corner, his long-fingered hands cuffed in front of him. Slender even for a Bith, he was well dressed and seemingly unharmed. Obi-Wan recalled that he had been arrested only the previous day. K'sar glanced up, but didn't return Obi-Wan's nod of greeting.»
- Ch24 L2063: «"Good job back there." Obi-Wan played along.»
- Ch24 L2071: «Anakin shrugged and joined Obi-Wan on the far side of the room.»
- Ch24 L2073: «"Patience," Obi-Wan said quietly.»
- Ch24 L2075: «Obi-Wan caught him before he hit the floor.»
- Ch24 L2081: «Anakin glanced at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch24 L2087: «Obi-Wan left it at that.»
- Ch24 L2091: «"Where you able to arrange anything?" Obi-Wan asked Travale.»
- Ch24 L2101: «"We can open the door," Obi-Wan assured him.»
- Ch24 L2117: «"Take the tunnel to the left of the guard station," Travale was telling Obi-Wan when Anakin returned. "Keep taking lefts until you reach a stairway, then follow that to the docking level."»
- Ch24 L2123: «"You're not going alone," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch24 L2127: «Obi-Wan didn't argue.»
- Ch24 L2137: «Spreading his hands, he snapped the cuffs from his wrists. Obi-Wan did the same, then snapped Travale's open. Travale smiled broadly.»
- Ch24 L2141: «Anakin and Obi-Wan were standing by the door when the cell's grime-encrusted illuminator faltered and died. Obi-Wan shoved his hands sideways through the air, and the door retracted.»
- Ch24 L2151: «A Force push from Obi-Wan sent the Aqualish flailing back into the anteroom and slamming into the turbolift wall.»
- Ch24 L2153: «By then Obi-Wan and Anakin were on them, dropping them with punches, side kicks, Force pushes.»
- Ch24 L2189: «As Travale and Obi-Wan were running off, Anakin noticed that one of the turbolift cars was descending.»
- Ch25 L2251: «"Someone's coming," Obi-Wan warned Travale.»
- Ch25 L2255: «Obi-Wan peered around the corner of the tower in the direction of the voices he had heard. Three Geonosian security guards were approaching the coupling station from a corridor on the far side of the shaft.»
- Ch25 L2259: «Obi-Wan considered his options, then made a flicking motion with the fingers of his right hand. An unidentifiable sound issued from deeper in the corridor the guards had taken. Whirling, the three Geonosians hurried off to investigate.»
- Ch25 L2261: «Travale shook his head back and forth in appreciation of Obi-Wan's skill.»
- Ch25 L2267: «Travale studied Obi-Wan for a moment.»
- Ch25 L2271: «Obi-Wan touched Travale on the arm, and motioned with his bearded chin to the tower.»
- Ch25 L2373: «Obi-Wan and Travale negotiated the cofferdam that linked Escarte's air lock to a docking ring just forward of the cruiser's tri-barreled thruster fantail.»
- Ch25 L2379: «Obi-Wan glanced at Thal K'sar, thinking the Bith might feel the same. Instead, K'sar was curled up on the hold's worn acceleration couch. Obi-Wan hurried on to the cockpit and strapped into the copilot's seat.»
- Ch25 L2391: «Off to port, Obi-Wan saw two Guild corvettes dead in space.»
- Ch25 L2399: «Obi-Wan regarded him for a moment.»
- Ch25 L2411: «Before Obi-Wan could reply, Anakin said: "Hyperspace coordinates coming in."»
- Ch26 L2513: «Dooku exhaled with purpose. "Skywalker and Kenobi."»
- Ch26 L2521: «Once Master and Padawan, Kenobi and Skywalker had become the scourge of Dooku's existence.»
- Ch26 L2521: «Also as instructed, Dooku had made Kenobi aware of the existence of Darth Sidious, as a means of confusing the Jedi Order by telling them the truth.»
- Ch26 L2521: «In the sloop's docking bay he had demonstrated his mastery to Kenobi and Skywalker - - although Skywalker hadn't been as easily defeated the second time they had dueled.»
- Ch26 L2527: «He paused to consider something, then said: "Yes, I begin to see the blazes along the trail Skywalker and Kenobi will follow."»
- Ch26 L2533: «"Nevertheless, Kenobi and young Skywalker will find their way to it."»
- Ch26 L2543: «"One small addendum. See to it that Obi-Wan Kenobi ceases to be an irritant."»
- Ch26 L2549: «Sidious shook his head. "But Skywalker does. And Kenobi... Kenobi has been as a father to him. Orphan Skywalker once and for all, and he will shift. "»
- Ch27 L2627: «"We can only hope that Obi-Wan and Anakin find their way to the source of this war before it's too late."»
- Ch28 L2635: «Reluctantly, Obi-Wan leaned in to peer at the slick boot, not wanting to get too close.»
- Ch28 L2641: «Obi-Wan straightened and shoved his hands deeper into the sleeves of his robe.»
- Ch28 L2667: «Despite the fact that the moon seemed a perfect place for a red-complected Twi'lek to hide, Obi-Wan doubted that Fa'ale Leh would be found here.»
- Ch28 L2669: «To Obi-Wan's mind the ship in question could only be the modified star courier that had belonged to the Sith he had killed on Naboo, and had been confiscated by the Republic after the battle there.»
- Ch28 L2671: «Obi-Wan and Anakin might have gone directly to the source - - to Raith Sienar - - had Supreme Chancellor Palpatine not vetoed the idea.»
- Ch28 L2671: «Under its subsidiary, Rothana Heavy Engineering - - the builders of the Acclamator-class assault ships, as well as the AT-TE walkers - - KDY had also supplied the Confederacy with the Storm Fleet, which had been "the Terror of the Perlemian" until retired from service with the help of Obi-Wan and Anakin.»
- Ch28 L2673: «Obi-Wan gestured to a nearby cantina.»
- Ch28 L2689: «Completing a circle, Obi-Wan pointed to a cantina diagonally across from them. The Desperate Pilot.»
- Ch28 L2703: «"Nothing." When Obi-Wan signaled for the human bartender, he added: "You order one more Twi'lek appetizer, I promise I'm going to cut your arm off."»
- Ch28 L2705: «Obi-Wan laughed. "I found the izzy-mold at the last place to be very flavorful."»
- Ch28 L2719: «"The way you were strutting around, what female could help herself?" Mimicking Anakin's voice, Obi-Wan said: "`Jedi business.'"»
- Ch28 L2729: «Seeing the bartender approach, Obi-Wan placed a sizable credit chip under his empty glass and slid it forward.»
- Ch28 L2743: «"Who isn't." Obi-Wan shook his head.»
- Ch28 L2755: «"Or a dancer," Obi-Wan thought to add.»
- Ch28 L2759: «"She used to be a pilot - - with a taste for spice." Obi-Wan watched the bartender closely. "She would have arrived on Naos Three within the past ten or so years."»
- Ch28 L2769: «"But you do know her," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch28 L2779: «Anakin and Obi-Wan traded confused glances.»
- Ch28 L2781: «"She's expecting us?" Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch28 L2801: «Obi-Wan led the way up the stairs and rapped his hand on room seven's green plastoid door.»
- Ch28 L2805: «They made certain that their lightsabers were in easy reach, but left them affixed to their belts and concealed. Obi-Wan hit the door-release stud, then followed Anakin into the chill room.»
- Ch28 L2807: «Wearing trousers, boots, and an insulated jacket, Genne - - perhaps Fa'ale Leh - - was lounging on a narrow bed, her back and lekku against the headboard, long legs extended and crossed at the ankle. Beside her on a small table stood a half-full bottle of what Obi-Wan guessed was the local rocket-fuel homebrew.»
- Ch28 L2819: «"Was that surprise or disappointment?" Anakin asked Obi-Wan.»
- Ch28 L2821: «"Who were you expecting?" Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch28 L2831: «"Do what here?" Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch28 L2839: «She glanced from him to Obi-Wan. "Bad Jedi. Good Jedi."»
- Ch28 L2843: «She nodded at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch28 L2857: «Obi-Wan looked around the room.»
- Ch28 L2863: «"No need," Obi-Wan said as Fa'ale was bringing her left head-tail over her shoulder.»
- Ch28 L2875: «Obi-Wan nodded. "We've had dealings with him."»
- Ch28 L2881: «Obi-Wan glanced at the ceiling. An instant before it blew inward - - raining plastoid rafters, ice-covered roof panels, ceiling tiles, and two heavily armed Trandoshans - - he had rushed to the bed and overturned it, dumping Fa'ale Leh, foam mattresses, and bedcovers onto the cold floor.»
- Ch28 L2885: «Whirling, Obi-Wan called his lightsaber from his belt and leapt to the doorway, his blade slicing both hands off one of the humans. An agonized howl pierced the frigid air as the man sank to his knees. Unstuck, the second one fell forward, directly onto Obi-Wan's blade.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Off to Obi-Wan's left Anakin stood unmoving in the center of the room, holding his own against the two reptilian aliens and the wielder of the vibro-ax.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Pivoting on his left foot, the Falleen swung the vibro-ax at Obi-Wan's head.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Ducking the swing, Obi-Wan got underneath the blade and just managed to nick the Falleen in the left thigh.»
- Ch28 L2887: «Raising the ax over his head, he rushed forward, intent on splitting Obi-Wan down the middle.»
- Ch28 L2887: «A backflip carried Obi-Wan out of the blade's path, but Fa'ale's bedside table wasn't as fortunate.»
- Ch28 L2889: «Obi-Wan tracked the flight of the alien's head across the room, out the door, and into the hallway, where someone loosed a bloodcurdling screech.»
- Ch28 L2889: «Shorter by half a meter but no less enraged, the humanoid sent the vibro-ax flying straight for Obi-Wan, then drew from his hip holster a large blaster and began firing.»
- Ch28 L2889: «In midflight from the vibrating blade, Obi-Wan watched Anakin rid the Falleen of blaster and hand, and thrust his lightsaber directly into the Falleen's chest.»
- Ch28 L2893: «Obi-Wan shouted: "Get her out of here!"»
- Ch28 L2911: «"Right on time," Obi-Wan said from the window. "Six more that I can see."»
- Ch28 L2923: «Obi-Wan and Anakin followed her out the door to a utility closet at the end of the hall.»
- Ch28 L2923: «Through the closed door, Obi-Wan could hear a crowd of beings race past the closet, heading for the Twi'lek's room.»
- Ch28 L2925: «In dim natural light Obi-Wan saw that he was in a cavern that had become an inlet for the river.»
- Ch28 L2933: «With the flip of a switch, the speeder's engine coughed to life, then began to purr loudly in the hollow of the cave. Obi-Wan mounted a second sled, while Fa'ale was positioning herself behind Anakin.»
- Ch28 L2937: «Obi-Wan was instantly confused. "Like this?"»
- Ch28 L2939: «"Like this, like this!" Anakin emphasized, demonstrating again, then indicating another set of switches on the control panel of Obi-Wan's machine.»
- Ch28 L2949: «Obi-Wan stared at her.»
- Ch28 L2953: «Glancing over his shoulder, Obi-Wan saw three sleds gaining on them from upriver.»
- Ch29 L2957: «Ominous clouds obscured the mountains to Obi-Wan's right.»
- Ch29 L2959: «The repulsorlift would have allowed him to fly over the obstacles - - as Anakin was doing, farther downriver - - but Obi-Wan just couldn't get the hang of it.»
- Ch29 L2961: «A vacillating roar in his right ear told him that one of the pursuit sleds had caught up with him. Out of the corner of his streaming eye, Obi-Wan saw the sled's human pilot bend low over the control bars to provide his Rodian rider with the clearance he needed to send a blaster bolt through Obi-Wan's head.»
- Ch29 L2963: «Braking, Obi-Wan allowed the sled to come alongside more quickly than the Rodian had planned. The rider's first shot raced past Obi-Wan's eyes; the second, he deflected slightly downward, straight into the sled's engine. The machine exploded instantly, flinging pilot and rider head over heels in opposite directions.»
- Ch29 L2965: «Twisting the throttle, the pilot drove his sled into Obi-Wan's, trying to send it spinning out of control or, better still, into the trunk of massive tree that was protruding acutely from the thick ice.»
- Ch29 L2965: «Narrowly missing the latter, Obi-Wan went into a sideways skid.»
- Ch29 L2965: «By then his crash-helmeted pursuer was well positioned to ram him a second time, but Obi-Wan was ready for him.»
- Ch29 L2965: «Water geysered into the air, drenching Obi-Wan as he raced past.»
- Ch29 L2967: «Unable to replicate Anakin's deft turns, Obi-Wan was falling farther behind with each quarter kilometer, and was now making himself an easy target for the assassins on the bridge.»
- Ch29 L2969: «Twisting the throttle, Obi-Wan swerved around the pilotless sled and continued to race upriver, out of range of the blasters.»
- Ch29 L2969: «Uncertain he could leap the gap even if he wanted to try, Obi-Wan applied the brakes - - hard!»
- Ch29 L2973: «"Stars' end," Obi-Wan muttered.»
- Ch29 L2979: «It was then that he caught sight of Obi-Wan being lifted from his sled.»
- Ch29 L2983: «The claw that had Obi-Wan in its grip was halfway to the skiff.»
- Ch29 L2997: «"I had a feeling you'd show up," Obi-Wan said from inside the claw.»
- Ch29 L3003: «Obi-Wan heard the snap-hiss of Anakin's lightsaber. Peering through the metal fingers of the claw, he saw what was coming.»
- Ch29 L3007: «An instant before the crash, Anakin severed the claw cable, and the cage plummeted, striking the slippery ground and racing down to the frozen river, out onto the ice, spinning crazily, with Obi-Wan bouncing around inside and Anakin Force-fastened to the outside through all the unpredictable pitches and tumbles.»
- Ch29 L3009: «The skiff crashed into the hillside. By the time the claw came to a rest on the far side of the river, the two Jedi were so covered in snow they looked like wampas. Anakin's lightsaber made short work of the fingers of the claw. Obi-Wan scrambled out, spitting snow, and shaking like a hound.»
- Ch29 L3013: «"Stop," Obi-Wan said. "I concede."»
- Ch29 L3025: «"A week in bacta and you'll be good as new," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch29 L3035: «"I don't think so," Obi-Wan interrupted. "Someone more important than Raith Sienar doesn't want us to learn too much about the star courier."»
- Ch30 L3061: «The Lethan pilot Obi-Wan and Anakin had searched out on Naos III hadn't been able to furnish anything more than a portrait of the building to which she had delivered the star courier.»
- Ch30 L3169: «Mace walked two meters into the darkness, and ran his hand down the tunnel's tiled wall. Hundreds of Senators are now under the influence of a Sith Lord called Darth Sidious, Dooku had told Obi-Wan on Geonosis.»
- Ch32 L3347: «"Do I need to demonstrate what responses you can expect from Cin Drallig or Obi-Wan Kenobi? From Mace Windu or, stars help you, Yoda?"»
- Ch32 L3371: «"Born, no doubt, of my failure at Naos Three. The ones I sent had every opportunity to kill Kenobi, Skywalker, and the Twi'lek pilot. Instead, they decided to attempt their capture, to extract additional funds from me, as well as to bolster their reputations."»
- Ch32 L3453: «"We won't have to, Lord Tyranus. Their own investigations will lead them to that conclusion. Moreover, Kenobi and Skywalker will oversee the counterattack."»
- Ch32 L3475: «"Hold Kenobi and Skywalker, Lord Tyranus. Entertain them. Play to their weakness. Demonstrate your mastery, as you have on previous occasions."»
- Ch33 L3497: «"Fa'ale is doing fine," Anakin said as he approached Obi-Wan jauntily. "Two more days of bacta and she'll be on her feet. She says she's through with Naos Three, though. She might even remain here on Belderone."»
- Ch33 L3499: «Obi-Wan looked at him askance.»
- Ch33 L3507: «Obi-Wan looked away.»
- Ch33 L3511: «Anakin stepped deliberately into Obi-Wan's gaze.»
- Ch33 L3515: «Obi-Wan sighed.»
- Ch33 L3521: «Anakin was staring dumbfounded at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch33 L3541: «"Exactly," Obi-Wan said. "But before it became a wasteland, it was headquarters for LiMerge Power."»
- Ch33 L3547: «Obi-Wan ran his hand over his mouth.»
- Ch33 L3553: «Obi-Wan grinned.»
- Ch33 L3557: «Anakin turned his back to Obi-Wan. His face was flushed when he finally swung around.»
- Ch33 L3561: «Obi-Wan blinked.»
- Ch33 L3573: «Obi-Wan took a moment to respond.»
- Ch33 L3591: «Obi-Wan inhaled slowly.»
- Ch33 L3597: «Obi-Wan narrowed his eyes.»
- Ch33 L3605: «"That's what you get for being so good at what you do," Obi-Wan said, hoping to lighten the mood.»
- Ch33 L3609: «Obi-Wan studied him.»
- Ch33 L3627: «Obi-Wan fell silent, as if struck by a sudden suspicion.»
- Ch33 L3639: «Obi-Wan frowned sympathetically.»
- Ch33 L3655: «"Because I think you believe it," Obi-Wan said calmly. "I think you know in your heart that you're meant for something extraordinary."»
- Ch33 L3659: «"Infinite sadness," Obi-Wan said, even while smiling.»
- Ch33 L3669: «Obi-Wan rested his hands on Anakin's shoulders.»
- Ch34 L3761: «"Only because our quarry is aware that we're closing in on him. He failed to silence the ones Obi-Wan and Anakin searched out, and by now he knows that we've discovered his and Dooku's den. It's unlikely he will wait around for us to surprise him."»
- Ch34 L3763: «"That's true. But there's much to gain from simply identifying him. If not here, then by means of something Obi-Wan and Anakin discover on Tythe."»
- Ch35 L3813: «"What does Master Kenobi say?"»
- Ch35 L3821: «"Obi-Wan is under the illusion that I can't deny my destiny - - no matter what I do."»
- Ch35 L3831: «"Obi-Wan is wise. But he has no heart, sir. He sees everything in terms of the Force."»
- Ch35 L3865: «"If they do, Obi-Wan and I will defeat them before they can implement it. "»
- Ch35 L3873: «Obi-Wan was in the MedStar's docking bay, waiting for the shuttle that would take him to the light cruiser Integrity. His arms were folded across his chest, and his small rucksack was sitting on the deck.»
- Ch35 L3885: «He sounded on the verge of tears. Obi-Wan merely nodded.»
- Ch35 L3893: «"You don't think I'm capable?" Obi-Wan asked around a forming grin.»
- Ch35 L3901: «Obi-Wan smiled, then glanced out the hold's magcon transparency.»
- Ch35 L3909: «He still wasn't smiling. Obi-Wan closed his hand around Anakin's upper right arm.»
- Ch37 L3953: «"The fact that we don't is what keeps us centered in the Force," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch37 L3965: «Obi-Wan kept his eyes on the counter at the bottom of the instrument panel's tactical display screen.»
- Ch37 L3975: «The Jedi had insisted nevertheless that an attempt be made to take Dooku alive. Obi-Wan and Anakin didn't need to be reminded of what had happened only weeks earlier on Cato Neimoidia when they had gone after Viceroy Gunray, but they were not about to forgo a chance to capture the Sith Lord.»
- Ch37 L3979: «"No signature for Grievous's cruiser," he said to Obi-Wan. "None of the ships of the Separatist leadership are here."»
- Ch37 L3981: «Obi-Wan glanced at the wire-frame display of his threat-assessment screen.»
- Ch37 L3987: «Obi-Wan was troubled by the thought, but didn't admit to it.»
- Ch37 L3993: «Obi-Wan acknowledged.»
- Ch37 L3999: «"We're almost at the insertion point," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch37 L4003: «There was no time to argue the point. Rolling to port, Obi-Wan fell in behind Anakin and fired his thrusters. Trailing behind, Red Squadron accelerated and banked for the narrow-waisted vessel.»
- Ch37 L4011: «Obi-Wan triggered the launchers and watched two torpedoes burn toward the target. Behind him, the rest of Red Squadron did the same. Hits began to score, fire and gas fountaining from breaches in the ship's dark hull.»
- Ch37 L4017: «Obi-Wan regained control of his craft and once more attached himself to Anakin's six.»
- Ch37 L4021: «Obi-Wan clamped his hands on the violently shaking yoke as Red Squadron ripped into Tythe's plundered atmosphere.»
- Ch37 L4023: «Punching through the blankets of clouds at suicidal velocity, Obi-Wan's starfighter went into a roll. Pinwheeling before his eyes, Tythe was a kaleidoscopic furor of white and brown, smeared occasionally with striations of blue-green. Anakin's voice grew loud in his ears.»
- Ch37 L4027: «With effort, Obi-Wan leveled out of his plummet, his stomach lurching up into his throat. Reaching forward, he engaged the starfighter's topographic sensors. The ship was dropping toward ice floes and bergs. Then, far below, peninsulas of rocky islands came into view.»
- Ch37 L4045: «Anakin and Obi-Wan paid out the last of their proton torpedoes in futile attempts to save Reds Three, Four, and Five.»
- Ch37 L4045: «R4-P17 howled as Obi-Wan twisted the starfighter through violent airbursts and superheated clouds of billowing smoke.»
- Ch37 L4047: «When they had juked their way through the worst of it, Anakin came alongside Obi-Wan. It was just the two of them now.»
- Ch37 L4051: «Obi-Wan gazed out the right side of the cockpit at what had been an enormous plasma-generating facility.»
- Ch37 L4055: «The words had scarcely left Obi-Wan's mouth when battle droids began to gush from the facility and out onto the landing platform. Bolts from the droids' blasters clawed at the pair of prowling starfighters.»
- Ch37 L4057: «"I guess we're not going in through the front door," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch37 L4061: «Obi-Wan looked over his left shoulder at the partially collapsed hemisphere. The lid that had once topped the plasma containment structure was long gone, and the resultant circular opening was large enough for a starfighter to thread.»
- Ch37 L4063: «Obi-Wan had misgivings, nevertheless.»
- Ch39 L4189: «In the ruined archive hall of LiMerge Power's plasma facility, Count Dooku waited for Kenobi and Skywalker to arrive.»
- Ch39 L4191: «Jedi like Kenobi and Skywalker, who wanted to believe as much.»
- Ch39 L4195: «That sense of entitlement was evident in the way Kenobi and Skywalker called on the Force in their fervor to confront him: opening doors with waves of their hands, clearing obstacles from their path with similar gestures, moving with what appeared to be numinous speed and agility, flourishing their blue blades as if they were powered by the will of the Force itself...»
- Ch39 L4197: «It was more important, though, that Kenobi and Skywalker be able to see him, than he them.»
- Ch39 L4207: «Instead of replying, Kenobi touched Skywalker on the arm, and the two of them began to scan the hall, no doubt in an attempt to locate him through the Force.»
- Ch39 L4211: «"We know you're here, Dooku," Kenobi said suddenly - - and with irksome audio distortion. "We can sense you."»
- Ch39 L4221: «Kenobi spread his legs and brought his blade directly in front of him.»
- Ch39 L4283: «"Dooku isn't leading this attack. Obi-Wan confirmed that he is on Tythe."»
- Ch41 L4365: «In what had served as the archive room for LiMerge Power's plasma facility, droid parts were piling up so fast and so high that Obi-Wan and Anakin could scarcely see Dooku's wavering holoimage any longer.»
- Ch41 L4365: «The business of destroying infantry droids - - for that's precisely what the confrontation had come down to - - was beginning to take a toll on Obi-Wan.»
- Ch41 L4367: «Force-pushing four droids to the floor, hewing half a dozen more with his flashing blade, Anakin leapt from Obi-Wan's side, landed on the head of a perplexed droid, and began to race toward the far side of the hall, using other heads as stepping-stones.»
- Ch41 L4373: «"Control your rage, Anakin," Obi-Wan managed to say between breaths. "Don't give him the satisfaction."»
- Ch41 L4377: «Before Obi-Wan could reply, twenty battle droids hurried into the room through the door behind him.»
- Ch41 L4379: «More droids appeared. To Dooku, this was nothing more than a game, Obi-Wan told himself. But if it was a demonstration of Force ability Dooku wanted, then Anakin was still more than willing to provide it. "Dooku!" he howled.»
- Ch45 L4663: «Initially, it had cheered him to observe that Skywalker and Kenobi had finally learned to fight together; to see how powerful they had become in partnership.»
- Ch45 L4663: «Kenobi making full use of his inherent discretion to balance young Skywalker's inattentive rowdiness.»
- Ch46 L4725: «And yet hadn't she done as much as anyone to encourage him to remain a Jedi; to heed the tutelage of Obi-Wan, Mace, and the others; to perpetuate the lie that was their secret life as husband and wife?»
- Ch48 L4833: «Qui-Gon, who had died - - even though, to his young mind, Jedi weren't supposed to... Beside him, Obi-Wan stirred and coughed.»
- Ch48 L4845: «"If you insist." Obi-Wan snorted dust from his nose. "Almost makes me nostalgic for Naos Three."»
- Ch48 L4851: «"Coward," Obi-Wan said. "He flees."»
- Ch48 L4853: «Anakin watched the sloop for a moment longer, then looked at Obi-Wan. "That's not the reason, Master. We've been tricked. Tythe was never the target. We were."»
- Ch52 L5047: «"We were all beginning to wonder if you were going to return," a human crew chief said by way of welcoming Obi-Wan and Anakin back to the assault cruiser's ventral landing bay. Obi-Wan descended the ladder affixed to the starfighter's cockpit. "When did the Separatists jump?"»
- Ch52 L5055: «"Do we know where they're headed?" Obi-Wan asked quickly. The crew chief turned to him.»
- Ch52 L5063: «Anakin took a sudden interest in the crew chief's remarks. "Could be local interference," Obi-Wan said.»
- Ch52 L5067: «Anakin shot Obi-Wan an embittered look and began to storm away.»
- Ch52 L5069: «"Anakin," Obi-Wan said, following in his footsteps.»
- Ch52 L5073: «Obi-Wan folded his arms across his chest. "You wouldn't be saying that if we'd captured Dooku."»
- Ch52 L5077: «Obi-Wan regarded him carefully. "See what, Anakin?"»
- Ch52 L5081: «Obi-Wan rested his hands on Anakin's shoulders. "Calm yourself."»
- Ch52 L5085: «Stung by the gravity in Anakin's voice, Obi-Wan fell silent for a moment, then said: "The Force is our ally, Anakin. When we're mindful of the Force, our actions are in accord with the will of the Force. Tythe wasn't a wrong choice. It's simply that we're ignorant of its import in the greater scheme."»
- Ch52 L5089: «Obi-Wan felt as if someone had knotted his insides. He had failed his apprentice and closest friend. Anakin was suffering, and the only balm he offered were Jedi platitudes. His body heaved a stuttering breath. He had his mouth open to speak when the crew chief interrupted.»
- Ch52 L5093: «Obi-Wan and Anakin swung to Anakin's starfighter.»
- Ch52 L5097: «"Does he understand droid?" the crew chief asked Obi-Wan as Anakin hurried past him.»
- Ch52 L5099: «"That droid," Obi-Wan said. Anakin began to scale the cockpit ladder.»
- Ch52 L5103: «The droid whistled and zithered. Throwing himself into open cockpit, Anakin toggled switches. Obi-Wan had just reached the base of the ladder when he heard Palpatine's voice issuing through the cockpit annunciators.»
- Ch52 L5107: «The crew chief's comlink toned. Obi-Wan glanced from the crew chief to Anakin and back again. "What is it?" he asked in a rush.»
- Ch52 L5111: «Obi-Wan gaped at him. Above him, Anakin lifted his face to the high ceiling and let out a sustained snarl. Glaring down at Obi-Wan, he said: "Why does fate target the people who are most important to me?"»
- Ch55 L5257: «"The depth of his anger. His willingness to go beyond the Force, as the Jedi know it, and to call on the power of the dark side. General Grievous will activate a special beacon that will call Skywalker and Kenobi back to Coruscant, and onto the stage we will set for them."»
- Ch55 L5261: «"Kill Kenobi.»
- Ch56 L5271: «Neither pilot wore a helmet, so Anakin could hear Obi-Wan plainly when he shouted: "For all the finks and jukes you've taken me through, there's no one else I'd rather fly with."»
- Ch56 L5275: «"To the best of my ability," Obi-Wan said. "I may not always be able to remain at your wing, but I won't be far off, and I'll always have your back."»
- Ch56 L5279: «"The day you call for help, I'll know that we're both in over our heads." Anakin adopted a serious look. "Obi-Wan, you don't know how many times you've already rescued me."»
- Ch56 L5281: «Obi-Wan swallowed the lump that formed in his throat. "Then whatever lies ahead for us shouldn't be a problem."»
- Ch56 L5285: «Obi-Wan returned a tight-lipped nod. "At least you said we."»

### B.6 Mace Windu: 230 entries. Aliases: `Mace|Windu`. Version: Mace, 19 BBY

- Ch7 L359: «"More the absence of it." Anakin laughed shortly. "If only Mace, Kit, or Shaak Ti could have seen you."»
- Ch10 L599: «To the extent that friendships could be forged with them, Bail had come to know a few: Jedi Masters Obi-Wan Kenobi, Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin - - the privileged few who also were allowed to meet personally with Palpatine.»
- Ch14 L1007: «"Master Sora Bulq and many others would agree, Anakin. But few Jedi have the stomach for such a ride. We're not all as self-composed as Yoda or Master Windu."»
- Ch17 L1311: «"Ten years ago it would have had all the makings of a full-blown diplomatic incident," Intelligence officer Dyne was explaining to Yoda and Mace Windu in the data room of the Jedi Temple.»
- Ch17 L1313: «"Since when are the Xi Char so forgiving?" Mace asked. Dressed in a brown belted tunic and beige trousers, he was poised on the edge of a desk, one booted foot planted on the shiny floor.»
- Ch17 L1317: «Mace glanced at Yoda, who was standing off to one side, both hands resting on the knob of his gimer stick. "Then the Xi Char Prelate isn't likely to report the incident to the Senate."»
- Ch17 L1323: «"We both know why," Mace said. "He's become Anakin's partisan."»
- Ch17 L1329: «Mace came to his feet. "Your people have been working on cracking that code for years."»
- Ch17 L1345: «"About his image, Sidious cares, ummm?" Mace's clean-shaven upper lip curled. "What was the source of the transmission?"»
- Ch17 L1351: «Mace nodded. "And Belderone next." Again his gaze fell on Dyne. "Anything further on the source of the original Sidious transmission?"»
- Ch17 L1355: «Mace paced away from the table. "Belderone is not a highly populated world, but it is friendly to the Republic. Grievous will kill millions just to make a point." He glanced at Yoda. "We can't let that happen."»
- Ch17 L1357: «Dyne looked from Mace to Yoda and back again. "If Republic forces are waiting when Grievous attacks, the Separatists will realize that we've managed to eavesdrop on their transmissions."»
- Ch17 L1365: «"The information stays here," Mace said firmly.»
- Ch17 L1369: «"Obi-Wan and Anakin aren't far from Belderone," Mace said. "But they're pursuing another lead to Sidious's whereabouts."»
- Ch20 L1549: «He and Mace Windu were in Yoda's chambers in the Jedi Temple, each atop a meditation dais.»
- Ch20 L1551: «"Representatives from Belderone have expressed their gratitude to the Senate," Mace said. "Despite the losses."»
- Ch20 L1555: «The muscles in Mace's jaw bunched. "Billions have died in this war. Belderone was saved, and, more importantly, we were able to keep Grievous on the run."»
- Ch20 L1563: «"Without apology," Mace said bluntly. "Our deference to him has to end."»
- Ch20 L1565: «"With the war's end, it will." Yoda turned slightly to regard Mace. "A terrible warning, Belderone is. Increasing, the power of the dark side is. Rooted out, Sidious must be."»
- Ch20 L1567: «Mace nodded gravely. "Rooted out and eliminated."»
- Ch23 L1721: «Mace Windu couldn't recall a visit to the Supreme Chancellor's chambers in the Senate Office Building when his attention hadn't been drawn to Palpatine's curious and somehow unsettling collection of quasi-religious statuary.»
- Ch23 L1721: «On one occasion, picking up on Mace's interest, Palpatine had offered lengthy and enthusiastic accounts of when and how he had come by some of the pieces.»
- Ch23 L1723: «Just now Mace's eyes were on a small bronzium statue Palpatine had once identified as Wapoe, the mythical artisan demigod of disguise.»
- Ch23 L1729: «He was seated for a change, atop a cushioned chair that made him appear even smaller than he was. Mace was at Yoda's left hand, sitting with legs widely spread, forearms resting on his knees.»
- Ch23 L1733: «"This is rather awkward, Master Yoda, but I suspect that the matter I have in mind is the very one that brought you and Master Windu here. By that I mean Belderone."»
- Ch23 L1741: «"We had no time to corroborate the intelligence we received," Mace said without hesitation. "We thought it best to commit as few Republic ships as could be spared. It was essentially a Jedi operation."»
- Ch23 L1749: «Mace leaned forward. "Wait for him to resurface, and strike again."»
- Ch23 L1753: «Before Mace could respond, he continued: "You see, the problem here is one of appearances.»
- Ch23 L1755: «Mace's nostrils flared. "We can't allow the Senate to go on dictating the course of the war."»
- Ch23 L1763: «Mace made it emphatic. "We're not rogues."»
- Ch23 L1783: «"The lessons learned from a thousand years of serving the Republic aren't entirely lost on us," Mace said strongly. "The Jedi Council is fully aware of such concerns."»
- Ch23 L1789: «Mace and Yoda waited.»
