- Ch23 L1793: «"A hyperwave transceiver that belonged to Viceroy Gunray was seized at Cato Neimoidia," Mace explained. "The device allowed Intelligence to decipher the Separatist code. A message transmitted by General Grievous to Viceroy Gunray regarding Belderone was monitored, and we acted on it."»
- Ch23 L1819: «This is getting out of control, Mace thought. He calmed himself before speaking.»
- Ch23 L1853: «"Hasten the end," Mace said.»
- Ch27 L2567: «A holorecording of Palpatine's speech was running in the center of the room when Bail was admitted. Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
- Ch27 L2585: «"What does he mean by that?" Mace Windu interjected.»
- Ch27 L2603: «Mace leaned forward in his chair. "What can Palpatine be thinking - - committing half of Coruscant's home force to the Outer Rim sieges?"»
- Ch27 L2615: «Mace ridiculed the idea. "When the body is damaged, it prioritizes. It doesn't rally its defenses to deal with a pinprick when the chest has been holed by a blaster bolt."»
- Ch27 L2625: «Mace ran a hand over his shaven head.»
- Ch30 L3047: «Macrobinoculars pressed to his eyes, Mace studied the distant building top to bottom, his gaze lingering on broken windows, fissured ledges, canted balconies.»
- Ch30 L3049: «Mace and his team of Jedi, clone commandos, and Intelligence analysts were a kilometer east of the structure, in an area of squat, peak-roofed foundries, lorded over by smoke-belching permacrete stacks.»
- Ch30 L3051: «A more dispiriting place this side of Eriadu or Korriban would have been hard to find, Mace told himself.»
- Ch30 L3053: «"Probe remotes are away, General Windu," the ARC reported.»
- Ch30 L3055: «Mace trained the macrobinoculars on the flock of meter-wide spherical droids that were maneuvering with purposeful unevenness toward the building.»
- Ch30 L3055: «Fortunately - - and admittedly unexpectedly - - Supreme Chancellor Palpatine had overruled the committee, and Mace had been allowed to compile a dream team that included not only ARC commander Valiant and Captain Dyne of Republic Intelligence, but also Jedi Master Shaak Ti and several capable Padawans.»
- Ch30 L3059: «Mace watched the black spheres begin to drift through shattered windows and into areas of the superstructure where the building's facade had disintegrated and the bones of its plasteel skeleton were exposed.»
- Ch30 L3069: «Mace lowered the macrobinoculars and shifted his gaze to the field holoprojector. Dazzled by diagonal lines of static, the 3-D images were of forlorn rooms, stretches of dark corridor, vast empty spaces.»
- Ch30 L3075: «"Doesn't mean much," Mace said. "Many structures in this district were self-powered, often by dangerous, highly unstable fuels." He gestured broadly. "They're still belching smoke."»
- Ch30 L3081: «Mace turned to Valiant.»
- Ch30 L3091: «No matter how often he heard it, the grunting, communal response to the ARC's rallying cry continued to disturb Mace on some level. Although it was probably no different from what the clone troopers heard when the Jedi said to one another, May the Force be with you.»
- Ch30 L3095: «She had proved instrumental in the defense of Kamino and Brentaal IV, and Mace was glad to have her with him now.»
- Ch30 L3101: «Leaping the gap, Mace activated his lightsaber and instructed the Padawans to follow suit. Weapons raised to their chests, the commandos spread out in fire-and-maneuver squads and began to move deeper in the building, checking out each room and alcove before declaring any level secure.»
- Ch30 L3103: «Mace's blade glowed amethyst in the gloom.»
- Ch30 L3103: «Yoda had warned Mace that the dark side might cloud his mind to certain rooms and passageways - - places that the Sith Lords didn't want Mace to discover - - but he felt alert in all ways.»
- Ch30 L3107: «Mace studied the 3-D map displayed by the ARC's wrist gauntlet projector.»
- Ch30 L3117: «"Everyone stay exactly where you are," Captain Dyne said when Mace and the rest of Aurek Team appeared at the mouth of a corridor that intersected the docking bay at its lengthwise centerpoint.»
- Ch30 L3119: «Thirty meters to Mace's right, Dyne and two other Intelligence officers were interpreting the data being sent to them by several probe droids meandering with design throughout the room, some of them misting the floor with a highly volatile substance.»
- Ch30 L3123: «"Dooku's ship," Mace said.»
- Ch30 L3125: «"A reasonable supposition, Master Windu," Dyne said loudly.»
- Ch30 L3133: «It was Dooku! Mace thought.»
- Ch30 L3135: «"Here, being one met with being two, lighter in weight, perhaps shorter in stature, and wearing - - " Dyne consulted what Mace assumed to be some sort of database.»
- Ch30 L3137: «Tasking the probe droids to track the prints of the second being, Dyne began to trail them, waving for Mace, Shaak Ti, and the commandos to follow.»
- Ch30 L3141: «Mace and Shaak Ti took the point, with the Padawans and commandos strung out behind. By the time the two Jedi Masters caught up with Dyne and his droids, the Intelligence analyst was standing at the door to a dated turbolift.»
- Ch30 L3149: «The turbolift was just roomy enough for Dyne, his associates, Mace, Shaak Ti, the two team commanders, and two probe droids. Comlinking troopers outside the building, Valiant ordered them to make their way to sub-basement two, but forewarned them to stay clear of the east turbolift and any nearby corridors or tunnels.»
- Ch30 L3155: «Stepping carefully from the car, he followed the probe droids to the entrance of a wide tunnel. After the droids had disappeared inside and returned, Dyne swung to Mace, who was waiting with everyone else at the base of the turbolift.»
- Ch30 L3159: «Mace and Shaak Ti joined Dyne and his teammates at the tunnel entrance. Shaak Ti peered into the darkness.»
- Ch30 L3163: «"Forget the branches," Mace said. "What's at the far end of this one?"»
- Ch30 L3169: «Mace walked two meters into the darkness, and ran his hand down the tunnel's tiled wall. Hundreds of Senators are now under the influence of a Sith Lord called Darth Sidious, Dooku had told Obi-Wan on Geonosis.»
- Ch30 L3171: «Turning to face Shaak Ti and the clone commanders, Mace said: "We're going to need more troops."»
- Ch31 L3265: «"These Sith to whom you keep referring." Palpatine fell silent in thought, then said: "When you were believed killed at Ithor, Master Windu said as much to me."»
- Ch32 L3347: «"Do I need to demonstrate what responses you can expect from Cin Drallig or Obi-Wan Kenobi? From Mace Windu or, stars help you, Yoda?"»
- Ch33 L3523: «Mace and Shaak Ti were able to locate the building in The Works.»
- Ch33 L3523: «Once inside, Mace's team discovered more than we could have even hoped for - - evidence of a more recent visit by Dooku, and of the person he apparently went to Coruscant to see."»
- Ch33 L3567: «"Anakin, Mace and Shaak Ti are more than capable of seeing to that - - if Sidious is even there."»
- Ch34 L3675: «Fifty meters ahead of Mace in the tunnel, Shaak Ti held up her hand, motioning for him to stop. Angling his purple blade to the floor, Mace turned to relay the signal to the commandos behind him.»
- Ch34 L3677: «Mace waved a signal to Commander Valiant, whose team moved forward stealthily, hugging the walls, their T-visor helmets allowing them to see in the dark.»
- Ch34 L3679: «Mace and Shaak Ti would ride in separate speeders behind the agents, intermingled with those of the commandos.»
- Ch34 L3683: «In the days since the raid, Mace's team had searched nearly every nook and cranny of the confusion of tunnels and shafts that undermined the Grungeon and similar assembly areas.»
- Ch34 L3685: «Still, Mace felt that the team was on the right track.»
- Ch34 L3687: «By now Mace could hear movement and soft voices ahead.»
- Ch34 L3687: «Standing nearby, Valiant looked to Mace for the go signal.»
- Ch34 L3687: «When Mace nodded, Valiant said: "Light it up!"»
- Ch34 L3689: «Weapons raised, gas and fragmentation grenades enabled, the commandos sprinted into the intersecting tunnel, firing tracer bolts into the gloom. Tight on their heels, Mace heard Valiant yell: "Down on the floor! Don't move! I said, don't move!"»
- Ch34 L3693: «All four of them? Mace thought. Edging through the commandos, he reached Valiant, whose BlasTech was aimed at a cowering crowd of thirty or so four-armed insectoid aliens, who were babbling in some language other than Basic, or speaking it with an accent so thick as to make their words unintelligible.»
- Ch34 L3695: «"Lower your weapons," Mace told the commandos. "And someone bring that interpreter droid forward!"»
- Ch34 L3697: «Mace's command was relayed down the line, and a moment later a highly polished silver protocol droid tottered into the tunnel, muttering to itself. "I don't understand how I've gone from serving the Separatists to serving the Republic. Did I undergo a partial memory wipe?"»
- Ch34 L3703: «"Droid!" Mace shouted.»
- Ch34 L3707: «Mace glanced at Valiant.»
- Ch34 L3711: «"Fine," Mace said, grabbing hold of TC-16 and pointing him in the direction of the terrified aliens. "See if you can make sense of what these folk are saying."»
- Ch34 L3713: «The droid listened to the babbling, responded in kind, and turned to face Mace.»
- Ch34 L3717: «Mace regarded the huddled, shivering group.»
- Ch34 L3725: «Mace frowned. The tunnels beneath the Grungeon Block held countless surprises.»
- Ch34 L3731: «"That's enough," Mace said. "Assure them that they're not going to be harmed, and that we'll see to it they reach a refugee camp."»
- Ch34 L3733: «He nodded to Valiant, who in turn told two of his troopers to carry out Mace's command.»
- Ch34 L3735: «"Talk about your corridor ghouls," Dyne said, eyeing the aliens as he approached Mace. "Squatters, death stick runners, lost droids, now undocumented refugees..."»
- Ch34 L3751: «Mace considered the idea, then shook his head.»
- Ch34 L3755: «Mace waved everyone back into motion, then fell into step with Shaak Ti.»
- Ch34 L3769: «Mace saw that the Intelligence analysts and commandos were gathered some twenty meters away.»
- Ch34 L3771: «Mace and Shaak Ti traded astonished looks.»
- Ch34 L3775: «Mace's brow furrowed.»
- Ch36 L3919: «Brown robe swirling behind him and lightsaber in hand, Mace barreled through the doorway, with Shaak Ti and the commandos close behind.»
- Ch36 L3923: «"Another corridor closer," Mace said, determined to put a good spin on it.»
- Ch36 L3931: «His eyes fixed on the display screen of his data processor, Captain Dyne ambled toward Mace and Shaak Ti.»
- Ch36 L3935: «Mace looked around the tunnel for signs of a concealed turbolift or staircase.»
- Ch36 L3943: «Mace tracked the droids as they moved deeper into the corridor. Five Hundred Republica: home to thousands of Coruscant's wealthiest Senators, celebrities, shipping magnates, and media tycoons. And one of them, very possibly a Sith Lord.»
- Ch38 L4073: «Mace could feel the oppressive weight of the structure bearing down on him as the team entered 500 Republica's level-one sub-basement - - square kilometers of supportive ferrocrete and durasteel, crammed with whining, whirring machines that kept the tower stable, aloft, secure, climate-controlled, and supplied with water and power.»
- Ch38 L4073: «For a time, Mace had considered appealing to Palpatine for permission, since the Supreme Chancellor had an upper-level suite in the building.»
- Ch38 L4085: «Mace gazed down the tunnel the team had taken.»
- Ch38 L4091: «Mace gestured to the smudged and stained ferrocrete floor.»
- Ch38 L4099: «"There'll be prints at his starting point," Mace said.»
- Ch38 L4105: «"How many more?" Mace said.»
- Ch38 L4115: «Mace glanced around.»
- Ch38 L4125: «"A quake?" Mace asked Shaak Ti.»
- Ch38 L4135: «It wouldn't be the first time an intoxicated or exhausted driver had veered from one of the free-travel skylanes and plowed into the side of a building, Mace told himself. And yet - -»
- Ch38 L4147: «Stretching out with the Force, Mace sensed danger, frenzy, pain, and death.»
- Ch38 L4155: «Mace's thoughts swirled. He turned to Valiant.»
- Ch38 L4161: «Mace looked at TC-16, then at Dyne.»
- Ch38 L4165: «Flanked by commandos, Mace and Shaak Ti raced off.»
- Ch38 L4167: «Mace tracked the ill-fated ship for a moment, then tilted his head back and put the edge of his hand to his brow. Distant buildings shimmered, as if miraged by heat.»
- Ch38 L4173: «In their oblate pools of white skin, Shaak Ti's eyes were wide when she looked at Mace.»
- Ch38 L4177: «Comlink already in hand, Mace activated the Jedi Temple frequency and held the device to his ear.»
- Ch38 L4183: «Mace's nostrils flared.»
- Ch39 L4259: «He perceived Mace running down corridors in the Temple; then turned as Mace rushed through the doorway.»
- Ch39 L4263: «"Tiin, Koon, Ki-Adi-Mundi, and some of the others are on their way up the well," Mace said. "I sent Stass Allie to assist Shaak Ti in guarding Chancellor Palpatine."»
- Ch39 L4269: «"Reports from naval command are garbled," Mace continued. "It's clear that the attack caught the home fleet by surprise. Groups of Separatist ships managed to penetrate the envelope before the fleet had time to engage. Now, by all accounts, our vessels are holding the line."»
- Ch39 L4275: «Mace's eyes narrowed.»
- Ch39 L4279: «"Secret, those routes were. Known to us and few others." Yoda looked at Mace. "Unrestricted access to the archives, Dooku had. Access enough to erase all mentions of Kamino. Access enough to learn of explorations in the Deep Core."»
- Ch39 L4281: «Mace went to the window wall and stared at the sky.»
- Ch39 L4291: «Mace swung back to Yoda.»
- Ch39 L4301: «Mace took a moment to answer.»
- Ch39 L4307: «Mace paced in front of the windows.»
- Ch39 L4313: «"The HoloNet is down," Mace said. "Surface communications are distorted by the defensive shields."»
- Ch40 L4321: «With a battle raging in space, and notwithstanding Mace Windu's command to the contrary, Dyne had been tempted to suspend the search for Sidious's lair and report back to the Intelligence division, as he had ordered the other analysts to do.»
- Ch40 L4331: «Dyne had decided to contact Mace Windu at the Jedi Temple for advice on how to proceed. But when his repeated attempts failed, he and Valiant had made a command decision to carry on without the Jedi.»
- Ch43 L4521: «"Intermittently, Master Yoda." He nodded his chin to the communications console. "Call Master Windu." Moments later, Windu's voice issued indistinctly from the console's annunciators.»
- Ch43 L4529: «Shutting his eyes to distance himself farther, he stretched out with his feelings, placing in his mind's eye Mace and Kit Fisto skimming through the deranged sky; Shaak Ti and Stass Allie hurrying toward Palpatine's quarters in 500 Republica; Saesee Tiin, Agen Kolar, Bultar Swan, and other Jedi Masters and Jedi Knights streaking from Coruscant's envelope in their starfighters, local space flashing with energy bolts and globular explosions, ships too numerous to count embroiled in a monumental battle...»
- Ch44 L4635: «Allie waited until the Red Guards had gathered around him. "Now - - quickly!" When everyone had moved into the hallway, Shaak Ti used her comlink to find Mace Windu. "Mace, Grievous is onworld," she said the moment she heard his voice.»
- Ch44 L4657: «Shaak Ti smiled at her and reactivated the comlink. "Mace," she said when he answered. "Another change in plans..."»
- Ch46 L4725: «And yet hadn't she done as much as anyone to encourage him to remain a Jedi; to heed the tutelage of Obi-Wan, Mace, and the others; to perpetuate the lie that was their secret life as husband and wife?»
- Ch47 L4769: «Mace asked himself as the mag-lev rushed at three hundred kilometers per hour toward the skytunnel that would spirit the train from the Senate District.»
- Ch47 L4769: «Through a gap in the protective circle the guards had forged, Mace caught a glimpse of Palpatine, his head of wavy gray hair lowered in what might have been anguish or deep concentration.»
- Ch47 L4771: «Mace asked himself.»
- Ch47 L4775: «Mace caught another glimpse of Palpatine, insulated by the flowing red robes of his handpicked bodyguards.»
- Ch47 L4775: «But Mace would make it his business to find the time later.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Yet another ill-timed concern, Mace thought.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Shaak Ti had told Mace and Kit about the Supreme Chancellor's earlier reluctance to leave his suite.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Mace didn't know what to make of it.»
- Ch47 L4781: «A blur of light off to the left-hand side of the car caught Mace's eye, and he hurried to the closest window.»
- Ch47 L4781: «Before Mace could utter a word of warning, cannon fire from one of the twin-winged ships stitched a broken line of holes across the blunt nose of a transport in the autonavigation lane.»
- Ch47 L4783: «Mace told the Jedi and Red Guards.»
- Ch47 L4783: «Not a moment later an earsplitting sound reached Mace from somewhere in the rear of the train and overhead.»
- Ch47 L4785: «Part of a group of mag-lev security personnel stationed there, a Weequay looked to Mace. "We won't be able to hold them back!"»
- Ch47 L4787: «In turn, Mace whirled to Shaak Ti and Allie. "Move the Chancellor into the forward car!"»
- Ch47 L4789: «Shaak Ti regarded him as if he had lost his mind. "It's packed, Mace!"»
- Ch47 L4791: «When there was space enough in the vestibule, Mace instructed the Weequay to unlock the door.»
- Ch47 L4793: «Mace allowed himself a moment of bewilderment.»
- Ch47 L4793: «But those passengers failed to recognize that one of the Jedi was Mace Windu - - rumored to have single-handedly destroyed a seismic tank on Dantooine and that the other was Kit Fisto, Nautolan hero of the Battle of Mon Calamari.»
- Ch47 L4795: «Sparks and smoke whirled through the car, and parts of spindly arms and legs flew about unavoidably, but Mace and Kit called on the Force to control even those.»
- Ch47 L4795: «No sooner had the final droid dropped than Mace leapt straight up through the rend, landing in a crouch on the roof of the next car down the line, holding himself in place by the Force with the wind whipping at the back of his shaved skull and coarse tunic.»
- Ch47 L4797: «Mace turned into the powerful wind and focused all his intention on a front flip that carried him back through the rend.»
- Ch47 L4797: «In what would surely have been a futile act, Mace raised his lightsaber.»
- Ch47 L4799: «Deactivating their blades, Mace and Kit rushed into the forward car, which was now filled with Palpatine's advisers and those passengers the Jedi women and Red Guards had relocated from the train's lead car.»
- Ch47 L4799: «Mace and Kit continued to squirm forward, arriving in the Supreme Chancellor's car just as the mag-lev was emerging from the skytunnel.»
- Ch47 L4801: «"Those fighters could easily have derailed us with a torpedo," Shaak Ti said as Mace and Kit approached. Mace leaned partway out the window, eyes searching the canyon.»
- Ch47 L4807: «"Grievous," Mace grumbled. Kit glanced at him. "Here we go again."»
- Ch47 L4811: «By the time they were ignited, Mace was already on and all over the cyborg, batting away at the two blades, swinging low at Grievous's artificial legs, thrusting at his skeletal face.»
- Ch47 L4811: «In a corner of Mace's mind he wondered to which Jedi Grievous's blades had belonged.»
- Ch47 L4811: «Just as the Force was keeping Mace from being blown from the mag-lev's roof, magnetism of some sort was keeping the general fastened in place.»
- Ch47 L4811: «For the cyborg, though, the coherence hindered as much as it helped, whereas Mace never remained in one place for very long.»
- Ch47 L4813: «As Mace already knew from Ki-Adi-Mundi and Shaak Ti, Grievous was well trained in the Jedi arts.»
- Ch47 L4813: «His strikes were as forceful as any Mace had ever had to counter, and his speed was astonishing.»
- Ch47 L4813: «But he didn't know Vaapad - - the technique of dark flirtation in which Mace excelled.»
- Ch47 L4815: «Successful at analyzing Mace's lightsaber style, those same computers suggested that Grievous alter his stance and posture, along with the angle of his parries, ripostes, and thrusts.»
- Ch47 L4817: «The result wasn't Vaapad, but it was close enough, and Mace wasn't interested in prolonging the contest any longer than necessary.»
- Ch47 L4817: «Mace saw by the surprised look in the cyborg's reptilian eyes that, for all his strength, dexterity, and resolve, the living part of him wasn't always in perfect sync with his alloy servos.»
- Ch47 L4819: «Clearly, Grievous - - onetime courageous commander of sentient troops - - realized what Mace had done and wanted to sidestep, where General Grievous - - current commander of droids and other war machines - - wanted nothing more than to impale Mace with lunging thrusts of the paired blades.»
- Ch47 L4819: «Slipping into the gap made by Mace's saber, Grievous's left talon lost magnetic purchase on the roof, and the general faltered.»
- Ch47 L4819: «Mace came out of his crouch prepared to drive his sword into Grievous's guts, but some last-instant firing of the general's cybersynapses compelled the cyborg's torso through a swift half twist that would have sent Mace's head hurtling into the canyon had the maneuver prevailed.»
- Ch47 L4821: «Instead Mace leapt backward, out of the range of the slicing blades, and Force-pushed outward, just at the instant of Grievous's single misstep.»
- Ch47 L4821: «Off the side of the car the general went, twisting and turning as he fell, Mace trying to track the general's contorted plunge, but unsuccessfully.»
- Ch47 L4823: «Mace couldn't take the time to puzzle it out.»
- Ch47 L4823: «Mace and Kit watched in awe as the two ships began to helix forward around the speeding mag-lev, exchanging constant fire.»
- Ch49 L4857: «Balanced on the balls of their feet, Mace and Kit hooked their lightsabers to their belts and drifted back down into the vestibule, as gently as the Force allowed.»
- Ch49 L4859: «A quick glance out the right side of the vestibule supplied Mace with the explanation.»
- Ch49 L4859: «Waiting for the train to quiet, Mace and Kit stood like statues in the vestibule.»
- Ch49 L4863: «Steps into Palpatine's car, Mace and Kit called on the Force to prevent everyone from flailing toward the vestibule door.»
- Ch49 L4865: «Centered in the Force, Mace directed all his energy toward keeping the Red Guards and others rooted in place.»
- Ch49 L4867: «Mace could feel an increasing sense of desperation sweep through the cars as passengers grew frantic to exit.»
- Ch49 L4871: «Mace recognized the more scorched of the pair of gunships as one of two that had been in pursuit of Grievous's gunboat.»
- Ch49 L4877: «Mace replayed the events in his mind.»
- Ch49 L4879: «Mace clenched his hands, and swung to Kit. "The gunboat could have caught him - - somehow." He gazed up at the pilot again. "Any word yet?"»
- Ch49 L4883: «"General Fisto and I are going with you." Mace turned to Shaak Ti, Allie, and the four newly arrived Jedi Knights.»
- Ch49 L4887: «Moments earlier, the gunship carrying Mace and Kit had banked north, back toward the Senate District, in apparent pursuit of Grievous's gunboat.»
- Ch50 L4905: «Mace and Kit stood in the open doorway of the Republic gunship as it threaded its way among the monads and skyscrapers of the Senate District.»
- Ch50 L4905: «Mace backed into the gunship as bolts sizzled past the doorway, nearly catching the underside of the starboard wing.»
- Ch50 L4905: «The fact that it had taken so little effort to track and catch up with the Separatist craft gnawed at Mace.»
- Ch50 L4907: «Mace leaned into the hatch to the gunner's compartment and called up to him.»
- Ch50 L4913: «Mace's brow furrowed. "I don't think so. Something's wrong about this." Overhead, missiles roared from the launchers and an explosion boomed and echoed from the surround of buildings. Black smoke and debris swept past the doorway, and the gunner whooped. "We got him, sir! He's trailing fire, and surface-bound!"»
- Ch50 L4915: «Mace and Kit leaned out the doorway in time to see the gunboat tip to one side, then begin a rapid downward spiral. "Stay with him, pilot!" Mace yelled.»
- Ch50 L4919: «Mace, Kit, and a dozen commandos jumped to the hot ground to secure the area.»
- Ch50 L4919: «Lightsabers ignited, Mace and Kit strode along the perimeter of the shallow well, alert to the slightest movements.»
- Ch50 L4921: «"I can accept that Grievous might have fallen from the mag-lev," Mace said, "but not that he would have included only two of his elite on a mission like this."»
- Ch50 L4925: «"Pilot!" Mace called toward the gunship. "Comlink the Supreme Chancellor's bunker, and arrange for us to be cleared through the shield."»
- Ch50 L4929: «Tyranus had warned him about Mace Windu's prowess with a blade, and now he understood.»
- Ch50 L4939: «Compared to Mace Windu, the four were mere novices, whose lightsaber techniques were some of the earliest Grievous had mastered.»
- Ch50 L4943: «Perhaps if Palpatine had been intelligent enough to have surrounded himself with real Jedi - - Jedi of the caliber of Windu and the tentacle-headed Kit Fisto - - the engagement might have gone differently.»
- Ch51 L4987: «Word of the firefight on the landing platform reached Mace and Kit in the gunship while they were returning to Sah'c.»
- Ch51 L4989: «An ARC commander verified that the hijacked gunship had been piloted by droids, but the same ARC would neither confirm nor deny that Grievous had been aboard the destroyed ship. That, alone, was cause for concern. Mace and Kit thought they knew what had occurred, and hoped they were wrong.»
- Ch51 L4991: «This time Mace and Kit didn't wait for the gunship to touch down.»
- Ch51 L4993: «Steps into the tunnel, their worst fears were realized when they saw three troopers hauling away a MagnaGuard, holed by more blaster bolts than would have been needed to demolish a police skimmer. The hijacked gunship had rescued Grievous after his fall from the mag-lev, Mace told himself.»
- Ch51 L4997: «Every meter of the tunnel presented Mace and Kit with fresh evidence of the ferocious fight that had taken place, in the form of slaughtered commandos and others.»
- Ch51 L4997: «Mace stopped counting after he reached forty.»
- Ch51 L4999: «Stass Allie, her face and hands blistered and her robes singed, was kneeling by the bodies of the four Jedi Knights with whom Mace had spoken briefly during the mag-lev evacuation.»
- Ch51 L5003: «Mace and Kit glanced at the door that led to those tunnels, then turned to Shaak Ti, who was standing by the bunker's holoprojector table as if lost. When Mace hurried over to her, she practically collapsed in his arms.»
- Ch51 L5007: «Mace supported her. "There will be no negotiations. The Supreme Chancellor won't allow it."»
- Ch51 L5009: «"The Senate may not see it that way, Mace." Shaak Ti composed herself and gazed around. "Grievous had help. Help from someone close to the top."»
- Ch51 L5013: «Mace looked at the commando. "How did they leave the complex?"»
- Ch51 L5017: «"How were they allowed through the shield?" Mace asked the commando.»
- Ch51 L5021: «Mace hadn't even thought to ask. Had assumed they had burned their way in - -»
- Ch51 L5025: «Mace and Kit glanced at each other in angry bewilderment. "What is the shuttle's location now?" Kit asked.»
- Ch51 L5029: «Mace's eyes widened in alert. "Do your gunners know that the Supreme Chancellor is aboard? Do they realize they can't fire on the shuttle?"»
- Ch51 L5037: «"On whose orders?" Mace fumed. "The Supreme Chancellor's top advisers."»
- Ch51 L5041: «Mace squared his shoulders. "Commander, scramble every available starfighter. That ship cannot be allowed to reach the Separatist fleet."»
- Ch53 L5117: «Seated in the copilot's seat of a Republic cruiser, Mace accepted that the shuttle couldn't be intercepted before it left Coruscant's envelope.»
- Ch53 L5119: «Mace and Kit's cruiser was still several kilometers behind the rising shuttle.»
- Ch53 L5127: «Despite his earlier misgivings, Mace thought for a moment that they might succeed. Then he watched in disquiet as five hundred droid fighters - - gushing from the great curving arms of a Trade Federation battleship - - swarmed forward to safeguard the shuttle in its flight to freedom.»
- Ch54 L5223: «Mace's voice rumbled from the speaker. "If we can ever break through this fighter screen, we'll storm the cruiser."»
- Ch54 L5235: «Mace fell silent for a moment. Yoda saw him in the cockpit of the cruiser he and Kit had piloted off Coruscant. "What should we do?"»
- Ch54 L5245: «Mace severed the transmission, and Yoda tottered to the windows.»

### B.7 Yoda: 154 entries. Aliases: `Yoda`. Version: Yoda, 19 BBY (Geonosis/Vjun lines are backstory)

- Ch4 L241: «Yes, just as Yoda had sensed after Qui-Gon Jinn and Obi-Wan had freed him from slavery on Tatooine and brought him to the Jedi Temple, he had a lot of anger in him.»
- Ch4 L241: «But what Yoda failed to realize was that anger could be a kind of fuel.»
- Ch10 L599: «To the extent that friendships could be forged with them, Bail had come to know a few: Jedi Masters Obi-Wan Kenobi, Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin - - the privileged few who also were allowed to meet personally with Palpatine.»
- Ch11 L667: «Yoda was aboard, eager to see for himself what Obi-Wan and Anakin had discovered.»
- Ch11 L669: «Feeling unnecessary, Obi-Wan and Yoda decided to take a stroll down the corridor of Viceroy Gunray's now appropriated palace.»
- Ch11 L669: «The venerable Jedi Master was pensive as they walked, the silence broken only by the sounds of distant blasterfire and the tick, tick of Yoda's gimer stick as it struck the polished floor.»
- Ch11 L671: «Yoda was unreadable.»
- Ch11 L671: «Obi-Wan wasn't sure if Yoda was pondering the image of Sidious, or the fact that two Jedi had been killed during the fighting on Cato Neimoidia.»
- Ch11 L673: «Reaching the end of the long corridor, Obi-Wan and Yoda turned and started back. Without taking his eyes from the floor, Yoda said: "Found something important, you have, Obi-Wan. That Count Dooku is in league with someone, proof this is. That in this war a greater part the Sith play than we realize."»
- Ch11 L675: «And yet, even after Dooku had revealed himself to be Sith-trained, Yoda and others on the Council continued to believe that he had been lying about Sidious.»
- Ch11 L681: «"Master Yoda," Obi-Wan said, "is it possible that Dooku wasn't lying about the Senate being under the control of Sidious?"»
- Ch11 L683: «Yoda gave his head a quick shake while they walked.»
- Ch11 L683: «Yoda paused for a moment, then added: "Perhaps at Geonosis, an accident it was that Dooku revealed himself.»
- Ch11 L695: «Yoda squeezed his eyes shut and nodded.»
- Ch11 L695: «Yoda stopped, then gestured to a carved bench.»
- Ch11 L699: «Yoda began.»
- Ch11 L711: «Yoda regarded him for a long moment. "Difficult to say," he said quickly. "Maybe, yes; maybe, no. More important the shroud of the dark side is. Many, many discussions Dooku had. With me, with other members of the Council. Most of all, with Master Sifo-Dyas."»
- Ch11 L713: «Yoda shook his head mournfully.»
- Ch11 L717: «Yoda gazed at the floor. "Saw with my own eyes what he had become, and refused to believe it, I did."»
- Ch11 L731: «Obi-Wan turned to face Yoda. "Master, did Sifo-Dyas order the clone army? "»
- Ch11 L733: «Yoda nodded. "Contacted the Kaminoans, he did."»
- Ch11 L755: «"Murdered?" Yoda compressed his thin lips. "Unsolved the crime remains, but, yes: murdered."»
- Ch11 L757: «"Someone knew," Obi-Wan said, more to himself. "Dooku?" he asked Yoda.»
- Ch11 L781: «"More lies," Yoda said. "Building a case to enlist you in his cause, Dooku was." You must join me, Obi-Wan, Dooku had said, and together we will destroy the Sith! "If Gunray hadn't been so keen on assassinating Padme Amidala,"»
- Ch11 L793: «Yoda shook his head. "Meant to learn of the clone army, we were. Destined to fight this war, we were."»
- Ch11 L797: «"Nonsense," Yoda said. "Warriors always have we been."»
- Ch11 L801: «Yoda grimaced. "Impatient with such talk I grow. Cryptic this conflict is - - the way it began, the way it unfolds. But for the ideals of the Republic we fight. To prevail and restore peace our priorities must remain. Then to the dark heart of this matter will we burrow. Expose the truth, we will."»
- Ch11 L803: «Yoda was correct, Obi-Wan told himself.»
- Ch11 L803: «But why hadn't Yoda told him sooner about Sifo-Dyas?»
- Ch11 L803: «Yoda's way of telling him to search for the thing that didn't seem to be there by analyzing its effects on the world around it.»
- Ch11 L805: «Yoda was regarding him when he lifted his head. "Reveal you, your thoughts do, Obi-Wan. Believe I should have told you sooner, you do."»
- Ch11 L813: «Yoda looked hard at Obi-Wan.»
- Ch12 L863: «"What incident?" Anakin was tempted to disclose the details of his and Obi-Wan's discovery, but Yoda had told him to remain silent about the mechno-chair. "Nothing important," he said. "But I always feel guilty when I become angry."»
- Ch14 L981: «Returning to the launching bay with Yoda, Obi-Wan observed Anakin and Yoda trade the briefest of looks, the meaning of which escaped him.»
- Ch14 L981: «Neither Jedi appeared to be bothered by the silent exchange, and yet Yoda doddered off without a word to speak with the Intelligence analysts huddled near the shuttle's boarding ramp.»
- Ch14 L983: «"Jedi Council business?" Anakin asked when Obi-Wan joined him. "Nothing of the sort. Yoda believes that the mechno-chair may yield clues to the whereabouts of Darth Sidious. He wants us to take up the search."»
- Ch14 L995: «"Decisions are not always unanimous. When we are truly divided, we defer to Yoda's counsel."»
- Ch14 L999: «Obi-Wan tried to discern Anakin's intent. "Even Yoda is not infallible, if that's what you're getting at."»
- Ch14 L1007: «"Master Sora Bulq and many others would agree, Anakin. But few Jedi have the stomach for such a ride. We're not all as self-composed as Yoda or Master Windu."»
- Ch14 L1019: «Too sure of themselves, the Jedi have become, Yoda had once told him.»
- Ch14 L1021: «"Carries on his shoulders the weight of the galaxy, Obi-Wan does," Yoda said, approaching with one of the Intelligence analysts. "Ease your concerns, this news might," he added before Obi-Wan could respond.»
- Ch14 L1025: «"Better you feel already, ummm?" Yoda said to Obi-Wan, motioning with his gimer stick.»
- Ch14 L1027: «"The chair bears the stamps of several of the manufacturers affiliated with Dooku," Dyne continued. "The hyperwave receiver is equipped with a type-summoning chip and transponding antenna that are similar to ones we discovered in a mine-laying chameleon droid Master Yoda brought back from Ilum."»
- Ch14 L1043: «Yoda laughed. "Ahead of Obi-Wan, the experts are," he said to Anakin. "We know who's responsible for the chair's Neimoidian engravings,"»
- Ch16 L1143: «But Yoda had insisted that Obi-Wan and Anakin attempt to speak personally with the Xi Charrian whose sigil had been discovered on the walking chair.»
- Ch17 L1311: «"Ten years ago it would have had all the makings of a full-blown diplomatic incident," Intelligence officer Dyne was explaining to Yoda and Mace Windu in the data room of the Jedi Temple.»
- Ch17 L1317: «Mace glanced at Yoda, who was standing off to one side, both hands resting on the knob of his gimer stick. "Then the Xi Char Prelate isn't likely to report the incident to the Senate."»
- Ch17 L1321: «"Reach the ears of the Supreme Chancellor, it won't," Yoda said. "But surprised I was by Obi-Wan's report. Losing some of his better judgment, Obi-Wan is."»
- Ch17 L1325: «"If the Chosen One Skywalker is, then a hundred such diplomatic incidents we should suffer without concern." Yoda shut his eyes for a moment, then looked at the Intelligence analyst. "But come to tell us of these things, Captain Dyne hasn't."»
- Ch17 L1333: «Yoda nodded. "Remember the incident well, we do."»
- Ch17 L1339: «"General Grievous," Yoda said, narrowing his eyes.»
- Ch17 L1349: «"Rendezvous of the Confederacy fleet, it is," Yoda said.»
- Ch17 L1355: «Mace paced away from the table. "Belderone is not a highly populated world, but it is friendly to the Republic. Grievous will kill millions just to make a point." He glanced at Yoda. "We can't let that happen."»
- Ch17 L1357: «Dyne looked from Mace to Yoda and back again. "If Republic forces are waiting when Grievous attacks, the Separatists will realize that we've managed to eavesdrop on their transmissions."»
- Ch17 L1359: «Yoda pressed his fingers to his lips in thought. "Act, we must. Lying in wait, Republic forces will be."»
- Ch17 L1361: «Dyne nodded. "You're right, of course. If no actions are taken, and word of this intelligence were to leak..." He regarded Yoda. "Do we inform the Supreme Chancellor?"»
- Ch17 L1363: «Yoda's ears twitched. "Difficult, this decision is."»
- Ch17 L1367: «Yoda sighed with purpose. "Agree I do. Use the beacon we will, to gather a force."»
- Ch17 L1371: «"Wait, the lead will. Needed Obi-Wan and Anakin will be." Yoda turned to the still image of General Grievous. "Prepare carefully for this battle, we must."»
- Ch20 L1547: «"Trained well by Dooku, General Grievous was," Yoda said.»
- Ch20 L1549: «He and Mace Windu were in Yoda's chambers in the Jedi Temple, each atop a meditation dais.»
- Ch20 L1549: «Yoda recalled his duel with Dooku in the solar sailer's docking bay on Geonosis.»
- Ch20 L1553: «Yoda shook his head sadly. "More than ten thousand killed. Twenty-seven Jedi."»
- Ch20 L1561: «Yoda fell silent for a moment, then said: "Speak with the Supreme Chancellor, we must."»
- Ch20 L1565: «"With the war's end, it will." Yoda turned slightly to regard Mace. "A terrible warning, Belderone is. Increasing, the power of the dark side is. Rooted out, Sidious must be."»
- Ch22 L1683: «None, perhaps, more than aged Yoda.»
- Ch22 L1693: «Though they hadn't enjoyed the smoothest of student-teacher relationships, he and Yoda had spoken openly of the portents.»
- Ch22 L1693: «But Yoda was living proof of a conservatism that came with extended life.»
- Ch23 L1727: «"Then speak of your matter first, we will," Yoda said.»
- Ch23 L1729: «He was seated for a change, atop a cushioned chair that made him appear even smaller than he was. Mace was at Yoda's left hand, sitting with legs widely spread, forearms resting on his knees.»
- Ch23 L1733: «"This is rather awkward, Master Yoda, but I suspect that the matter I have in mind is the very one that brought you and Master Windu here. By that I mean Belderone."»
- Ch23 L1735: «Yoda compressed his lips. "Fail you, your intuition doesn't. About Belderone, much to say, we have."»
- Ch23 L1745: «"A rout it was not," Yoda said. "To hyperspace Grievous fled. But protecting the Separatist leaders, he was."»
- Ch23 L1753: «Didn't you and I have this discussion after Master Yoda was thought to have been killed at Ithor?»
- Ch23 L1757: «Yoda nodded, sagely. "Miring the Jedi in uncertainty, some of the Senate's decisions are."»
- Ch23 L1773: «Yoda frowned. "In collusion we must be, if victory the goal remains."»
- Ch23 L1777: «"Master Yoda, far be it from me to lecture someone of your vast experience on the nature of politics.»
- Ch23 L1789: «Mace and Yoda waited.»
- Ch23 L1799: «"Unlikely," Yoda said. "After Belderone."»
- Ch23 L1809: «"No," Yoda almost barked. "But into this office, come and go many. Our own counsel we kept."»
- Ch23 L1817: «"I fear you delude yourself in this instance, Master Yoda, if you believe that the passage of time makes those `reproaches' any less valid to your critics."»
- Ch23 L1841: «Turning back to Yoda, he said: "Forgive me, but why is this of such great concern?"»
- Ch23 L1845: «Again, Palpatine paused to consider Yoda's words.»
- Ch26 L2441: «Mastering evil, Yoda would have said.»
- Ch26 L2451: «More than once it had occurred to Dooku that Sidious and Yoda had certain qualities in common.»
- Ch26 L2451: «On Geonosis, Yoda's easy parrying and, indeed, handling of the Sith lightning Dooku hurled at him had come as a surprise.»
- Ch26 L2451: «Had made him wonder if, on some level during the course of Yoda's eight-hundred-odd years, the Jedi Master hadn't delved into the dark arts, if only as a means of familiarizing himself with his perceived enemy.»
- Ch26 L2451: «And on Vjun, only months ago, Yoda himself had admitted as much.»
- Ch26 L2451: «Yoda probably believed that he had defeated Dooku on Geonosis.»
- Ch27 L2567: «A holorecording of Palpatine's speech was running in the center of the room when Bail was admitted. Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
- Ch27 L2571: «"A show of force, this is," Yoda remarked.»
- Ch27 L2575: «Yoda harrumphed.»
- Ch27 L2591: «Turning to Bail, Yoda said, "Appreciate your visit, we do, Senator Organa."»
- Ch27 L2601: «"Premature, any celebration is," Yoda said ruefully.»
- Ch27 L2611: «"Separatist bastions, they are," Yoda said. "But so remote, so insignificant."»
- Ch27 L2621: «Yoda compressed his lips in indignation.»
- Ch28 L2659: «Anakin winced. "You know it's time to end the partnership when that happens. In fact, I could see you and Yoda teaming up. You share the same fondness for caution and lectures."»
- Ch28 L2661: «"Yes, we're two of a kind, old Yoda and me."»
- Ch30 L3103: «Yoda had warned Mace that the dark side might cloud his mind to certain rooms and passageways - - places that the Sith Lords didn't want Mace to discover - - but he felt alert in all ways.»
- Ch31 L3175: «In the Supreme Chancellor's Senate Office Building chambers, Yoda sat staring across the desk at Palpatine, silhouetted against the long window that overlooked western Coruscant.»
- Ch31 L3179: «"I understand your concerns entirely, Master Yoda," he was saying.»
- Ch31 L3181: «"Exhort me, you need not, Supreme Chancellor," Yoda said.»
- Ch31 L3189: «"My address was a reflection of the spirit of the times, Master Yoda. What's more, I spoke from my heart."»
- Ch31 L3205: «"Master Yoda, forgive my frankness, but if the Jedi are indeed too widely scattered to coordinate the sieges, then the burden will have to fall to my naval commanders."»
- Ch31 L3207: «Yoda compressed his lips and shook his head.»
- Ch31 L3215: «"Not true," Yoda snapped. "For the Republic, and none other."»
- Ch31 L3219: «Yoda made a glum face.»
- Ch31 L3239: «Yoda shook his head.»
- Ch31 L3245: «"That does not instill confidence, Master Yoda. If it's nothing more than an oath, then you are duty-bound to reconsider."»
- Ch31 L3261: «Yoda wagged his forefinger at Palpatine.»
- Ch31 L3277: «Yoda allowed his sadness to show.»
- Ch31 L3283: «Yoda raised his gaze.»
- Ch31 L3289: «Yoda's eyes fidgeted.»
- Ch31 L3299: «"Only Dooku's determination. Different it will be, because a Sith late he has become." Yoda shook his head. "Hard to know if Dooku a true Sith is, or simply with the power of the dark side infatuated."»
- Ch31 L3303: «Yoda made a gesture of dismissal.»
- Ch31 L3313: «"You are persuasive, Master Yoda. You have my word that I will bear this conversation in mind when I meet with the Senate to discuss our campaigns."»
- Ch31 L3321: «Yoda leaned forward for emphasis.»
- Ch32 L3335: «The conversation he had had with Yoda on dreary Vjun was never far from his thoughts these days.»
- Ch32 L3335: «For all his flair with words, all his Force-given personal power, Yoda was nothing more than an old one, unwilling to embrace anything new, indisposed to see any way but his own.»
- Ch32 L3347: «"Do I need to demonstrate what responses you can expect from Cin Drallig or Obi-Wan Kenobi? From Mace Windu or, stars help you, Yoda?"»
- Ch33 L3549: «"The Council was able to convince Palpatine of the need to retake Tythe, and he has authorized a full battle group to divert there. It seems he is finally willing to follow Master Yoda's advice about concentrating on dismantling the Confederacy leadership."»
- Ch39 L4193: «Such mastery was one of the skills that distinguished Sidious from Yoda, who believed the future was so much in motion it could not be read with any clarity - - especially during times when the dark side was on the ascendant.»
- Ch39 L4193: «But how could Yoda be expected to see the whole picture with one eye closed?»
- Ch39 L4229: «Yoda surrendered himself to the current of the Force.»
- Ch39 L4231: «Master Yoda, he might say, we still have much to learn. The Force remains a code only partially deciphered. But another key has been found. We will become stronger than we have ever been...»
- Ch39 L4233: «Today the current was interrupted by eddies and whirlpools, hydraulic traps whose roar overpowered the voices Yoda sought to hear.»
- Ch39 L4239: «Some Jedi believed that Yoda wasn't aware of these things, or that he hadn't done enough to stem the tide of the dark side.»
- Ch39 L4241: «Yoda was not that one. Aged, experienced, diplomatic, informative, brilliant with a lightsaber... Yes, all of these things. And not unacquainted with the power of the dark side.»
- Ch39 L4251: «Yoda's eyes snapped open. A disturbance in the Force - - of such magnitude that he had been hurled from the current. At his thought command, the window shutters in his quarters opened, and he gazed out on Coruscant, over the plain of The Works and beyond.»
- Ch39 L4265: «Yoda nodded sagely.»
- Ch39 L4271: «Yoda adopted an expression that mixed anger and bafflement.»
- Ch39 L4279: «"Secret, those routes were. Known to us and few others." Yoda looked at Mace. "Unrestricted access to the archives, Dooku had. Access enough to erase all mentions of Kamino. Access enough to learn of explorations in the Deep Core."»
- Ch39 L4291: «Mace swung back to Yoda.»
- Ch39 L4295: «"Desperate he is," Yoda said, more to himself. "It's not in his programming."»
- Ch39 L4297: «Yoda looked up.»
- Ch39 L4315: «Yoda nodded.»
- Ch43 L4507: «Yoda continued to pace the floor of the Temple's computer room, then stopped and pointed the business end of his gimer stick at the Jedi seated at the beacon's control console.»
- Ch43 L4511: «"Dooku," Yoda cut her off. "Shares our secrets with his confederates, he does."»
- Ch43 L4515: «Yoda nodded. "Already considered that, Master Tiin has. Attempt to recall Jedi from Belderone, Tythe, and other worlds, he will."»
- Ch43 L4519: «"Hmph. On Grievous's objective, that depends. Leave Coruscant soon and only slightly bruised, he might. Wait, we must, until he reveals his plan." Yoda paused to consider his own words, then leaned his weight on the gimer stick and looked hard at Lari Oll. "Enabled the comm is?"»
- Ch43 L4521: «"Intermittently, Master Yoda." He nodded his chin to the communications console. "Call Master Windu." Moments later, Windu's voice issued indistinctly from the console's annunciators.»
- Ch43 L4525: «"Raised, the defense shields are. Among one another, districts are unable to communicate." Yoda grimaced, then nodded once more. "Master Ti, try."»
- Ch43 L4529: «Yoda paced away from the console, deliberately turning his back to the glut of devices, screens, data displays, in a kind of countermeasure.»
- Ch43 L4531: «Yoda stretched out farther, immersing himself fully in the Force - - only to feel his breath catch in his throat. Frigid, the current became. Arctic. And for the first time he could feel Sidious. Feel him on Coruscant!»
- Ch45 L4665: «On Vjun, Yoda had implied that the Jedi Temple would always be open to Dooku's return...»
- Ch48 L4829: «Yoda's voice in his ear: Fear leads to anger; anger to hated; hatred to the dark side...»
- Ch49 L4865: «They would need Yoda.»
- Ch49 L4865: «Perhaps five Yodas.»
- Ch49 L4885: «Stass Allie and the four Jedi Knights Yoda had dispatched from the Temple stood just inside the door, shoulder-to-shoulder with commandos and government agents.»
- Ch54 L5221: «In his quarters in the Temple, Yoda watched a HoloNet feed that showed the bodies of two Jedi drifting in space, close to the flagship of the Separatist fleet. The corners of his mouth pulled down in sadness, he turned to the comlink. "See them, I do."»
- Ch54 L5235: «Mace fell silent for a moment. Yoda saw him in the cockpit of the cruiser he and Kit had piloted off Coruscant. "What should we do?"»
- Ch54 L5239: «"Master Yoda, we were close to capturing Sidious. I could feel it."»
- Ch54 L5241: «"Knew this, Sidious did. Hiding, he is." No longer on Coruscant, Yoda thought.»
- Ch54 L5245: «Mace severed the transmission, and Yoda tottered to the windows.»

### B.8 Kit Fisto: 47 entries. Aliases: `Kit\b|Fisto|Nautolan`. Version: Kit, 19 BBY

- Ch7 L359: «"More the absence of it." Anakin laughed shortly. "If only Mace, Kit, or Shaak Ti could have seen you."»
- Ch27 L2567: «A holorecording of Palpatine's speech was running in the center of the room when Bail was admitted. Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
- Ch43 L4523: «"... Fisto and I... Senate building. Shaak... Allie... to the Chancellor's quarters in Five Hundred Republica. We... with them - - "»
- Ch43 L4529: «Shutting his eyes to distance himself farther, he stretched out with his feelings, placing in his mind's eye Mace and Kit Fisto skimming through the deranged sky; Shaak Ti and Stass Allie hurrying toward Palpatine's quarters in 500 Republica; Saesee Tiin, Agen Kolar, Bultar Swan, and other Jedi Masters and Jedi Knights streaking from Coruscant's envelope in their starfighters, local space flashing with energy bolts and globular explosions, ships too numerous to count embroiled in a monumental battle...»
- Ch44 L4641: «"Kit and I are nearby."»
- Ch47 L4769: «Having boarded the mag-lev at its 500 Republica platform, he, Kit Fisto, Shaak Ti, and Stass Allie were in the car the Supreme Chancellor's Red Guards had commandeered - - second in a train of some twenty cars.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Shaak Ti had told Mace and Kit about the Supreme Chancellor's earlier reluctance to leave his suite.»
- Ch47 L4791: «He gestured for Kit Fisto, and the two of them shouldered through the cluster of security personnel at the rear of the car and activated their lightsabers.»
- Ch47 L4791: «Without hesitation he and Kit dashed through the vestibule and on into the rear car, where most of the mixed-species passengers were heaped atop seats to both sides of the wide aisle.»
- Ch47 L4793: «But those passengers failed to recognize that one of the Jedi was Mace Windu - - rumored to have single-handedly destroyed a seismic tank on Dantooine and that the other was Kit Fisto, Nautolan hero of the Battle of Mon Calamari.»
- Ch47 L4795: «Sparks and smoke whirled through the car, and parts of spindly arms and legs flew about unavoidably, but Mace and Kit called on the Force to control even those.»
- Ch47 L4799: «Deactivating their blades, Mace and Kit rushed into the forward car, which was now filled with Palpatine's advisers and those passengers the Jedi women and Red Guards had relocated from the train's lead car.»
- Ch47 L4799: «Mace and Kit continued to squirm forward, arriving in the Supreme Chancellor's car just as the mag-lev was emerging from the skytunnel.»
- Ch47 L4801: «"Those fighters could easily have derailed us with a torpedo," Shaak Ti said as Mace and Kit approached. Mace leaned partway out the window, eyes searching the canyon.»
- Ch47 L4803: «"And battle droids don't just drop from the sky. There's a third craft." Kit's bulging black eyes indicated Palpatine. "They want to take him alive."»
- Ch47 L4807: «"Grievous," Mace grumbled. Kit glanced at him. "Here we go again."»
- Ch47 L4813: «To the rear of the car, where Grievous's pair of MagnaGuards had made the mistake of pitting themselves against Kit Fisto, the Nautolan's blade was a cyclone of blazing blue light.»
- Ch47 L4813: «Resistant to the energy outpourings of a lightsaber, the phrik alloy staffs were potent weapons, but like any weapon they needed to find their target, and Kit simply wasn't allowing that.»
- Ch47 L4823: «Mace and Kit watched in awe as the two ships began to helix forward around the speeding mag-lev, exchanging constant fire.»
- Ch49 L4857: «Balanced on the balls of their feet, Mace and Kit hooked their lightsabers to their belts and drifted back down into the vestibule, as gently as the Force allowed.»
- Ch49 L4859: «Waiting for the train to quiet, Mace and Kit stood like statues in the vestibule.»
- Ch49 L4863: «Steps into Palpatine's car, Mace and Kit called on the Force to prevent everyone from flailing toward the vestibule door.»
- Ch49 L4865: «He wondered if he, Kit, Shaak Ti, and Allie - - acting in concert - - could support the entire train, but dismissed the idea immediately.»
- Ch49 L4867: «"The emergency repulsorlifts," Kit said.»
- Ch49 L4867: «He and Kit did their best to make that happen as quickly as possible.»
- Ch49 L4879: «Mace clenched his hands, and swung to Kit. "The gunboat could have caught him - - somehow." He gazed up at the pilot again. "Any word yet?"»
- Ch49 L4883: «"General Fisto and I are going with you." Mace turned to Shaak Ti, Allie, and the four newly arrived Jedi Knights.»
- Ch49 L4887: «Moments earlier, the gunship carrying Mace and Kit had banked north, back toward the Senate District, in apparent pursuit of Grievous's gunboat.»
- Ch50 L4905: «Mace and Kit stood in the open doorway of the Republic gunship as it threaded its way among the monads and skyscrapers of the Senate District.»
- Ch50 L4907: «Neither he nor Kit could shake the feeling that the gunboat had practically been waiting for them above the squat Senate Building, and had only then attempted to go evasive.»
- Ch50 L4911: «"The ship could have gone down," Kit suggested.»
- Ch50 L4915: «Mace and Kit leaned out the doorway in time to see the gunboat tip to one side, then begin a rapid downward spiral. "Stay with him, pilot!" Mace yelled.»
- Ch50 L4919: «Mace, Kit, and a dozen commandos jumped to the hot ground to secure the area.»
- Ch50 L4919: «Lightsabers ignited, Mace and Kit strode along the perimeter of the shallow well, alert to the slightest movements.»
- Ch50 L4923: «Kit gazed at the wedge of night sky. "There could be a second assault craft."»
- Ch50 L4943: «Perhaps if Palpatine had been intelligent enough to have surrounded himself with real Jedi - - Jedi of the caliber of Windu and the tentacle-headed Kit Fisto - - the engagement might have gone differently.»
- Ch51 L4987: «Word of the firefight on the landing platform reached Mace and Kit in the gunship while they were returning to Sah'c.»
- Ch51 L4989: «An ARC commander verified that the hijacked gunship had been piloted by droids, but the same ARC would neither confirm nor deny that Grievous had been aboard the destroyed ship. That, alone, was cause for concern. Mace and Kit thought they knew what had occurred, and hoped they were wrong.»
- Ch51 L4991: «This time Mace and Kit didn't wait for the gunship to touch down.»
- Ch51 L4997: «Every meter of the tunnel presented Mace and Kit with fresh evidence of the ferocious fight that had taken place, in the form of slaughtered commandos and others.»
- Ch51 L5003: «Mace and Kit glanced at the door that led to those tunnels, then turned to Shaak Ti, who was standing by the bunker's holoprojector table as if lost. When Mace hurried over to her, she practically collapsed in his arms.»
- Ch51 L5011: «Kit nodded. "We'll find out who. But our first priority is to rescue the Supreme Chancellor."»
- Ch51 L5025: «Mace and Kit glanced at each other in angry bewilderment. "What is the shuttle's location now?" Kit asked.»
- Ch51 L5033: «"Who else knows of the abduction?" Kit thought to ask. "Has this been released or leaked to the media?"»
- Ch53 L5119: «Mace and Kit's cruiser was still several kilometers behind the rising shuttle.»
- Ch53 L5125: «Calling full power from the cruiser, Kit Fisto joined the three Jedi starfighters in a flat-out race for the shuttle.»
- Ch54 L5235: «Mace fell silent for a moment. Yoda saw him in the cockpit of the cruiser he and Kit had piloted off Coruscant. "What should we do?"»

### B.9 Shaak Ti: 94 entries. Aliases: `Shaak Ti`. Version: Shaak Ti, 19 BBY

- Ch7 L359: «"More the absence of it." Anakin laughed shortly. "If only Mace, Kit, or Shaak Ti could have seen you."»
- Ch27 L2567: «A holorecording of Palpatine's speech was running in the center of the room when Bail was admitted. Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
- Ch30 L3055: «Fortunately - - and admittedly unexpectedly - - Supreme Chancellor Palpatine had overruled the committee, and Mace had been allowed to compile a dream team that included not only ARC commander Valiant and Captain Dyne of Republic Intelligence, but also Jedi Master Shaak Ti and several capable Padawans.»
- Ch30 L3093: «He swung and waved a signal to Shaak Ti. "I'll ride with Aurek Team. You have Bacta."»
- Ch30 L3095: «As beautiful as a flower, as deadly as a viper, Shaak Ti was the Jedi Master one wanted by one's side in chaotic circumstances.»
- Ch30 L3119: «Deployed in a circle formation, Shaak Ti and the Padawans who had entered with Bacta were clustered in the middle of the floor.»
- Ch30 L3137: «Tasking the probe droids to track the prints of the second being, Dyne began to trail them, waving for Mace, Shaak Ti, and the commandos to follow.»
- Ch30 L3141: «Mace and Shaak Ti took the point, with the Padawans and commandos strung out behind. By the time the two Jedi Masters caught up with Dyne and his droids, the Intelligence analyst was standing at the door to a dated turbolift.»
- Ch30 L3149: «The turbolift was just roomy enough for Dyne, his associates, Mace, Shaak Ti, the two team commanders, and two probe droids. Comlinking troopers outside the building, Valiant ordered them to make their way to sub-basement two, but forewarned them to stay clear of the east turbolift and any nearby corridors or tunnels.»
- Ch30 L3159: «Mace and Shaak Ti joined Dyne and his teammates at the tunnel entrance. Shaak Ti peered into the darkness.»
- Ch30 L3171: «Turning to face Shaak Ti and the clone commanders, Mace said: "We're going to need more troops."»
- Ch33 L3523: «Mace and Shaak Ti were able to locate the building in The Works.»
- Ch33 L3567: «"Anakin, Mace and Shaak Ti are more than capable of seeing to that - - if Sidious is even there."»
- Ch34 L3675: «Fifty meters ahead of Mace in the tunnel, Shaak Ti held up her hand, motioning for him to stop. Angling his purple blade to the floor, Mace turned to relay the signal to the commandos behind him.»
- Ch34 L3677: «Shaak Ti's whisper reached him through the Force: Movement ahead.»
- Ch34 L3679: «Mace and Shaak Ti would ride in separate speeders behind the agents, intermingled with those of the commandos.»
- Ch34 L3739: «Shaak Ti joined them.»
- Ch34 L3747: «"How far are we from the Senate District?" Shaak Ti asked.»
- Ch34 L3755: «Mace waved everyone back into motion, then fell into step with Shaak Ti.»
- Ch34 L3769: «He and Shaak Ti had been so engrossed in their private thoughts that neither of them had noticed the probe droids stopping to investigate something.»
- Ch34 L3771: «Mace and Shaak Ti traded astonished looks.»
- Ch36 L3919: «Brown robe swirling behind him and lightsaber in hand, Mace barreled through the doorway, with Shaak Ti and the commandos close behind.»
- Ch36 L3921: «"Surprise," Shaak Ti said flatly. "Another corridor."»
- Ch36 L3931: «His eyes fixed on the display screen of his data processor, Captain Dyne ambled toward Mace and Shaak Ti.»
- Ch36 L3937: «"Up or down?" Shaak Ti asked, equally bewildered.»
- Ch38 L4079: «"In other words, he could have arrived here from just about anywhere on Coruscant," Shaak Ti said.»
- Ch38 L4113: «"You'll have whatever clearance you need," Shaak Ti promised.»
- Ch38 L4125: «"A quake?" Mace asked Shaak Ti.»
- Ch38 L4141: «"We're tiers below midlevel," Shaak Ti said.»
- Ch38 L4157: «"Commander, Shaak Ti and I will need half your squad. You and the rest of your team will assist Captain Dyne with the search. Keep me informed of your progress."»
- Ch38 L4165: «Flanked by commandos, Mace and Shaak Ti raced off.»
- Ch38 L4173: «In their oblate pools of white skin, Shaak Ti's eyes were wide when she looked at Mace.»
- Ch38 L4181: «"The deflector shield," Shaak Ti said. She craned her neck, striped montrals and head-tail quivering. "Or could they be jamming transmissions?"»
- Ch38 L4185: «"Crowd control!" he told the commandos. To Shaak Ti, he said: "Find Palpatine. See to it he's conveyed to safety. I'll send backup."»
- Ch39 L4263: «"Tiin, Koon, Ki-Adi-Mundi, and some of the others are on their way up the well," Mace said. "I sent Stass Allie to assist Shaak Ti in guarding Chancellor Palpatine."»
- Ch42 L4403: «With them were two female Jedi - - Masters Shaak Ti and Stass Allie.»
- Ch42 L4407: «"We were just there," Shaak Ti said. "The office is empty. Even his guards are gone."»
- Ch42 L4415: «"Five Hundred Republica," Shaak Ti muttered to herself in seeming frustration. "I was just there."»
- Ch42 L4425: «"Won't you and Shaak Ti need it?" Padme asked. "We'll use the speeder bike I arrived on," Shaak Ti said.»
- Ch42 L4433: «"They're with us," Shaak Ti said.»
- Ch42 L4433: «Shaak Ti swung one leg over the seat and started the engine.»
- Ch43 L4529: «Shutting his eyes to distance himself farther, he stretched out with his feelings, placing in his mind's eye Mace and Kit Fisto skimming through the deranged sky; Shaak Ti and Stass Allie hurrying toward Palpatine's quarters in 500 Republica; Saesee Tiin, Agen Kolar, Bultar Swan, and other Jedi Masters and Jedi Knights streaking from Coruscant's envelope in their starfighters, local space flashing with energy bolts and globular explosions, ships too numerous to count embroiled in a monumental battle...»
- Ch44 L4549: «Shaak Ti demanded of the three Red Guards stationed outside the entrance to Palpatine's suite in 500 Republica.»
- Ch44 L4551: «"Where?" Stass Allie said, making it clear that she was going to get past them, one way or another. Shaak Ti had her hand raised to part the doors with a Force wave when the guards lowered their pikes and stepped aside. One punched a code into a wall panel, and the pair of burnished doors opened.»
- Ch44 L4555: «Lightning or plasma, Shaak Ti told herself.»
- Ch44 L4555: «Additional Red Guards and several of Palpatine's advisers stood watching him, some with comlinks plugged into their ears, others with devices Shaak Ti understood to be vital to the continued operation of the Republic military.»
- Ch44 L4555: «Should anything befall the Chancellor, authority to initiate battle campaigns and issue war codes would pass temporarily to Speaker of the Senate, Mas Amedda, who, Shaak Ti had learned, was already safely ensconced in a hardened bunker deep beneath the Great Rotunda.»
- Ch44 L4561: «Shaak Ti practically had to plant herself in Palpatine's path to get his attention.»
- Ch44 L4575: «The fact that Palpatine was flustered, confused, possibly frightened was obvious. But when Shaak Ti attempted to read him through the Force, she found it difficult to get a sense of what he was truly feeling.»
- Ch44 L4585: «Shaak Ti traded looks with one of the guards, wishing she could see through the face shield of his red cowl. The situation was becoming dangerous. A shiver born in the Force moved her to glance out the window.»
- Ch44 L4603: «Before a further word could fly from his mouth, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were in motion; Shaak Ti tackling Palpatine to the floor, while Allie ignited her blade and brought it vertically in front of her.»
- Ch44 L4607: «Shaak Ti kept him pinned to the floor and called her lightsaber into her hand.»
- Ch44 L4607: «As the craft hovered closer to the window Shaak Ti gaped in disbelief.»
- Ch44 L4611: «For a moment Shaak Ti was certain that Allie was going to throw herself aboard the hovering gunboat, but there were simply too many droids standing in the way.»
- Ch44 L4613: «Releasing Palpatine to the custody of two guards, Shaak Ti raced to the window and gazed down into the clouds.»
- Ch44 L4623: «"Negative," Shaak Ti said, shaking her head. "Grievous knew enough to come here. We have to assume that the escape route has been compromised, as well."»
- Ch44 L4627: «"No," Shaak Ti agreed. "But there are other ways to reach the bunker complex."»
- Ch44 L4635: «Allie waited until the Red Guards had gathered around him. "Now - - quickly!" When everyone had moved into the hallway, Shaak Ti used her comlink to find Mace Windu. "Mace, Grievous is onworld," she said the moment she heard his voice.»
- Ch44 L4643: «Pressed into the turbolift with Stass Allie, Palpatine's guards and advisers, and Republica's security personnel, Shaak Ti watched the display tick off the floors. No one spoke until the car had reached the first sublevel.»
- Ch44 L4645: «"Don't stop," Shaak Ti told the security man closest to the controls. "The deeper we go, the better."»
- Ch44 L4647: «As they hurried for the tunnel, Shaak Ti took a moment to survey the huge space for some sign of Captain Dyne's team.»
- Ch44 L4649: «"Wait here," Shaak Ti instructed the Red Guards and Palpatine when they had reached the mouth of the tunnel.»
- Ch44 L4649: «Shaak Ti looked straight up at the sliver of sky.»
- Ch44 L4653: «Shaak Ti laid a hand on her upper arm. "Too risky. We should remain as close to ground as possible."»
- Ch44 L4657: «Shaak Ti smiled at her and reactivated the comlink. "Mace," she said when he answered. "Another change in plans..."»
- Ch47 L4769: «Having boarded the mag-lev at its 500 Republica platform, he, Kit Fisto, Shaak Ti, and Stass Allie were in the car the Supreme Chancellor's Red Guards had commandeered - - second in a train of some twenty cars.»
- Ch47 L4777: «Shaak Ti hadn't seen them when she and Palpatine's protectors had whisked the Supreme Chancellor through 500 Republica's sub-basement.»
- Ch47 L4779: «Shaak Ti had told Mace and Kit about the Supreme Chancellor's earlier reluctance to leave his suite.»
- Ch47 L4787: «In turn, Mace whirled to Shaak Ti and Allie. "Move the Chancellor into the forward car!"»
- Ch47 L4789: «Shaak Ti regarded him as if he had lost his mind. "It's packed, Mace!"»
- Ch47 L4799: «And at a fixed-pane window they had deliberately shattered, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were gazing toward the rear of the train.»
- Ch47 L4801: «"Those fighters could easily have derailed us with a torpedo," Shaak Ti said as Mace and Kit approached. Mace leaned partway out the window, eyes searching the canyon.»
- Ch47 L4813: «As Mace already knew from Ki-Adi-Mundi and Shaak Ti, Grievous was well trained in the Jedi arts.»
- Ch49 L4863: «Farther forward in the car, Shaak Ti and Stass Allie were keeping the Supreme Chancellor on his feet.»
- Ch49 L4865: «He wondered if he, Kit, Shaak Ti, and Allie - - acting in concert - - could support the entire train, but dismissed the idea immediately.»
- Ch49 L4871: «Behind them came four Jedi Knights, who rushed to join Shaak Ti and Stass Allie in guarding Palpatine.»
- Ch49 L4883: «"General Fisto and I are going with you." Mace turned to Shaak Ti, Allie, and the four newly arrived Jedi Knights.»
- Ch49 L4885: «Shaak Ti nodded at him.»
- Ch49 L4885: «Shaak Ti was the last to board the gunship that would deliver Palpatine to shelter, somewhere deep in the narrow service chasms that fractured the exclusive Sah'c neighborhood.»
- Ch49 L4885: «Shaak Ti knew the human male Jedi and the female Twi'lek by sight, but she couldn't recall ever running into the other two - - a male Talz and a male Ithorian.»
- Ch49 L4891: «Shaak Ti didn't allow herself to relax until the gunships had been cleared through the shimmering screen and had been issued approach vectors for landing.»
- Ch49 L4891: «The escort gunship shot ahead and was already on the pad when Shaak Ti and the rest arrived moments later.»
- Ch49 L4891: «Shaak Ti instructed the four Jedi Knights to accompany the Red Guards, promising to join them after she and Stass Allie had apprised the Temple of their safe arrival.»
- Ch49 L4893: «After several failed attempts to reach the Temple, she glanced at Shaak Ti.»
- Ch49 L4895: «Retaining consciousness, Shaak Ti used the momentum to propel herself through a tucked roll that carried her almost to the edge of the landing platform.»
- Ch49 L4897: «Shaak Ti saw several soldiers jump from the gunship's doors and move with astounding speed into the mouth of the access tunnel. She raised herself to one knee, then sprinted to Stass Allie's side to put out the flames that had engulfed her cloak. Allie stirred and raised herself on the palms of her hands.»
- Ch49 L4899: «"Stay down," Shaak Ti warned.»
- Ch49 L4901: «Shaak Ti curled her body and tucked her head to her chest. A wave of intense heat washed over her and Allie, and a hail of fragments clanged and clattered down around her. One of the last pieces to land - - not two meters from her face - - was the charred head of a battle droid.»
- Ch50 L4965: «"Four Jedi Knights, all these soldiers and guards," Palpatine went on, gesturing broadly. "Why not wait until Shaak Ti and Stass Allie arrive." He cocked his head to one side. "I think I hear them coming. They are Masters, after all."»
- Ch51 L4987: «It hadn't taken long to piece together what had happened: the Separatists had managed to hijack a Republic gunship and infiltrate the bunker complex shield by timing their arrival to coincide with that of the ship carrying Palpatine, Shaak Ti, and the others.»
- Ch51 L5003: «Mace and Kit glanced at the door that led to those tunnels, then turned to Shaak Ti, who was standing by the bunker's holoprojector table as if lost. When Mace hurried over to her, she practically collapsed in his arms.»
- Ch51 L5009: «"The Senate may not see it that way, Mace." Shaak Ti composed herself and gazed around. "Grievous had help. Help from someone close to the top."»
- Ch51 L5015: «"I can show you," Shaak Ti said. Turning, she activated a security recording that had captured Grievous and several of his humanoid guards dragging Palpatine to the south landing pad, butchering the handful of troopers posted there, scrambling into a waiting tri-winged shuttle, lifting off into sunset clouds...»
- Ch51 L5039: «Shaak Ti forced an exhale. "All of Coruscant will panic."»

### B.10 Stass Allie: 46 entries. Aliases: `Stass|Allie\b`. Version: Stass Allie, 19 BBY

- Ch27 L2567: «A holorecording of Palpatine's speech was running in the center of the room when Bail was admitted. Around the holoprojector table sat Council members Yoda, Mace Windu, Saesee Tiin, Ki-Adi-Mundi, Shaak Ti, Stass Allie, Plo Koon, and Kit Fisto.»
- Ch27 L2589: «"Had enough?" Stass Allie asked while the Senate broke into extended applause. Tall, slender, and dark-complected, she wore a Tholoth headdress similar in design to that worn by her immediate predecessor on the Council, Adi Gallia. When no one objected, she deactivated the holoprojector.»
- Ch39 L4263: «"Tiin, Koon, Ki-Adi-Mundi, and some of the others are on their way up the well," Mace said. "I sent Stass Allie to assist Shaak Ti in guarding Chancellor Palpatine."»
- Ch42 L4403: «With them were two female Jedi - - Masters Shaak Ti and Stass Allie.»
- Ch42 L4419: «"I wasn't looking for him then," the Jedi started to say, then allowed her words to trail off. "Master Allie and I will check the Senate Office Building and Republica." She glanced at Padme, Bail, and the others. "Where are you going?"»
- Ch42 L4423: «"The turbolifts to the shelters are overwhelmed," Stass Allie said. "It'll be hours before the Senate is evacuated. My skimmer is at the plaza's northwest landing platform. You can pilot that directly to the shelters."»
- Ch42 L4429: «Stass Allie took his arm. "We'll escort you."»
- Ch42 L4435: «Stass Allie settled in behind her. "Good luck," she said.»
- Ch43 L4523: «"... Fisto and I... Senate building. Shaak... Allie... to the Chancellor's quarters in Five Hundred Republica. We... with them - - "»
- Ch43 L4529: «Shutting his eyes to distance himself farther, he stretched out with his feelings, placing in his mind's eye Mace and Kit Fisto skimming through the deranged sky; Shaak Ti and Stass Allie hurrying toward Palpatine's quarters in 500 Republica; Saesee Tiin, Agen Kolar, Bultar Swan, and other Jedi Masters and Jedi Knights streaking from Coruscant's envelope in their starfighters, local space flashing with energy bolts and globular explosions, ships too numerous to count embroiled in a monumental battle...»
- Ch44 L4549: «Alongside her hurried Stass Allie, one hand on the hilt of her lightsaber.»
- Ch44 L4551: «"Where?" Stass Allie said, making it clear that she was going to get past them, one way or another. Shaak Ti had her hand raised to part the doors with a Force wave when the guards lowered their pikes and stepped aside. One punched a code into a wall panel, and the pair of burnished doors opened.»
- Ch44 L4557: «She couldn't help noticing that Pestage and Isard - - two of Palpatine's closest advisers - - looked nervous. "Why is he still here?" Stass Allie directed at Isard.»
- Ch44 L4577: «"Chancellor, I'm sorry," Stass Allie chimed in, "but the Jedi are obliged to make this decision for you."»

## Proposed verdicts.md changes
*(Proposals only. verdicts.md was not edited.)*

1. **NEW: General Grievous (19 BBY, LoE, pre-crush) vs Jedi Knights.** Confidence: high, Grievous, low diff.
   - Evidence: Ch50 (4 Knights at once: «Compared to Mace Windu, the four were mere novices», L4939; one beheaded, one stabbed, one dismembered, one stabbed through the heart, L4945–4955) and Ch54 (2 «more proficient» Knights killed in one move, L5181–5183). Durability: «The burns did little more than add a new odor to the room.» (L4951)
   - Version note: use the pre-chest-crush Grievous only. The 2003 *Clone Wars* raid on Coruscant conflicts with LoE, so don't merge the kill lists.
2. **NEW: Mace Windu (Vaapad) vs Grievous (19 BBY).** Confidence: high, Mace (≈8/10, low–mid diff).
   - Evidence: Ch47 (L4811–4821) and Grievous's concession (L4929).
   - Note: Grievous's computers adapt («close enough», L4817), and the fight ends on terrain plus a Force push, not on a wound.
   - Counter: Wookieepedia's "duel Mace Windu evenly" (wiki text) (grievous-legends-quotes.txt L103) is not what the novel shows.
3. **NEW: Dooku (ROTS era) vs Grievous (19 BBY).** Confidence: medium-high, Dooku (≈7.5/10, mid diff).
   - Evidence: L1669–1671, L3343–3351, L3361.
4. **NEW: Grievous vs Council Masters (Kit/Shaak Ti/Obi-Wan tier).** Confidence: medium, the Master, mid diff. Shaak Ti and Stass Allie are closer to high diff.
   - Evidence: L3343, L3347, L4813–4815 (Kit), L4943.
5. **AMEND: Darth Vader vs Dooku (L177), the "For Dooku" bullet.**
   - Current: "a long record of handling bigger and stronger opponents (Grievous spars, Labyrinth of Evil)".
   - Proposed: "…(Grievous spars, Labyrinth of Evil, though LoE says Dooku was at times «hard-pressed to outduel the cyborg» and «had kept some secrets to himself», L1669–1671; his on-page demo is a Makashi disarm of two MagnaGuards with the tip at Grievous's face, L3349)."
6. **AMEND: Yoda (Clone Wars era) vs Dooku (L151–157), add a Statements bullet.**
   - Text: Labyrinth of Evil (L2451), Dooku's POV: «On Geonosis, Yoda's easy parrying and, indeed, handling of the Sith lightning Dooku hurled at him had come as a surprise», and Yoda learned the new Sith's danger only after fighting Dooku (L4243).
   - Dooku's counter-claim, «Dooku had only fled the fight to safeguard the plans» (L2451), is face-saving (C-grade).
   - Confidence unchanged (high), slightly strengthened.
7. **AMEND: Mace vs Dooku (L158–164), add a "For Mace" bullet.**
   - Text: LoE, Dooku to Grievous: «Do I need to demonstrate what responses you can expect from Cin Drallig or Obi-Wan Kenobi? From Mace Windu or, stars help you, Yoda?» (L3347). Also «Tyranus had warned him about Mace Windu's prowess with a blade» (L4929).
   - Effect: Dooku's own ranking puts Mace just under Yoda. Keep 6/10 Mace; low-medium → medium is defensible.
8. **AMEND: Darth Sidious vs Dooku (L181–182), add to the Labyrinth framing.**
   - Text: Dooku's dark-side training was «a crash course … a mere taste of which had proved intoxicating» (L2445). Yoda doubts he is «a true Sith» (L3299). Sidious scripts the Invisible Hand duel with «I will control the outcome» (L5261).
9. **AMEND: Yoda vs Sidious (L124–133), add to the Statements bullet.**
   - Text: LoE (Dooku POV, knew both): Sidious's foresight «was one of the skills that distinguished Sidious from Yoda» (L4193). LoE (Yoda POV): «This was Sidious: powerful enough to hide in plain sight.» (L4245)
   - Effect: supports Sidious on prep and information; no change to the 6/10.
10. **AMEND: Mace vs Sidious (L142–150), optional context bullet.**
    - Text: LoE shows Sidious hiding from Mace face-to-face (Ch23, Ch31) and the Jedi missing a clouded clue: «How did we miss seeing this?» / «The answer is in the question.» (L3773–3777).
    - Effect: supports Sidious choosing the ground; no change.
11. **NEW (provisional): Dooku (19 BBY) vs Anakin (19 BBY), pre-Invisible Hand.** Confidence: low-medium.
    - Leans: Dooku vs calm Anakin, high diff (≈6/10); rage-amped Anakin, mid–high diff (≈6.5/10).
    - Evidence: L2521, L4381, L4661–4663, L4835–4837, L5261.
    - Coordinate with the ROTS-novel worker, whose on-page duel should decide this.
12. **TIER NOTE: place Grievous (19 BBY, pre-crush) provisionally at the bottom of tier 6 or the top of tier 7**, below Dooku (mid) and Mace (≈8/10).
    - Open: vs Maul (my lean is Maul, mid–high, reasoning only) and vs Sion.
    - Add to the change log: "2026-10-19: LoE read (primary). Grievous placed provisionally."
13. **No change** to Sidious vs Plagueis, Plagueis vs Mace, or Krayt vs Plagueis: LoE has only the one-line Plagueis mention (L2451).
