<!-- 022-dark-lord-trilogy/rots-novel-notes.md (verbatim copy) -->

# Revenge of the Sith (Matthew Stover novelization): Power-Scaling Research Notes

- **Book:** *Star Wars: Episode III – Revenge of the Sith*, by Matthew Woodring Stover (Del Rey, 2005)
- **Continuity:** Legends (Del Rey novelization of the film; the most-cited prose source for the ROTS duels)
- **Source file:** `[local path]` (8,890 lines, 113,836 words). Every citation is `L####` (line number in that file).
- **Method:** The text was split into its 21 chapters plus the Introduction and part-epigraphs (`=N=` markers) and read in chunks. Every chapter was read line by line: Ch1–7, 15–17 and 19–20 in the first pass, and Ch8–14, 18 and 21 in a follow-up line-by-line pass (2026-10-19), whose finds are marked "Line-by-line additions" (§1), §2.14, S39–S51 (§3) and §9.0 (pronoun-only lines). Quotes are exact. All guillemet-delimited quotes in this file are script-checked against the source at the end of this file. OCR noise in the source (e.g. «Lighting blasted», «|which», «[Dooku»») is reproduced *verbatim* and marked *(sic)* where it matters.
- **Version tags:** Every feat and statement carries a version tag. Statements count only for the version they describe. The whole novel is **19 BBY**, so tags separate *states*: e.g. "Anakin, Jedi Knight (pre-fall)" vs "Anakin, rage-amped ('gift of fury')" vs "Vader, newly turned (unsuited)"; "Sidious, concealed" vs "Sidious, unmasked"; "Mace, base" vs "Mace, Vaapad 'superconducting loop' + shatterpoint". Amps (rage, Vaapad, shatterpoint perception, Force-exhaustion debuffs, drugged/hurt states) count as separate versions.
- **Scope note:** A sibling worker covers *Darth Plagueis* and *Labyrinth of Evil*. Plagueis appears here only as Palpatine's opera-scene legend (Ch12), noted as a statement, not a feat.

## Cast and version table

| Character | Version tag(s) used | Role in this book |
|---|---|---|
| Darth Sidious / Palpatine | Sidious (hologram to Dooku); Sidious concealed as Palpatine; Sidious unmasked (Mace duel); Sidious scarred / Emperor (Yoda duel) | Main antagonist. Duels Mace + three Masters, then Yoda. |
| Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader | Anakin, Jedi Knight (pre-fall); Anakin rage-amped ("gift of fury", Ch3–4); Anakin mid-fall; Vader newly turned (unsuited); Vader post-Mustafar (maimed/suited) | Kills Dooku. Cuts off Mace's hand. Temple, Mustafar, loses to Obi-Wan. |
| Obi-Wan Kenobi | Obi-Wan, Jedi Master (Soresu), 19 BBY; Obi-Wan (Mustafar duel) | Duels Dooku (with Anakin), Grievous, Vader. |
| Count Dooku / Darth Tyranus | Dooku, 83 years old, 19 BBY; Dooku Force-exhausted (late Ch3) | Duel vs Anakin + Obi-Wan; executed. |
| General Grievous | Grievous, 19 BBY (cyborg, four arms, combat algorithms) | Bridge escape; Utapau duel; killed by blaster. |
| Mace Windu | Mace base; Mace Vaapad; Mace Vaapad "superconducting loop" + shatterpoint perception (office duel) | Leads the arrest; disarms Sidious; dies. |
| Yoda | Yoda, Grand Master, 19 BBY; Yoda (Senate duel) | Duels Sidious; withdraws. |
| Padmé Amidala | Padmé, 19 BBY | Non-combatant; Force-choked. |
| Supporting Jedi | Saesee Tiin, Agen Kolar, Kit Fisto (killed by Sidious); Shaak Ti, Depa Billaba, K'Kruhk etc. (referenced) | Fodder/scaling anchors. |

## 1. Chapter-by-chapter plot notes

**Introduction: The Age of Heroes (L29–124).** A second-person HoloNet framing of the Battle of Coruscant. Grievous has kidnapped the Chancellor. Grievous is introduced as «an abomination of nature, a fusion of flesh and droid» (L75). Younglings across the galaxy believe «when Anakin and Obi-Wan get there, those dirty Seppers are going to wish they'd stayed in bed today» (L97). The adults believe «legendary heroes are merely legends» (L109). *Scaling:* only public reputation, not feats.

**Part One epigraph, "Victory" (L125–142).** «The dark is generous.» The thematic thesis is «With each victory of the light, it is the dark that wins.» (L139). This frames every Jedi "win" in the book (Dooku, Grievous) as Sidious's win.

**Ch1 Anakin and Obi-Wan (L143–834).** A space dogfight over Coruscant. Obi-Wan is «Jedi Master. General in the Grand Army of the Republic. Member of the Jedi Council.» (L275). Anakin can «reach into the Force and feel the engagement throughout Coruscant space as though the whole battle were happening inside his head» (L323). Anakin threads his fighter through a hull slit «with centimeters to spare» (L557). Buzz droids hit Obi-Wan's ship. They land in Invisible Hand's hangar. The chapter carries the book's key Anakin hype passage: «The most powerful Jedi of his generation. Perhaps of any generation. The fastest. The strongest.» (L443). The schoolyard debate over whether Obi-Wan's cleverness can beat Anakin's power is set up at L825. *Scaling:* Anakin's battlefield-wide Force sense; piloting feats.

**Ch2 Dooku (L835–1190).** Dooku POV. He waits beside the shackled Palpatine, as «Count Dooku» (L953). He is «numbered among the Lost» (L1007) and «felt every hour of his eighty-three years» (L1069). He suggests recruiting Kenobi instead (L1107). His Master says of Anakin: «He is powerful. Potentially more powerful than even myself.» (L1123). Dooku reflects that his Master's manipulation is a power that «might be considered to dwarf even the power of the dark side itself» (L1141). Dooku believes the duel will be a staged win for him. *Scaling:* Sidious's hype of Anakin's potential. Dooku's age.

**Ch3 The Way of the Sith (L1191–1580).** Anakin and Obi-Wan reach the General's quarters. In Dooku's Force-perception, Palpatine is «an event horizon» and «A black hole of the Force» (L1351–1353). The duel follows; see Feats §2.3 for exchanges. Dooku dominates at first, trapping Anakin with telekinetic furniture. Then the two Jedi trap him: Anakin is «a Djem So stylist, and as fine a one as Dooku had ever seen» (L1485) and Kenobi «had become a master of Soresu» (L1497). Dooku hurls Obi-Wan aside. He begins to flag from Force exhaustion. Then he duels Anakin alone, and Anakin shows «the gift of fury».

**Ch4 Jedi Trap (L1581–1804).** Present-tense passage. Anakin understands that fear can be a weapon. «Dooku is dead already» (L1593). He smashes aside a table fragment that Dooku hurls «with all the power that the dark side can draw from throughout the universe» (L1613). He takes both of Dooku's hands. Palpatine orders «Do it! Now!» (L1657) and Anakin beheads him. Guilt follows (L1725). Palpatine says «Revenge is the foundation of justice» (L1751). Anakin unlocks the shackles through the Force (L1771) and carries the unconscious Obi-Wan out.

**Ch5 Grievous (L1805–2016).** Grievous POV on the bridge. He kills a gunnery officer with one punch (L1835). His body is described (L1853–1859) and his trophy lightsabers taken from Jedi he killed (L1863–1865). He threatens to disembowel Palpatine live on the HoloNet (L1971). The heroes cross a turbolift shaft that has turned into a hallway (L1891). Palpatine whispers: «You have destroyed their political head» (L1915).

**Ch6 Rescue (L2017–2438).** The gravity flips in the turbolift shaft. They trigger the ray-shield trap. Obi-Wan tests it with his lightsaber (L2289, 2313). Destroyer droids and Grievous's MagnaGuards appear: «Grievous's personal bodyguard droids» (L2361). They surrender: «He's got it.» (L2393). The chapter also has a flash to Anakin's Knighting, with the Padawan braid given to Padmé (L2209).

**Ch7 Obi-Wan and Anakin 2 (L2439–2702).** «This is Obi-Wan Kenobi in the light». On the bridge Obi-Wan is so deep in the Force that he, R2 and Anakin act as one. R2's fusion-welder distracts Grievous, and Obi-Wan uses the Force to «reverse the polarity of the electrodrivers in the general's mech» [anical hands, truncated in the excerpt] (L2505). This is his signature trick, reused on Mustafar. Grievous hurls a console (L2521). He thinks «Kenobi, though-The ease with which Kenobi had taken command of the situation was frightening» and «of the two, Skywalker was reportedly the greater warrior» (L2529). He decides survival is the goal (L2531), breaks a viewport, and flees in a pod. Anakin crash-lands the burning half of the ship: «Between his will and the will of the Force, there is no contest» (L2685).

**Part Two epigraph, "Seduction" (L2687–2702).** «The dark's patience is infinite.»

**Ch8 Fault Lines (L2703–3058).** Mace meets the shuttle. Anakin «seemed to tower over his companions» (L2733). Palpatine's spin is that Anakin was «simply more . . . highly motivated» than Dooku (L2785). Mace senses that Anakin is «a pivot point, the fulcrum of a lever» (L2805). Mace and Obi-Wan discuss suspects for Sidious (L2905) and the deep conspiracy (L2937–2961). Anakin and Padmé are reunited. Palpatine says he has «a certain gift," he murmured, "for getting my way.» (L3057).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* Mace on the landing: «Skywalker again. The chosen one.» / «Who else could have brought in this hulk?» (L2717–2719). Anakin's new «Presence.» (L2735). Obi-Wan: «I was . . . taking a nap.» (L2765). Palpatine says Dooku was «a trace overconfident» (L2781). Mace's gift is defined at L2799: «to find the shear planes that can cause them to break in useful ways». He has seen Palpatine as a shatterpoint since his election (L2801). The fault lines «stank of the dark side» (L2807). Mace to Obi-Wan: «Skywalker is arguably the most powerful Jedi alive, and he is still getting stronger. But he is not stable.» (L2941). Obi-Wan quotes «We don't have to win. All we have to do is fight.» (L2973).

**Ch9 Padmé (L3059–3316).** Padmé POV. «He is a wild creature who has come gently to her hand, a vine tiger» (L3097). She reveals the pregnancy. Anakin has his nightmares of her death. Anakin boasts: «I'm more powerful than any Jedi Master alive. But someone is blocking me.» (L3277).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* «The Hero With No Fear. What a joke . . .» (L3161). «my dreams are literal» (L3249). Padmé recalls Anakin's description of Obi-Wan: «as wise as Yoda and as powerful as Mace Windu» (L3265). Anakin wants to «show them what he'd discovered within himself in the General's Quarters» (L3297).

**Ch10 Masters (L3317–3808).** Yoda, Mace and Obi-Wan meet in Yoda's quarters about the draft resolution: «Palpatine is not to be trusted» (L3357). They discuss Grievous's location. Mace: Grievous «is more than a match for most Jedi.» (L3413). A Sidious hologram meets the Separatist Council (L3443–3481). Anakin reaches the Temple, the «greatest nexus of Force energy in the Republic» (L3587). Palpatine names Anakin his personal representative on the Council (L3779).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* Yoda: «Have the advantage already, they do!» (L3391). Mace: «I have fought him blade-to-blade.» (L3413). Grievous «seemed to teleport» across the room to menace Gunray (L3449). Sidious: «Dooku was not lost, he was sacrificed» (L3509) and «All will be clear, once you meet my new apprentice.» (L3517). Anakin's prophetic dreams «had never been wrong» (L3529). Anakin's Force perceptions are «far from the most subtle» (L3551). Yoda: «Sense the future, once all Jedi could; now few alone have this skill.» (L3557). Palpatine to Anakin: «You may become more powerful than all of them together.» (L3697).

**Ch11 Politics (L3809–4256).** In the Council, Kolar says Anakin is «the one Jedi we can best hope would survive an encounter with a Sith Lord» (L3839). Anakin is seated but not made a Master. He is asked to spy on Palpatine. The Delegation of 2,000 politics with Bail and Padmé (L4001–4027). Obi-Wan and Yoda tell Anakin the mission: «Master Windu traced Darth Sidious to Five Hundred Republica» (L4167). Yoda: «Always in motion, the future is» (L4195).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* Ki-Adi-Mundi: «young Skywalker has the power to battle a Sith Lord alone, if need be; he has proven that with Dooku.» (L3837). Kolar on Yoda: «It is your sensitivity to the broader currents of the Force that a Sith Lord has most reason to fear.» (L3845). Yoda: «destroy the droid armies I can» (L3847). Mace calls Obi-Wan «our most cunning and insightful Master-and our most tenacious» (L3849). Anakin's outburst in Council: «It was the voice of Dooku's destroyer.» (L3953) / «No Jedi in this room can match my power-no Jedi in the galaxy!» (L3955); «Go ahead and Vaapad this» (L3963). To Obi-Wan: «I have the power of any five Masters. Any ten.» (L4053). Obi-Wan replies «Power alone is no credit to you-» (L4055) and «How can you be a Jedi Master when you have not mastered yourself?» (L4079). Mace notes there have been only two Sith at a time since Bane (L4197). Obi-Wan: «I am firmly convinced that Anakin can do anything. Except betray a friend.» (L4243).

**Ch12 Not from a Jedi (L4257–4616).** Anakin is frustrated with Padmé (L4321–4367). At the Galaxies Opera, Palpatine says «Good is a point of view, Anakin» (L4461) and tells the Plagueis legend: a Dark Lord who could «directly influence the midi-chlorians to create life» (L4539). The apprentice «goes on to become the greatest Dark Lord the Sith have ever known» (L4559). Palpatine: «The dark side seems to be-from my reading-the pathway to many abilities some would consider unnatural» (L4543).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* the railing «groaned in his mechanical grip» (L4367). Palpatine: «you are the best man to apprehend him» (L4421), and «a Jedi gains power through understanding, and a Sith gains understanding through power. This is the true reason the Sith have always been more powerful than the Jedi.» (L4481). On Plagueis: «one of the most powerful men in the galaxy, not to mention virtually immortal» (L4571; statement only, Plagueis belongs to the sibling worker). «Well, clearly,» he said, «not from a Jedi.» (L4581).

**Ch13 The Will of the Force (L4617–4948).** Obi-Wan probes Padmé. The Council chooses Obi-Wan for Utapau: «The Council will decide this," Mace said sternly. "Not the Chancellor.» (L4759). The Delegation's first meetings (L4805–4861). Anakin wants Obi-Wan to stay (L4881).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* Obi-Wan to Padmé: «He is-changing. Quickly.» (L4675); the prophecy says «nowhere does it say he has to be a Jedi» (L4679). Yoda: «Dangerous, Grievous is. To face him, steady minds are needed-Masters, we should send.» (L4761). Obi-Wan's POV: «Mace and Agen Kolar-both among the greatest bladesbeings the Jedi Order had ever produced» and Anakin «a brigade's worth of firepower in his own right» (L4767). Obi-Wan on Grievous: «I have a feel for how he fights now-and for how he runs away.» (L4773). Anakin senses that «Somewhere down there was all the power he would ever need.» (L4881). Obi-Wan's farewell: «you have far surpassed my humble efforts at instruction» (L4931).

**Ch14 Free Fall in the Dark (L4949–5280).** Palpatine sows rumors that Kenobi is plotting (L5009). Anakin is consumed by suspicion. Padmé: «You don't need more power, Anakin.» … «I believe you can save me from anything, just as you are.» (L5215). The Delegation presents the petition to Palpatine (L5239–5261).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* Palpatine: «Your ability to read evil intent» and «the most powerful Jedi in the galaxy» (L5029–5033). Anakin's anger burns away his fatigue (L5067–5071). Force-psychometry: «In the Force, the whole apartment stank of Obi-Wan.» (L5095), and he reads the «Force-echoes of their presence» of visiting Senators (L5117). «I'm one of the most powerful Jedi alive, but it's not enough.» (L5199). «I have found a key to truths deeper than the Jedi could ever teach me.» (L5213).

**Ch15 Death on Utapau (L5281–6216).** Obi-Wan infiltrates the sinkhole city. Yoda says the true enemy is the dark side (L5409). In Palpatine's office, Palpatine more or less reveals himself to Anakin and offers him apprenticeship: «Join me on the path of true power. Be my friend, Anakin. Be my student. My apprentice.» (L5739). Anakin declines, «Not yet» (L5743). On Utapau, Obi-Wan announces himself and drops in on Grievous. He makes the droids destroy each other (L5861) and fights Grievous; see Feats §2.6. Mace's earlier briefing is recounted: «Not a master. The master» (L6029). The Boga chase follows, then hand-to-hand combat, and Grievous dies by blaster: «"So ..." He summoned a condemnation among the most offensive in his vocabulary. "... uncivilized."». Order 66 comes into view.

**Ch16 Revelation (L6217–6374).** Mace and the Yoda holo discuss what to do: «Masters will be necessary, if the Lord of the Sith you must face.» (L6229). Anakin collapses and tells Mace that Palpatine is Sidious. Mace's shatterpoint sense on Anakin: «Anakin Skywalker no longer had shatter-points. He was a shatterpoint. The shatterpoint.» (L6273). Mace weighs «the risk of facing the last Dark Lord of the Sith without» Anakin (L6339). Mace and Yoda meet on the HoloNet (L6351).

**Part Three epigraph, "Apocalypse" (L6363–6374).**

**Ch17 The Face of the Dark (L6375–7006).** Anakin wrestles with his "dragon" in the Council Chamber (L6421). A shadow-POV passage: «the shadow felt the boy's anguish, and it was good» (L6475). There is a transcript of the arrest recording (L6549–6583). Sidious kills Tiin (L6595) and Kolar (L6605). Fisto dies off-page while Anakin flies over, and his head is on the desk (L6649). Mace in Vaapad reaches «Impasse» (L6703), uses his shatterpoint sense, cuts Sidious's saber in half on the ledge (L6725), and reflects the lightning. Then Anakin cuts off Mace's hand and Sidious kills him (L6811–6815). Anakin pledges himself and is named Vader (L6949). Sidious is now scarred; the yellow-eyed deformation is at L6853. Vader goes to the Temple (L6995, Gate Master).

**Ch18 Order Sixty-Six (L7007–7568).** Cody gets the order (L7039). The Temple burns (L7071). There is an essay on why the Jedi lost: «By fighting at all, the Jedi lost» (L7149). Obi-Wan survives the fall into the sinkhole lake (L7335) and plays a mind trick on the droids (L7405). Bail Organa rescues him (L7487). Yoda: «Of the dark side, despair is» (L7533).

  - *Line-by-line additions:* Kenobi TK-recalls the lightsaber Cody hands back (L7033). A child Jedi of about ten kills troopers before he dies (L7119). Cody has «a very clear and unsentimental estimate of just how hard to kill the unassuming Jedi Master was» and masses heavy weapons from five companies (L7173). Boga shields Obi-Wan with her body (L7345–7347). Vader to Padmé: «There aren't any Jedi. There's just me.» (L7303); «He had paid for them with innocent blood.» (L7327). Bail: «if I had to bet on one, my money'd be on Yoda.» (L7395). Obi-Wan's beast-suggestion turns a cave monster on a seeker droid (L7405–7407). He escapes in Grievous's starfighter (L7451). Yoda's Kashyyyk escape is off-page (Wookiee pod, L7441).

**Ch19 The Face of the Sith (L7569–7934).** Mustafar (L7573). Sidious's holo tells the Separatists that their reward is coming (L7621). Yoda and Obi-Wan infiltrate the Temple and kill Decoy Squad Five: «Six seconds later, all eight of them were dead.» (L7687). Palpatine declares the Empire: «These Jedi murderers left me scarred, left me deformed» (L7693). Vader massacres the Separatist Council (L7779–7847). The Temple hologram shows Anakin killing younglings (L7887). Yoda assigns the duels: «Strong enough to face Lord Sidious, you will never be. Die you will, and painfully.» (L7925). Obi-Wan: Palpatine faced «four of the greatest swordsmen our Order has ever produced. By himself.» (L7931).

**Ch20 Chiaroscuro (L7935–8604).** Obi-Wan visits Padmé (L7939–8013). Vader on Mustafar plans to overthrow his Master: «He had the measure of his "Master" already» (L8051). Padmé arrives. The Yoda vs Sidious and Obi-Wan vs Vader duels are intercut; see Feats §2.9–2.10. Yoda falls, then escapes through the service accessway (L8471). Obi-Wan maims Vader on the riverbank (L8569–8575). «You were the chosen one!» (L8585). «I hate you» (L8587).

**Ch21 A New Jedi Order (L8605–8890).** Polis Massa. Qui-Gon's voice comes through the Force (L8639). Yoda: «A power greater than all, it is.» (L8657, on retaining identity after death). Padmé dies of a «systemic failure» that «cannot be explained» (L8687). The twins are hidden. Vader is rebuilt («He lives», L8793). The closing meditation is on the mask: «Or perhaps the simulacrum is perfect, and it is the world that is hideous.» (L8833).


  - *Line-by-line additions:* Sidious lifts Vader «with effortless strength» and «set his will upon him. / Live, Lord Vader.» (L8613–8617). Yoda: «My failure, this was.» (L8633); «Too old I was,» Yoda said. «Too rigid.» (L8641); «Your apprentice, I gratefully become.» (L8667). Padmé: «there ... is still good in him» (L8721). Yoda: «Strong the Force runs, in the Skywalker line.» (L8767). **Key scaling line, suited Vader:** «you are so far less now than what you were, you are more than half machine» … «the power you can touch is only a memory, and so with all your world-destroying fury it is only droids around you that implode» (L8867).
## 2. Feats by character (chapter, quote, result, scaling implication)

Format: **#. [Version tag] (Ch, L####)** «quote» → *result* → **scaling.**

### 2.1 Darth Sidious / Palpatine

1. **[Sidious concealed, 19 BBY] (Ch3, L1351–1353).** Dooku's Force-sight: «Palpatine was an event horizon» / «A black hole of the Force.» → Dooku perceives him only as an absence. → **Concealment/presence hype from a Sith Lord who knows him; the strongest statement for Sidious > Dooku.**
2. **[Sidious concealed] (Ch3, L1573).** Mid-duel, he changes the plan on the spot: «Don't fear what you're feeling, Anakin, use it!» … «Rage is your weapon. Strike now! Strike! Kill him!» → Dooku is betrayed and dies. → **Strategic feat (live manipulation of a Sith apprentice and a Jedi).**
3. **[Sidious concealed] (Ch2, L1141).** His manipulation «might be considered to dwarf even the power of the dark side itself» (Dooku POV). → **Strategic hype, not combat.**
4. **[Sidious concealed] (Ch15, L5739).** He offers apprenticeship openly: «Join me on the path of true power. Be my friend, Anakin. Be my student. My apprentice.» → Anakin declines «Not yet» (L5743). Sidious still wins one chapter later. → **Manipulation feat.**
5. **[Sidious concealed → unmasked] (Ch17, L6593–6595).** «Tiin frowned and cocked his head. His blade dipped. A smear of red-flashing darkness hurtled from behind the desk.» / «Saesee Tiin's head bounced when it hit the floor.» → **Kills Master Saesee Tiin instantly after a telepathy bait. Speed and deception blitz.**
6. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6605).** «A small, neat hole in the middle of his forehead leaked smoke» → **Kills Agen Kolar instantly.** «Palpatine stood at the doorway» with «a blade the color of fire» (L6611). Covers the room in a blink.
7. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6649).** «On Palpatine's desk lay the head of Kit Fisto» → **Kills Kit Fisto off-page in the few seconds Anakin is in transit.** The novel does not show the exchanges. Fisto lasts longer than Tiin and Kolar, but not by much.
8. **[Sidious vs Mace, Vaapad] (Ch17, L6701–6703).** «Vaapad made him an open channel, half of a superconducting loop completed by the shadow» … «no wound taken on either side» … «Impasse.» → **A true sword stalemate with Mace at his peak style.** See §2.8 for the full duel.
9. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6717).** «a dark surge of the Force nearly blew him right out of the gap he had just cut. Only a desperate Force-push of his own altered his path» → **One distraction-window Force blast nearly kills Mace.**
10. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6727).** Saber cut in half on the ledge (Mace's feat, Sidious's loss). The text frames the shadow as afraid: «the shadow had become a pulsar of fear» (L6719). Then: «Do you think the fear you feel is mine?» (L6745). → **Reading ambiguity:** the "fear" Mace exploited may have been projected, or may have been Anakin's. See §6.
11. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6753–6755).** His lightning is reflected back by Vaapad: «Palpatine staggered, snarling, but the blistering energy that loured from his hands only intensified.» / «He fed the power with his pain.» → **Tanks his own reflected lightning and escalates.**
12. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6769–6779).** «he ramped up the lightning bursting from his hands, bending the fountain of Mace's blade back toward the Korun Master's face» → Mace: «This was beyond Vaapad; he had no strength left to fight against his own blade» (L6773); «Anakin, he's too strong for me-» (L6779). → **Wins the lightning-vs-Vaapad power struggle on raw output; Mace is losing before the fake surrender.**
13. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6783–6785).** He ends the lightning himself and fakes the surrender: «I ... can't. I give up. I ... I am too weak, in the end.» → **A deliberate act; he «was not afraid. Mace could feel it: he wasn't worried at all» (L6759).**
14. **[Sidious] (Ch17, L6811).** «Palpatine roared back to his feet and lightning speared from the Sith Lord's hands» → **Kills the disarmed Mace instantly.**
15. **[Sidious unmasked/scarred] (Ch17, L6853).** The yellow-eyed, bone-swollen deformation. → **Side effect of the lightning feedback (cosmetic in the novel).**
16. **[Sidious, Emperor] (Ch20, L8185).** «Even a fraction of the dark side is more power than your Jedi arrogance can conceive» → **Self-statement (biased).**
17. **[Sidious vs Yoda] (Ch20, L8191, 8301).** «Lightning speared from outstretched hands, and the battle was on.»; «lightning forked from the hands of a Sith, and bent away from the gesture of a Jedi to shock Redrobes into unconsciousness.» → **Yoda redirects the opening lightning.**
18. **[Sidious vs Yoda] (Ch20, L8305).** «it was the Lord of all Sith scorching the Master of all Jedi into a smoldering huddle of clothing and green flesh.» → **Lands a full lightning hit on Yoda early, but Yoda recovers instantly (L8311).**
19. **[Sidious vs Yoda] (Ch20, L8317).** He uses a holdout saber: «a small weapon-a holdout, an easily concealed backup, a tiny bit of treachery expressing the core of Sith mastery» → **Sword parity with Yoda (duel continues).**
20. **[Sidious vs Yoda] (Ch20, L8387).** «the Force and the podium's controls ripped delegation pods free» → **Mass telekinesis in a Senate-scale arena (pods as projectiles).**
21. **[Sidious vs Yoda] (Ch20, L8453–8455).** «the creature had reached the limits of his strength.» … «The shadow unleashed its lightning while the creature was still in the air, and the little green freak took its full power.» → **Wins the endurance contest; knocks Yoda off the podium (100 m fall).**
22. **[Sidious post-Yoda] (Ch20, L8459).** «the victorious shadow became once again only Palpatine: a very old, very tired man, gasping for air as he leaned on the pod's rail.» → **The novel's on-page cost: near exhaustion after Yoda.**
23. **[Sidious] (Ch19, L7693, Ch18 L7149).** The Order 66 / Empire declaration. «By fighting at all, the Jedi lost» (L7149). → **The capstone strategic feat. A thousand years of Sith planning comes together here (L8403–8405).**

### 2.2 Anakin Skywalker / Darth Vader

1. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch1, L323).** «reach into the Force and feel the engagement throughout Coruscant space as though the whole battle were happening inside his head» → **Battle-scale Force awareness.**
2. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch1, L557).** He threads a starfighter through a hull slit «with centimeters to spare». → **Elite piloting with Force-aided reflexes.**
3. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch3, L1435–1437).** The Force-thrown situation table almost breaks Dooku's spine: «he barely managed to lift himself enough that he could backroll over it instead of having it shatter his spine.» / «The boy has some power after all.» → **Raw TK enough to threaten Dooku.**
4. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch3, L1481–1485).** «The second bent Dooku's wrist. The third flash of blue forced Dooku's scarlet blade so far to the inside that his own lightsaber scorched his shoulder» / «a Djem So stylist, and as fine a one as Dooku had ever seen» → **Overpowers Makashi head-on (2v1 context).**
5. **[Anakin pre-fall, rising rage] (Ch3, L1511–1513).** «every overhand chop crashed against Dooku's defense with the unstoppable power of a meteor strike» / «Each parry cost Dooku more power than he'd used to throw Kenobi across the room; each block aged him a decade.» → **Solo pressure that drains Dooku into Force exhaustion.**
6. **[Anakin] (Ch3, L1535).** Boot to Dooku's face at «something resembling terminal velocity» knocks him over the balcony rail. → **Dooku has to Force-cushion his landing (L1539).**
7. **[Anakin, "gift of fury"] (Ch3, L1553–1559).** «Skywalker was a natural.» / «He held the Force in the clench of a white-hot fist. He was half Sith already» / «This boy had the gift of fury.» / «even now, he was holding himself back» → **Dooku states that Anakin is holding back. A rage-amp version exists above what is shown.**
8. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch3, L1567).** A taunt makes him lose his edge: «Skywalker had lost his edge: a simple taunt was all that had been required» → **Psychological weakness. Dooku "met the boy's charge easily" when Anakin restrains himself.**
9. **[Anakin rage-amped] (Ch4, L1597).** «fury is a blade that makes his lightsaber into a toy» (Ch4 prose). → **Rage amp, narrated.**
10. **[Anakin rage-amped] (Ch4, L1601).** «Dooku's decades of combat experience are irrelevant. His mastery of swordplay is useless.» → **Overwhelms Dooku outright (Dooku already exhausted).**
11. **[Anakin rage-amped] (Ch4, L1613).** Dooku hurls the table fragment «with all the power that the dark side can draw from throughout the universe» → Anakin smashes it aside. → **Shrugs off Dooku's best TK.**
12. **[Anakin] (Ch4, L1631, 1685).** «Dooku crumples to his knees» … both hands taken; then the beheading. → **Kill: Count Dooku (Force-exhausted).**
13. **[Anakin] (Ch4, L1771).** «the field-signatures of the magnetic locks on the Chancellor's shackles were as clear as text» → **Fine technopathic TK.** He carries Obi-Wan out with Force-boosted strength (L1779–1795).
14. **[Anakin] (Ch7, L2685).** He lands half of Invisible Hand: «Between his will and the will of the Force, there is no contest» → **Piloting/Force synergy (the novel frames it as near-impossible, L2647).**
15. **[Anakin mid-fall] (Ch17, L6811).** «a sudden arc of blue plasma sheared through his wrist» → **Cuts off Mace's hand (Mace distracted, mid-execution). No duel-scaling value.**
16. **[Vader, newly turned] (Ch17–19, L6995; L7887).** The Temple massacre, including younglings (hologram). → **Fodder slaughter.**
17. **[Vader, newly turned] (Ch19, L7777–7785).** «There were screams, and tears, and pleas for mercy. None of them mattered. The Sith had come to Mustafar.» Poggle is beheaded by «a bar of lightning» (L7779); Shu Mai dies. «Darth Vader left nothing living behind» (L7839). «a black-gloved hand made a fist. The door crumpled and fell.» (L7843) → **Fodder kills; TK door-crush.**
18. **[Vader] (Ch20, L8283).** He Force-chokes Padmé into unconsciousness. → **Choke feat (non-resisting target).**
19. **[Vader vs Obi-Wan] (Ch20, L8413).** «Blade-to-blade, they were identical.» → **Sword parity with Obi-Wan (see §2.10).**
20. **[Vader] (Ch20, L8419).** «Easily, contemptuously, Anakin sent them back» (Obi-Wan's redirected bolts). → **Blaster deflection.**
21. **[Vader] (Ch20, L8425).** «A roar of the Force blasted Obi-Wan back into a wall, smashing breath from his lungs, leaving him swaying, half stunned.» → **Out-TKs Obi-Wan in a direct exchange.**
22. **[Vader] (Ch20, L8443–8447).** «His hands seized Obi-Wan's wrists with impossible strength» / «Obi-Wan felt the bones of his forearms bending» → **Dark-side physical strength amp.**
23. **[Vader] (Ch20, L8539).** Cable-swinging and leaps over lava: «the Force carried him within reach of another cable» → **Agility.**
24. **[Vader] (Ch20, L8567–8573).** «Anakin leaned aside and deflected the thrust almost contemptuously» … then «Half a second too slow.» → **Loses legs and arm on the leap.**
25. **[Vader, maimed] (Ch20, L8575–8587).** «his grip was too powerful for the lava bank and it crumbled» → **Survives burning; «I hate you».**
26. **[Vader, newly turned] (Ch20, L8051).** «He had the measure of his "Master" already» → **Biased self-assessment (fresh Sith, Mustafar-era). Contradicted by the book's whole structure.**

### 2.3 Dooku vs Anakin + Obi-Wan: exchange by exchange (Ch3, L1417–1579; Ch4, L1581–1685)

Version tags: **Dooku, 83, 19 BBY, fresh → Force-exhausted**; **Anakin pre-fall (restrained) → rage-amped**; **Obi-Wan Soresu master**.

| # | Lines | Exchange | Edge |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | L1417–1425 | A double attack from both flanks. Dooku is «no longer between them»; his boot «smacked him tumbling». | Dooku |
| 2 | L1427–1431 | One-handed bind on Obi-Wan while Dooku TK-flings chairs and the situation table to pin Anakin. «Dooku barely even seemed to be paying attention to him.» | Dooku (dominant) |
| 3 | L1435–1437 | Anakin's TK table almost shatters Dooku's spine. «The boy has some power after all.» | Anakin |
| 4 | L1443–1457 | Dooku sidesteps so that each Jedi blocks the other. «Really,» Dooku said, «this is pathetic.» | Dooku |
| 5 | L1459–1465 | «They allowed the Force to direct them; Dooku directed the Force.» He could have killed them «as casually as that creature Maul had destroyed the vigos of the Black Sun» (self-assessment), but «only one death was in his plan» and «not a young man». | Dooku (holding back, tiring) |
| 6 | L1467–1475 | Obi-Wan's bait-leap opens a lane for Anakin's thrust at the heart. It only scorches the cloak. «Dooku thought, What?» | Jedi |
| 7 | L1479–1485 | Obi-Wan's defensive web plus Anakin's Djem So: wrist bent, his own saber scorches his shoulder. «His own elegant Makashi simply did not generate the kinetic power to meet Djem So head-to-head.» | Jedi (clear) |
| 8 | L1489–1503 | A reverse ankle-sweep on Anakin. Dooku tries to kill Kenobi via his Ataro habits, but «Kenobi had become a master of Soresu.» «These clowns might-just possibly-actually be able to beat him.» | Jedi |
| 9 | L1507 | Dooku summons power «from throughout the universe»; a flick sends Kenobi «flying backward to crash hard against the wall». | Dooku |
| 10 | L1509–1517 | Anakin alone: the «meteor strike» blows. «each block aged him a decade.» Force exhaustion sets in. | Anakin (dominant) |
| 11 | L1519–1531 | Dooku calls the SBDs. Kenobi destroys them. Dooku side-stamps Anakin, wheel-kicks Kenobi off the balcony and TK-accelerates his fall. Kenobi is out of the fight. | Dooku (using his last burst) |
| 12 | L1535–1539 | Anakin's boot sends Dooku over the rail. He barely Force-cushions his landing. | Anakin |
| 13 | L1545–1561 | Dooku re-channels («the weight of his years dropped away»). Anakin presses with the «gift of fury», yet «he was holding himself back». | Anakin |
| 14 | L1563–1569 | Taunt: «Aren't you a little old to be afraid of the dark?» → «Dooku met the boy's charge easily». | Dooku |
| 15 | L1573–1579 | Sidious: «Strike now! Strike! Kill him!» | — |
| 16 | Ch4 L1593–1631 | Anakin rage-amped: «Dooku is dead already»; Dooku's best TK throw is smashed aside; both hands taken. | Anakin (stomp) |
| 17 | Ch4 L1657–1685 | «Do it! Now!» → beheading. | Kill |

**Takeaways.** (a) Fresh Dooku beats either Jedi in isolated moments and controls the 2v1 early, but the 2v1 turns against him by exchange 7–8, and he admits they «might-just possibly-actually be able to beat him». (b) The fight is decided by **Dooku's stamina** («each block aged him a decade», «Force exhaustion») plus Anakin's escalating rage. (c) Restrained Anakin is *Dooku-level* (exchange 14). Rage-amped Anakin is *clearly above an exhausted Dooku* (exchange 16). The novel gives no clean read on rage-Anakin vs *fresh* Dooku. (d) Dooku was partly holding back for most of the fight (he planned only Kenobi's death).

### 2.4 Obi-Wan Kenobi

1. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch3, L1479, 1497).** «a defensive velocity so bewilderingly fast that Dooku dared not even» [attack]; «Kenobi had become a master of Soresu.» → **Defensive parity-plus with Dooku.**
2. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch3, L1525).** «a single slash of his lightsaber dismantled both droids» (SBDs). → **Fodder.**
3. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch7, L2501–2507).** «Obi-Wan might be able to temporarily reverse the polarity of the electrodrivers in the general's mech» / «Which is exactly what he did.» → **Grievous disarmed by a fine-control Force trick. Later reused on Vader's hand (L8431).**
4. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch7, L2511).** A TK saber-recall slices his binders in mid-flight; he frees Anakin's at the same time (L2515). → **Precision TK.**
5. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch7, L2529).** Grievous: «The ease with which Kenobi had taken command of the situation was frightening.» → **Enemy-assessed composure.**
6. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch15, L5861).** He makes droids destroy each other. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch18, L7405)** mind-tricks droid spheres. → **Utility Force.**
7. **[Obi-Wan vs Grievous] (Ch15, L6033).** «each of the twelve strikes per second came from a different angle» … «Not one touched him.» → **Defends against 12 strikes/sec.**
8. **[Obi-Wan vs Grievous] (Ch15, L6037–6039).** «at twenty strikes per second, he overloaded Obi-Wan's defense. So Obi-Wan used his defense to attack.» → He cuts a lightsaber handgrip, and «the duranium thumb and first finger» go with it. → **Counter-cut against a 20/sec attack.**
9. **[Obi-Wan vs Grievous] (Ch15, L6045–6049).** «Grievous lunged. / Obi-Wan parried. / Pieces of lightsabers bounced on the durasteel deck.» → **Destroys all of Grievous's remaining sabers in one parry.**
10. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch15, L6067).** He cuts through «the tangled mob of droids all the way to the arch in a single sustained surge». → **Fodder.**
11. **[Obi-Wan vs Grievous] (Ch15, L6139–6147).** He TK-flips the electrostaff to block a blaster shot, then «spun the staff, catching every bolt, not even slowing down». → **Reaction feat.**
12. **[Obi-Wan vs Grievous] (Ch15, L6157–6159).** «Obi-Wan's arm had the Force to give it strength» / «Grievous's forearm bent like a cheap spoon.» → **Force-augmented strength beats the cyborg's «ten times stronger than human» limb (L1859).**
13. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch15, L6163, 6173–6177).** He rips off the stomach plate, TK-pulls the blaster, shoots the gut sac. → **Kill: Grievous.**
14. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch18, L7183, 7335).** He survives the AT-TE shot and the fall into the sinkhole lake. → **Durability/luck.**
15. **[Obi-Wan + Yoda] (Ch19, L7687).** «Six seconds later, all eight of them were dead.» → **Speed blitz on clone squad (shared feat).**
16. **[Obi-Wan vs Vader] (Ch20, L8413–8415).** «Blade-to-blade, they were identical.» / «In every exchange, Obi-Wan gave ground.» → **Defensive parity.**
17. **[Obi-Wan vs Vader] (Ch20, L8431–8435).** The polarity trick disarms Vader, and Obi-Wan holds both blades: «The flaw of power is arrogance.» → **Wins a full exchange; he could arguably have pressed the advantage.**
18. **[Obi-Wan vs Vader] (Ch20, L8497).** «with the Force caught up his own blade, reversing it» → escapes the grip. **Ch20 L8535**: he cuts the cable under Anakin. → **Terrain/tactics.**
19. **[Obi-Wan vs Vader] (Ch20, L8569–8573).** He feints with a dive and roll, takes the high ground, and cuts as Anakin is «Half a second too slow». → **Wins.**

### 2.5 General Grievous

1. **[Grievous] (Ch5, L1835).** The gunnery officer's face is crushed «under the impact of an armorplast fist». → **Fodder strength.**
2. **[Grievous] (Ch5, L1859).** «ceramic armorplast plates» that «can stop a burst from a starfighter's laser cannon» and limbs «ten times stronger than human» (narrated spec). → **Durability/strength statement.**
3. **[Grievous] (Ch5, L1863–1865).** «Each and all, he has taken from the dead hands of Jedi he has killed» (named: K'Kruhk, Jmmaar, Puroth, Nystammall). → **Jedi-killer résumé (Knights/Masters of unspecified level).**
4. **[Grievous] (Ch7, L2519–2521).** «He ripped the console wholly into the air» and hurled it. → **Lifting strength.**
5. **[Grievous] (Ch7, L2529–2531).** «Grievous was starting to think less about winning this particular encounter than about surviving it.» → **Self-assessed as outmatched by Kenobi + Skywalker together.**
6. **[Grievous] (Ch15, L6013).** Mace reports that Grievous's computers «had been able to respond in kind after a single exchange» against Vaapad. → **Adaptive-combat statement (from Mace's mag-lev duel; outcome not stated).**
7. **[Grievous vs Obi-Wan] (Ch15, L6033–6037).** Four arms, «thrice in a single second» each, «twelve strikes per second», ramping to «twenty strikes per second». → **Speed feat that overloads Soresu for a moment.**
8. **[Grievous] (Ch15, L6065).** «General Grievous was very good at running away.» → **Characterization.**
9. **[Grievous vs Obi-Wan] (Ch15, L6135).** Holdout blaster: «fired so fast that his arm blurred to invisiblity» (sic). → **Draw speed.**
10. **[Grievous vs Obi-Wan] (Ch15, L6151–6153).** «Do you think I am foolish enough to arm my bodyguards with weapons that can actually hurt me?» → throws Obi-Wan «with effortless strength». → **Electrostaff-immune; strength.**
11. **[Grievous] (Ch15, L6159, 6177).** Forearm bent; killed by a blaster bolt to the gut sac. → **Loss.**

### 2.6 Grievous vs Obi-Wan: exchange by exchange (Ch15)

| # | Lines | Exchange | Edge |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | L5861–5877 | Obi-Wan makes the droids wreck each other and offers to take Grievous alive. | Obi-Wan |
| 2 | L6033–6035 | Grievous throws 12 strikes/sec; «Not one touched him.» | Obi-Wan (defense) |
| 3 | L6037–6039 | 20/sec overloads the defense, and Obi-Wan uses his defense to attack: hand and lightsaber cut. | Obi-Wan |
| 4 | L6045–6051 | Lunge, parry: «Pieces of lightsabers bounced on the durasteel deck.» Grievous flees. | Obi-Wan (decisive) |
| 5 | L6113–6125 | Boga chase. Obi-Wan drops his lightsaber and boots Grievous's skull. | Even |
| 6 | L6135–6149 | Holdout blaster vs TK-staff; Obi-Wan dents the stomach plate. | Obi-Wan |
| 7 | L6151–6153 | The staff can't hurt Grievous; he throws Obi-Wan. | Grievous |
| 8 | L6157–6163 | Force-strength block bends the forearm; stomach plate ripped off. | Obi-Wan |
| 9 | L6165 | Thrown again toward the precipice; Obi-Wan anchors himself through the Force and is winded. | Grievous |
| 10 | L6173–6181 | TK-pulled blaster to the gut sac. Kill. | Obi-Wan |

**Takeaway:** With sabers, Obi-Wan wins **low-to-mid diff**. Grievous's best output is countered within one exchange, and he loses every saber. Unarmed, it is **mid-high diff**: Obi-Wan is thrown twice and winded, and wins with a pulled blaster.

### 2.7 Mace Windu

1. **[Mace base] (Ch8, L2805; Ch16, L6273).** Shatterpoint sense: Anakin is «a pivot point, the fulcrum of a lever»; later «He was a shatterpoint. The shatterpoint.» → **Unique perception ability.**
2. **[Mace base] (Ch15, L6013–6029).** He has fought Grievous before and recommends Obi-Wan: «of all living Jedi, you have the best chance to defeat him» / «Not a master. The master». → **Statement about Obi-Wan from a peer swordsman.**
3. **[Mace Vaapad] (Ch17, L6665).** «With a vaapad, one never knew how many tentacles it had until it was dead: they move too fast to count. Almost too fast to see. So did Mace's blade.» → **Speed hype.**
4. **[Mace Vaapad] (Ch17, L6673).** «an oblate sphere of purple fire within which there seemed to be dozens of swords slashing in all directions at once» (Anakin POV) → **Visible-speed feat.**
5. **[Mace Vaapad loop] (Ch17, L6691–6703).** «He accepted the furious speed of the Sith Lord, drew the shadow's rage and power into his inmost center-And let it fountain out again.» → «Impasse.» → **Sword stalemate with Sidious.**
6. **[Mace shatterpoint] (Ch17, L6707–6713).** «his mind slid along the circuit of dark power, tracing it back to its limitless source. / Feeling for its shatterpoint.» → it leads to Anakin. → **The way out of the stalemate runs through Anakin.**
7. **[Mace] (Ch17, L6715).** «he slashed away the transparisteel with a single flourish» → He cuts open the window.
8. **[Mace] (Ch17, L6719–6725).** «he turned the shadow's fear into a weapon» … «slash the shadow's lightsaber in half.» → **Disarms Sidious.**
9. **[Mace Vaapad] (Ch17, L6749–6751).** «Power passed into him and out again without touching him. / And the circuit completed itself: the lightning reflected back to its source.» → **Reflects lightning (with Sidious letting it rebound; see §6).**
10. **[Mace] (Ch17, L6773, 6779).** «This was beyond Vaapad; he had no strength left to fight against his own blade»; «Anakin, he's too strong for me-» → **Losing the lightning struggle.**
11. **[Mace] (Ch17, L6811–6815).** «He had been so intent on Palpatine's shatterpoint that he'd never thought to look for Anakin's.» → **Dies.**
12. **[Mace, referenced] (Ch19, L7931).** Obi-Wan: Palpatine faced «Mace and Agen and Kit and Saesee-four of the greatest swordsmen our Order has ever produced. By himself.» → **In-universe hype for the 4v1 (Obi-Wan's appraisal, partly inaccurate: three died fast).**

### 2.8 Mace (and three Masters) vs Sidious: exchange by exchange (Ch17, L6585–6815)

Versions: **Sidious concealed → unmasked, 19 BBY**; **Mace base → Vaapad loop + shatterpoint**; Tiin, Kolar, Fisto (Masters).

| # | Lines | Exchange | Edge |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | L6585–6591 | Arrest: four blades out. Palpatine plays the frightened old man and baits Tiin's telepathy. | — |
| 2 | L6593–6597 | Tiin is beheaded by «A smear of red-flashing darkness». | Sidious (instant kill) |
| 3 | L6603–6611 | Kolar is pierced through the forehead. Palpatine «stood at the doorway». | Sidious (instant kill) |
| 4 | L6639–6649 | (Off-page during Anakin's flight) Fisto is killed; his head is left on the desk. | Sidious |
| 5 | L6659–6703 | Mace in Vaapad, a «superconducting loop», «no wound taken on either side». «Impasse.» | Even |
| 6 | L6705–6713 | Mace reads the shatterpoint and finds Anakin at the door. | (Info) |
| 7 | L6715–6717 | Mace leaps and cuts the window open; Sidious's Force surge «nearly blew him right out of the gap». | Sidious |
| 8 | L6719–6727 | On the ledge, the «pulsar of fear» is exploited; Sidious's saber is cut in half. | Mace |
| 9 | L6729–6735 | «For all your power, you are no Jedi.» Arrest declared. | — |
| 10 | L6741–6755 | «Do you think the fear you feel is mine?» Lightning; Vaapad reflects it; Sidious «fed the power with his pain». | Even |
| 11 | L6759–6765 | Sidious is unafraid with Anakin behind Mace; Mace realizes «Palpatine trusts Anakin Skywalker». | (Info) |
| 12 | L6769–6779 | The lightning pushes Mace's own blade toward his face. «This was beyond Vaapad»; «he's too strong for me-» | Sidious |
| 13 | L6781–6785 | Sidious ends the lightning and fakes surrender. | Sidious (deception) |
| 14 | L6787–6809 | The Anakin argument. Mace moves to execute. | — |
| 15 | L6811–6815 | Anakin cuts off Mace's hand; lightning kills Mace. | Sidious |

**Reading.** The novel gives the sword phase to neither side (an impasse). It gives the *disarm* to Mace, through fear on a ledge. It gives the *lightning/power* phase to Sidious by text (exchanges 12–13). And it frames Sidious's whole confidence through «Palpatine trusts Anakin Skywalker». The disarm was set up by a shatterpoint path that led to Anakin. The fear Mace exploited is explicitly put in doubt («Do you think the fear you feel is mine?»). Sidious also had just killed three Masters in seconds. Nothing in Ch17 shows Mace beating Sidious *without Anakin in the room*. But nothing shows Sidious beating a Vaapad-looped Mace in sword combat either.

### 2.9 Yoda

1. **[Yoda] (Ch19, L7687).** With Obi-Wan: «Six seconds later, all eight of them were dead.» → **Speed blitz.**
2. **[Yoda] (Ch19, L7919, 7925).** «Make a Jedi fall, one cannot; beyond even Lord Sidious, this is.» / «Strong enough to face Lord Sidious, you will never be. Die you will, and painfully.» → **His assessment: Obi-Wan ≪ Sidious.**
3. **[Yoda, Senate duel] (Ch20, L8157–8159).** «the truth of him could be seen only in the Force. / In the Force, he was a fountain of light.» → **Presence hype.**
4. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8173–8175).** (Sidious tells Amedda to flee.) Before the duel: «A lightsaber appeared, green as sunlight in a forest.» (L8183).
5. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8301).** He bends the lightning onto the Redrobes. → **Redirect.**
6. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8305–8311).** Scorched by lightning, then «it was the whole of the Jedi Order that rocketed from its huddle, making of its own body a weapon to blast the Sith to the ground.» → **Durability plus a counterattack that floors Sidious.**
7. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8385).** «a flying kick or a surgically precise punch, though these were traded, too» → **Hand-to-hand parity.**
8. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8397–8401).** «the fiercest, most implacable, most devastatingly powerful foe the darkness had ever known... just-didn't-have it.» / «He had lost before he was born.» → **Thematic/strategic defeat, not explicitly power. See §6.**
9. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8453).** «bend back his lightnings into the cage of energy that enclosed them both; the creature had reached the limits of his strength» → **Holds a lightning cage until his strength runs out.**
10. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8455–8457).** «the little green freak took its full power» → he falls 100 m and survives.
11. **[Yoda] (Ch20, L8471).** «Yoda sprinted along the service accessway below the Arena faster than a human being could run» → **Escape.**
12. **[Yoda] (Ch21, L8657).** «A power greater than all, it is.» (On Qui-Gon's retained identity.) → **Not a feat.**

### 2.10 Yoda vs Sidious: exchange by exchange (Ch20)

| # | Lines | Exchange | Edge |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | L8161–8189 | Banter. Sidious tells Amedda to flee. «Even a fraction of the dark side is more power than your Jedi arrogance can conceive» | — |
| 2 | L8191, 8301 | Opening lightning; Yoda bends it onto the Redrobes. | Yoda |
| 3 | L8305 | Sidious scorches Yoda «into a smoldering huddle». | Sidious |
| 4 | L8311 | Yoda «rocketed from its huddle»: a body-blast that puts the Sith on the ground. | Yoda |
| 5 | L8315–8323 | Blades: «Light against dark. / Winner take all.» | Even |
| 6 | L8385–8387 | Lightning, blades, kicks, punches. The podium rises 100 m+, and pods are ripped free «the Force and the podium's controls». | Even |
| 7 | L8389–8411 | Yoda's insight: «just-didn't-have it»; «if he fell here, that hope would die with him». | Thematic: Sidious |
| 8 | L8453 | The lightning cage: Yoda «had reached the limits of his strength». Sidious breaks off and jumps to a pod; Yoda follows. | Sidious |
| 9 | L8455–8457 | Lightning hits Yoda in the air. He falls 100 m. | Sidious |
| 10 | L8459 | Sidious is «a very old, very tired man, gasping for air». | (Cost) |
| 11 | L8471 | Yoda escapes; Sidious «did not see a body» (L8461). | Draw/retreat |

**Reading:** Sidious wins on endurance. L8453 ends «the creature leapt to follow-Half a second too slow.» This is the same phrase Stover uses for Anakin's fatal leap on Mustafar (L8569), a deliberate parallel: both «chosen» champions lose by overcommitting to a leap. Sidious ends the fight drained. Yoda chooses to withdraw, *because* «if he fell here, that hope would die with him». That makes it a **mid-high to high-diff Sidious win**, not a stomp.

### 2.11 Vader vs Obi-Wan: exchange by exchange (Ch20)

| # | Lines | Exchange | Edge |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | L8339 | «Don't make me kill you, Obi-Wan. If you are not with me, you are against me.» | — |
| 2 | L8413–8415 | «Blade-to-blade, they were identical.»; «Obi-Wan gave ground». | Even (Anakin pressing) |
| 3 | L8417–8419 | Through the control center; Obi-Wan's flipped bolts are sent back «Easily, contemptuously». | Vader |
| 4 | L8421–8425 | «You're no match for the power of the dark side.» → «A roar of the Force blasted Obi-Wan back into a wall». | Vader |
| 5 | L8427–8435 | The polarity trick: Vader's saber falls, and Obi-Wan holds both blades. | Obi-Wan |
| 6 | L8437–8447 | Vader body-crashes him and grips his wrists «with impossible strength», bending the bones. | Vader |
| 7 | L8493–8497 | Obi-Wan lets go of everything; he retrieves his blade and cuts through the walls. | Obi-Wan (escape) |
| 8 | L8499–8541 | Balcony, plant collapse, cable duel; Obi-Wan cuts a cable; Anakin swings away. | Even |
| 9 | L8563–8569 | Obi-Wan dives and rolls to the bank: «Half a second too slow.» | Obi-Wan |
| 10 | L8571–8573 | Both knees, then the left arm. | Obi-Wan (decisive) |

**Reading:** The novel calls the swordplay a dead heat. In raw power (TK roar, grip strength) Vader is *ahead*. Obi-Wan's edges are a technique (polarity trick), detachment («He let it go», L8531) and terrain/feint. **High diff, Obi-Wan.**

### 2.14 Feats added from the line-by-line pass (Ch8–14, 18, 21)

1. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch14, L5095, L5117).** «In the Force, the whole apartment stank of Obi-Wan.» / he scans for «Force-echoes of their presence» of specific Senators. → **Psychometric residue-sensing (utility).**
2. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch12, L4367).** «The durasteel piping groaned in his mechanical grip.» → **Casual strength of the prosthetic hand.**
3. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch14, L4957; L5067–5071).** «The Force could keep him upright, keep him moving, keep him thinking, but it could not give him rest.» Then anger «began to burn away» his fatigue. → **Force-sustained sleeplessness; rage as a stamina amp. This anticipates the Dooku-duel mechanics.**
4. **[Anakin pre-fall] (Ch9, L3529).** His prophetic dreams «had never been wrong. Not in the slightest detail.» → **Precognition (narrated as reliable).**
5. **[Grievous] (Ch10, L3449).** «Grievous moved so swiftly that he seemed to teleport from the window to half a meter in front of Gunray.» → **Burst speed (against a non-combatant).**
6. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch18, L7033).** «The weapon floated gently up to Kenobi's hand» → **TK recall.**
7. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch18, L7173–7183).** Cody brings heavy weapons from five companies because he knows «just how hard to kill» Kenobi is. Boga absorbs the volley (L7345–7347). → **Clone-assessed durability hype. The survival comes from his mount's sacrifice, not from his own feat.**
8. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch18, L7405–7407).** He breathes into the Force «a suggestion» that makes a cave monster eat a seeker droid. → **Beast influence.**
9. **[Obi-Wan] (Ch18, L7337; L7545).** He survives underwater on a rebreather. «Empty, he found clarity. Scrubbed clean, the Force shone through him.» → **Mental recovery (thematic setup for Mustafar's «He let it go»).**
10. **[Yoda] (Ch11, L3847).** «destroy the droid armies I can, and still be available to Coruscant» → **Self-assessed army-level utility (Kashyyyk itself is off-page).**
11. **[Sidious] (Ch21, L8613–8617).** «had already leapt for the cliffbank above with effortless strength» … «he set his will upon him. / Live, Lord Vader. Live, my apprentice.» → **Physical leap carrying Vader; life-sustaining will (Force-heal adjacent).**
12. **[Sidious] (Ch10, L3509; Ch21 L8805).** «Dooku was not lost, he was sacrificed-a strategic sacrifice» → **Strategy.**
13. **[Vader, suited] (Ch21, L8867).** «with all your world-destroying fury it is only droids around you that implode, and equipment, and the table on which you were strapped shatters, and in the end, you cannot touch the shadow.» → **Feat (TK implosion of droids, equipment and the table) plus an explicit nerf. See S46.**
14. **[Child Jedi, Temple] (Ch18, L7119).** «A child, no more than ten years old, swinging a lightsaber» kills several clones before he is «shot to rags». → **Fodder anchor for the Temple raid.**

## 3. Statement table (curated, with reliability)

Reliability: **A** = omniscient narrator (non-POV) or Stover's authorial essay voice. **B** = POV narration from a knowledgeable character (can be biased or mistaken). **C** = in-character dialogue from an informed speaker. **D** = boast, manipulation or a known liar (Palpatine), or an uninformed speaker.

| # | Ch / Line | Speaker → subject | Quote | Version | Rel. | Use |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| S1 | Ch1 L443 | Narration (Obi-Wan POV) → Anakin | «The most powerful Jedi of his generation. Perhaps of any generation. The fastest. The strongest.» | Anakin pre-fall | B | Anakin potential/raw power hype |
| S2 | Ch2 L1123 | Sidious → Anakin | «He is powerful. Potentially more powerful than even myself.» | Anakin *potential* (not 19 BBY actual) | C | Ceiling, not current level |
| S3 | Ch2 L1141 | Dooku POV → Sidious | «might be considered to dwarf even the power of the dark side itself» | Sidious concealed | B | Strategy hype |
| S4 | Ch3 L1351–1353 | Dooku POV → Palpatine | «Palpatine was an event horizon» / «A black hole of the Force.» | Sidious concealed | B | Sidious ≫ Dooku |
| S5 | Ch3 L1415 | Dooku → Jedi | «Unless one of you happens to be carrying Yoda in his pocket» | Dooku | D (taunt) | Dooku sees Yoda as his threat |
| S6 | Ch3 L1485 | Dooku POV → Anakin | «a Djem So stylist, and as fine a one as Dooku had ever seen» | Anakin pre-fall | B | Anakin's sword skill |
| S7 | Ch3 L1497 | Dooku POV → Obi-Wan | «Kenobi had become a master of Soresu.» | Obi-Wan | B | Obi-Wan's defense |
| S8 | Ch3 L1503 | Dooku POV → both | «These clowns might-just possibly-actually be able to beat him.» | both 2v1 | B | 2v1 ≥ Dooku |
| S9 | Ch3 L1557 | Dooku POV → Anakin | «This boy had the gift of fury.» | Anakin rage-amp | B | Rage amp exists |
| S10 | Ch3 L1559 | Dooku POV → Anakin | «even now, he was holding himself back» | Anakin pre-fall | B | Headroom above shown |
| S11 | Ch4 L1733 | Palpatine → Dooku | «he had powers beyond your imagination» | Dooku | D | Palpatine flattering Anakin |
| S12 | Ch7 L2529 | Grievous POV → both | «Skywalker was reportedly the greater warrior» | Anakin vs Obi-Wan | B (hearsay) | Rumor ranking |
| S13 | Ch9 L3277 | Anakin → self | «I'm more powerful than any Jedi Master alive.» | Anakin pre-fall | D (boast) | Self-assessment |
| S14 | Ch10 L3413 | Mace → Grievous | «more than a match for most Jedi.» | Grievous | C | Grievous > average Jedi |
| S15 | Ch11 L3839 | Kolar → Anakin | «the one Jedi we can best hope would survive an encounter with a Sith Lord» | Anakin pre-fall | C | Council's view |
| S16 | Ch15 L6015 | Mace → Obi-Wan | «of all living Jedi, you have the best chance to defeat him» | Obi-Wan vs Grievous | C (expert) | Obi-Wan's Soresu ≥ Mace for this matchup |
| S17 | Ch15 L6029 | Mace → Obi-Wan | «Not a master. The master» | Obi-Wan Soresu | C (expert) | Best Soresu user |
| S18 | Ch15 L6007–6011 | Narration → Obi-Wan | «He is simplicity itself.» / «That is his power.» | Obi-Wan | A | Style |
| S19 | Ch17 L6669 | Narration → Mace | «Mace Windu created this style, and he was its only living master.» | Mace Vaapad | A | Vaapad uniqueness |
| S20 | Ch17 L6703 | Narration → Mace/Sidious | «Impasse.» | Mace Vaapad loop vs Sidious | A | Sword parity |
| S21 | Ch17 L6773 | Mace POV | «This was beyond Vaapad; he had no strength left to fight against his own blade» | Mace vs Sidious lightning | B | Sidious > Mace in power |
| S22 | Ch17 L6779 | Mace → Anakin | «Anakin, he's too strong for me-» | Mace vs Sidious | C | same (note: possibly sincere, not tactical) |
| S23 | Ch17 L6785 | Palpatine | «I am too weak, in the end. Too old, and too weak.» | Sidious | D (feint) | Discard |
| S24 | Ch17 L6845 | Sidious → Anakin | «You are the greatest of the Jedi. You can be the greatest of the Sith» | Anakin mid-fall | D | Manipulation |
| S25 | Ch19 L7919 | Yoda → Obi-Wan | «Make a Jedi fall, one cannot; beyond even Lord Sidious, this is.» | Sidious | C | Limit of Sith power (no direct corruption) |
| S26 | Ch19 L7925 | Yoda → Obi-Wan | «Strong enough to face Lord Sidious, you will never be. Die you will, and painfully.» | Obi-Wan vs Sidious | C (expert) | Obi-Wan ≪ Sidious |
| S27 | Ch19 L7931 | Obi-Wan → Yoda | «four of the greatest swordsmen our Order has ever produced. By himself. Even both of us together wouldn't have a chance.» | Sidious | C | 4v1 hype; pessimism |
| S28 | Ch20 L8051 | Vader POV → Sidious | «He had the measure of his "Master" already» | Vader new | D (hubris) | Discard |
| S29 | Ch20 L8159 | Narration → Yoda | «In the Force, he was a fountain of light.» | Yoda | A | Presence |
| S30 | Ch20 L8185 | Sidious → Yoda | «Even a fraction of the dark side is more power than your Jedi arrogance can conceive» | Sidious | D | Boast |

| S32 | Ch20 L8403 | Narration | «The Sith had changed. The Sith had grown, had adapted» | Sidious (lineage) | A | Strategic superiority |
| S33 | Ch20 L8413 | Narration | «Blade-to-blade, they were identical.» | Obi-Wan vs Vader | A | Sword parity |
| S34 | Ch20 L8421 | Vader → Obi-Wan | «You're no match for the power of the dark side.» | Vader | D | Boast (half true on power) |
| S35 | Ch20 L8435 | Obi-Wan → Vader | «The flaw of power is arrogance.» | Vader | C | Thematic key to the loss |
| S36 | Ch20 L8453 | Sidious POV → Yoda | «the creature had reached the limits of his strength» | Yoda vs Sidious | B (victor's POV) | Sidious outlasts Yoda |
| S37 | Ch20 L8459 | Narration → Sidious | «a very old, very tired man, gasping for air» | Sidious post-duel | A | Duel cost Sidious heavily |
